from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class LS_Command():
    chan:int
    comm:str
    addr: list[str]

#커맨드 종합
def separate_addr(addr_list):
    comm_list = []
    for chan, adr in addr_list.items():
        if adr[0]:
            comm_list.extend(separate_chan_addr(adr[0], chan))
        if adr[1]:
            comm_list.extend(separate_chan_addr(adr[1], chan))
    print(comm_list)
    return comm_list

#단일 채널의 커맨드 리스트 반환
def separate_chan_addr(ls, chan):
    adr_stack = []
    hex_list = []
    for i in ls:
        adr_stack.append(i)
        if len(adr_stack) == 16:
            hex_list.append(makecommand(adr_stack, chan))
            adr_stack = []
    
    if adr_stack:
        hex_list.append(makecommand(adr_stack, chan))
    return hex_list

#단일 포맷 반환
def makecommand(addrs, chan):
    len_addrs = [f"{len(x):02d}{x}" for x in addrs]

    return LS_Command(
        chan = chan,
        comm=f"\x05{chan:02d}RSS{len(len_addrs):02x}{"".join(len_addrs)}\x04",
        addr=addrs
    )

■ File : DataList.py

import re
from typing import Dict

# LS주소 구조 (채널: [[부울], [정수]])
LS_addr_list: Dict[int, list[list[str]]] = {}
LS_addr_static_list: Dict[int, list[list[str]]] = {}

def add_LS_addr(addr: str):
    chan = int(addr[:2]) 
    adr = addr[2:]   

    if chan not in LS_addr_list:
        LS_addr_list[chan] = [[], []]

    if adr[2] == "X" and not adr in LS_addr_list[chan][0]:
        LS_addr_list[chan][0].append(adr)  # 부울 영역에 추가
    if adr[2] == "W" and not adr in LS_addr_list[chan][1]:
        LS_addr_list[chan][1].append(adr)  # 정수 영역에 추가

def add_LS_static_addr(addr: str):
    chan = int(addr[:2]) 
    adr = addr[2:]   

    if chan not in LS_addr_static_list:
        LS_addr_static_list[chan] = [[], []]

    if adr[2] == "X" and not adr in LS_addr_static_list[chan][0]:
        LS_addr_static_list[chan][0].append(adr)  # 부울 영역에 추가
    if adr[2] == "W" and not adr in LS_addr_static_list[chan][1]:
        LS_addr_static_list[chan][1].append(adr)  # 정수 영역에 추가

def sort_LS_addresses():
    global LS_addr_list, LS_addr_static_list

    def extract_number(addr_str: str) -> int:
        match = re.search(r'\d+', addr_str)
        return int(match.group()) if match else 0

    LS_addr_list = dict(sorted(LS_addr_list.items()))
    for chan in LS_addr_list:
        LS_addr_list[chan][0].sort(key=extract_number)  # 부울 리스트 정렬
        LS_addr_list[chan][1].sort(key=extract_number)  # 정수 리스트 정렬

    LS_addr_static_list = dict(sorted(LS_addr_static_list.items()))
    for chan in LS_addr_static_list:
        LS_addr_static_list[chan][0].sort(key=extract_number)
        LS_addr_static_list[chan][1].sort(key=extract_number)

■ File : Ethernet.py
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\fac_server\DataLayer\Ethernet_Layer\Ethernet.py

import asyncio
import websockets
import json
import time

uri = ""

async def send_periodic_data(ws, AdrMap):
    # 💡 무한 루프는 내부 비동기 태스크가 살아있는 동안만 유지되도록 설정
    while True:
        payload = AdrMap
        await ws.send(json.dumps(payload))
        await asyncio.sleep(1)  # 1초 주기

async def receive_server_commands(ws):
    async for message in ws:
        packet = json.loads(message)
        
        if packet.get("type") == "COMMAND_REQUEST":
            req_id = packet.get("request_id")
            print(f"📩 사용자 요청 들어옴! [ID: {req_id}]")
            
            # 조회 결과를 서버로 즉시 응답
            response = {
                "type": "COMMAND_RESPONSE",
                "request_id": req_id,
                "data": {"40005": 1024}
            }
            await ws.send(json.dumps(response))

async def Ethernet_Loop(AdrMap):
    # 💡 [핵심] 연결 끊김을 감지하고 무한히 재시도하는 루프
    while True:
        try:
            print(f"🔄 서버 웹소켓 연결 시도 중... ({uri})")
            
            async with websockets.connect(
                uri, 
                ping_interval=None, 
                ping_timeout=None
            ) as ws:
                print("✅ 서버 웹소켓 연결 완료")
                await asyncio.gather(
                    send_periodic_data(ws, AdrMap),
                    receive_server_commands(ws)
                )
                
        except (websockets.exceptions.ConnectionClosed, ConnectionRefusedError, OSError) as e:
            # 서버가 꺼져있거나(Refused), 네트워크가 단선되었을 때(OSError) 잡히는 예외구간
            print(f"❌ 웹소켓 연결 유실 또는 실패: {e}")
            print("⏳ 3초 후 재연결을 시도합니다...")
            await asyncio.sleep(3)  # 3초간 비동기 대기 후 while문 처음으로 돌아가 재연결 실행
            
        except Exception as e:
            # 기타 예상치 못한 에러가 나더라도 루프가 완전히 죽지 않도록 방어
            print(f"⚠️ 예상치 못한 에러 발생: {e}")
            await asyncio.sleep(3)

def Ethernet_Run(AdrMap):
    print(uri)
    asyncio.run(Ethernet_Loop(AdrMap))

# ==========================================
# 2. 실행 함수

def init(target_room_id: int = None):
    for room_id, roles in MAPPING_DATA.items():
        # 특정 room_id만 필터링해서 처리하고 싶은 경우
        if target_room_id is not None and room_id != target_room_id:
            continue

        for role_name, items in roles.items():
            for item in items:
                adr = item[0]
                label = item[1]
                degree = item[2] if len(item) > 2 else 0

                DL.add_LS_addr(adr)
                MP.Mapping(adr, f"{room_id}.{role_name}_{label}", degree)

■ File : Machine_Transmission.py
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\fac_server\DataLayer\Machine_Layer\Machine_Transmission.py

import serial
import time
import queue

# 스레드 안전한 FIFO 큐 생성
send_queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=200)
recv_queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=200)

# 시리얼 포트 설정
ser = serial.Serial(
    port='COM3',          
    baudrate=115200,        
    bytesize=serial.EIGHTBITS,
    parity=serial.PARITY_NONE,
    stopbits=serial.STOPBITS_ONE,
    timeout=1             
)

stop_communication = False

#RS485통신 루프(메시지 큐 방식)
def background_serial_worker():
    global stop_communication
    print("[BG] Serial Thread Started.")
    
    while not stop_communication:
        try:
            while True:
                try:
                    data_to_send = send_queue.get_nowait()
                except queue.Empty:
                    break
                
                framenum = int(data_to_send[0])
                real_send = data_to_send[1]

                ser.write(real_send.encode('ascii'))
                ser.flush()
                
                response = ser.read_until(expected=b'\x03')

                if response and response.endswith(b'\x03'):
                    recv_queue.put((framenum, response))
                    send_queue.task_done()
                else:
                    print(f"[BG] 통신 에러: '{real_send.strip()}'에 대한 응답에 \\x03가 없습니다.")
                    
                    ser.reset_input_buffer()

                    recv_queue.put((framenum, "ERROR_TIMEOUT"))
                    send_queue.task_done()

                    print("[BG] 남은 송신 대기열을 모두 취소합니다.")
                    try:
                        while True:
                            send_queue.get_nowait()
                            send_queue.task_done()
                    except queue.Empty:
                        pass
                    
                    break

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[BG] Error: {e}")
            
        time.sleep(1.0)

    print("[BG] Serial Thread Stopped.")

import threading
import time
import InitData as ID
from typing import Dict

import Command_Layer.Command as CM
import Machine_Layer.Machine_Transmission as MT
import Data_Layer.DataList as DL
import Mapping_Layer.Mapping as MP
import Ethernet_Layer.Ethernet as ET
import config 

#컨피그 설정 적용
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ET.uri = f"ws://127.0.0.1:8000/api/facilities/data/set/{config.facility_id}"
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

ID.init()
DL.sort_LS_addresses()

comm_list = CM.separate_addr(DL.LS_addr_list)
print(comm_list)

data_list: Dict[str, int] = {}

framenum = 0
framelist: Dict[int, CM.LS_Command] = {}

#동적 값들 받는 루프
def Dynamic_EnQueue_Loop():
    global framenum
    while True:
        for i in comm_list:
            MT.send_queue.put((framenum, i.comm)) # A 명령어
            framelist[framenum] = i
            if framenum < 1000:
                framenum += 1
            else:
                framenum = 0

        # 수신 큐 확인
        try:
            while True:
                response_data = MT.recv_queue.get_nowait()
                frameind = int(response_data[0])
                MP.insert_data(framelist[frameind].addr, response_data[1])
        except MT.queue.Empty:
            pass

        time.sleep(1)

try:
    comm_thread = threading.Thread(target=MT.background_serial_worker, daemon=True)
    comm_thread.start()

    queue_thread = threading.Thread(target=Dynamic_EnQueue_Loop, daemon=True)
    queue_thread.start()

    ET.Ethernet_Run(AdrMap=MP.LabelMap)

    while True:
        time.sleep(1)

except KeyboardInterrupt:
    print("\n[Main] 종료 요청 감지")
    
finally:
    stop_communication = True
    comm_thread.join(timeout=2)
    if MT.ser.is_open:
        MT.ser.close()
    print("프로그램 완전히 종료됨")

■ File : Mapping.py
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\fac_server\DataLayer\Mapping_Layer\Mapping.py
======================================================================

from typing import Dict
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class MapInfo():
    Adr: str
    degree: int

AdrMap: Dict[str, int] = {}
Adr_LabelMap: Dict[str, MapInfo] = {}
LabelMap: Dict[str, float] = {}

#받아온 응답에서 데이터 주소에 대입
def insert_data(adrs, response: bytes):
    if not response or not adrs:
        return

    if response[0] != 6:
        print("잘못된 제어문자 시작:", response[:1])
        print(response)
        return

    chan_str = response[1:3].decode()
    dresponse = response.decode()
    idx = 6 
    
    rlen = int(dresponse[idx:idx+2], 16)
    idx += 2

    chan_prefix = f"{int(chan_str):02d}"
    limit = min(rlen, len(adrs))
    
    for i in range(limit):
        if len(dresponse) - idx <= 2:
            break
            
        slen = int(dresponse[idx:idx+2], 16) * 2
        idx += 2

        if len(dresponse) - idx < slen:
            break
        
        tadr = f"{chan_prefix}{adrs[i]}"
        hex_segment = dresponse[idx:idx+slen]
        
        val = int.from_bytes(bytes.fromhex(hex_segment), byteorder='big', signed=True)
        
        AdrMap[tadr] = val
        if (label := Adr_LabelMap.get(tadr)) is not None:
            calculated_val = val * (10 ** label.degree)
            
            if label.degree < 0:
                LabelMap[label.Adr] = round(calculated_val, abs(label.degree))
            else:
                LabelMap[label.Adr] = calculated_val
            
        idx += slen  # 포인터 이동

def Mapping(adr: str, label: str, degree:int=0):
    Adr_LabelMap[adr] = MapInfo(label, degree)

// 경로: lib/auth_service.dart
// 명세: 로그인 상태(유저 ID) 보관 및 로컬 저장소 연동
//
// config.dart는 개발자마다 서버 주소가 달라 .gitignore에 등록되어 있다.
// AuthService는 모두가 공유하는 로직이라 이 파일로 분리해 버전 관리에 포함시킨다.
part of 'main.dart';

class AuthService {
  static int? _currentUserId;

  static bool get isLoggedIn => _currentUserId != null;
  static int? getCurrentUserId() => _currentUserId;

  static Future<void> init() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    _currentUserId = prefs.getInt('userId');
  }

  /// [persist]가 false면 앱을 껐다 켰을 때 로그인이 풀리도록 메모리에만 보관한다.
  /// (로그인 화면의 '로그인 상태 유지' 체크박스와 연결된다)
  static Future<void> setUserId(int? id, {bool persist = true}) async {
    _currentUserId = id;
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    if (id != null && persist) {
      await prefs.setInt('userId', id);
    } else {
      await prefs.remove('userId');
    }
  }
}

/// Box #1: 일반 로그인 박스 (이메일/비밀번호 입력 + 제출)
class StandardLoginBox extends StatefulWidget {
  final TextEditingController emailController;
  final TextEditingController passwordController;
  final Future<void> Function() onLoginPressed;

  const StandardLoginBox({
    super.key,
    required this.emailController,
    required this.passwordController,
    required this.onLoginPressed,
  });

  @override
  State<StandardLoginBox> createState() => _StandardLoginBoxState();
}

class _StandardLoginBoxState extends State<StandardLoginBox> {
  bool _obscurePassword = true;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        TextField(
          controller: widget.emailController,
          keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            labelText: '이메일',
            hintText: 'name@example.com',
            prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.mail_outline_rounded),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 14),
        TextField(
          controller: widget.passwordController,
          obscureText: _obscurePassword,
          onSubmitted: (_) => widget.onLoginPressed(),
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            labelText: '비밀번호',
            prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline_rounded),
            suffixIcon: IconButton(
              tooltip: '비밀번호 표시',
              icon: Icon(
                _obscurePassword ? Icons.visibility_outlined : Icons.visibility_off_outlined,
                color: context.colors.textSecondary,
              ),
              onPressed: () => setState(() => _obscurePassword = !_obscurePassword),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        AsyncActionButton(label: '로그인', onPressed: widget.onLoginPressed),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _ProviderBadge extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final Color background;
  final IconData? icon;

  const _ProviderBadge({required this.label, required this.background, this.icon});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: 22,
      height: 22,
      alignment: Alignment.center,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: background, shape: BoxShape.circle),
      child: icon != null
          ? Icon(icon, size: 14, color: context.colors.onPrimary)
          : Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.onPrimary, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
            ),
    );
  }
}

/// Box #3: 로그인 상태 유지 체크박스
class KeepLoginStateBox extends StatelessWidget {

  const KeepLoginStateBox({
    super.key,
    required this.isChecked,
    required this.onChanged,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: () => onChanged(!isChecked),
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
      child: Row(

        children: [
          Checkbox(value: isChecked, onChanged: onChanged),
          const SizedBox(width: 4),
          Text('로그인 상태 유지하기', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import '../../../main.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// Box #3, #4: 고정 ID 권한 주입 박스

// 경로 : lib/components/common/opening/open_inj_static.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart'; // GoRouter 추가
import '../../../main.dart';

/// Box #3, #4: 고정 ID 권한 주입 박스
class AuthInjectionBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final int userIdToInject;

  const AuthInjectionBox({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.userIdToInject,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.grey),
      ),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12.0),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () {
              // 1. 전역 로그인 상태(AuthService) 업데이트
              AuthService.setUserId(userIdToInject);

              // 2. GoRouter를 활용하여 메인 색인 페이지로 이동 (id 파라미터 전달)
              context.go('/index/$userIdToInject/home');
            },
            child: const Text('버튼(이동)'),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(title),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import '../../../main.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// Box #5: 텍스트 박스 입력형 권한 주입 박스
// 경로 : lib/components/common/opening/open_inj_text.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart'; // GoRouter 추가
import '../../../main.dart';

/// Box #5: 텍스트 박스 입력형 권한 주입 박스
class CustomAuthInjectionBox extends StatefulWidget {
  const CustomAuthInjectionBox({super.key});

  @override
  State<CustomAuthInjectionBox> createState() => CustomAuthInjectionBoxState();
}

class CustomAuthInjectionBoxState extends State<CustomAuthInjectionBox> {
  final TextEditingController _idController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _idController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.grey),
      ),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12.0),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () {
              final int? id = int.tryParse(_idController.text.trim());

              if (id != null) {
                // 1. 전역 로그인 상태(AuthService) 업데이트
                AuthService.setUserId(id);

                // 2. 입력받은 id를 파라미터로 하여 메인 색인 페이지로 이동
                context.go('/index/$id/home');
              } else {
                // 숫자가 아니거나 비어있을 경우 경고창
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  const SnackBar(content: Text('유효한 유저 ID(숫자)를 입력해주세요.')),
                );
              }
            },
            child: const Text('버튼(이동)'),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: TextField(
              controller: _idController,
              keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                hintText: '권한 주입 계정(user_입력)',
                border: UnderlineInputBorder(),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.onPressed,
    this.icon = Icons.chevron_right_rounded,
  });

  @override

    return Material(
      color: context.colors.surface,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),

          ),

            children: [

                child: Text(
                  title,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 14,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    color: context.colors.textPrimary,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              Icon(icon, size: 20, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

// 경로: lib/components/signup/signup_components.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// Box #1: 일반 계정 생성 박스
class StandardSignupBox extends StatefulWidget {
  final TextEditingController emailController;
  final TextEditingController passwordController;
  final TextEditingController nameController;
  final TextEditingController phoneController;
  final VoidCallback onSignupPressed;

  const StandardSignupBox({
    super.key,
    required this.emailController,
    required this.passwordController,
    required this.nameController,
    required this.phoneController,
    required this.onSignupPressed,
  });

  @override
  State<StandardSignupBox> createState() => _StandardSignupBoxState();
}

class _StandardSignupBoxState extends State<StandardSignupBox> {
  bool _obscurePassword = true;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        TextField(
          controller: widget.emailController,
          keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            labelText: '이메일',
            prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.mail_outline_rounded),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 14),
        TextField(
          controller: widget.passwordController,
          obscureText: _obscurePassword,
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            labelText: '비밀번호',
            prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline_rounded),
            suffixIcon: IconButton(
              tooltip: '비밀번호 표시',
              icon: Icon(
                _obscurePassword ? Icons.visibility_outlined : Icons.visibility_off_outlined,
                color: context.colors.textSecondary,
              ),
              onPressed: () => setState(() => _obscurePassword = !_obscurePassword),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 14),
        TextField(
          controller: widget.nameController,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            labelText: '이름',
            prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.badge_outlined),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 14),
        TextField(
          controller: widget.phoneController,
          keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            labelText: '전화번호 (선택)',
            prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.phone_outlined),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        ElevatedButton(
          onPressed: widget.onSignupPressed,
          child: const Text('계정 생성'),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// Box #2: 외부 프로바이더 사용 계정 생성 박스
class SocialSignupBox extends StatelessWidget {

  const SocialSignupBox({super.key, required this.onSocialSignupPressed});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        Row(
          children: [
            const Expanded(child: Divider()),

          ],
        ),

        OutlinedButton.icon(
          onPressed: () => onSocialSignupPressed('google'),
          icon: const _ProviderBadge(
            label: 'G',
            background: Color(0xFF4285F4),
          ),

          style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
            foregroundColor: context.colors.textPrimary,
            side: BorderSide(color: context.colors.outline),
            alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        OutlinedButton.icon(
          onPressed: () => onSocialSignupPressed('apple'),
          icon: const _ProviderBadge(
            label: '',
            background: Colors.black,
            icon: Icons.apple,
          ),
          label: const Text('Apple로 계속하기'),
          style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
            foregroundColor: context.colors.textPrimary,
            side: BorderSide(color: context.colors.outline),
            alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _ProviderBadge extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final Color background;
  final IconData? icon;

  const _ProviderBadge({required this.label, required this.background, this.icon});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: 22,
      height: 22,
      alignment: Alignment.center,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: background, shape: BoxShape.circle),
      child: icon != null
          ? Icon(icon, size: 14, color: context.colors.onPrimary)
          : Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.onPrimary, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
            ),
    );
  }
}

// ===============================================================
// 경로: lib/components/cross_compontents/board/post_detail_components.dart
// 명세: 게시글 상세 페이지를 위한 UI 박스 컴포넌트
// ===============================================================
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_quill/flutter_quill.dart' as quill;
import 'package:flutter_quill_extensions/flutter_quill_extensions.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// Box #1: 게시글 본문 렌더링 박스 (읽기 전용 에디터 포함)
///
/// FocusNode/ScrollController는 build()가 아니라 State가 들고 있어야 한다.
/// build()에서 만들면 다시 그릴 때마다 새로 생성되어 스크롤 위치가 초기화된다.
class PostContentBox extends StatefulWidget {
  final Map<String, dynamic> post;
  final quill.QuillController quillController;

  const PostContentBox({
    super.key,
    required this.post,
    required this.quillController,
  });

  @override
  State<PostContentBox> createState() => _PostContentBoxState();
}

class _PostContentBoxState extends State<PostContentBox> {
  final FocusNode _focusNode = FocusNode();
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _focusNode.dispose();
    _scrollController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final post = widget.post;
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20.0),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        boxShadow: context.colors.softShadow,
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            '[${post['facility_name']}]',
            style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.primary, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 12),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            post['title'] ?? '제목 없음',
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: context.colors.textPrimary),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            '작성자: ${post['creator_name']} · 작성일: ${post['created_at']}',
            style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12),
          ),
          Divider(height: 28, color: context.colors.outline),
          // 읽기 전용 Quill Editor 영역
          quill.QuillEditor(
            controller: widget.quillController,
            focusNode: _focusNode,
            scrollController: _scrollController,
            config: quill.QuillEditorConfig(
              showCursor: false,
              autoFocus: false,
              // 본문에 들어간 이미지를 그리려면 extensions의 빌더가 필요하다.
              embedBuilders: FlutterQuillEmbeds.editorBuilders(),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Box #2: 게시글 제어 박스 (수정/삭제 버튼)
class PostControlBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final VoidCallback onEdit;
  final VoidCallback onDelete;

  const PostControlBox({
    super.key,
    required this.onEdit,
    required this.onDelete,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,

        OutlinedButton.icon(
          onPressed: onEdit,
          icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_outlined, size: 16),

          style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(minimumSize: const Size(0, 40)),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        OutlinedButton.icon(
          onPressed: onDelete,
          style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
            minimumSize: const Size(0, 40),
            foregroundColor: context.colors.error,
            side: BorderSide(color: context.colors.error.withValues(alpha: 0.4)),
          ),
          icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline_rounded, size: 16),
          label: const Text('삭제'),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// Box #3: 댓글 박스 (리스트 및 입력창)
class CommentBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<dynamic> comments;
  final String currentUserId;
  final bool isAdmin;
  final TextEditingController commentController;
  final VoidCallback onSubmit;
  final Function(int) onDelete;

  const CommentBox({
    super.key,
    required this.comments,
    required this.currentUserId,
    required this.isAdmin,
    required this.commentController,
    required this.onSubmit,
    required this.onDelete,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Text('댓글 ${comments.length}', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: context.colors.textPrimary)),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),

          // 댓글 리스트
          ListView.separated(
            shrinkWrap: true,
            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            itemCount: comments.length,
            separatorBuilder: (_, __) => Divider(height: 1, color: context.colors.outline),
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final comment = comments[index];
              final bool isCreator = comment['creator_id'].toString() == currentUserId;

              return ListTile(
                dense: true,
                contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                title: Text(comment['content'] ?? '', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, color: context.colors.textPrimary)),
                subtitle: Text(
                  '${comment['creator_name']} · ${comment['created_at']}',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                ),
                trailing: (isCreator || isAdmin)
                    ? IconButton(
                      tooltip: '댓글 삭제',
                  icon: Icon(Icons.close_rounded, size: 16, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                  onPressed: () => onDelete(comment['comment_id']),
                )
                    : null,
              );
            },
          ),

          if (comments.isEmpty)
            Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16.0),
              child: Text('등록된 댓글이 없습니다.', style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13)),
            ),

          const SizedBox(height: 12),

          // 댓글 입력부
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: TextField(
                  controller: commentController,
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(
                    hintText: '댓글을 입력하세요...',
                    isDense: true,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              SizedBox(
                height: 48,
                child: ElevatedButton(
                  onPressed: onSubmit,
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(minimumSize: const Size(64, 48)),
                  child: const Text('등록'),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Box #2: 에디터 툴바 및 작성 영역 박스
///
/// FocusNode/ScrollController를 build() 안에서 만들면 화면이 다시 그려질 때마다
/// 새로 생성되어 입력 도중 포커스와 스크롤 위치가 날아간다. State가 들고 있어야 한다.
class QuillEditorBox extends StatefulWidget {
  final quill.QuillController quillController;

  const QuillEditorBox({super.key, required this.quillController});

  @override
  State<QuillEditorBox> createState() => _QuillEditorBoxState();
}

class _QuillEditorBoxState extends State<QuillEditorBox> {
  final FocusNode _focusNode = FocusNode();
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _focusNode.dispose();
    _scrollController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
      ),
      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          // 툴바 영역
          Container(
            color: context.colors.background,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4.0),
            child: quill.QuillSimpleToolbar(
              controller: widget.quillController,
              config: const quill.QuillSimpleToolbarConfig(),
            ),
          ),
          Divider(height: 1, color: context.colors.outline),
          // 에디터 영역 (고정 높이 부여 후 자체 스크롤 비활성화 위임)
          Container(
            height: 400,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12.0),
            color: context.colors.surface,
            child: quill.QuillEditor(
              controller: widget.quillController,
              focusNode: _focusNode,
              scrollController: _scrollController,
              config: quill.QuillEditorConfig(
                // 이미지 블록은 본체가 그리지 못해 extensions의 빌더를 넘겨야 한다.
                embedBuilders: FlutterQuillEmbeds.editorBuilders(),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Box #3: 제출(완료) 버튼 박스
class SubmitBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final String buttonLabel;
  final Future<void> Function() onSubmit;

  const SubmitBox({
    super.key,
    required this.buttonLabel,
    required this.onSubmit,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AsyncActionButton(label: buttonLabel, onPressed: onSubmit);
  }
}

// 명세: 통합/시설 게시판 공통 UI 컴포넌트 (검색, 필터, 리스트, 페이징)
// ===============================================================
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:idcs/models/post_model.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// Box #1: 보드 헤더 박스 (검색 및 필터)
class BoardHeaderBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final TextEditingController searchController;
  final String selectedSearchType;
  final ValueChanged<String?> onSearchTypeChanged;
  final VoidCallback onSearch;
  final VoidCallback? onCreatePost;

  const BoardHeaderBox({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.searchController,
    required this.selectedSearchType,
    required this.onSearchTypeChanged,
    required this.onSearch,
    this.onCreatePost,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(title, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: context.colors.textPrimary)),
              if (onCreatePost != null)
                ElevatedButton.icon(
                  onPressed: onCreatePost,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_outlined, size: 16),
                  label: const Text('새 글 작성'),
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(minimumSize: const Size(0, 40)),
                ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                height: 48,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                ),
                child: DropdownButtonHideUnderline(
                  child: DropdownButton<String>(
                    value: selectedSearchType,
                    icon: Icon(Icons.expand_more_rounded, size: 20, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                    isDense: true,
                    items: const [
                      DropdownMenuItem(value: 'all', child: Text('전체')),
                      DropdownMenuItem(value: 'title', child: Text('제목')),
                      DropdownMenuItem(value: 'creator', child: Text('작성자')),
                      DropdownMenuItem(value: 'content', child: Text('내용')),
                    ],
                    onChanged: onSearchTypeChanged,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Expanded(
                child: TextField(
                  controller: searchController,
                  onSubmitted: (_) => onSearch(),
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(
                    hintText: '검색어를 입력하세요',
                    isDense: true,
                    prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search_rounded, size: 20),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              SizedBox(
                height: 48,
                child: ElevatedButton(
                  onPressed: onSearch,
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(minimumSize: const Size(64, 48)),
                  child: const Text('검색'),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Box #2 & #3: 보드 리스트 및 페이징 박스
class BoardListAndPagingBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<PostModel> posts;
  final bool isLoading;
  final int currentPage;
  final int totalPages;
  final Function(int) onPageChanged;
  final Function(int) onPostTapped;

  const BoardListAndPagingBox({
    super.key,
    required this.posts,
    required this.isLoading,
    required this.currentPage,
    required this.totalPages,
    required this.onPageChanged,
    required this.onPostTapped,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        boxShadow: context.colors.softShadow,
      ),
      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          // Box #2: 리스트 영역
          SizedBox(
            height: 400,
            child: isLoading
                ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
                : posts.isEmpty
                ? Center(
                    child: Text('등록된 포스트가 없습니다.', style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.textSecondary)),
                  )
                : ListView.separated(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
              itemCount: posts.length,
              separatorBuilder: (context, index) => Divider(height: 1, color: context.colors.outline),
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                final post = posts[index];
                return ListTile(
                  title: Text(
                    post.title,
                    maxLines: 1,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: context.colors.textPrimary),
                  ),
                  subtitle: Text(
                    '[${post.facilityName}] 작성자: ${post.creatorName}',
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                  ),
                  trailing: Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded, size: 20, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                  onTap: () => onPostTapped(post.postId),
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
          Divider(height: 1, color: context.colors.outline),

          // Box #3: 페이징 영역
          _buildPagingControl(context),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPagingControl(BuildContext context) {
    final int blockSize = 5;
    final int currentBlock = ((currentPage - 1) ~/ blockSize);
    final int startPage = currentBlock * blockSize + 1;
    final int endPage = (startPage + blockSize - 1 > totalPages) ? totalPages : startPage + blockSize - 1;
    final safeTotalPages = totalPages > 0 ? totalPages : 1;

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8.0),
      child: SingleChildScrollView(
        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
        child: Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            IconButton(
              tooltip: '첫 페이지',
              constraints: const BoxConstraints(),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
              icon: Icon(Icons.keyboard_double_arrow_left_rounded, size: 18, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
              onPressed: currentPage > 1 ? () => onPageChanged(1) : null,
            ),
            IconButton(
              tooltip: '이전 페이지',
              constraints: const BoxConstraints(),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
              icon: Icon(Icons.chevron_left_rounded, size: 18, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
              onPressed: startPage > 1 ? () => onPageChanged(startPage - 1) : null,
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 4),

            ...List.generate(endPage - startPage + 1, (index) {
              final pageNum = startPage + index;
              final bool isActive = currentPage == pageNum;
              return InkWell(
                onTap: () => onPageChanged(pageNum),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                child: Container(
                  margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 2),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: isActive ? context.colors.primary : Colors.transparent,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    '$pageNum',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: isActive ? context.colors.onPrimary : context.colors.textPrimary,
                      fontWeight: isActive ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w500,
                      fontSize: 13,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              );
            }),

            const SizedBox(width: 4),
            IconButton(
              tooltip: '다음 페이지',
              constraints: const BoxConstraints(),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
              icon: Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded, size: 18, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
              onPressed: endPage < safeTotalPages ? () => onPageChanged(endPage + 1) : null,
            ),
            IconButton(
              tooltip: '마지막 페이지',
              constraints: const BoxConstraints(),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
              icon: Icon(Icons.keyboard_double_arrow_right_rounded, size: 18, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
              onPressed: currentPage < safeTotalPages ? () => onPageChanged(safeTotalPages) : null,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// Box #2: 채팅방 목록 박스 (유지)
class ChatRoomListBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<dynamic> rooms;
  final Function(int) onRoomTapped;
  final Function(int) onRoomLongPressed;

  const ChatRoomListBox({super.key, required this.rooms, required this.onRoomTapped, required this.onRoomLongPressed});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (rooms.isEmpty) {
      return Container(
        width: double.infinity,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 32),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: context.colors.surface,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
        ),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.chat_bubble_outline_rounded, color: context.colors.textSecondary.withValues(alpha: 0.6), size: 28),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text('참여 중인 채팅방이 없습니다.', style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13)),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
      ),
      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
      child: ListView.separated(
        shrinkWrap: true,
        physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
        itemCount: rooms.length,
        separatorBuilder: (_, __) => Divider(height: 1, color: context.colors.outline),
        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
          final room = rooms[index];
          final isPinned = room['sort_priority'] < 4;
          final String roomName = room['room_name'] ?? '';
          final bool isGroup = room['type'] == 'GROUP';
          return ListTile(
            tileColor: isPinned ? context.colors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.05) : null,
            leading: Stack(
              clipBehavior: Clip.none,
              children: [
                CircleAvatar(
                  backgroundColor: room['type'] == 'FACILITY' ? context.colors.primary : context.colors.primaryDark,
                  child: isGroup
                      ? Icon(Icons.group_rounded, color: context.colors.onPrimary, size: 20)
                      : Text(
                          roomName.isNotEmpty ? roomName.substring(0, 1) : '?',
                          style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.onPrimary, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 15),
                        ),
                ),
                if (isPinned)
                  Positioned(
                    right: -2, bottom: -2,
                    child: Icon(Icons.push_pin_rounded, size: 14, color: context.colors.error),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
            title: Text(roomName, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 14, color: context.colors.textPrimary)),
            subtitle: Text(
              room['last_message'],
              maxLines: 1,
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
            ),
            trailing: room['unread_count'] > 0
                ? CircleAvatar(
                    radius: 13,
                    backgroundColor: context.colors.error,
                    child: Text('${room['unread_count']}', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
                  )
                : null,
            onTap: () => onRoomTapped(room['room_id']),
            onLongPress: () => onRoomLongPressed(room['room_id']),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Box #2: 메시지 리스트 박스 (답장 UI 및 고정 아이콘 추가)
class ChatMessageListBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<dynamic> messages;
  final String currentUserId;
  final Function(Map<String, dynamic>, String action) onAction; // 메뉴 이벤트 상위 전달

  const ChatMessageListBox({super.key, required this.messages, required this.currentUserId, required this.onAction});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (messages.isEmpty) {
      return Center(
        child: Text('메시지가 없습니다.', style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13)),
      );
    }

    return Container(
      color: context.colors.background,
      child: ListView.builder(
        reverse: true,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
        itemCount: messages.length,
        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
          final msg = messages[index];
          final isMine = msg['user_id'].toString() == currentUserId;
          final isPinned = msg['is_pinned'] == true;

          return Align(
            alignment: isMine ? Alignment.centerRight : Alignment.centerLeft,
            child: GestureDetector(
              onLongPressStart: (details) {
                _showMessageMenu(context, details.globalPosition, msg);
              },
              child: Container(
                margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4, horizontal: 12),
                constraints: BoxConstraints(maxWidth: MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * 0.75),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: isMine ? CrossAxisAlignment.end : CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    if (!isMine)
                      Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 2, left: 4),
                        child: Text('User ${msg['user_id']}', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.textSecondary)),
                      ),

                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: isMine ? context.colors.primary : context.colors.surface,
                        border: isMine ? null : Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16).copyWith(
                          bottomRight: isMine ? const Radius.circular(4) : null,
                          bottomLeft: !isMine ? const Radius.circular(4) : null,
                        ),
                      ),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          // 답장 원문 표시
                          if (msg['parent_content'] != null)
                            Container(
                              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 6),
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                border: Border(left: BorderSide(color: isMine ? context.colors.onPrimary.withValues(alpha: 0.7) : context.colors.textSecondary, width: 2)),
                              ),
                              child: Text(
                                msg['parent_content'],
                                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: isMine ? context.colors.onPrimary.withValues(alpha: 0.7) : context.colors.textSecondary),
                                maxLines: 1,
                                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                              ),
                            ),

                          Row(
                            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                            children: [
                              if (isPinned) Icon(Icons.push_pin_rounded, size: 13, color: isMine ? context.colors.onPrimary : context.colors.error),
                              if (isPinned) const SizedBox(width: 4),
                              Flexible(
                                child: Text(
                                  msg['content'],
                                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, color: isMine ? context.colors.onPrimary : context.colors.textPrimary),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 2, left: 4, right: 4),
                      child: Text(msg['created_at'] ?? '', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.textSecondary)),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showMessageMenu(BuildContext context, Offset position, Map<String, dynamic> msg) {
    showMenu(
      context: context,
      position: RelativeRect.fromLTRB(position.dx, position.dy, position.dx, position.dy),
      items: [
        const PopupMenuItem(value: 'reply', child: Row(children: [Icon(Icons.reply_rounded, size: 18), SizedBox(width: 8), Text('답장')])),
        const PopupMenuItem(value: 'pin', child: Row(children: [Icon(Icons.push_pin_outlined, size: 18), SizedBox(width: 8), Text('고정 토글')])),
        const PopupMenuItem(value: 'edit', enabled: false, child: Row(children: [Icon(Icons.edit_outlined, size: 18, color: Colors.grey), SizedBox(width: 8), Text('수정', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey))])),
        const PopupMenuItem(value: 'delete', enabled: false, child: Row(children: [Icon(Icons.delete_outline_rounded, size: 18, color: Colors.grey), SizedBox(width: 8), Text('삭제', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey))])),
      ],
    ).then((value) {
      if (value != null) onAction(msg, value);
    });
  }
}

/// Box #3: 채팅 입력 박스 (답장 뷰잉 추가)
class ChatInputBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final TextEditingController controller;
  final Map<String, dynamic>? replyingTo;
  final VoidCallback onSend;
  final VoidCallback onCancelReply;

  const ChatInputBox({super.key, required this.controller, required this.replyingTo, required this.onSend, required this.onCancelReply});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colors.surface,
        border: Border(top: BorderSide(color: context.colors.outline)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          if (replyingTo != null)
            Container(
              color: context.colors.background,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.reply_rounded, size: 16, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      '답장: ${replyingTo!['content']}',
                      maxLines: 1,
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                    ),
                  ),
                  IconButton(
                    tooltip: '닫기',
                    icon: Icon(Icons.close_rounded, size: 16, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                    onPressed: onCancelReply,
                    constraints: const BoxConstraints(),
                    padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8.0, vertical: 8.0),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                IconButton(
                  tooltip: '첨부',
                  icon: Icon(Icons.add_circle_outline_rounded, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                  onPressed: () {},
                ),
                Expanded(
                  child: TextField(
                    controller: controller,
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(
                      hintText: '메시지를 입력하세요...',
                      border: InputBorder.none,
                      filled: false,
                      isDense: true,
                    ),
                    onSubmitted: (_) => onSend(),
                  ),
                ),
                IconButton(
                  tooltip: '보내기',
                  icon: Icon(Icons.send_rounded, color: context.colors.primary),
                  onPressed: onSend,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ===============================================================
// 경로: lib/components/facility/chart/chart_components.dart
// 명세: 차트 시각화를 위한 화면 분할 Box 컴포넌트
