import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:fl_chart/fl_chart.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// Box #1: 차트 필터 박스 (해상도 및 조회 기간 설정)
class ChartFilterBox extends StatelessWidget {
  // [수정: 데이터 타입 종속성 해제]
  // chart_group_table의 구조와 그룹핑이 단일 데이터 타입(temp, gear 등)을 가리지 않고
  // 복합적인 데이터 포인트 롤을 한 번에 렌더링할 수 있도록 변경되었습니다.
  // 이에 따라 기존에 존재하던 currentDataType 및 onDataTypeChanged 파라미터를 완전히 제거했습니다.
  final String currentScale;
  final String currentTimeRange;
  final Function(String) onScaleChanged;
  final Function(String) onTimeRangeChanged;
  final VoidCallback onApply;

  /// 조회 대상 호실. 예전에는 1번 호실로 고정되어 있어 다른 호실에서는 차트가 아예 뜨지 않았다.
  final List<dynamic> rooms;
  final int? selectedRoomId;
  final Function(int) onRoomChanged;

  const ChartFilterBox({
    super.key,
    required this.currentScale,
    required this.currentTimeRange,
    required this.onScaleChanged,
    required this.onTimeRangeChanged,
    required this.onApply,
    required this.rooms,
    required this.selectedRoomId,
    required this.onRoomChanged,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return _SectionCard(
      title: '조회 조건',
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          DropdownButtonFormField<int>(
            value: selectedRoomId,
            isExpanded: true,
            decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: '호실'),
            items: rooms
                .map((room) => DropdownMenuItem<int>(
                      value: room['id'] is int ? room['id'] : int.tryParse(room['id'].toString()),
                      child: Text(room['name']?.toString() ?? '이름 없는 호실', overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                    ))
                .toList(),
            onChanged: (val) { if (val != null) onRoomChanged(val); },
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  value: currentScale,
                  isExpanded: true,
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: '해상도'),
                  items: const [
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: '10s', child: Text('10초 단위', overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)),
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: '1m', child: Text('1분 단위', overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)),
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: '5m', child: Text('5분 단위', overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)),
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: '1h', child: Text('1시간 단위', overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)),
                  ],
                  onChanged: (val) { if (val != null) onScaleChanged(val); },
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Expanded(
                child: DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  value: currentTimeRange,
                  isExpanded: true,
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: '조회 기간'),
                  items: const [
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: '1h', child: Text('최근 1시간', overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)),
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: '12h', child: Text('최근 12시간', overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)),
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: '1d', child: Text('최근 1일', overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)),
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: '7d', child: Text('최근 7일', overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)),
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: '15d', child: Text('최근 15일', overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)),
                  ],
                  onChanged: (val) { if (val != null) onTimeRangeChanged(val); },
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              SizedBox(
                height: 58,
                child: ElevatedButton(
                  onPressed: onApply,
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 18)),
                  child: const Text('적용'),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Box #2: 데이터 포인트(시리즈) 선택 박스
class ChartSeriesBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<Map<String, dynamic>> availableGroups; // 이제 개별 role이 아닌 Group 리스트를 받습니다.
  final int? selectedGroupId; // 라디오 버튼처럼 단일 선택으로 변경 (차트 하나당 한 그룹)
  final Function(int) onGroupSelected;

  /// 그룹과 상관없이 호실의 항목을 직접 고르는 모드.
  final bool directMode;
  final VoidCallback onDirectModeSelected;

  /// 호실에 등록된 전체 포인트 롤. 그룹 API는 이름을 주지 않아 따로 받아 맞춘다.
  final List<dynamic> roles;

  /// 지금 차트에 그릴 롤. 그룹을 고르면 그룹의 롤로 채워지고, 여기서 개별로 끄고 켤 수 있다.
  final Set<int> visibleRoleIds;
  final Function(int, bool) onRoleToggled;

  const ChartSeriesBox({
    super.key,
    required this.availableGroups,
    required this.selectedGroupId,
    required this.onGroupSelected,
    required this.directMode,
    required this.onDirectModeSelected,
    required this.roles,
    required this.visibleRoleIds,
    required this.onRoleToggled,
  });

  int? _roleId(dynamic role) =>
      role['role_id'] is int ? role['role_id'] : int.tryParse(role['role_id'].toString());

  /// 체크박스로 보여줄 항목. 그룹 모드면 그룹에 묶인 것만, 직접 선택이면 전체.
  List<dynamic> get _shownRoles {
    if (directMode) return roles;

    final group = availableGroups.where((g) => g['group_id'] == selectedGroupId).firstOrNull;
    if (group == null) return const [];

    final groupRoleIds = (group['role_ids'] as List?)?.whereType<int>().toSet() ?? <int>{};
    return roles.where((r) => groupRoleIds.contains(_roleId(r))).toList();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final shown = _shownRoles;

    return _SectionCard(
      title: '데이터 그룹',
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          if (availableGroups.isEmpty && !directMode)
            Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
              child: Text('등록된 그룹이 없습니다.', style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13)),
            ),
          Wrap(
            spacing: 8.0,
            runSpacing: 8.0,
            children: [
              ...availableGroups.map((group) {
                final isSelected = !directMode && selectedGroupId == group['group_id'];
                return _chip(context,
                  label: group['group_name'],
                  selected: isSelected,
                  onSelected: () => onGroupSelected(group['group_id']),
                );
              }),
              _chip(context,
                label: '직접 선택',
                selected: directMode,
                onSelected: onDirectModeSelected,
              ),
            ],
          ),
          if (shown.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Divider(height: 1, color: context.colors.outline),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            Text(
              directMode ? '표시할 항목을 고르세요' : '이 그룹에 묶인 항목',
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
            ),
            // 선이 6개를 넘어가면 서로 가려서 추세를 읽기 어려워진다.
            if (visibleRoleIds.length > _readableSeriesLimit) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              Row(
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.info_outline_rounded, size: 14, color: context.colors.warning),
                  const SizedBox(width: 5),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      '${visibleRoleIds.length}개를 한 번에 그리면 알아보기 어렵습니다. '
                      '$_readableSeriesLimit개 이하를 권합니다.',
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.warning),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            ConstrainedBox(
              constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxHeight: 168),
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                child: Wrap(
                  spacing: 6,
                  runSpacing: 6,
                  children: shown.map((role) {
                    final id = _roleId(role);
                    final checked = id != null && visibleRoleIds.contains(id);
                    return FilterChip(
                      label: Text(role['role_name']?.toString() ?? '이름 없음'),
                      selected: checked,
                      onSelected: id == null ? null : (val) => onRoleToggled(id, val),
                      labelStyle: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        color: checked ? context.colors.primary : context.colors.textSecondary,
                      ),
                      showCheckmark: true,
                      checkmarkColor: context.colors.primary,
                      selectedColor: context.colors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                      backgroundColor: context.colors.background,
                      side: BorderSide(color: checked ? context.colors.primary : context.colors.outline),
                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9)),
                    );
                  }).toList(),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _chip(BuildContext context, {required String label, required bool selected, required VoidCallback onSelected}) {
    return ChoiceChip(
      label: Text(label),
      selected: selected,
      onSelected: (val) { if (val) onSelected(); },
      labelStyle: TextStyle(
        color: selected ? context.colors.onPrimary : context.colors.textPrimary,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
        fontSize: 13,
      ),
      selectedColor: context.colors.primary,
      backgroundColor: context.colors.background,
      side: BorderSide(color: selected ? context.colors.primary : context.colors.outline),
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
    );
  }
}

/// 한 차트에 이 개수를 넘게 그리면 선이 서로 가려 추세를 읽기 어렵다.
const int _readableSeriesLimit = 6;

/// 차트 위에 붙는 범례. 어떤 선이 어떤 항목인지 차트 안에서 바로 알 수 있게 한다.
/// 색 표시만으로는 부족하므로 선 모양(실선/파선/점선)도 함께 그린다.
class _ChartLegend extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<String> names;
  final List<List<int>?> dashes;
  final List<Color> colors;

  const _ChartLegend({required this.names, required this.dashes, required this.colors});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (names.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 4, right: 4, bottom: 4),
      child: Wrap(
        spacing: 12,
        runSpacing: 4,
        children: List.generate(names.length, (i) {
          final color = colors[i % colors.length];
          return Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              CustomPaint(
                size: const Size(18, 2),
                painter: _LegendLinePainter(color: color, dash: dashes[i]),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 5),
              Text(
                names[i],
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.textSecondary, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
              ),
            ],
          );
        }),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 범례의 선 미리보기. 차트에 그려지는 것과 같은 파선 패턴을 재현한다.
class _LegendLinePainter extends CustomPainter {
  final Color color;
  final List<int>? dash;

  _LegendLinePainter({required this.color, required this.dash});

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final paint = Paint()
      ..color = color
      ..strokeWidth = 2
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round;

    final y = size.height / 2;
    if (dash == null) {
      canvas.drawLine(Offset(0, y), Offset(size.width, y), paint);
      return;
    }

    double x = 0;
    int i = 0;
    while (x < size.width) {
      final len = dash![i % dash!.length].toDouble();
      if (i.isEven) {
        canvas.drawLine(Offset(x, y), Offset((x + len).clamp(0, size.width), y), paint);
      }
      x += len;
      i++;
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_LegendLinePainter old) => old.color != color || old.dash != dash;
}

/// 시리즈별 선 모양. 색을 못 쓰는 상황(색각 이상, 흑백 인쇄)에서도 구분되게 한다.
/// null이면 실선.
const List<List<int>?> _seriesDashPatterns = [
  null,        // 실선
  [6, 3],      // 파선
  [2, 3],      // 점선
  [10, 3, 2, 3], // 일점쇄선
  [8, 4],
  [3, 2, 8, 2],
];

// 시리즈 색상 팔레트는 테마마다 달라야 해서(어두운 배경에서 진한 색이 안 보인다)
// AppPalette.chartSeries로 옮겼다. 통계 박스와 캔버스가 같은 순서로 순회하므로
// 항상 같은 색이 매칭된다.

/// 조회된 구간의 최소/평균/최대값을 시리즈별로 요약해서 보여주는 박스.
/// 그래프를 눈으로 훑지 않아도 한눈에 수치 요약을 볼 수 있게 한다.
class ChartStatsBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final Map<String, dynamic>? chartData;

  const ChartStatsBox({super.key, required this.chartData});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final List<dynamic> datasets = chartData?['data']?['datasets'] ?? [];
    if (datasets.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();

    final List<_SeriesStat> stats = [];
    for (int i = 0; i < datasets.length; i++) {
      final ds = datasets[i];
      final List<dynamic> points = ds['data'] ?? [];
      if (points.isEmpty) continue;

      double sum = 0;
      int count = 0;
      double? minVal;
      double? maxVal;

      for (final p in points) {
        // 온도/기어처럼 집계된 min/max가 있으면 그것을, 없으면(정적/불리언) mean·status만 사용.
        final double? mean = (p['mean'] as num?)?.toDouble();
        final double? pMin = (p['min'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? mean;
        final double? pMax = (p['max'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? mean;
        final double? statusVal = p.containsKey('status') ? (p['status'] == true ? 1.0 : 0.0) : null;

        final double? value = mean ?? statusVal;
        if (value != null) {
          sum += value;
          count++;
        }
        if (pMin != null) minVal = (minVal == null) ? pMin : (pMin < minVal ? pMin : minVal);
        if (pMax != null) maxVal = (maxVal == null) ? pMax : (pMax > maxVal ? pMax : maxVal);
        if (statusVal != null) {
          minVal = (minVal == null) ? statusVal : (statusVal < minVal ? statusVal : minVal);
          maxVal = (maxVal == null) ? statusVal : (statusVal > maxVal ? statusVal : maxVal);
        }
      }

      if (count == 0 && minVal == null) continue;

      stats.add(_SeriesStat(
        name: ds['display_name'] ?? ds['role_name'] ?? '시리즈 ${i + 1}',
        color: context.colors.chartSeries[i % context.colors.chartSeries.length],
        min: minVal,
        avg: count > 0 ? sum / count : null,
        max: maxVal,
      ));
    }

    if (stats.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();

    return _SectionCard(
      title: '구간 요약',
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: stats.map((s) => Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Container(width: 8, height: 8, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: s.color, shape: BoxShape.circle)),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(s.name, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: context.colors.textPrimary), overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
              ),
              _statChip(context, '최소', s.min),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              _statChip(context, '평균', s.avg),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              _statChip(context, '최대', s.max),
            ],
          ),
        )).toList(),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _statChip(BuildContext context, String label, double? value) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: context.colors.background, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
      child: Text(
        '$label ${value != null ? value.toStringAsFixed(1) : '-'}',
        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.textSecondary, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SeriesStat {
  final String name;
  final Color color;
  final double? min;
  final double? avg;
  final double? max;

  _SeriesStat({required this.name, required this.color, this.min, this.avg, this.max});
}

/// Box #3: 차트 렌더링 캔버스 영역
class ChartCanvasBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final Map<String, dynamic>? chartData;
  final bool isLoading;
  final bool showTargetLines;

  const ChartCanvasBox({
    super.key,
    required this.chartData,
    required this.isLoading,
    this.showTargetLines = true,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (isLoading) {
      return _buildContainer(context, child: const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
    }

    if (chartData == null || chartData!['data']['datasets'] == null || (chartData!['data']['datasets'] as List).isEmpty) {
      return _buildContainer(context,
        child: Center(
          child: Text('조회된 데이터가 없습니다.', style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13)),
        ),
      );
    }

    // [수정] 백엔드에서 data_type을 제거했으므로 해당 변수 추출(String dataType = ...) 구문을 삭제했습니다.
    final List<dynamic> datasets = chartData!['data']['datasets'];
    final List<dynamic> alarms = chartData!['data']['alarms'] ?? [];

    List<LineChartBarData> lineBars = [];
    List<HorizontalLine> targetLines = [];
    // 범례와 툴팁에서 쓸 시리즈 이름/선 모양 (lineBars와 같은 순서)
    final List<String> seriesNames = [];
    final List<List<int>?> seriesDashes = [];
    double minX = double.infinity;
    double maxX = -double.infinity;
    double minY = double.infinity;
    double maxY = -double.infinity;

    final List<Color> colors = context.colors.chartSeries;

    for (int i = 0; i < datasets.length; i++) {
      final ds = datasets[i];
      final List<dynamic> dataPoints = ds['data'];
      List<FlSpot> spots = [];
      final Color seriesColor = colors[i % colors.length];

      // target_value, target_low_limit 등은 Null일 수 있으므로 파싱 방어 추가.
      // 데이터 자체 범위와 무관하게 "안전 기준선"을 그리는 데 사용한다.
      final String? rawLow = ds['target_low_limit'];
      final String? rawHigh = ds['target_high_limit'];
      final double? targetLow = rawLow != null ? double.tryParse(rawLow) : null;
      final double? targetHigh = rawHigh != null ? double.tryParse(rawHigh) : null;

      for (var point in dataPoints) {
        final DateTime dt = DateTime.parse(point['timestamp']).toLocal();
        final double x = dt.millisecondsSinceEpoch.toDouble();

        double y = 0.0;

        // [수정] 전역 dataType 대신 개별 포인트 내부의 key(mean vs status) 존재 여부로 파악합니다.
        if (point.containsKey('mean')) {
          y = (point['mean'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0;
        } else if (point.containsKey('status')) {
          y = point['status'] == true ? 1.0 : 0.0;
        }

        spots.add(FlSpot(x, y));

        if (x < minX) minX = x;
        if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
        if (y < minY) minY = y;
        if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
      }

      if (spots.isNotEmpty) {
        // 색만으로 시리즈를 구분하면 색각 이상이나 흑백 출력(포스터·인쇄)에서 구분이 안 된다.
        // 실선/파선/점선을 함께 돌려 색 없이도 구분되게 한다.
        seriesNames.add(ds['display_name'] ?? ds['role_name'] ?? '시리즈 ${i + 1}');
        final dash = _seriesDashPatterns[i % _seriesDashPatterns.length];
        seriesDashes.add(dash);

        lineBars.add(
          LineChartBarData(
            spots: spots,
            isCurved: true,
            color: seriesColor,
            barWidth: 2,
            isStrokeCapRound: true,
            dashArray: dash,
            dotData: const FlDotData(show: false),
            belowBarData: BarAreaData(show: false),
          ),
        );

        if (showTargetLines) {
          if (targetLow != null) {
            targetLines.add(_targetLine(targetLow, seriesColor, '하한 ${targetLow.toStringAsFixed(1)}'));
            if (targetLow < minY) minY = targetLow;
            if (targetLow > maxY) maxY = targetLow;
          }
          if (targetHigh != null) {
            targetLines.add(_targetLine(targetHigh, seriesColor, '상한 ${targetHigh.toStringAsFixed(1)}'));
            if (targetHigh < minY) minY = targetHigh;
            if (targetHigh > maxY) maxY = targetHigh;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // 조회 기간 안에서 발생한 알람을 세로 점선으로 표시해, 수치 변화와 알람 발생 시점을 한눈에 대조할 수 있게 한다.
    List<VerticalLine> alarmLines = [];
    if (minX != double.infinity && maxX != -double.infinity) {
      for (final a in alarms) {
        final DateTime? ts = DateTime.tryParse(a['timestamp']?.toString() ?? '')?.toLocal();
        if (ts == null) continue;
        final double x = ts.millisecondsSinceEpoch.toDouble();
        if (x < minX || x > maxX) continue;
        final String name = a['alarm_name']?.toString() ?? '알람';
        alarmLines.add(
          VerticalLine(
            x: x,
            color: context.colors.error.withValues(alpha: 0.7),
            strokeWidth: 1.5,
            dashArray: [4, 4],
            label: VerticalLineLabel(
              show: true,
              alignment: Alignment.topLeft,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 4, bottom: 2),
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.error, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
              labelResolver: (line) => name,
            ),
          ),
        );
      }
    }

    if (minY == double.infinity || maxY == -double.infinity) {
      minY = 0; maxY = 10;
    } else if (minY == maxY) {
      minY -= 5; maxY += 5;
    } else {
      final padding = (maxY - minY) * 0.1;
      minY -= padding; maxY += padding;
    }

    if (lineBars.isEmpty) {
      return _buildContainer(context,
        child: Center(child: Text('유효한 그래프 데이터가 없습니다.', style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13))),
      );
    }

    return _buildContainer(context,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          _ChartLegend(names: seriesNames, dashes: seriesDashes, colors: colors),
          Expanded(
            child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 16.0, top: 8.0),
        child: LineChart(
          LineChartData(
            minX: minX,
            maxX: maxX,
            minY: minY,
            maxY: maxY,
            lineBarsData: lineBars,
            lineTouchData: LineTouchData(
              touchTooltipData: LineTouchTooltipData(
                getTooltipItems: (touchedSpots) {
                  return touchedSpots.map((spot) {
                    final dt = DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(spot.x.toInt());
                    final timeStr = DateFormat('MM-dd HH:mm').format(dt);
                    // 여러 선이 겹칠 때 어느 시리즈의 값인지 알 수 없어 이름을 함께 보여준다.
                    final name = spot.barIndex < seriesNames.length ? seriesNames[spot.barIndex] : '';
                    return LineTooltipItem(
                      '$name\n$timeStr\n${spot.y.toStringAsFixed(1)}',
                      const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 12),
                    );
                  }).toList();
                },
              ),
            ),
            gridData: FlGridData(
              show: true,
              drawVerticalLine: true,
              getDrawingHorizontalLine: (value) => FlLine(color: context.colors.outline, strokeWidth: 1),
              getDrawingVerticalLine: (value) => FlLine(color: context.colors.outline, strokeWidth: 1),
            ),
            titlesData: FlTitlesData(
              topTitles: const AxisTitles(sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false)),
              rightTitles: const AxisTitles(sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false)),
              bottomTitles: AxisTitles(
                sideTitles: SideTitles(
                  showTitles: true,
                  reservedSize: 30,
                  getTitlesWidget: (value, meta) {
                    if (value == minX || value == maxX) return const SizedBox.shrink();
                    final dt = DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(value.toInt());
                    return Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8.0),
                      child: Text(
                        DateFormat('HH:mm').format(dt),
                        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                      ),
                    );
                  },
                ),
              ),
              leftTitles: AxisTitles(
                sideTitles: SideTitles(
                  showTitles: true,
                  reservedSize: 40,
                  getTitlesWidget: (value, meta) {
                    return Text(
                      value.toStringAsFixed(1),
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                    );
                  },
                ),
              ),
            ),
            borderData: FlBorderData(show: false),
            extraLinesData: ExtraLinesData(horizontalLines: targetLines, verticalLines: alarmLines),
          ),
        ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // 기준선(안전 범위 상/하한)을 시리즈와 같은 색의 점선으로 표시한다.
  HorizontalLine _targetLine(double y, Color color, String label) {
    return HorizontalLine(
      y: y,
      color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.6),
      strokeWidth: 1.5,
      dashArray: [6, 4],
      label: HorizontalLineLabel(
        show: true,
        alignment: Alignment.topRight,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 4, bottom: 2),
        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: color, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
        labelResolver: (line) => label,
      ),
    );
  }

  // 차트 캔버스는 화면의 핵심 콘텐츠이므로 주변 설정 카드보다 한 단계 부각시킨다.
  Widget _buildContainer(BuildContext context, {required Widget child}) {
    return Container(
      height: 400,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        boxShadow: context.colors.softShadow,
      ),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
      child: child,
    );
  }
}

/// Box #4: 차트 도구 박스 (제어 및 어노테이션 도구)
class ChartToolBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final bool showTargetLines;
  final ValueChanged<bool> onToggleTargetLines;

  const ChartToolBox({
    super.key,
    required this.showTargetLines,
    required this.onToggleTargetLines,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return _SectionCard(
      title: '탐색 도구',
      child: Row(
        children: [
          const Spacer(),
          IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.pan_tool_outlined, color: context.colors.textSecondary), onPressed: null, tooltip: '이동 (비활성)'),
          IconButton(
            icon: Icon(
              Icons.show_chart_rounded,
              color: showTargetLines ? context.colors.primary : context.colors.textSecondary,
            ),
            onPressed: () => onToggleTargetLines(!showTargetLines),
            tooltip: showTargetLines ? '기준선 숨기기' : '기준선 표시',
          ),
          IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.text_fields_rounded, color: context.colors.textSecondary), onPressed: null, tooltip: '메모 (비활성)'),
          IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded, color: context.colors.primary), onPressed: () {}, tooltip: '시점 초기화'),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SectionCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final Widget child;
  final Widget? trailing;

  const _SectionCard({required this.title, required this.child, this.trailing});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(title, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: context.colors.textSecondary)),
              if (trailing != null) trailing!,
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          child,
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';

class MonitoringCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final Map<String, String> fields; // 평평한 1층짜리 딕셔너리 (속성명: 속성값)

  // 확인 버튼을 눌렀을 때 입력값을 전달받을 콜백
  final Function(String label, String inputValue)? OnConfirm;

  const MonitoringCard({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.fields,
    this.OnConfirm,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // 제어 가능한(OnConfirm이 있는) 카드는 정적 정보 카드보다 시각적으로 한 단계 부각시킨다.
    final bool isControl = OnConfirm != null;

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16.0),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: isControl ? context.colors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.18) : context.colors.outline),
        boxShadow: isControl ? context.colors.softShadow : null,
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Text(
                title,
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: context.colors.textSecondary, letterSpacing: 0.3),
              ),
              if (isControl) ...[
                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: context.colors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                  ),
                  child: Text('제어', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: context.colors.primary)),
                ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          // 독립된 StatefulWidget으로 개별 Row 생성
          ...fields.entries.map((entry) => _EditableRowItem(
            key: ValueKey(entry.key), // label을 키값으로 설정하여 위젯 재사용성 보장
            label: entry.key,
            value: entry.value,
            onConfirm: OnConfirm,
          )),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// 입력 상태를 유지하기 위해 개별 Row를 StatefulWidget으로 분리
class _EditableRowItem extends StatefulWidget {
  final String label;
  final String value;
  final Function(String label, String inputValue)? onConfirm;

  const _EditableRowItem({
    super.key,
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
    this.onConfirm,
  });

  @override
  State<_EditableRowItem> createState() => _EditableRowItemState();
}

class _EditableRowItemState extends State<_EditableRowItem> {
  // State 내부에서 컨트롤러를 선언하여 부모가 re-build되어도 입력값이 유지됨
  late final TextEditingController _inputController;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _inputController = TextEditingController();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    // 메모리 누수 방지를 위한 자원 해제
    _inputController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8.0),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          // 1. 기존 읽기 전용 필드 (실시간 웹소켓 값 표시)
          Expanded(
            flex: 4,
            child: TextFormField(
              key: ValueKey(widget.value), // 실시간 값이 변할 때 텍스트 업데이트
              initialValue: widget.value,
              readOnly: true,
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: context.colors.textPrimary),
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                labelText: widget.label,
                labelStyle: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                isDense: true,
                contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12.0, vertical: 12.0),
                fillColor: context.colors.background,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),

          // 2. 작성 가능한 텍스트 필드 (입력 중인 값 유지)
          if (widget.onConfirm != null) ...[
            Expanded(
              flex: 4,
              child: TextField(
                controller: _inputController,
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14),
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  hintText: '입력',
                  isDense: true,
                  contentPadding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12.0, vertical: 12.0),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),

            // 3. 확인 버튼
            SizedBox(
              height: 44,
              child: ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: () {
                  final inputText = _inputController.text;
                  widget.onConfirm!(widget.label, inputText);
                },
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  minimumSize: const Size(64, 44),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14.0),
                ),
                child: const Text('확인', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Box #4: 비밀번호 수정 박스
class PasswordEditBox extends StatefulWidget {
  final TextEditingController currentPwController;
  final TextEditingController newPwController;
  final String collapseMessage;
  final Future<void> Function() onSubmit;

  const PasswordEditBox({
    super.key,
    required this.currentPwController,
    required this.newPwController,
    required this.collapseMessage,
    required this.onSubmit,
  });

  @override
  State<PasswordEditBox> createState() => _PasswordEditBoxState();
}

class _PasswordEditBoxState extends State<PasswordEditBox> {
  bool _obscureCurrent = true;
  bool _obscureNew = true;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return _EditCard(
      title: '비밀번호 변경',
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          TextField(
            controller: widget.currentPwController,
            obscureText: _obscureCurrent,
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              labelText: '기존 비밀번호',
              prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline_rounded),
              suffixIcon: IconButton(
                tooltip: '비밀번호 표시',
                icon: Icon(_obscureCurrent ? Icons.visibility_outlined : Icons.visibility_off_outlined, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                onPressed: () => setState(() => _obscureCurrent = !_obscureCurrent),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          TextField(
            controller: widget.newPwController,
            obscureText: _obscureNew,
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              labelText: '새 비밀번호',
              prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline_rounded),
              suffixIcon: IconButton(
                tooltip: '비밀번호 표시',
                icon: Icon(_obscureNew ? Icons.visibility_outlined : Icons.visibility_off_outlined, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                onPressed: () => setState(() => _obscureNew = !_obscureNew),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          if (widget.collapseMessage.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            Text(
              widget.collapseMessage,
              style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.primary, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
            ),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          AsyncActionButton(label: '비밀번호 변경', onPressed: widget.onSubmit),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Box #5: 외부 프로바이더 연동 박스
class SocialLinkBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<dynamic> linkedProviders;
  final Function(String) onLinkPressed;

  const SocialLinkBox({
    super.key,
    required this.linkedProviders,
    required this.onLinkPressed,
  });

  bool _isLinked(String provider) =>
      linkedProviders.any((p) => p.toString().toLowerCase() == provider);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return _EditCard(
      title: '연동 계정',
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          _ProviderRow(
            label: 'Google',
            isLinked: _isLinked('google'),
            onPressed: () => onLinkPressed('google'),
            badge: Container(
              width: 22,
              height: 22,
              alignment: Alignment.center,
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: Color(0xFF4285F4), shape: BoxShape.circle),
              child: const Text('G', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          _ProviderRow(
            label: 'Apple',
            isLinked: _isLinked('apple'),
            onPressed: () => onLinkPressed('apple'),
            badge: Container(
              width: 22,
              height: 22,
              alignment: Alignment.center,
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black, shape: BoxShape.circle),
              child: const Icon(Icons.apple, color: Colors.white, size: 13),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 프로바이더 한 줄. 이미 연동된 계정은 '연동됨'으로 표시하고 버튼을 없앤다.
class _ProviderRow extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final bool isLinked;
  final Widget badge;
  final VoidCallback onPressed;

  const _ProviderRow({
    required this.label,
    required this.isLinked,
    required this.badge,
    required this.onPressed,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          badge,
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: context.colors.textPrimary),
            ),
          ),
          if (isLinked)
            Row(
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.check_circle_rounded, color: context.colors.success, size: 17),
                const SizedBox(width: 5),
                Text(
                  '연동됨',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: context.colors.success),
                ),
              ],
            )
          else
            TextButton(onPressed: onPressed, child: const Text('연동하기')),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 화면 테마(밝게/어둡게/시스템 설정) 선택 박스.
class ThemeModeBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final ThemeMode current;
  final ValueChanged<ThemeMode> onChanged;

  const ThemeModeBox({super.key, required this.current, required this.onChanged});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return _EditCard(
      title: '화면 테마',
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: ThemeMode.values.map((mode) {
          final selected = current == mode;
          return ListTile(
            contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
            dense: true,
            onTap: () => onChanged(mode),
            leading: Icon(
              selected ? Icons.radio_button_checked_rounded : Icons.radio_button_unchecked_rounded,
              color: selected ? context.colors.primary : context.colors.textSecondary,
              size: 20,
            ),
            title: Text(
              ThemeController.labelOf(mode),
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 14,
                fontWeight: selected ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w500,
                color: context.colors.textPrimary,
              ),
            ),
          );
        }).toList(),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 계정 탈퇴 박스. 되돌릴 수 없는 동작이라 다른 카드와 분리해 경고색으로 표시한다.
class WithdrawBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final VoidCallback onWithdraw;

  const WithdrawBox({super.key, required this.onWithdraw});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: context.colors.error.withValues(alpha: 0.35)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Text(
            '계정 탈퇴',
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: context.colors.error),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            '탈퇴하면 계정 정보와 시설 소속 정보가 모두 삭제되며 되돌릴 수 없습니다.',
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          OutlinedButton(
            onPressed: onWithdraw,
            style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
              foregroundColor: context.colors.error,
              side: BorderSide(color: context.colors.error.withValues(alpha: 0.5)),
            ),
            child: const Text('계정 탈퇴'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/shared/alarm_row.dart';

/// Box #2: 시설 알람 박스 (순수 목록 표시 역할)
class FacilityAlarmBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<GroupedAlarm> alarms;
  final bool isSearching;

  const FacilityAlarmBox({
    super.key,
    required this.alarms,
    this.isSearching = false,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (alarms.isEmpty) {
      return SliverToBoxAdapter(
        child: EmptyListState(
          icon: Icons.notifications_off_outlined,
          message: isSearching ? '검색 결과가 없습니다.' : '조회할 수 있는 알람이 없습니다.',
        ),
      );
    }

    // 알람은 계속 쌓이므로 화면에 보이는 만큼만 만드는 Sliver 목록을 쓴다.
    return SliverList.separated(
      itemCount: alarms.length,
      separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(height: 8),
      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
        final group = alarms[index];

        return GroupedAlarmTile(
          group: group,
          title: group.alarm['alarm_name'] ?? '알람 이름 없음',
          subtitle: group.alarm['room_name'] ?? '호실 이름 없음',
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/shared/alarm_row.dart';

/// Box #2: 통합 알람 박스 (순수 목록 표시 및 이벤트 전달)
class UserAlarmBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<GroupedAlarm> alarms;
  final Function(int) onFacilityPressed;
  final bool isSearching;

  const UserAlarmBox({
    super.key,
    required this.alarms,
    required this.onFacilityPressed,
    this.isSearching = false,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (alarms.isEmpty) {
      return SliverToBoxAdapter(
        child: EmptyListState(
          icon: Icons.notifications_off_outlined,
          message: isSearching ? '검색 결과가 없습니다.' : '조회할 수 있는 알람이 없습니다.',
        ),
      );
    }

    // 알람은 계속 쌓여서 한 유저에 100건이 넘는다. shrinkWrap을 쓰면 화면 밖까지
    // 전부 미리 만들어지므로, 보이는 만큼만 만드는 Sliver 목록으로 둔다.
    return SliverList.separated(
      itemCount: alarms.length,
      separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(height: 8),
      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
        final group = alarms[index];
        final representative = group.alarm;

        return GroupedAlarmTile(
          group: group,
          title: representative['alarm_name'] ?? '알람 이름 없음',
          subtitle:
              '${representative['facility_name'] ?? '시설 이름 없음'} · ${representative['room_name'] ?? '룸 이름 없음'}',
          onAlarmTap: (alarm) {
            final facilityId = alarm['facility_id'] is int
                ? alarm['facility_id']
                : int.tryParse(alarm['facility_id']?.toString() ?? '0') ?? 0;
            onFacilityPressed(facilityId);
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// Box #2: 통합 시설 박스
class IntegratedFacilityBox extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<dynamic> facilities;
  final Function(int) onFacilityPressed;
  final String label;
  final String emptyMessage;

  const IntegratedFacilityBox({
    super.key,
    required this.facilities,
    required this.onFacilityPressed,
    this.label = '내 시설',
    this.emptyMessage = '조회할 수 있는 시설이 없습니다.',
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        Text(
          label,
          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: context.colors.textSecondary, letterSpacing: 0.4),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),

        if (facilities.isEmpty)
          Container(
            width: double.infinity,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 32),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: context.colors.surface,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
            ),
            child: Column(
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.domain_disabled_outlined, color: context.colors.textSecondary.withValues(alpha: 0.6), size: 28),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(emptyMessage, style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13)),
              ],
            ),
          )
        else
          GridView.builder(
            shrinkWrap: true,
            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
              crossAxisCount: 2,
              crossAxisSpacing: 10,
              mainAxisSpacing: 10,
              childAspectRatio: 2.5,
            ),
            itemCount: facilities.length,
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final fac = facilities[index];
              // TODO: 알람 발생 여부는 추후 백엔드 데이터에 맞춰 실제 필드로 교체
              final bool hasAlarm = index % 2 == 0; // 임시: 짝수 인덱스에만 알람 표시

              return Material(
                color: context.colors.surface,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                child: InkWell(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                  onTap: () => onFacilityPressed(fac['id']),
                  child: Container(
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                      border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
                    ),
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                    child: Stack(
                      children: [
                        Center(
                          child: Text(
                            fac['name'],
                            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                            style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: context.colors.textPrimary, fontSize: 13),
                          ),
                        ),
                        if (hasAlarm)
                          Positioned(
                            right: 4,
                            top: 4,
                            child: Container(
                              width: 9,
                              height: 9,
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: context.colors.error,
                                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                border: Border.all(color: context.colors.surface, width: 1.5),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

// 경로: lib/components/shared/alarm_row.dart
// 명세: 알람 목록 행과 목록 가공(상태 필터·반복 묶음) — user_alarm/facility_alarm 두 화면에서 공유
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// 알람 목록 상태 필터.
enum AlarmStatusFilter { all, active, ended }

/// 반복 발생한 알람을 한 줄로 묶은 결과.
/// [members]는 최신순이며, 펼쳤을 때 개별 발생 건을 보여주는 데 쓴다.
class GroupedAlarm {
  final List<dynamic> members;

  const GroupedAlarm(this.members);

  /// 목록에 대표로 보여줄 건 (가장 최근 발생).
  dynamic get alarm => members.first;

  int get count => members.length;
}

/// 진행 여부로 목록을 거른다.
List<dynamic> filterAlarmsByStatus(List<dynamic> alarms, AlarmStatusFilter filter) {
  switch (filter) {
    case AlarmStatusFilter.all:
      return alarms;
    case AlarmStatusFilter.active:
      return alarms.where(isAlarmActive).toList();
    case AlarmStatusFilter.ended:
      return alarms.where((a) => !isAlarmActive(a)).toList();
  }
}

/// 같은 지점(role_id)에서 같은 이름으로 반복 발생한 알람을 한 줄로 묶는다.
/// 서버가 최신순으로 내려주므로 먼저 만난 건을 대표로 두고 건수만 센다.
List<GroupedAlarm> groupAlarms(List<dynamic> alarms) {
  final Map<String, List<dynamic>> groups = {};
  final List<String> order = [];

  for (final alarm in alarms) {
    final key = '${alarm['role_id']}|${alarm['alarm_name']}';
    if (!groups.containsKey(key)) {
      groups[key] = [];
      order.add(key);
    }
    groups[key]!.add(alarm);
  }

  return order.map((key) => GroupedAlarm(groups[key]!)).toList();
}

/// 알람이 아직 진행 중인지 판단한다.
/// DB의 severity는 point_role_table에 해당 컬럼이 없어 항상 'a'로 저장되므로
/// 구분에 쓸 수 없다. 대신 services/alarm.py와 같은 기준(end_time 경과 여부)을 쓴다.
bool isAlarmActive(dynamic alarm) {
  final endTime = DateTime.tryParse(alarm['end_time']?.toString() ?? '');
  if (endTime == null) return alarm['is_triggered'] == true;
  return endTime.isAfter(DateTime.now());
}

/// 서버가 내려주는 ISO 시간 문자열을 목록에서 읽기 쉬운 형태로 바꾼다.
String formatAlarmTime(String raw) {
  final parsed = DateTime.tryParse(raw);
  if (parsed == null) return raw;
  return DateFormat('MM/dd HH:mm').format(parsed.toLocal());
}

/// 알람 상태를 아이콘 대신 좌측 색상 바로 표현하는 리스트 행.
class AlarmRow extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final String timestamp;
  final bool isActive;

  /// 같은 알람이 반복 발생했을 때 묶인 건수. 1이면 배지를 숨긴다.
  final int count;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;

  /// 묶인 건을 펼칠 수 있는 행이면 화살표를 보여준다.
  final bool isExpandable;
  final bool isExpanded;

  const AlarmRow({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    required this.timestamp,
    required this.isActive,
    this.count = 1,
    this.onTap,
    this.isExpandable = false,
    this.isExpanded = false,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final Color accent = isActive ? context.colors.error : context.colors.textSecondary.withValues(alpha: 0.4);

    return Material(
      color: context.colors.surface,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
      child: InkWell(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        onTap: onTap,
        child: Container(
          clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
            border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
          ),
          // 좌측 색상 바는 Stack으로 높이를 맞춘다. IntrinsicHeight를 쓰면 텍스트 높이
          // 반올림 때문에 1px 오버플로가 생긴다.
          child: Stack(
            children: [
              Positioned(left: 0, top: 0, bottom: 0, width: 3, child: ColoredBox(color: accent)),
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 17, right: 14, top: 12, bottom: 12),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Row(
                            children: [
                              Flexible(
                                child: Text(
                                  title,
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 14,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                    color: context.colors.textPrimary,
                                  ),
                                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                ),
                              ),
                              if (count > 1) ...[
                                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                                Container(
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                    color: accent.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                                  ),
                                  child: Text(
                                    '×$count',
                                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: accent),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ],
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 3),
                          Text(
                            subtitle,
                            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          isActive ? '진행중' : '종료',
                          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: accent),
                        ),
                        if (timestamp.isNotEmpty) ...[
                          const SizedBox(height: 3),
                          Text(timestamp, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.textSecondary)),
                        ],
                      ],
                    ),
                    if (isExpandable)
                      Icon(
                        isExpanded ? Icons.expand_less_rounded : Icons.expand_more_rounded,
                        size: 20,
                        color: context.colors.textSecondary,
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 묶인 알람 한 덩어리. 2건 이상이면 눌러서 개별 발생 건을 펼쳐볼 수 있다.
class GroupedAlarmTile extends StatefulWidget {
  final GroupedAlarm group;
  final String title;

  /// 대표 행에 보여줄 부제 (시설·호실 등).
  final String subtitle;

  /// 개별 건을 눌렀을 때 동작. 묶이지 않은 단일 알람의 탭에도 쓰인다.
  final void Function(dynamic alarm)? onAlarmTap;

  const GroupedAlarmTile({
    super.key,
    required this.group,
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    this.onAlarmTap,
  });

  @override
  State<GroupedAlarmTile> createState() => _GroupedAlarmTileState();
}

class _GroupedAlarmTileState extends State<GroupedAlarmTile> {
  bool _expanded = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final group = widget.group;
    final isGrouped = group.count > 1;

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        AlarmRow(
          title: widget.title,
          subtitle: widget.subtitle,
          timestamp: formatAlarmTime(group.alarm['timestamp']?.toString() ?? ''),
          isActive: isAlarmActive(group.alarm),
          count: group.count,
          isExpandable: isGrouped,
          isExpanded: _expanded,
          // 묶인 행은 펼치기/접기, 단일 행은 원래 동작을 그대로 한다.
          onTap: isGrouped
              ? () => setState(() => _expanded = !_expanded)
              : (widget.onAlarmTap == null ? null : () => widget.onAlarmTap!(group.alarm)),
        ),
        if (_expanded)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 14, top: 6),
            child: Column(
              children: group.members
                  .map((alarm) => _OccurrenceRow(
                        alarm: alarm,
                        onTap: widget.onAlarmTap == null ? null : () => widget.onAlarmTap!(alarm),
                      ))
                  .toList(),
            ),
          ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// 펼쳤을 때 보이는 개별 발생 건 한 줄.
class _OccurrenceRow extends StatelessWidget {
  final dynamic alarm;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;

  const _OccurrenceRow({required this.alarm, this.onTap});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final active = isAlarmActive(alarm);
    final color = active ? context.colors.error : context.colors.textSecondary;

    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Container(
              width: 5,
              height: 5,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color, shape: BoxShape.circle),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 10),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                formatAlarmTime(alarm['timestamp']?.toString() ?? ''),
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
              ),
            ),
            Text(
              active ? '진행중' : '종료',
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: color),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 상태 필터 칩과 '묶어서 보기' 토글. 두 알람 화면이 함께 쓴다.
class AlarmFilterBar extends StatelessWidget {
  final AlarmStatusFilter statusFilter;
  final ValueChanged<AlarmStatusFilter> onStatusChanged;
  final bool grouped;
  final ValueChanged<bool> onGroupedChanged;

  const AlarmFilterBar({
    super.key,
    required this.statusFilter,
    required this.onStatusChanged,
    required this.grouped,
    required this.onGroupedChanged,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Expanded(
          child: Wrap(
            spacing: 6,
            children: [
              _chip(context, '전체', AlarmStatusFilter.all),
              _chip(context, '진행중', AlarmStatusFilter.active),
              _chip(context, '종료', AlarmStatusFilter.ended),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        Text('묶어서 보기', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.textSecondary)),
        Switch(value: grouped, onChanged: onGroupedChanged),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _chip(BuildContext context, String label, AlarmStatusFilter value) {
    final selected = statusFilter == value;
    return ChoiceChip(
      label: Text(label),
      selected: selected,
      onSelected: (_) => onStatusChanged(value),
      labelStyle: TextStyle(
        fontSize: 12,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
        color: selected ? context.colors.onPrimary : context.colors.textPrimary,
      ),
      selectedColor: context.colors.primary,
      backgroundColor: context.colors.background,
      side: BorderSide(color: selected ? context.colors.primary : context.colors.outline),
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9)),
    );
  }
}

/// 목록 계열 화면 공용 빈 상태 표시.
class EmptyListState extends StatelessWidget {
  final String message;
  final IconData icon;

  const EmptyListState({super.key, required this.message, this.icon = Icons.inbox_outlined});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 32),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Icon(icon, color: context.colors.textSecondary.withValues(alpha: 0.6), size: 28),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(message, style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// 경로: lib/components/shared/async_action_button.dart
// 명세: 서버 요청처럼 시간이 걸리는 동작을 실행하는 버튼
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// 누르면 비동기 동작을 실행하고, 끝날 때까지 스피너를 보여주는 버튼.
///
/// 기존에는 저장/로그인 버튼을 눌러도 요청이 끝날 때까지 아무 반응이 없어서
/// 눌린 건지 알 수 없었고, 여러 번 눌러 같은 요청이 중복으로 나가기도 했다.
class AsyncActionButton extends StatefulWidget {
  final String label;
  final Future<void> Function() onPressed;
  final ButtonStyle? style;

  const AsyncActionButton({
    super.key,
    required this.label,
    required this.onPressed,
    this.style,
  });

  @override
  State<AsyncActionButton> createState() => _AsyncActionButtonState();
}

class _AsyncActionButtonState extends State<AsyncActionButton> {
  bool _running = false;

  Future<void> _handlePressed() async {
    if (_running) return;
    setState(() => _running = true);
    try {
      await widget.onPressed();
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _running = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Semantics(
      // 진행 중에는 라벨이 스피너로 바뀌어 스크린 리더가 버튼 이름을 잃는다.
      // 이름을 유지하고 진행 상태도 함께 알린다.
      label: widget.label,
      button: true,
      enabled: !_running,
      child: ElevatedButton(
        // 진행 중에는 null을 넘겨 비활성 상태가 되게 한다 (중복 탭 방지 + 시각적 표시)
        onPressed: _running ? null : _handlePressed,
        style: widget.style,
        child: _running
            // 비활성 배경은 옅은 회색이라 흰색 스피너는 보이지 않는다.
            ? SizedBox(
                width: 18,
                height: 18,
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: context.colors.primary),
              )
            : Text(widget.label),
      ),
    );
  }
}

void main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();

  // 💡 앱 초기화 로직을 config.dart로 추상화하여 main.dart 수정을 원천 차단
  await AppConfig.initializeAll();

  // 알림 채널/권한/수신 리스너 등록. 그동안 호출되는 곳이 없어 푸시가 와도
  // 로컬 알림이 뜨지 않았다. flutter_local_notifications는 웹을 지원하지 않으므로 건너뛴다.
  if (!kIsWeb) {
    await FcmService.initialize();
  }

  // 저장된 테마 선택값 복원 (config.dart는 .gitignore 대상이라 여기서 호출한다)
  await ThemeController.load();

  runApp(const MyApp());
}

class MyApp extends StatelessWidget {
  const MyApp({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ValueListenableBuilder<ThemeMode>(
      valueListenable: ThemeController.mode,
      builder: (context, themeMode, _) => MaterialApp.router(
        title: 'IDCool Systems Routing',
        debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
        theme: AppTheme.light,
        darkTheme: AppTheme.dark,
        themeMode: themeMode,
        routerConfig: appRouter, // routes.dart의 라우터가 마치 한 파일처럼 인식됨
        localizationsDelegates: const [
          GlobalMaterialLocalizations.delegate,
          GlobalWidgetsLocalizations.delegate,
          GlobalCupertinoLocalizations.delegate,
          FlutterQuillLocalizations.delegate,
        ],
        supportedLocales: const [Locale('ko', 'KR'), Locale('en', 'US')],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class PostModel {
  final int postId;
  final int facilityId;
  final int creatorId;
  final String title;
  final String content;
  final String facilityName;
  final String creatorName;

  PostModel({
    required this.postId, required this.facilityId, required this.creatorId,
    required this.title, required this.content, required this.facilityName, required this.creatorName,
  });

  factory PostModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return PostModel(
      postId: json['post_id'], facilityId: json['facility_id'], creatorId: json['creator_id'],
      title: json['title'], content: json['content'],
      facilityName: json['facility_name'] ?? '', creatorName: json['creator_name'] ?? '알 수 없음',
    );
  }
}

// 경로: lib/pages/common/find_id_page.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/common/find_id/find_id_components.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/find_id/auth_api_service.dart';

class FindIdPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const FindIdPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<FindIdPage> createState() => _FindIdPageState();
}

class _FindIdPageState extends State<FindIdPage> {
  final TextEditingController _emailController = TextEditingController();

  bool _isExpanded = false;
  String _resultMessage = '';

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _emailController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _handleCheckAccount() async {
    final email = _emailController.text.trim();

    if (email.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('확인할 이메일을 입력해주세요.')),
      );
      return;
    }

    try {
      final result = await AuthApiService.checkAccountExistence(email);

      setState(() {
        _isExpanded = true;
        _resultMessage = result['message'];
      });

    } catch (e) {
      setState(() {
        _isExpanded = true;
        _resultMessage = '오류 발생: ${e.toString()}';
      });
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24.0),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text('아이디 찾기', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineMedium),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              Text(
                '가입하신 이메일을 입력하면 계정 정보를 확인해드려요.',
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 32),
              FindIdBox(
                emailController: _emailController,
                onSubmit: _handleCheckAccount,
                resultMessage: _resultMessage,
                isExpanded: _isExpanded,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


======================================================================
■ File : find_pw_page.dart
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_app\IDCS\lib\pages\common\find_pw_page.dart
======================================================================

// 경로: lib/pages/common/find_pw_page.dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import 'package:idcs/services/common/find_pw/auth_api_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';

class FindPwPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const FindPwPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<FindPwPage> createState() => _FindPwPageState();
}

class _FindPwPageState extends State<FindPwPage> {
  // 단계 제어 (1: 이메일, 2: 코드 입력, 3: 새 비밀번호 설정)
  int _currentStep = 1;

  // 상태 변수
  String _jwtToken = '';       // Box 1에서 발급받은 코드 확인용 JWT
  String _resetToken = '';     // Box 2에서 발급받은 비밀번호 변경용 JWT
  String _box1Message = '';
  String _box2Message = '';
  String _box3Message = '';

  // 컨트롤러
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _codeController = TextEditingController();
  final _pwController = TextEditingController();
  final _pwConfirmController = TextEditingController();

  bool _obscurePw = true;
  bool _obscurePwConfirm = true;

  // 타이머 관련
  Timer? _timer;
  int _remainingSeconds = 300; // 5분

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    _emailController.dispose();
    _codeController.dispose();
    _pwController.dispose();
    _pwConfirmController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _startTimer() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    _remainingSeconds = 300;
    _timer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (timer) {
      setState(() {
        if (_remainingSeconds > 0) {
          _remainingSeconds--;
        } else {
          _timer?.cancel();
          _box2Message = '입력 시간이 초과되었습니다.';
        }
      });
    });
  }

  String _formatTime(int seconds) {
    final m = (seconds / 60).floor();
    final s = seconds % 60;
    return '${m.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${s.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }

  // Box #1 제출 로직
  Future<void> _handleEmailSubmit() async {
    final email = _emailController.text.trim();
    if (email.isEmpty) return;

    try {
      final res = await AuthApiService.requestPasswordCode(email);
      setState(() {
        _jwtToken = res['token'];
        // 개발용 콘솔에만 찍히도록 백엔드에서 준 dev_code 표시 가능 (테스트용)
        _box1Message = res['message'] + " (Test Code: ${res['dev_code']})";
        _currentStep = 2; // Box 2 열기
      });
      _startTimer();
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() => _box1Message = e.toString());
    }
  }

  // Box #2 제출 로직
  Future<void> _handleCodeSubmit() async {
    final code = _codeController.text.trim();
    if (code.isEmpty || _remainingSeconds <= 0) return;

    try {
      final res = await AuthApiService.verifyPasswordCode(_jwtToken, code);
      setState(() {
        _resetToken = res['reset_token'];
        _box2Message = '코드 인증 성공!';
        _timer?.cancel();
        _currentStep = 3; // Box 3 열기
      });
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() => _box2Message = e.toString());
    }
  }

  // Box #3 제출 로직
  Future<void> _handlePasswordReset() async {
    final pw1 = _pwController.text;
    final pw2 = _pwConfirmController.text;

    if (pw1.isEmpty || pw2.isEmpty) return;
    if (pw1 != pw2) {
      setState(() => _box3Message = '비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
      return;
    }

    try {
      final res = await AuthApiService.resetPassword(_resetToken, pw1);
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(res['message'])));
      context.go('/login'); // 완료 시 로그인 페이지 이동
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() => _box3Message = e.toString());
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24.0),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text('비밀번호 재설정', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineMedium),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              Text(
                '이메일 인증 후 새 비밀번호를 설정해주세요.',
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 28),

              // Step #1: 이메일 입력
              _buildStepCard(
                step: 1,
                title: '이메일 인증',
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                  children: [
                    _buildInputRow(
                      icon: Icons.mail_outline_rounded,
                      hint: '이메일',
                      controller: _emailController,
                      isEnabled: _currentStep == 1,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 14),
                    ElevatedButton(
                      onPressed: _currentStep == 1 ? _handleEmailSubmit : null,
                      child: const Text('인증 코드 받기'),
                    ),
                    if (_box1Message.isNotEmpty) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 10),
                      _buildMessage(_box1Message),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),

              // Step #2: 코드 입력
              AnimatedSize(
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
                curve: Curves.easeOut,
                child: _currentStep >= 2
                    ? _buildStepCard(
                        step: 2,
                        title: '인증 코드 입력',
                        child: Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                          children: [
                            _buildInputRow(
                              icon: Icons.pin_outlined,
                              hint: '인증 코드',
                              controller: _codeController,
                              isEnabled: _currentStep == 2,
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 10),
                            Row(
                              children: [
                                Icon(Icons.timer_outlined, size: 16, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                                Text(
                                  '남은 시간 ${_formatTime(_remainingSeconds)}',
                                  style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 14),
                            ElevatedButton(
                              onPressed: _currentStep == 2 ? _handleCodeSubmit : null,
                              child: const Text('코드 확인'),
                            ),
                            if (_box2Message.isNotEmpty) ...[
                              const SizedBox(height: 10),
                              _buildMessage(_box2Message),
                            ],
                          ],
                        ),
                      )
                    : const SizedBox.shrink(),
              ),
              SizedBox(height: _currentStep >= 2 ? 16 : 0),

              // Step #3: 새 비밀번호 입력
              AnimatedSize(
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
                curve: Curves.easeOut,
                child: _currentStep >= 3
                    ? _buildStepCard(
                        step: 3,
                        title: '새 비밀번호 설정',
                        child: Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                          children: [
                            _buildInputRow(
                              icon: Icons.lock_outline_rounded,
                              hint: '새 비밀번호',
                              controller: _pwController,
                              isObscure: _obscurePw,
                              onToggleObscure: () => setState(() => _obscurePw = !_obscurePw),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 10),
                            _buildInputRow(
                              icon: Icons.lock_outline_rounded,
                              hint: '비밀번호 재입력',
                              controller: _pwConfirmController,
                              isObscure: _obscurePwConfirm,
                              onToggleObscure: () => setState(() => _obscurePwConfirm = !_obscurePwConfirm),
                            ),
                            if (_box3Message.isNotEmpty) ...[
                              const SizedBox(height: 10),
                              _buildMessage(_box3Message),
                            ],
                            const SizedBox(height: 14),
                            ElevatedButton(
                              onPressed: _handlePasswordReset,
                              child: const Text('비밀번호 재설정'),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      )
                    : const SizedBox.shrink(),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 24),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // 단계 카드 공통 UI
  Widget _buildStepCard({required int step, required String title, required Widget child}) {
    final bool isActive = step == _currentStep;
    final bool isDone = step < _currentStep;

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(18.0),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        border: Border.all(color: isActive ? context.colors.primary : context.colors.outline, width: isActive ? 1.4 : 1),
      ),
      child: Opacity(
        opacity: isActive || isDone ? 1.0 : 0.5,
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            Row(
              children: [
                _StepBadge(isActive: isActive, isDone: isDone, number: step),
                const SizedBox(width: 10),
                Text(
                  title,
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: context.colors.textPrimary),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            child,
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMessage(String message) {
    return Text(
      message,
      style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.primary, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, height: 1.4),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildInputRow({
    required IconData icon,
    required String hint,
    required TextEditingController controller,
    bool isEnabled = true,
    bool? isObscure,
    VoidCallback? onToggleObscure,
  }) {
    return TextField(
      controller: controller,
      obscureText: isObscure ?? false,
      enabled: isEnabled,
      decoration: InputDecoration(
        labelText: hint,
        prefixIcon: Icon(icon),
        suffixIcon: onToggleObscure == null
            ? null
            : IconButton(
              tooltip: '비밀번호 표시',
                icon: Icon(
                  isObscure! ? Icons.visibility_outlined : Icons.visibility_off_outlined,
                  color: context.colors.textSecondary,
                ),
                onPressed: onToggleObscure,
              ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _StepBadge extends StatelessWidget {
  final bool isActive;
  final bool isDone;
  final int number;

  const _StepBadge({required this.isActive, required this.isDone, required this.number});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final Color background = isDone
        ? context.colors.primary
        : isActive
            ? context.colors.primary
            : context.colors.outline;

    return Container(
      width: 24,
      height: 24,
      alignment: Alignment.center,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: background, shape: BoxShape.circle),
      child: isDone
          ? Icon(Icons.check, size: 14, color: context.colors.onPrimary)
          : Text(
              '$number',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                color: isActive ? context.colors.onPrimary : context.colors.textSecondary,
              ),
            ),
    );
  }
}
