// ===============================================================
// 경로 : lib/pages/common/login_page.dart
// 명세 : 로그인 페이지
// ===============================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import 'package:idcs/components/common/login/login_components.dart';
import 'package:idcs/main.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/login/auth_api_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/social_auth_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_logo.dart';

class LoginPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const LoginPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<LoginPage> createState() => _LoginPageState();
}

class _LoginPageState extends State<LoginPage> {
  final TextEditingController _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _passwordController = TextEditingController();

  // 로그인 상태 유지 여부를 상위에서 관리
  bool _keepLogin = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _emailController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  // API 연동: 일반 로그인 처리
  Future<void> _handleGeneralLogin() async {
    final email = _emailController.text.trim();
    final password = _passwordController.text.trim();

    if (email.isEmpty || password.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('이메일과 비밀번호를 모두 입력해주세요.')),
      );
      return;
    }

    try {
      final result = await AuthApiService.loginGeneral(
        email: email,
        password: password,
        keepLogin: _keepLogin,
      );

      // 1. AuthService에 발급받은 user_id 저장 (이 시점에 isLoggedIn이 true가 됨)
      final userId = result['user_id'];
      await AuthService.setUserId(userId, persist: _keepLogin);

      // [비동기 컨텍스트 방어 로직] 화면이 파괴되지 않았는지 확인
      if (!mounted) return;

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text(result['message'])),
      );

      // 2. 메인 색인 페이지로 라우팅 (이제 리다이렉트에 걸리지 않고 정상 이동!)
      context.go('/index/$userId/home');

    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())),
      );
    }
  }

  // API 연동: 소셜 로그인 처리
  Future<void> _handleSocialLogin(String provider) async {
    try {
      Map<String, String>? socialData;

      if (provider == 'google') {
        socialData = await SocialAuthService.signInWithGoogle();
      } else if (provider == 'apple') {
        socialData = await SocialAuthService.signInWithApple();
      }

      if (socialData == null) {
        return; // 로그인 취소
      }

      // 백엔드로 소셜 로그인 API 호출
      final result = await AuthApiService.loginSocial(
        provider: socialData['provider']!,
        providerId: socialData['provider_id']!,
        keepLogin: _keepLogin,
      );

      final userId = result['user_id'];
      await AuthService.setUserId(userId, persist: _keepLogin);

      if (!mounted) return;

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text(result['message'])),
      );

      context.go('/index/$userId/home');

    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const AppWordmark(fontSize: 17)),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24.0),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text('다시 만나서 반가워요', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineMedium),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              Text(
                '계정 정보를 입력하고 로그인해주세요.',
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 32),

              StandardLoginBox(
                emailController: _emailController,
                passwordController: _passwordController,
                onLoginPressed: _handleGeneralLogin,
              ),

              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  KeepLoginStateBox(
                    isChecked: _keepLogin,
                    onChanged: (bool? value) {
                      setState(() {
                        _keepLogin = value ?? false;
                      });
                    },
                  ),
                  TextButton(
                    onPressed: () => context.push('/find_pw'),
                    child: const Text('비밀번호 찾기'),
                  ),
                ],
              ),

              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              SocialLoginBox(
                onSocialLoginPressed: _handleSocialLogin,
              ),

              const SizedBox(height: 40),

              Center(
                child: Wrap(
                  alignment: WrapAlignment.center,
                  crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      '계정이 없으신가요? ',
                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
                    ),
                    GestureDetector(
                      onTap: () => context.push('/signup'),
                      child: Text(
                        '회원가입',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: context.colors.primary,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    Text(
                      '  ·  ',
                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
                    ),
                    GestureDetector(
                      onTap: () => context.push('/find_id'),
                      child: Text(
                        '아이디 찾기',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: context.colors.primary,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 24),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// 경로: lib/pages/common/signup_page.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/common/signup/signup_components.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/signup/auth_api_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/social_auth_service.dart';

class SignupPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const SignupPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<SignupPage> createState() => _SignupPageState();
}

class _SignupPageState extends State<SignupPage> {
  final TextEditingController _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _phoneController = TextEditingController();

  // --- Box 3 (애플 폴백) 관련 상태 변수 ---
  bool _showAppleFallback = false;
  String _pendingAppleProviderId = '';
  String _pendingAppleName = '';
  final TextEditingController _fallbackEmailController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _emailController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    _nameController.dispose();
    _phoneController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  // API 연동: 일반 계정 생성 처리
  Future<void> _handleGeneralSignup() async {
    final email = _emailController.text.trim();
    final password = _passwordController.text.trim();
    final name = _nameController.text.trim();
    final phone = _phoneController.text.trim();

    if (email.isEmpty || password.isEmpty || name.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('필수 항목을 모두 입력해주세요.')),
      );
      return;
    }

    try {
      final result = await AuthApiService.signupGeneral(
        email: email,
        password: password,
        name: name,
        phoneNumber: phone,
      );

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text(result['message'])),
      );
      // 가입 성공 시 로그인 페이지로 이동
      context.go('/login');
    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())),
      );
    }
  }

  // API 연동: 소셜 계정 생성 처리
  Future<void> _handleSocialSignup(String provider) async {
    try {
      Map<String, String>? socialData;

      if (provider == 'google') {
        socialData = await SocialAuthService.signInWithGoogle();
      } else if (provider == 'apple') {
        socialData = await SocialAuthService.signInWithApple();
      }

      if (socialData == null) return;

      // 이메일 누락 방어 로직 (Box 3 트리거)
      if (socialData['email'] == null || socialData['email']!.isEmpty) {
        if (provider == 'apple') {
          setState(() {
            _showAppleFallback = true; // Box 3 열기
            _pendingAppleProviderId = socialData!['provider_id']!;
            _pendingAppleName = socialData['name']!;
          });
          return; // 여기서 흐름을 멈추고 유저의 수동 입력을 기다립니다.
        } else {
          throw Exception('이메일 정보를 가져올 수 없습니다. 설정에서 이메일을 허용해주세요.');
        }
      }

      // 정상적으로 이메일이 있는 경우 바로 통신
      await _submitSocialSignup(
        provider: socialData['provider']!,
        providerId: socialData['provider_id']!,
        email: socialData['email']!,
        name: socialData['name']!,
      );

    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
    }
  }

  // API 연동: 애플 폴백 박스(Box 3)에서 직접 입력 완료 시 실행
  Future<void> _handleAppleFallbackSubmit() async {
    final email = _fallbackEmailController.text.trim();
    if (email.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('이메일을 입력해주세요.')),
      );
      return;
    }

    await _submitSocialSignup(
      provider: 'apple',
      providerId: _pendingAppleProviderId,
      email: email,
      name: _pendingAppleName,
    );
  }

  // 중복되는 소셜 통신 로직 분리
  Future<void> _submitSocialSignup({
    required String provider,
    required String providerId,
    required String email,
    required String name
  }) async {
    try {
      final result = await AuthApiService.signupSocial(
        provider: provider,
        providerId: providerId,
        email: email,
        name: name,
      );

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(result['message'])));
      context.go('/login');
    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24.0),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text('계정 만들기', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineMedium),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              Text(
                '아래 정보를 입력하고 계정을 생성해주세요.',
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 32),

              StandardSignupBox(
                emailController: _emailController,
                passwordController: _passwordController,
                nameController: _nameController,
                phoneController: _phoneController,
                onSignupPressed: _handleGeneralSignup, // 기존 로직
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),

              SocialSignupBox(
                onSocialSignupPressed: _handleSocialSignup,
              ),

              // Box #3: _showAppleFallback이 true일 때만 화면에 노출됨
              if (_showAppleFallback) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                AppleEmailFallbackBox(
                  fallbackEmailController: _fallbackEmailController,
                  onSubmit: _handleAppleFallbackSubmit,
                  onCancel: () {
                    setState(() {
                      _showAppleFallback = false; // 취소 시 다시 닫음
                      _fallbackEmailController.clear();
                    });
                  },
                ),
              ],
              const SizedBox(height: 24),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ===============================================================
// 경로: lib/pages/facility/board/facility_board_page.dart
// 명세: 시설 소요 기록 페이지 (단일 시설 포스트 조회)
// ===============================================================
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:idcs/models/post_model.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/cross_compontents/board/shared_board_compontents.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/board/board_api_service.dart';

class FacilityBoardPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  final String facilityId;

  const FacilityBoardPage({super.key, required this.userId, required this.facilityId});

  @override
  State<FacilityBoardPage> createState() => _FacilityBoardPageState();
}

class _FacilityBoardPageState extends State<FacilityBoardPage> {
  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();
  String _selectedSearchType = 'all';

  List<PostModel> _posts = [];
  bool _isLoading = true;
  int _currentPage = 1;
  int _totalPages = 1;
  final int _limit = 10;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _fetchPosts();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _fetchPosts() async {
    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    try {
      final result = await BoardApiService.fetchFacilityPosts(
        facilityId: widget.facilityId,
        userId: widget.userId,
        page: _currentPage,
        limit: _limit,
        searchType: _selectedSearchType,
        keyword: _searchController.text,
      );

      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _posts = result['posts'];
          _totalPages = result['totalPages'];
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$e')));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  void _handleSearch() {
    _currentPage = 1;
    _fetchPosts();
  }

  void _handlePostTapped(int postId) {
    context.push('/post_detail/${widget.userId}/$postId').then((_) => _fetchPosts());
  }

  void _handleCreatePost() {
    context.push('/post_create/${widget.userId}/${widget.facilityId}').then((_) => _fetchPosts());
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('소요 기록')),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            BoardHeaderBox(
              title: '게시글 검색',
              searchController: _searchController,
              selectedSearchType: _selectedSearchType,
              onSearchTypeChanged: (val) {
                if (val != null) setState(() => _selectedSearchType = val);
              },
              onSearch: _handleSearch,
              onCreatePost: _handleCreatePost,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            BoardListAndPagingBox(
              posts: _posts,
              isLoading: _isLoading,
              currentPage: _currentPage,
              totalPages: _totalPages,
              onPageChanged: (page) {
                setState(() => _currentPage = page);
                _fetchPosts();
              },
              onPostTapped: _handlePostTapped,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ===============================================================
// 경로: lib/pages/facility/board/post_create_page.dart
// 명세: 새 게시글 작성 페이지
// ===============================================================
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:flutter_quill/flutter_quill.dart' as quill;
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/cross_compontents/board/post_editor_components.dart.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/board/board_api_service.dart';

class PostCreatePage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String facilityId;
  final String userId;

  const PostCreatePage({super.key, required this.facilityId, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<PostCreatePage> createState() => _PostCreatePageState();
}

class _PostCreatePageState extends State<PostCreatePage> {
  final TextEditingController _titleController = TextEditingController();
  late quill.QuillController _quillController;
  final List<int> _uploadedImageIds = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _quillController = quill.QuillController(
      document: quill.Document(),
      selection: const TextSelection.collapsed(offset: 0),
      readOnly: false,
    );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _titleController.dispose();
    _quillController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _handleImageAttach() async {
    final picked = await ImagePicker().pickImage(source: ImageSource.gallery);
    if (picked == null) return;

    try {
      final uploaded = await BoardApiService.uploadImage(
        bytes: await picked.readAsBytes(),
        filename: picked.name,
      );

      final index = _quillController.selection.baseOffset;
      final length = _quillController.selection.extentOffset - index;
      _quillController.replaceText(index, length, quill.BlockEmbed.image(uploaded.imageUrl), null);
      _uploadedImageIds.add(uploaded.imageId);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('이미지 첨부 실패: $e')));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handleSubmit() async {
    if (_titleController.text.trim().isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('제목을 입력해주세요.')));
      return;
    }

    final String contentJson = jsonEncode(_quillController.document.toDelta().toJson());

    try {
      await BoardApiService.createPost(
        userId: widget.userId,
        facilityId: int.parse(widget.facilityId),
        title: _titleController.text,
        contentJson: contentJson,
        imageIds: _uploadedImageIds,
      );

      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('게시글이 작성되었습니다.')));
        context.pop();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('게시글 작성 실패: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('새 게시글 작성 (시설 ${widget.facilityId})')),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            PostInputBox(
              titleController: _titleController,
              onImageAttach: _handleImageAttach,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            QuillEditorBox(quillController: _quillController),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            SubmitBox(
              buttonLabel: '게시글 등록하기',
              onSubmit: _handleSubmit,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ===============================================================
// 경로: lib/pages/facility/board/post_detail_page.dart
// 명세: 게시글 상세 조회 페이지
// ===============================================================
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:flutter_quill/flutter_quill.dart' as quill;
import 'package:idcs/components/cross_compontents/board/post_detail_components.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/board/board_api_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/user_api_service.dart';

class PostDetailPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final int postId;
  final String userId;

  const PostDetailPage({super.key, required this.postId, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<PostDetailPage> createState() => _PostDetailPageState();
}

class _PostDetailPageState extends State<PostDetailPage> {
  Map<String, dynamic>? _post;
  List<dynamic> _comments = [];
  bool _isLoading = true;
  String _userRole = 'USER';

  quill.QuillController? _quillController;
  final TextEditingController _commentController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _fetchPostDetail();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _commentController.dispose();
    _quillController?.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _fetchPostDetail() async {
    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    try {
      _userRole = await UserApiService.getUserRole(widget.userId);

      final decoded = await BoardApiService.fetchPostDetail(
        postId: widget.postId,
        userId: widget.userId,
      );

      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _post = decoded['data'];
          _comments = decoded['comments'] ?? [];
          _initQuillController(_post!['content']);
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('게시글 로드 실패: $e')));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  void _initQuillController(String contentStr) {
    try {
      final doc = quill.Document.fromJson(jsonDecode(contentStr));
      _quillController = quill.QuillController(
        document: doc,
        selection: const TextSelection.collapsed(offset: 0),
        readOnly: true,
      );
    } catch (e) {
      final doc = quill.Document()..insert(0, contentStr);
      _quillController = quill.QuillController(
        document: doc,
        selection: const TextSelection.collapsed(offset: 0),
        readOnly: true,
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handleDeletePost() async {
    try {
      await BoardApiService.deletePost(postId: widget.postId, userId: widget.userId);
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('삭제되었습니다.')));
        context.pop();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('삭제 실패: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handleAddComment() async {
    final content = _commentController.text.trim();
    if (content.isEmpty) return;

    try {
      await BoardApiService.addComment(
        postId: widget.postId,
        userId: widget.userId,
        content: content,
      );
      _commentController.clear();
      _fetchPostDetail();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('댓글 작성 실패: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handleDeleteComment(int commentId) async {
    try {
      await BoardApiService.deleteComment(commentId: commentId, userId: widget.userId);
      _fetchPostDetail();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('댓글 삭제 실패: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_isLoading) return const Scaffold(body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
    if (_post == null || _quillController == null) return const Scaffold(body: Center(child: Text('데이터를 불러올 수 없습니다.')));

    final bool isCreator = _post!['creator_id'].toString() == widget.userId;
    final bool isAdmin = _userRole == 'ADMIN' || _userRole == 'DEVELOPER';
    final bool canControl = isCreator || isAdmin;

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('게시글 상세 조회')),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            PostContentBox(post: _post!, quillController: _quillController!),
            if (canControl) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              PostControlBox(
                onEdit: () {
                  context.push('/post_edit/${widget.userId}/${widget.postId}').then((_) => _fetchPostDetail());
                },
                onDelete: _handleDeletePost,
              ),
            ],
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            CommentBox(
              comments: _comments,
              currentUserId: widget.userId,
              isAdmin: isAdmin,
              commentController: _commentController,
              onSubmit: _handleAddComment,
              onDelete: _handleDeleteComment,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ===============================================================
// 경로: lib/pages/facility/board/post_edit_page.dart
// 명세: 기존 게시글 수정 페이지
// ===============================================================
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:flutter_quill/flutter_quill.dart' as quill;
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/cross_compontents/board/post_editor_components.dart.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/board/board_api_service.dart';

class PostEditPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final int postId;
  final String userId;

  const PostEditPage({super.key, required this.postId, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<PostEditPage> createState() => _PostEditPageState();
}

class _PostEditPageState extends State<PostEditPage> {
  final TextEditingController _titleController = TextEditingController();
  quill.QuillController? _quillController;
  final List<int> _newImageIds = [];
  bool _isLoading = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _fetchOriginalPost();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _titleController.dispose();
    _quillController?.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _fetchOriginalPost() async {
    try {
      final decoded = await BoardApiService.fetchPostDetail(
        postId: widget.postId,
        userId: widget.userId,
      );
      final postData = decoded['data'];

      _titleController.text = postData['title'];
      _initQuillController(postData['content']);
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('원문 로드 실패: $e')));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  void _initQuillController(String contentStr) {
    try {
      final doc = quill.Document.fromJson(jsonDecode(contentStr));
      _quillController = quill.QuillController(
        document: doc,
        selection: const TextSelection.collapsed(offset: 0),
        readOnly: false,
      );
    } catch (e) {
      final doc = quill.Document()..insert(0, contentStr);
      _quillController = quill.QuillController(
        document: doc,
        selection: const TextSelection.collapsed(offset: 0),
        readOnly: false,
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handleImageAttach() async {
    final controller = _quillController;
    if (controller == null) return;

    final picked = await ImagePicker().pickImage(source: ImageSource.gallery);
    if (picked == null) return;

    try {
      final uploaded = await BoardApiService.uploadImage(
        bytes: await picked.readAsBytes(),
        filename: picked.name,
      );

      final index = controller.selection.baseOffset;
      final length = controller.selection.extentOffset - index;
      controller.replaceText(index, length, quill.BlockEmbed.image(uploaded.imageUrl), null);
      _newImageIds.add(uploaded.imageId);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('이미지 첨부 실패: $e')));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handleSubmit() async {
    if (_titleController.text.trim().isEmpty) return;

    final String contentJson = jsonEncode(_quillController!.document.toDelta().toJson());

    try {
      await BoardApiService.updatePost(
        postId: widget.postId,
        userId: widget.userId,
        title: _titleController.text,
        contentJson: contentJson,
        imageIds: _newImageIds,
      );

      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('수정 완료되었습니다.')));
        context.pop();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('수정 실패: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_isLoading || _quillController == null) {
      return const Scaffold(body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
    }

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('게시글 수정')),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            PostInputBox(
              titleController: _titleController,
              onImageAttach: _handleImageAttach,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            QuillEditorBox(quillController: _quillController!),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            SubmitBox(
              buttonLabel: '수정 완료하기',
              onSubmit: _handleSubmit,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

■ File : facility_chart_page.dart
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_app\IDCS\lib\pages\facility\chart\facility_chart_page.dart
======================================================================

// ===============================================================
// 경로: lib/pages/facility/chart/facility_chart_page.dart
// ===============================================================
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/facility/chart/chart_components.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/chart/chart_api_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/facility_api_service.dart';

class FacilityChartPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  final String facilityId;

  const FacilityChartPage({super.key, required this.userId, required this.facilityId});

  @override
  State<FacilityChartPage> createState() => _FacilityChartPageState();
}

class _FacilityChartPageState extends State<FacilityChartPage> {
  // 1. 필터 상태 (dataType 완전 제거)
  String _currentScale = '1m';
  String _currentTimeRange = '1d';

  // 2. 조회 대상 호실 상태
  List<dynamic> _rooms = [];
  int? _selectedRoomId;

  // 3. 데이터 포인트 선택 상태
  int? _selectedGroupId;
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _availableGroups = []; // _allGroups 변수도 불필요하여 제거

  /// 호실의 전체 포인트 롤. 그룹에 어떤 항목이 묶여 있는지 이름으로 보여주는 데 쓴다.
  List<dynamic> _roles = [];

  /// 실제로 차트에 그릴 롤. 그룹을 고르면 채워지고, 개별로 끄고 켤 수 있다.
  Set<int> _visibleRoleIds = {};

  /// 그룹과 무관하게 항목을 직접 고르는 모드.
  bool _directMode = false;

  // 4. 차트 렌더링 상태
  bool _isLoading = false;
  Map<String, dynamic>? _chartData;
  bool _showTargetLines = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadRooms();
  }

  // 0단계: 시설의 호실 목록 조회 후 첫 호실을 기본 선택한다.
  // (예전에는 호실이 1번으로 고정되어 있어 1번 호실이 없는 시설에서는 차트가 아예 뜨지 않았다)
  Future<void> _loadRooms() async {
    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      final rooms = await FacilityApiService.getFacilityRooms(widget.facilityId);

      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _rooms = rooms;
        _selectedRoomId = rooms.isEmpty
            ? null
            : (rooms.first['id'] is int
                ? rooms.first['id']
                : int.tryParse(rooms.first['id'].toString()));
      });

      if (_selectedRoomId != null) {
        await _loadGroupsAndChart();
      } else {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('호실 조회 오류: $e')));
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      }
    }
  }

  // 1단계: 전체 그룹 목록 조회 (타입 필터링 제거)
  Future<void> _loadGroupsAndChart() async {
    final int? roomId = _selectedRoomId;
    if (roomId == null) return;

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      // 웹 어드민에서 생성된 그룹 리스트와, 호실의 전체 포인트 롤을 함께 불러온다.
      // 그룹 API는 role_id만 주기 때문에 이름을 보여주려면 롤 목록이 필요하다.
      final facilityId = int.parse(widget.facilityId);
      final results = await Future.wait([
        ChartApiService.getChartGroups(facilityId, roomId),
        ChartApiService.getPointRoles(facilityId, roomId),
      ]);

      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          // 필터링 없이 바로 화면에 렌더링할 그룹 목록에 할당
          _availableGroups = List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(results[0]);
          _roles = results[1];

          // 선택된 그룹이 없거나 기존 선택 그룹이 삭제된 경우, 첫 번째 그룹 자동 선택
          bool shouldResetGroup = _selectedGroupId == null ||
              !_availableGroups.any((g) => g['group_id'] == _selectedGroupId);

          if (shouldResetGroup && _availableGroups.isNotEmpty) {
            _selectedGroupId = _availableGroups.first['group_id'];
          } else if (_availableGroups.isEmpty) {
            _selectedGroupId = null;
          }

          _visibleRoleIds = _directMode ? _allRoleIds() : _roleIdsOfGroup(_selectedGroupId);
        });

        if (_visibleRoleIds.isNotEmpty) {
          await _fetchChartData();
        } else {
          setState(() {
            _chartData = null;
            _isLoading = false;
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('그룹 로드 오류: $e')));
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      }
    }
  }

  Set<int> _allRoleIds() => _roles
      .map((r) => r['role_id'] is int ? r['role_id'] as int : int.tryParse(r['role_id'].toString()))
      .whereType<int>()
      .toSet();

  Set<int> _roleIdsOfGroup(int? groupId) {
    final group = _availableGroups.where((g) => g['group_id'] == groupId).firstOrNull;
    return (group?['role_ids'] as List?)?.whereType<int>().toSet() ?? <int>{};
  }

  void _selectGroup(int groupId) {
    setState(() {
      _directMode = false;
      _selectedGroupId = groupId;
      _visibleRoleIds = _roleIdsOfGroup(groupId);
    });
    _fetchChartData();
  }

  void _enterDirectMode() {
    // 호실의 모든 항목을 한꺼번에 켜면(42개까지 나온다) 눈금 범위가 뭉개져 차트를 읽을 수 없다.
    // 그래서 보고 있던 선택을 그대로 두고, 목록만 전체로 펼친다.
    setState(() => _directMode = true);
  }

  void _toggleRole(int roleId, bool visible) {
    setState(() {
      final next = Set<int>.from(_visibleRoleIds);
      visible ? next.add(roleId) : next.remove(roleId);
      _visibleRoleIds = next;
    });
    _fetchChartData();
  }

  // 2단계: 선택된 항목(role_ids)으로 차트 API 호출
  Future<void> _fetchChartData() async {
    final int? roomId = _selectedRoomId;
    if (roomId == null) return;

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      final List<int> roleIdsToFetch = _visibleRoleIds.toList();

      if (roleIdsToFetch.isEmpty) {
        setState(() {
          _chartData = null;
          _isLoading = false;
        });
        return;
      }

      // 파라미터에서 dataType 완전 제거됨
      final result = await ChartApiService.getHistoricalData(
        facilityId: int.parse(widget.facilityId),
        roomId: roomId,
        scale: _currentScale,
        timeRange: _currentTimeRange,
        roleIds: roleIdsToFetch,
      );

      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _chartData = result;
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('차트 오류: $e')));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('데이터 차트')),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            // Box 1
            ChartFilterBox(
              // currentDataType: _currentDataType 파라미터 완전 제거
              currentScale: _currentScale,
              currentTimeRange: _currentTimeRange,
              onScaleChanged: (val) => setState(() => _currentScale = val),
              onTimeRangeChanged: (val) => setState(() => _currentTimeRange = val),
              onApply: _fetchChartData,
              rooms: _rooms,
              selectedRoomId: _selectedRoomId,
              onRoomChanged: (roomId) {
                setState(() {
                  _selectedRoomId = roomId;
                  _chartData = null;
                });
                // 호실이 바뀌면 그 호실에 등록된 그룹부터 다시 불러와야 한다.
                _loadGroupsAndChart();
              },
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            // Box 2
            ChartSeriesBox(
              availableGroups: _availableGroups,
              selectedGroupId: _selectedGroupId,
              onGroupSelected: _selectGroup,
              directMode: _directMode,
              onDirectModeSelected: _enterDirectMode,
              roles: _roles,
              visibleRoleIds: _visibleRoleIds,
              onRoleToggled: _toggleRole,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            // Box 4
            ChartToolBox(
              showTargetLines: _showTargetLines,
              onToggleTargetLines: (val) => setState(() => _showTargetLines = val),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),

            // Box 3
            ChartCanvasBox(
              chartData: _chartData,
              isLoading: _isLoading,
              showTargetLines: _showTargetLines,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            ChartStatsBox(chartData: _chartData),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// 경로: lib/pages/facility/chat/chat_room_page.dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:idcs/main.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/web_socket_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/chat/chat_api_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/cross_compontents/chat/chat_room_components.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';

class ChatRoomPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  final int roomId;

  const ChatRoomPage({super.key, required this.userId, required this.roomId});

  @override
  State<ChatRoomPage> createState() => _ChatRoomPageState();
}

class _ChatRoomPageState extends State<ChatRoomPage> {
  final TextEditingController _msgController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();
  final WebSocketService _wsService = WebSocketService();
  final GlobalKey<ScaffoldState> _scaffoldKey = GlobalKey<ScaffoldState>();

  List<dynamic> _messages = [];
  List<dynamic> _participants = [];
  Map<String, dynamic>? _replyingTo; // 답장 대상 메시지 저장
  bool _isLoadingHistory = true;
  bool _isSearchMode = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _initChatRoom();
  }

  Future<void> _initChatRoom() async {
    try {
      await ChatApiService.markAsRead(widget.roomId, widget.userId);
      _participants = await ChatApiService.getParticipants(widget.roomId);
      await _loadMessages();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoadingHistory = false);
    }

    _wsService.connect('${AppConfig.basewsUrl}/chat/ws/${widget.roomId}/${widget.userId}');
    _wsService.messageStream.listen((data) {
      final decoded = jsonDecode(data);
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _messages.insert(0, decoded);
        });
        ChatApiService.markAsRead(widget.roomId, widget.userId);
      }
    });
  }

  Future<void> _loadMessages({String keyword = ""}) async {
    setState(() => _isLoadingHistory = true);
    try {
      final pastMessages = await ChatApiService.getRoomMessages(widget.roomId, widget.userId, keyword: keyword);
      if (mounted) setState(() => _messages = pastMessages);
    } catch (e) {
      // 에러 로직 생략
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoadingHistory = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _wsService.disconnect();
    _msgController.dispose();
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _sendMessage() {
    final text = _msgController.text.trim();
    if (text.isEmpty) return;

    // 답장 여부에 따라 parent_message_id 포함
    final payload = {
      "type": "TEXT",
      "content": text,
    };
    if (_replyingTo != null) {
      payload["parent_message_id"] = _replyingTo!['message_id'];
    }

    _wsService.send(jsonEncode(payload));
    _msgController.clear();
    setState(() => _replyingTo = null);
  }

  void _handleMessageAction(Map<String, dynamic> msg, String action) async {
    if (action == 'reply') {
      setState(() => _replyingTo = msg);
    } else if (action == 'pin') {
      try {
        await ChatApiService.togglePinMessage(widget.roomId, msg['message_id'], widget.userId);
        _loadMessages(); // 상태 리프레시
      } catch (e) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      key: _scaffoldKey,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: _isSearchMode
            ? TextField(
          controller: _searchController,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(hintText: '메시지 검색...', border: InputBorder.none, filled: false),
          style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.textPrimary),
          onSubmitted: (val) => _loadMessages(keyword: val),
        )
            : const Text('채팅방'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            tooltip: '닫기',
            icon: Icon(_isSearchMode ? Icons.close_rounded : Icons.search_rounded),
            onPressed: () {
              setState(() {
                _isSearchMode = !_isSearchMode;
                if (!_isSearchMode) {
                  _searchController.clear();
                  _loadMessages();
                }
              });
            },
          )
        ],
      ),
      endDrawer: Drawer(
        child: Column(
          children: [
            DrawerHeader(
              decoration: BoxDecoration(color: context.colors.primary),
              child: Center(child: Text('참여중인 유저 목록', style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.onPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700))),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: ListView.builder(
                itemCount: _participants.length,
                itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                  final p = _participants[index];
                  final bool isAdmin = p['role'] != 'USER';
                  final String name = p['name'] ?? '';
                  return ListTile(
                    leading: CircleAvatar(
                      backgroundColor: isAdmin ? context.colors.primary : context.colors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                      child: isAdmin
                          ? Icon(Icons.shield_rounded, color: context.colors.onPrimary, size: 18)
                          : Text(
                              name.isNotEmpty ? name.substring(0, 1) : '?',
                              style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.primary, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
                            ),
                    ),
                    title: Text('${p['name']} (${p['role']})'),
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      body: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          ChatRoomHeaderBox(
            roomName: '채팅방 ${widget.roomId}',
            onMenuPressed: () => _scaffoldKey.currentState?.openEndDrawer(),
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: _isLoadingHistory
                ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
                : ChatMessageListBox(
              messages: _messages,
              currentUserId: widget.userId,
              onAction: _handleMessageAction, // 액션 핸들러 연동
            ),
          ),
          ChatInputBox(
            controller: _msgController,
            replyingTo: _replyingTo,
            onSend: _sendMessage,
            onCancelReply: () => setState(() => _replyingTo = null),
          ),
          const SafeArea(child: SizedBox.shrink()),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

■ File : facility_chat_list_page.dart

// 경로: lib/pages/facility/chat/facility_chat_list_page.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/cross_compontents/chat/chat_list_components.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/chat/chat_api_service.dart';

class FacilityChatListPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  final String facilityId;

  const FacilityChatListPage({super.key, required this.userId, required this.facilityId});

  @override
  State<FacilityChatListPage> createState() => _FacilityChatListPageState();
}

class _FacilityChatListPageState extends State<FacilityChatListPage> {
  bool _isAdmin = false;
  bool _isLoading = true;
  List<dynamic> _rooms = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _fetchRooms();
  }

  Future<void> _fetchRooms() async {
    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    try {
      final res = await ChatApiService.getChatRooms(int.parse(widget.facilityId), widget.userId);
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _isAdmin = res['is_admin'];
          _rooms = res['rooms'];
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  void _showCreateGroupDialog() {
    final TextEditingController nameCtrl = TextEditingController();
    final TextEditingController usersCtrl = TextEditingController();

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('단체 채팅방 생성'),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            TextField(controller: nameCtrl, decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: '방 이름')),
            TextField(controller: usersCtrl, decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: '초대할 유저 ID (콤마로 구분)')),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context), child: const Text('취소')),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () async {
              final ids = usersCtrl.text.split(',').map((e) => int.tryParse(e.trim())).whereType<int>().toList();
              if (nameCtrl.text.isNotEmpty && ids.isNotEmpty) {
                Navigator.pop(context);
                await ChatApiService.createGroupChat(widget.userId, nameCtrl.text, ids);
                _fetchRooms();
              }
            },
            child: const Text('생성'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _handleRoomLongPress(int roomId) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => SafeArea(
        child: Wrap(
          children: [
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.exit_to_app, color: Colors.red),
              title: const Text('채팅방 나가기 (관리자는 단체방 삭제 가능)'),
              onTap: () async {
                Navigator.pop(context);
                try {
                  await ChatApiService.leaveOrDeleteRoom(roomId, widget.userId, action: 'delete');
                  _fetchRooms();
                } catch (e) {
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
                }
              },
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('메신저')),
      body: _isLoading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            if (_isAdmin) ...[
              ChatRoomControlBox(onCreateGroup: _showCreateGroupDialog),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
            ],
            ChatRoomListBox(
              rooms: _rooms,
              onRoomTapped: (roomId) => context.push('/chat_room/${widget.userId}/$roomId').then((_) => _fetchRooms()),
              onRoomLongPressed: _handleRoomLongPress,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

■ File : facility_alarm_page.dart
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_app\IDCS\lib\pages\facility\facility_alarm_page.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/indices/main_alarm/facility_alarm_components.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/shared/alarm_row.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/alarm/alarm_api_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';

class FacilityAlarmPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  final String facilityId;

  const FacilityAlarmPage({super.key, required this.userId, required this.facilityId});

  @override
  State<FacilityAlarmPage> createState() => _FacilityAlarmPageState();
}

class _FacilityAlarmPageState extends State<FacilityAlarmPage> {
  bool _isLoading = true;
  List<dynamic> _alarms = [];

  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();
  String _searchQuery = '';

  // 💡 검색 필터 기준 상태 변수 추가 ('name' 또는 'room')
  String _searchTarget = 'name';

  AlarmStatusFilter _statusFilter = AlarmStatusFilter.all;
  bool _grouped = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _fetchUserData();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _fetchUserData() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
    });

    try {
      final alarms = await AlarmApiService.getFacilitiesAlarms(widget.facilityId);
      setState(() {
        _alarms = alarms;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('권한 조회 실패: $e')));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _isLoading = false;
        });
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final searchedAlarms = filterAlarmsByStatus(_alarms, _statusFilter).where((alarm) {
      final query = _searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      if (query.isEmpty) return true;

      if (_searchTarget == 'room') {
        final room = (alarm['room_name'] ?? '').toString().toLowerCase();
        return room.contains(query);
      } else {
        final name = (alarm['alarm_name'] ?? '').toString().toLowerCase();
        return name.contains(query);
      }
    }).toList();

    // 묶어서 보기가 꺼져 있으면 건마다 count 1로 그대로 보여준다.
    final displayAlarms = _grouped
        ? groupAlarms(searchedAlarms)
        : searchedAlarms.map((a) => GroupedAlarm([a])).toList();

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('알람')),
      body: _isLoading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : CustomScrollView(
              slivers: [
                SliverPadding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 8),
                  sliver: SliverToBoxAdapter(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                      children: [
                        // 검색창과 드롭다운을 가로 배치
                        Row(
                          children: [
                            // 1. 검색 입력창
                            Expanded(
                              child: TextField(
                                controller: _searchController,
                                decoration: InputDecoration(
                                  // 선택된 필터 기준에 따라 힌트 문구 변경
                                  hintText: _searchTarget == 'name' ? '알람 명으로 검색...' : '룸 이름으로 검색...',
                                  prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search_rounded, size: 20),
                                  suffixIcon: _searchQuery.isNotEmpty
                                      ? IconButton(
                                        tooltip: '검색어 지우기',
                                          icon: const Icon(Icons.clear_rounded, size: 20),
                                          onPressed: () {
                                            setState(() {
                                              _searchController.clear();
                                              _searchQuery = '';
                                            });
                                          },
                                        )
                                      : null,
                                  isDense: true,
                                ),
                                onChanged: (value) {
                                  setState(() {
                                    _searchQuery = value;
                                  });
                                },
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(width: 8),

                            // 2. 오른쪽 필터 선택 드롭다운
                            Container(
                              height: 52,
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: context.colors.surface,
                                border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                              ),
                              child: DropdownButtonHideUnderline(
                                child: DropdownButton<String>(
                                  value: _searchTarget,
                                  icon: Icon(Icons.expand_more_rounded, size: 20, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                                  isDense: true,
                                  items: const [
                                    DropdownMenuItem(
                                      value: 'name',
                                      child: Text('알람 명', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14)),
                                    ),
                                    DropdownMenuItem(
                                      value: 'room',
                                      child: Text('룸 ID', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14)),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                  onChanged: (value) {
                                    if (value != null) {
                                      setState(() {
                                        _searchTarget = value;
                                      });
                                    }
                                  },
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),

                        AlarmFilterBar(
                          statusFilter: _statusFilter,
                          onStatusChanged: (val) => setState(() => _statusFilter = val),
                          grouped: _grouped,
                          onGroupedChanged: (val) => setState(() => _grouped = val),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                // 3. 필터링된 알람 데이터 전달
                SliverPadding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 16),
                  sliver: FacilityAlarmBox(alarms: displayAlarms, isSearching: _searchQuery.isNotEmpty),
                ),
              ],
            ),
    );
  }
}

■ File : facility_shell_page.dart

======================================================================

// 경로: lib/pages/facility/facility_shell_page.dart
// 명세: 시설 내부 5개 섹션(모니터링/차트/소요기록/메신저/알람)을 위한
//       하단 네비게이션 셸. 각 섹션은 독립된 라우트를 유지하며,
//       세부 화면(채팅방, 게시글 등)으로 이동할 때는 이 셸 위로 풀스크린 push된다.
//
//       go_router의 StatefulShellRoute는 브랜치마다 별도의 내부 Navigator를 두므로,
//       탭 루트 화면(모니터링/차트 등)의 AppBar가 자동으로 만들어주는 뒤로가기는
//       셸 바깥(유저 홈)이 아니라 그 브랜치 내부 스택만 본다 — 즉 셸에 처음 들어오면
//       뒤로 갈 방법이 전혀 없었다. 그래서 셸 자체에 "어느 시설에 들어와 있는지"와
//       "홈으로 돌아가기"를 보여주는 얇은 바를 하나 두고, 실제 라우터 pop을 호출한다.
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/facility_api_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';

class FacilityShellPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  final String facilityId;
  final StatefulNavigationShell navigationShell;

  const FacilityShellPage({
    super.key,
    required this.userId,
    required this.facilityId,
    required this.navigationShell,
  });

  @override
  State<FacilityShellPage> createState() => _FacilityShellPageState();
}

class _FacilityShellPageState extends State<FacilityShellPage> {
  String? _facilityName;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadFacilityName();
  }

  Future<void> _loadFacilityName() async {
    try {
      final facilities = await FacilityApiService.getFacilities(widget.userId);
      final match = facilities.firstWhere(
        (f) => f['id'].toString() == widget.facilityId,
        orElse: () => null,
      );
      if (mounted && match != null) {
        setState(() => _facilityName = match['name']);
      }
    } catch (_) {
      // 이름을 못 불러와도 시설 ID로 대체 표시하므로 별도 에러 처리는 생략
    }
  }

  void _backToHome() {
    if (context.canPop()) {
      context.pop();
    } else {
      context.go('/index/${widget.userId}/home');
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        leading: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back_rounded),
          tooltip: '내 시설 목록으로',
          onPressed: _backToHome,
        ),
        title: Text(_facilityName ?? '시설 ${widget.facilityId}'),
        centerTitle: true,
        elevation: 0,
        backgroundColor: context.colors.surface,
        foregroundColor: context.colors.textPrimary,
      ),
      body: widget.navigationShell,
      bottomNavigationBar: NavigationBar(
        selectedIndex: widget.navigationShell.currentIndex,
        onDestinationSelected: (index) => widget.navigationShell.goBranch(
          index,
          initialLocation: index == widget.navigationShell.currentIndex,
        ),
        destinations: const [
          NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(Icons.sensors_outlined), selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.sensors_rounded), label: '모니터링'),
          NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(Icons.show_chart_rounded), label: '차트'),
          NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(Icons.assignment_outlined), selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.assignment_rounded), label: '소요기록'),
          NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(Icons.chat_bubble_outline_rounded), selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.chat_bubble_rounded), label: '메신저'),
          NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(Icons.notifications_none_rounded), selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.notifications_rounded), label: '알람'),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/facility/monitoring/monitoring.dart';
import 'package:idcs/main.dart';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/web_socket_service.dart';

class MonitoringPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  final String facilityId;
  final String roomId;

  const MonitoringPage({super.key, required this.userId, required this.facilityId, required this.roomId});

  @override
  State<MonitoringPage> createState() => _MonitoringPageState();
}

class _MonitoringPageState extends State<MonitoringPage> {
  // 웹소켓 통신 클래스 인스턴스 생성
  final WebSocketService _webSocketService = WebSocketService();
  StreamSubscription<String>? _wsSubscription; // 스트림 구독 해제를 위한 객체

  // 평평한 1층 딕셔너리 데이터 구조
  late Map<String, String> infoData;
  Map<String, String> realtempData = {'H_COMP': 'NA', '고내온도_#1': 'NA', '설정값_#1': 'NA', '편차값_#1': 'NA', 'ZERO_#1': 'NA'};
  Map<String, String> deforestData = {'제상용 전자변': 'NA', '제상타임_#1': 'NA', '싸이클_#1': 'NA', '시스템분_#1': 'NA', '시스템시간_#1': 'NA'};
  Map<String, String> emergencyData = {'CINT': 'NA', 'HIGH': 'NA', 'UF/OCR': 'NA', '감금알람': 'NA', '토출온도이상': 'NA', '가스부족': 'NA', '온도센서이상': 'NA', '역상방지이상': 'NA', 'LOW 스위치': 'NA', '과열TIC': 'NA', 'ARLAM': 'NA', 'RESET': 'NA'};
  Map<String, String> inverterData = {'인버터 에러': 'NA', 'Hz_#1': 'NA', 'KW_#1': 'NA', '출력전압_#1': 'NA', '출력전류_#1': 'NA', '출력주파수_#1': 'NA'};
  Map<String, String> equipmentData = {'토출온도_#1': 'NA', '고압_#1': 'NA', '저압_#1': 'NA'};
  Map<String, String> controlData = {'운전SW': 'NA', '룸온도스위치': 'NA', '방열기 팬 #1': 'NA', '방열기 팬 #2': 'NA', '액 전자변': 'NA', '전자변': 'NA', '쿨러 팬': 'NA', '아날로그TC': 'NA', '쿨러 팬 자동여부': 'NA', '히터정지': 'NA', '초기지연': 'NA', '콤프지연': 'NA', '쿨러지연': 'NA', '수동제상': 'NA', '컴퓨터 S/W': 'NA', '운전시간_#1': 'NA', '운전시간만_#1': 'NA', '초기지연_#1': 'NA', 'H_콤프지연_#1': 'NA', '쿨러지연타임_#1': 'NA', 'DF 지연_#1': 'NA'};
  static const Map<String, String> deviceKeyMap = {
    'control_runsw': '운전SW',
    'emergency_cint': 'CINT',
    'emergency_high': 'HIGH',
    'emergency_uf_ocr': 'UF/OCR',
    'emergency_locked': '감금알람',
    'emergency_outtemp': '토출온도이상',
    'emergency_gas': '가스부족',
    'emergency_tempsensor': '온도센서이상',
    'emergency_defemg': '역상방지이상',
    'emergency_lowsw': 'LOW 스위치',
    'emergency_hottic': '과열TIC',
    'control_roomtemp': '룸온도스위치',
    'temp_comp': 'H_COMP',
    'control_cffan1': '방열기 팬 #1',
    'control_cffan2': '방열기 팬 #2',
    'control_lsv': '액 전자변',
    'control_ingsv': '전자변',
    'control_uffan': '쿨러 팬',
    'deforest_def': '제상용 전자변',
    'emergency_emg': 'ARLAM',
    'control_tica': '아날로그TC',
    'control_efa': '쿨러 팬 자동여부',
    'control_heatdelflag': '히터정지',
    'control_initdelflag': '초기지연',
    'control_compdelflag': '콤프지연',
    'control_coolerdelflag': '쿨러지연',
    'control_mdf': '수동제상',
    'control_sw': '컴퓨터 S/W',
    'emergency_emgreset': 'RESET',
    'inverter_error': '인버터 에러',
    'equipment_temp1': '토출온도_#1',
    'temp_pv': '고내온도_#1',
    'temp_sv': '설정값_#1',
    'temp_hey': '편차값_#1',
    'temp_zero': 'ZERO_#1',
    'equipment_press1': '고압_#1',
    'equipment_press2': '저압_#1',
    'control_runtimelow': '운전시간_#1',
    'control_runtimehigh': '운전시간만_#1',
    'deforest_dfcm': '제상타임_#1',
    'deforest_dfct': '싸이클_#1',
    'deforest_sysm': '시스템분_#1',
    'deforest_syst': '시스템시간_#1',
    'control_initdelst': '초기지연_#1',
    'control_compdelst': 'H_콤프지연_#1',
    'control_cooldelst': '쿨러지연타임_#1',
    'control_defdelst': 'DF 지연_#1',
    'control_initdeltm': '초기지연_#1',
    'control_compdeltm': '콤프지연_#1',
    'control_cooldeltm': '쿨러지연타임_#1',
    'control_defdeltm': 'DF 지연_#1',
    'inverter_hz': 'Hz_#1',
    'inverter_kw': 'KW_#1',
    'inverter_outv': '출력전압_#1',
    'inverter_outa': '출력전류_#1',
    'inverter_outhz': '출력주파수_#1',
  };
  static final Map<String, String> reverseDeviceKeyMap = Map.fromEntries(
    deviceKeyMap.entries.map((entry) => MapEntry(entry.value, entry.key)),
  );

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    infoData = {
      '시설 ID': widget.facilityId,
      '호실 ID': widget.roomId,
      '담당 관리자 ID': widget.userId,
      '운영 상태': '정상 작동 중 (ACTIVE)',
    };

    // 💡 [핵심] 페이지 진입 시 웹소켓 서비스 연결 및 스트림 구독 시작
    _webSocketService.connect('${AppConfig.basewsUrl}/facilities/data/get/${widget.facilityId}');

    // 서비스 내부에서 데이터가 들어올 때마다 매핑 함수를 작동시킴
    _wsSubscription = _webSocketService.messageStream.listen((rawJson) {
      _parseAndMapDeviceMessage(rawJson);
    });
  }

  // 통신 클래스로부터 넘겨받은 날것의 데이터를 매핑하는 비즈니스 로직
  void _parseAndMapDeviceMessage(String rawJsonString) {
    try {
      final Map<String, dynamic> parsedJson = jsonDecode(rawJsonString);
      final Map<String, dynamic> parsedSJson = parsedJson['datas'];

      setState(() {
        for (var entry in parsedSJson.entries) {
          // 방법 1 적용 예시 (키 조립)
          final List<String> parts = entry.key.split('.');

          if(int.parse(widget.roomId) != int.parse(parts[0]))
            continue;

          final String? appKey = deviceKeyMap[parts[1]];

          if (appKey != null) {
            final String newValue = entry.value.toString();
            if (realtempData.containsKey(appKey)) {
              realtempData[appKey] = newValue;
            } else if(deforestData.containsKey(appKey)) {
              deforestData[appKey] = newValue;
            } else if(emergencyData.containsKey(appKey)) {
              emergencyData[appKey] = newValue;
            } else if(inverterData.containsKey(appKey)) {
              inverterData[appKey] = newValue;
            } else if(equipmentData.containsKey(appKey)) {
              equipmentData[appKey] = newValue;
            } else if(controlData.containsKey(appKey)) {
              controlData[appKey] = newValue;
            }
          }
        }
      });
    } catch (e) {
      print('에러: $e');
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    // 💡 매우 중요: 메모리 누수 방지를 위해 페이지를 나갈 때 스트림 구독을 해제하고 통신을 닫습니다.
    _wsSubscription?.cancel();
    _webSocketService.disconnect();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void remoteClickEvent(String label, String input) {// 1. 한글 라벨을 원본 장비 키(예: 'control_runsw')로 변환
    final String? rawKey = reverseDeviceKeyMap[label];

    if (rawKey == null) {
      print('매핑할 수 없는 키입니다: $label');
      return;
    }

    // 2. 서버 형식에 맞게 req_id 생성 (예: "1.control_runsw")
    final String reqId = '${widget.roomId}.$rawKey';

    Map<String, String> temp = {
      'type': "WR",
      'req_id': reqId,
      'req_val': input
    };
    if(_webSocketService.isConnected) {
      print(jsonEncode(temp));
      _webSocketService.send(jsonEncode(temp));
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text('시설 ID: ${widget.facilityId} 실시간 제어판'),
        centerTitle: true,
      ),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            MonitoringCard(title: '시설 기본 정보', fields: infoData),
            MonitoringCard(title: '실제 온도', fields: realtempData, OnConfirm: remoteClickEvent),
            MonitoringCard(title: '제상 정보', fields: deforestData, OnConfirm: remoteClickEvent),
            MonitoringCard(title: '이상 정보', fields: emergencyData, OnConfirm: remoteClickEvent),
            MonitoringCard(title: '인버터 정보', fields: inverterData, OnConfirm: remoteClickEvent),
            MonitoringCard(title: '장비 정보', fields: equipmentData, OnConfirm: remoteClickEvent),
            MonitoringCard(title: '제어 정보', fields: controlData, OnConfirm: remoteClickEvent),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

■ File : monitoring_room.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/facility_api_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/indices/main_index/main_index_components.dart';

class MonitoringRoom extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  final String facilityId;

  const MonitoringRoom({super.key, required this.userId, required this.facilityId});

  @override
  State<MonitoringRoom> createState() => _MonitoringRoomPage();
}

class _MonitoringRoomPage extends State<MonitoringRoom> {
  bool _isLoading = true;
  List<dynamic> _rooms = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _fetchFacilityData();
  }

  // 1. 유저 데이터(권한 등)를 불러오는 초기화 함수
  Future<void> _fetchFacilityData() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true; // 로딩 상태 활성화
    });

    try {
      final rooms = await FacilityApiService.getFacilityRooms(widget.facilityId);
      setState(() {
        _rooms = rooms;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text('권한 조회 실패: $e')),
      );
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _isLoading = false; // 로딩 상태 해제
        });
      }
    }
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('호실 선택'), centerTitle: true),
      body: _isLoading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : SingleChildScrollView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  IntegratedFacilityBox(
                    label: '호실 목록',
                    emptyMessage: '등록된 호실이 없습니다.',
                    facilities: _rooms,
                    onFacilityPressed: (roomId) {
                      // 터치 시 호실 상세 제어판으로 이동
                      context.push('/facility_monitoring/${widget.userId}/${widget.facilityId}/$roomId');
                    },
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
    );
  }
}

■ File : account_edit_page.dart
