import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/indices/account_edit/account_edit_components.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/user_api_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/theme_controller.dart';
import 'package:idcs/main.dart'; // AuthService 접근용

class PersonalPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;

  const PersonalPage({super.key, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<PersonalPage> createState() => _PersonalPageState();
}

class _PersonalPageState extends State<PersonalPage> {
  final TextEditingController _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _phoneController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _currentPwController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _newPwController = TextEditingController();

  bool _isAdmin = false;
  bool _isLoading = true;
  List<dynamic> _linkedProviders = [];

  /// 소셜로만 가입한 계정은 비밀번호가 없어 변경 UI를 숨긴다.
  bool _hasPassword = true;
  String _pwCollapseMessage = '';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadInitialData();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _phoneController.dispose();
    _currentPwController.dispose();
    _newPwController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _loadInitialData() async {
    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    try {
      // 권한 정보 및 기본 프로필 조회
      final role = await UserApiService.getUserRole(widget.userId);
      final profile = await UserApiService.getUserProfile(widget.userId);

      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _isAdmin = (role == 'ADMIN' || role == 'DEVELOPER');
          _nameController.text = profile['name'] ?? '';
          _phoneController.text = profile['phone_number'] ?? '';
          _linkedProviders = profile['linked_providers'] ?? [];
          _hasPassword = profile['has_password'] ?? true;
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handleUpdateName() async {
    try {
      final msg = await UserApiService.updateName(widget.userId, _nameController.text.trim());
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {}); // 상단 프로필 카드에 바뀐 이름을 바로 반영
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(msg)));
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handleUpdatePhone() async {
    try {
      final msg = await UserApiService.updatePhone(widget.userId, _phoneController.text.trim());
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(msg)));
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handleUpdatePassword() async {
    final currentPw = _currentPwController.text.trim();
    final newPw = _newPwController.text.trim();

    if (currentPw.isEmpty || newPw.isEmpty) {
      setState(() => _pwCollapseMessage = '비밀번호를 모두 입력해주세요.');
      return;
    }
    if (currentPw == newPw) {
      setState(() => _pwCollapseMessage = '새 비밀번호가 기존 비밀번호와 동일합니다.');
      return;
    }

    try {
      final msg = await UserApiService.updatePassword(widget.userId, currentPw, newPw);
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _pwCollapseMessage = '성공적으로 변경되었습니다.';
        _currentPwController.clear();
        _newPwController.clear();
      });
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(msg)));
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() => _pwCollapseMessage = '오류: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handleLogout() async {
    await AuthService.setUserId(null);
    if (!mounted) return;
    context.go('/login');
  }

  Future<void> _handleWithdraw() async {
    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (dialogContext) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('정말 탈퇴하시겠습니까?'),
        content: const Text('계정 정보와 시설 소속 정보가 모두 삭제되며 되돌릴 수 없습니다.'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialogContext, false), child: const Text('취소')),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialogContext, true),
            style: TextButton.styleFrom(foregroundColor: context.colors.error),
            child: const Text('탈퇴하기'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    if (confirmed != true) return;

    try {
      final msg = await UserApiService.withdraw(widget.userId);
      await AuthService.setUserId(null);
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(msg)));
      context.go('/login');
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handleSocialLink(String provider) async {
    if (_linkedProviders.contains(provider)) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('이미 $provider 계정과 연동되어 있습니다.')));
      return;
    }
    // TODO: 소셜 로그인 SDK 호출 후 연동 API(AuthApiService.linkSocial 등) 트리거 로직 작성
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$provider 연동은 백엔드 /link-social API로 연결해야 합니다.')));
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('내 정보')),
      body: _isLoading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            // 계정 요약 + 로그아웃
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: context.colors.surface,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Container(
                    width: 42,
                    height: 42,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: context.colors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    ),
                    alignment: Alignment.center,
                    child: Icon(Icons.person_rounded, color: context.colors.primary, size: 22),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          _nameController.text.isEmpty ? '이름 없음' : _nameController.text,
                          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 15, color: context.colors.textPrimary),
                        ),
                        Text('ID ${widget.userId}', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.textSecondary)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  TextButton(onPressed: _handleLogout, child: const Text('로그아웃')),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            if (_isAdmin) ...[
              _AdminLinkCard(
                title: '통합 소요 기록',
                icon: Icons.assignment_outlined,
                onPressed: () => context.push('/total_board/${widget.userId}'),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
            ],

            // Box #2
            UsernameEditBox(
              controller: _nameController,
              onSubmit: _handleUpdateName,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            // Box #3
            PhoneEditBox(
              controller: _phoneController,
              onSubmit: _handleUpdatePhone,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            // Box #4 — 소셜로만 가입한 계정은 비밀번호 자체가 없으므로 숨긴다
            if (_hasPassword) ...[
              PasswordEditBox(
                currentPwController: _currentPwController,
                newPwController: _newPwController,
                collapseMessage: _pwCollapseMessage,
                onSubmit: _handleUpdatePassword,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
            ],

            // Box #5
            SocialLinkBox(
              linkedProviders: _linkedProviders,
              onLinkPressed: _handleSocialLink,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            ValueListenableBuilder<ThemeMode>(
              valueListenable: ThemeController.mode,
              builder: (context, mode, _) => ThemeModeBox(
                current: mode,
                onChanged: ThemeController.set,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            WithdrawBox(onWithdraw: _handleWithdraw),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// 관리자 전용 화면으로 이동하는 카드 (평소엔 탭이 아니라 여기서만 노출)
class _AdminLinkCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final IconData icon;
  final VoidCallback onPressed;

  const _AdminLinkCard({required this.title, required this.icon, required this.onPressed});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Material(
      color: context.colors.surface,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
      child: InkWell(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        onTap: onPressed,
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 14),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
            border: Border.all(color: context.colors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
          ),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Icon(icon, color: context.colors.primary, size: 20),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(title, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 14, color: context.colors.textPrimary)),
              ),
              Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded, color: context.colors.textSecondary, size: 20),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ===============================================================
// 경로: lib/pages/indices/alarm_settings_page.dart
// 명세: 알람이 어떻게 울릴지(소리/진동/잠금화면/다시 알림) 설정하는 화면
// ===============================================================
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/alarm/alarm_settings.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';

class AlarmSettingsPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const AlarmSettingsPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<AlarmSettingsPage> createState() => _AlarmSettingsPageState();
}

class _AlarmSettingsPageState extends State<AlarmSettingsPage> {
  AlarmSettings _settings = const AlarmSettings();
  bool _isLoading = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    final loaded = await AlarmSettings.load();
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _settings = loaded;
      _isLoading = false;
    });
  }

  Future<void> _update(AlarmSettings next) async {
    setState(() => _settings = next);
    await next.save();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('알람 설정')),
      body: _isLoading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              children: [
                _SettingsCard(
                  title: '알림 방식',
                  children: [
                    SwitchListTile(
                      contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                      title: const Text('소리'),
                      subtitle: const Text('알람이 올 때 소리로 알립니다.'),
                      value: _settings.sound,
                      onChanged: (val) => _update(_settings.copyWith(sound: val)),
                    ),
                    SwitchListTile(
                      contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                      title: const Text('진동'),
                      subtitle: const Text('알람이 올 때 진동으로 알립니다.'),
                      value: _settings.vibration,
                      onChanged: (val) => _update(_settings.copyWith(vibration: val)),
                    ),
                    SwitchListTile(
                      contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                      title: const Text('잠금화면에 내용 표시'),
                      subtitle: const Text('꺼두면 잠금화면에서는 알람이 왔다는 사실만 보입니다.'),
                      value: _settings.showOnLockScreen,
                      onChanged: (val) => _update(_settings.copyWith(showOnLockScreen: val)),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                _SettingsCard(
                  title: '다시 알림',
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      '알람을 확인하지 못했을 때 설정한 간격으로 최대 '
                      '${AlarmSettings.maxRepeatCount}번까지 다시 알려줍니다.',
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    Wrap(
                      spacing: 8,
                      children: AlarmSettings.repeatOptions.map((minutes) {
                        final selected = _settings.repeatMinutes == minutes;
                        return ChoiceChip(
                          label: Text(minutes == 0 ? '없음' : '$minutes분'),
                          selected: selected,
                          onSelected: (_) => _update(_settings.copyWith(repeatMinutes: minutes)),
                          labelStyle: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 13,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                            color: selected ? context.colors.onPrimary : context.colors.textPrimary,
                          ),
                          selectedColor: context.colors.primary,
                          backgroundColor: context.colors.background,
                          side: BorderSide(color: selected ? context.colors.primary : context.colors.outline),
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                        );
                      }).toList(),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                Text(
                  '이 설정은 이 기기에만 적용됩니다.',
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                ),
              ],
            ),
    );
  }
}

class _SettingsCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final List<Widget> children;

  const _SettingsCard({required this.title, required this.children});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: context.colors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Text(
            title,
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          ...children,
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

■ File : facility_register_page.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/indices/facility_register/facility_register.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/user_api_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/facility_api_service.dart';

class FacilityRegisterPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  const FacilityRegisterPage({super.key, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<FacilityRegisterPage> createState() => _FacilityRegisterPageState();
}

class _FacilityRegisterPageState extends State<FacilityRegisterPage> {
  final TextEditingController _codeController = TextEditingController();
  bool _isAdmin = false;
  bool _isLoading = true;
  String _collapseMessage = '';
  List<dynamic> _facilities = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadData();
  }

  Future<void> _loadData() async {
    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    try {
      final role = await UserApiService.getUserRole(widget.userId);
      _isAdmin = (role == 'ADMIN' || role == 'DEVELOPER');

      // ADMIN은 애초에 이 페이지 접근할 필요 없지만 들어왔을 경우 방어
      if (!_isAdmin) {
        _facilities = await FacilityApiService.getFacilities(widget.userId);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handleRegister() async {
    final code = _codeController.text.trim();
    if (code.isEmpty) return;

    try {
      final msg = await FacilityApiService.registerFacility(widget.userId, code);
      setState(() {
        _collapseMessage = msg;
        _codeController.clear();
      });
      await _loadData(); // 목록 새로고침
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() => _collapseMessage = '등록 실패: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _handleUnlink(int facilityId) async {
    try {
      final msg = await FacilityApiService.unlinkFacility(widget.userId, facilityId);
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(msg)));
      await _loadData();
    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(e.toString())));
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_isAdmin) {
      return Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('시설 등록 페이지')),
        body: const Center(child: Text('관리자 및 개발자는 시설 등록이 필요하지 않습니다.')),
      );
    }

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('시설 등록 페이지')),
      body: _isLoading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            FacilityInputBox(
              codeController: _codeController, onSubmit: _handleRegister, collapseMessage: _collapseMessage,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            FacilityListEditBox(
              facilities: _facilities, onUnlink: _handleUnlink,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

■ File : main_alarm_page.dart
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_app\IDCS\lib\pages\indices\main_alarm_page.dart
======================================================================

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/indices/main_alarm/user_alarm_components.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/shared/alarm_row.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/alarm/alarm_api_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/theme/app_theme.dart';

class MainAlarmPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  const MainAlarmPage({super.key, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<MainAlarmPage> createState() => _MainAlarmPageState();
}

class _MainAlarmPageState extends State<MainAlarmPage> {
  bool _isLoading = true;
  List<dynamic> _alarms = [];

  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();
  String _searchQuery = '';

  // 💡 검색 대상 상태 변수 ('name', 'room', 'facility')
  String _searchTarget = 'name';

  AlarmStatusFilter _statusFilter = AlarmStatusFilter.all;
  bool _grouped = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _fetchUserData();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _fetchUserData() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
    });

    try {
      final alarms = await AlarmApiService.getUserAlarms(widget.userId);
      setState(() {
        _alarms = alarms;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('권한 조회 실패: $e')));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _isLoading = false;
        });
      }
    }
  }

  void onPressed(int facilityId) {
    context.push('/facility/${widget.userId}/$facilityId/monitoring');
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // 💡 선택된 검색 기준(_searchTarget)별 필터링 조건
    final searchedAlarms = filterAlarmsByStatus(_alarms, _statusFilter).where((alarm) {
      final query = _searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      if (query.isEmpty) return true;

      if (_searchTarget == 'facility') {
        final facility = (alarm['facility_name'] ?? '').toString().toLowerCase();
        return facility.contains(query);
      } else if (_searchTarget == 'room') {
        final room = (alarm['room_name'] ?? '').toString().toLowerCase();
        return room.contains(query);
      } else {
        final name = (alarm['alarm_name'] ?? '').toString().toLowerCase();
        return name.contains(query);
      }
    }).toList();

    // 묶어서 보기가 꺼져 있으면 건마다 count 1로 그대로 보여준다.
    final displayAlarms = _grouped
        ? groupAlarms(searchedAlarms)
        : searchedAlarms.map((a) => GroupedAlarm([a])).toList();

    // 선택된 필터 기준에 따른 힌트 문구
    String getHintText() {
      switch (_searchTarget) {
        case 'facility':
          return '시설 이름으로 검색...';
        case 'room':
          return '룸 이름으로 검색...';
        case 'name':
        default:
          return '알람 이름으로 검색...';
      }
    }

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('전체 알람'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.settings_outlined),
            tooltip: '알람 설정',
            onPressed: () => context.push('/alarm_settings'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: _isLoading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : CustomScrollView(
              slivers: [
                SliverPadding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 8),
                  sliver: SliverToBoxAdapter(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                      children: [
                        // 검색창 + 필터 드롭다운 레이아웃
                        Row(
                          children: [
                            Expanded(
                              child: TextField(
                                controller: _searchController,
                                decoration: InputDecoration(
                                  hintText: getHintText(),
                                  prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search_rounded, size: 20),
                                  suffixIcon: _searchQuery.isNotEmpty
                                      ? IconButton(
                                        tooltip: '검색어 지우기',
                                          icon: const Icon(Icons.clear_rounded, size: 20),
                                          onPressed: () {
                                            setState(() {
                                              _searchController.clear();
                                              _searchQuery = '';
                                            });
                                          },
                                        )
                                      : null,
                                  isDense: true,
                                ),
                                onChanged: (value) {
                                  setState(() {
                                    _searchQuery = value;
                                  });
                                },
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(width: 8),

                            // 필터 옵션 드롭다운 (알람 명 / 룸 이름 / 시설 이름)
                            Container(
                              height: 52,
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: context.colors.surface,
                                border: Border.all(color: context.colors.outline),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                              ),
                              child: DropdownButtonHideUnderline(
                                child: DropdownButton<String>(
                                  value: _searchTarget,
                                  icon: Icon(Icons.expand_more_rounded, size: 20, color: context.colors.textSecondary),
                                  isDense: true,
                                  items: const [
                                    DropdownMenuItem(
                                      value: 'name',
                                      child: Text('알람 명', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14)),
                                    ),
                                    DropdownMenuItem(
                                      value: 'room',
                                      child: Text('룸 이름', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14)),
                                    ),
                                    DropdownMenuItem(
                                      value: 'facility',
                                      child: Text('시설 이름', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14)),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                  onChanged: (value) {
                                    if (value != null) {
                                      setState(() {
                                        _searchTarget = value;
                                      });
                                    }
                                  },
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),

                        AlarmFilterBar(
                          statusFilter: _statusFilter,
                          onStatusChanged: (val) => setState(() => _statusFilter = val),
                          grouped: _grouped,
                          onGroupedChanged: (val) => setState(() => _grouped = val),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                // Box #2: 통합 알람 박스
                SliverPadding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 16),
                  sliver: UserAlarmBox(
                    alarms: displayAlarms,
                    onFacilityPressed: onPressed,
                    isSearching: _searchQuery.isNotEmpty,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/indices/main_index/main_index_components.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/facility_api_service.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/common/user_api_service.dart';

class MainIndexPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;

  const MainIndexPage({super.key, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<MainIndexPage> createState() => _MainIndexPageState();
}

class _MainIndexPageState extends State<MainIndexPage> {
  bool _isAdmin = false;
  bool _isLoading = true;
  List<dynamic> _facilities = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _fetchUserData();
  }

  // 1. 유저 데이터(권한 등)를 불러오는 초기화 함수
  Future<void> _fetchUserData() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true; // 로딩 상태 활성화
    });

    try {
      final role = await UserApiService.getUserRole(widget.userId);
      final facilities = await FacilityApiService.getFacilities(widget.userId);
      setState(() {
        // ADMIN이거나 DEVELOPER인 경우 관리자 박스 노출
        _isAdmin = (role == 'ADMIN' || role == 'DEVELOPER');
        _facilities = facilities;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text('권한 조회 실패: $e')),
      );
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _isLoading = false; // 로딩 상태 해제
        });
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('내 시설'),
        actions: [
          // 관리자는 웹 콘솔에서 시설을 관리하므로, 코드 등록은 일반 유저에게만 노출
          if (!_isAdmin)
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.add_business_outlined),
              tooltip: '시설 등록',
              onPressed: () => context.push('/facility_register/${widget.userId}'),
            ),
        ],
      ),
      body: _isLoading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()) // 데이터 로딩 중 처리
          : RefreshIndicator(
              onRefresh: _fetchUserData,
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                child: IntegratedFacilityBox(
                  facilities: _facilities,
                  onFacilityPressed: (facilityId) {
                    // 터치 시 시설 라우팅용 nav 페이지로 이동
                    context.push('/facility/${widget.userId}/$facilityId/monitoring');
                  },
                ),
              ),
            ),
    );
  }
}

// ===============================================================
// 경로: lib/pages/indices/total_board_page.dart
// 명세: 통합 소요 기록 페이지 (모든 시설 포스트 조회)
// ===============================================================
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:idcs/models/post_model.dart';
import 'package:idcs/components/cross_compontents/board/shared_board_compontents.dart';
import 'package:idcs/services/board/board_api_service.dart';

class TotalBoardPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  const TotalBoardPage({super.key, required this.userId});

  @override
  State<TotalBoardPage> createState() => _TotalBoardPageState();
}

class _TotalBoardPageState extends State<TotalBoardPage> {
  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();

  // 📌 1. 검색 필터 선택 상태값 추가
  String _selectedSearchType = 'all';

  List<PostModel> _posts = [];
  bool _isLoading = true;
  int _currentPage = 1;
  int _totalPages = 1;
  final int _limit = 10;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _fetchPosts();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  // 📌 2. BoardApiService 적용 및 검색 파라미터 전달
  Future<void> _fetchPosts() async {
    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    try {
      final result = await BoardApiService.fetchIntegratedPosts(
        userId: widget.userId,
        page: _currentPage,
        limit: _limit,
        searchType: _selectedSearchType,
        keyword: _searchController.text,
      );

      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _posts = result['posts'];
          _totalPages = result['totalPages'];
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$e')));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  void _handlePageChange(int newPage) {
    setState(() => _currentPage = newPage);
    _fetchPosts();
  }

  void _handlePostTapped(int postId) {
    context.push('/post_detail/${widget.userId}/$postId').then((_) => _fetchPosts());
  }

  void _handleSearch() {
    _currentPage = 1;
    _fetchPosts();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('통합 소요 기록 (관리자)')),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            // 📌 3. 필수 전달 인자 누락 에러 수정 부분
            BoardHeaderBox(
              title: '통합 검색 및 필터',
              searchController: _searchController,
              selectedSearchType: _selectedSearchType,
              onSearchTypeChanged: (val) {
                if (val != null) setState(() => _selectedSearchType = val);
              },
              onSearch: _handleSearch,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            // Box #2 & #3
            BoardListAndPagingBox(
              posts: _posts,
              isLoading: _isLoading,
              currentPage: _currentPage,
              totalPages: _totalPages,
              onPageChanged: _handlePageChange,
              onPostTapped: _handlePostTapped,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// 경로: lib/pages/indices/user_shell_page.dart
// 명세: 유저(시설 미선택) 레벨 3개 섹션(홈/알람/내 정보)을 위한 하단 네비게이션 셸.
//       각 탭 화면 안에 반복해서 내비게이션 목록을 넣던 기존 방식(GlobalNavigationBox)을
//       대체한다. 시설 등록/통합 소요기록처럼 자주 쓰지 않는 화면은 탭이 아니라
//       해당 탭 콘텐츠 안의 버튼으로 진입하는 풀스크린 push 화면으로 유지한다.
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

class UserShellPage extends StatelessWidget {
  final StatefulNavigationShell navigationShell;

  const UserShellPage({super.key, required this.navigationShell});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: navigationShell,
      bottomNavigationBar: NavigationBar(
        selectedIndex: navigationShell.currentIndex,
        onDestinationSelected: (index) => navigationShell.goBranch(
          index,
          initialLocation: index == navigationShell.currentIndex,
        ),
        destinations: const [
          NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(Icons.dashboard_outlined), selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.dashboard_rounded), label: '홈'),
          NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(Icons.notifications_none_rounded), selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.notifications_rounded), label: '알람'),
          NavigationDestination(icon: Icon(Icons.person_outline_rounded), selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.person_rounded), label: '내 정보'),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// 경로: lib/routes.dart
part of 'main.dart';

final GoRouter appRouter = GoRouter(
  initialLocation: '/',
  errorBuilder: (context, state) => const NotFoundScreen(),

  redirect: (context, state) {
    final bool loggedIn = AuthService.isLoggedIn;
    final String path = state.matchedLocation;

    final allowedPaths = ['/', '/login', '/signup', '/find_id', '/find_pw'];

    if (!loggedIn && !allowedPaths.contains(path)) {
      return '/login';
    }

    // 로그인이 유지된 상태면 스플래시/로그인 화면을 건너뛰고 바로 홈으로 보낸다.
    if (loggedIn && (path == '/' || path == '/login')) {
      return '/index/${AuthService.getCurrentUserId()}/home';
    }
    return null;
  },


  routes: [
    // [common]
    GoRoute(path: '/', builder: (context, state) => const OpeningPage()),
    GoRoute(path: '/login', builder: (context, state) => const LoginPage()),
    GoRoute(path: '/signup', builder: (context, state) => const SignupPage()),
    GoRoute(path: '/find_id', builder: (context, state) => const FindIdPage()),
    GoRoute(path: '/find_pw', builder: (context, state) => const FindPwPage()),
    // GoRoute(path: '/reset_pw', builder: (context, state) => const ResetPwPage()),

    // [index] - 유저 레벨 3개 섹션(홈/알람/내 정보) 하단 네비게이션 셸.
    // 시설 셸과 동일한 이유로 상위 라우트가 :userId를 소유하고 브랜치는 상대 경로만 갖는다.
    GoRoute(
      path: '/index/:userId',
      redirect: (context, state) => state.matchedLocation == state.uri.toString()
          ? '${state.matchedLocation}/home'
          : null,
      routes: [
        StatefulShellRoute.indexedStack(
          builder: (context, state, navigationShell) => UserShellPage(navigationShell: navigationShell),
          branches: [
            StatefulShellBranch(routes: [
              GoRoute(
                path: 'home',
                builder: (context, state) => MainIndexPage(userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!),
              ),
            ]),
            StatefulShellBranch(routes: [
              GoRoute(
                path: 'alarm',
                builder: (context, state) => MainAlarmPage(userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!),
              ),
            ]),
            StatefulShellBranch(routes: [
              GoRoute(
                path: 'profile',
                builder: (context, state) => PersonalPage(userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!),
              ),
            ]),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    ),

    // 자주 쓰지 않는 화면 - 셸 위로 풀스크린 push (시설 등록: 관리자 제외, 통합 소요기록: 관리자 전용)
    GoRoute(path: '/facility_register/:userId', builder: (context, state) => FacilityRegisterPage(userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!)),
    GoRoute(
      path: '/total_board/:userId',
      builder: (context, state) => TotalBoardPage(
        userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!,
      ),
    ),
    GoRoute(path: '/alarm_settings', builder: (context, state) => const AlarmSettingsPage()),

    // [facility] - 유저 ID & 시설 ID 기반
    // 시설 내부 5개 섹션(모니터링/차트/소요기록/메신저/알람) 하단 네비게이션 셸.
    // go_router 제약상 브랜치 라우트 자체는 파라미터를 가질 수 없으므로,
    // userId/facilityId는 상위 라우트가 소유하고 브랜치는 상대 경로(monitoring, chart 등)만 갖는다.
    GoRoute(
      path: '/facility/:userId/:facilityId',
      // 하위 라우트(monitoring/chart/board/chat/alarm) 없이 이 경로만 단독으로
      // 방문되는 경우는 없으므로(항상 구체적 탭 경로로 이동) 별도 redirect는 두지 않는다.
      redirect: (context, state) => state.matchedLocation == state.uri.toString()
          ? '${state.matchedLocation}/monitoring'
          : null,
      routes: [
        StatefulShellRoute.indexedStack(
          builder: (context, state, navigationShell) => FacilityShellPage(
            userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!,
            facilityId: state.pathParameters['facilityId']!,
            navigationShell: navigationShell,
          ),
          branches: [
            StatefulShellBranch(routes: [
              GoRoute(
                path: 'monitoring',
                builder: (context, state) => MonitoringRoom(
                  userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!,
                  facilityId: state.pathParameters['facilityId']!,
                ),
              ),
            ]),
            StatefulShellBranch(routes: [
              GoRoute(
                path: 'chart',
                builder: (context, state) => FacilityChartPage(
                  userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!,
                  facilityId: state.pathParameters['facilityId']!,
                ),
              ),
            ]),
            StatefulShellBranch(routes: [
              GoRoute(
                path: 'board',
                builder: (context, state) => FacilityBoardPage(
                  userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!,
                  facilityId: state.pathParameters['facilityId']!,
                ),
              ),
            ]),
            StatefulShellBranch(routes: [
              GoRoute(
                path: 'chat',
                builder: (context, state) => FacilityChatListPage(
                  userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!,
                  facilityId: state.pathParameters['facilityId']!,
                ),
              ),
            ]),
            StatefulShellBranch(routes: [
              GoRoute(
                path: 'alarm',
                builder: (context, state) => FacilityAlarmPage(
                  userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!,
                  facilityId: state.pathParameters['facilityId']!,
                ),
              ),
            ]),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    ),

    // 1. 게시글 상세 페이지
    GoRoute(
      path: '/post_detail/:userId/:postId',
      builder: (context, state) => PostDetailPage(
        userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!,
        postId: int.parse(state.pathParameters['postId']!),
      ),
    ),

    // 3. 게시글 작성 페이지
    GoRoute(
      path: '/post_create/:userId/:facilityId',
      builder: (context, state) => PostCreatePage(
        userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!,
        facilityId: state.pathParameters['facilityId']!,
      ),
    ),

    // 4. 게시글 수정 페이지
    GoRoute(
      path: '/post_edit/:userId/:postId',
      builder: (context, state) => PostEditPage(
        userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!,
        postId: int.parse(state.pathParameters['postId']!),
      ),
    ),

    //여기서부터는 모니터링 관련
    //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // [facility_monitoring] - 호실 상세(라이브 제어판). 셸 위로 풀스크린 push.
    GoRoute(
      path: '/facility_monitoring/:userId/:facilityId/:roomId',
      builder: (context, state) => MonitoringPage(
        userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!,
        facilityId: state.pathParameters['facilityId']!,
        roomId: state.pathParameters['roomId']!,
      ),
    ),
    //모니터링 끝-------------------------------------------------------------------

    // [chat_room] - 채팅방 상세. 셸 위로 풀스크린 push.
    GoRoute(
      path: '/chat_room/:userId/:roomId',
      builder: (context, state) => ChatRoomPage(
        userId: state.pathParameters['userId']!,
        roomId: int.parse(state.pathParameters['roomId']!),
      ),
    ),

  ],
);

// ===============================================================
// 경로: lib/services/board/board_api_service.dart
// 명세: 게시판(통합/시설/상세/작성/수정/댓글) 전용 API 서비스
// ===============================================================
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:idcs/main.dart'; // AppConfig
import 'package:idcs/models/post_model.dart';

class BoardApiService {
  static String get _baseUrl => '${AppConfig.baseUrl}/board';

  /// 1. 시설 게시판 목록 조회
  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> fetchFacilityPosts({
    required String facilityId,
    required String userId,
    required int page,
    required int limit,
    required String searchType,
    required String keyword,
  }) async {
    final encodedKeyword = Uri.encodeComponent(keyword.trim());
    final url = Uri.parse(
      '$_baseUrl/facility/$facilityId?user_id=$userId&page=$page&limit=$limit&search_type=$searchType&keyword=$encodedKeyword',
    );

    final response = await http.get(url);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final decoded = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      final posts = (decoded['data'] as List).map((p) => PostModel.fromJson(p)).toList();
      final totalPages = decoded['meta']['total_pages'] ?? 1;
      return {'posts': posts, 'totalPages': totalPages};
    }
    throw Exception('시설 게시글을 불러오는데 실패했습니다.');
  }

  /// 2. 통합 게시판 목록 조회
  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> fetchIntegratedPosts({
    required String userId,
    required int page,
    required int limit,
    required String searchType,
    required String keyword,
  }) async {
    final encodedKeyword = Uri.encodeComponent(keyword.trim());
    final url = Uri.parse(
      '$_baseUrl/integrated?user_id=$userId&page=$page&limit=$limit&search_type=$searchType&keyword=$encodedKeyword',
    );

    final response = await http.get(url);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final decoded = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      final posts = (decoded['data'] as List).map((p) => PostModel.fromJson(p)).toList();
      final totalPages = decoded['meta']['total_pages'] ?? 1;
      return {'posts': posts, 'totalPages': totalPages};
    }
    throw Exception('통합 게시글을 불러오는데 실패했습니다.');
  }

  /// 3. 단일 게시글 상세 조회 (댓글 포함)
  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> fetchPostDetail({
    required int postId,
    required String userId,
  }) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/post/$postId?user_id=$userId');
    final response = await http.get(url);

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      return jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    }
    throw Exception('게시글 상세 정보를 불러오는데 실패했습니다.');
  }

  /// 4. 새 게시글 생성
  /// 에디터 이미지 사전 업로드. 글을 저장할 때 반환된 image_id를 함께 보내면
  /// 서버가 그 이미지를 해당 글에 묶어준다.
  static Future<({int imageId, String imageUrl})> uploadImage({
    required List<int> bytes,
    required String filename,
  }) async {
    final request = http.MultipartRequest('POST', Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/image/upload'))
      ..files.add(http.MultipartFile.fromBytes('file', bytes, filename: filename));

    final streamed = await request.send();
    final body = utf8.decode(await streamed.stream.toBytes());
    final decoded = jsonDecode(body);

    if (streamed.statusCode == 200) {
      return (imageId: decoded['image_id'] as int, imageUrl: decoded['image_url'] as String);
    }
    throw Exception(decoded['detail'] ?? '이미지 업로드에 실패했습니다.');
  }

  static Future<int> createPost({
    required String userId,
    required int facilityId,
    required String title,
    required String contentJson,
    required List<int> imageIds,
  }) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/post?user_id=$userId');
    final response = await http.post(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({
        "facility_id": facilityId,
        "title": title,
        "content": contentJson,
        "image_ids": imageIds,
      }),
    );

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final decoded = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      return decoded['post_id'];
    }
    throw Exception('게시글 작성에 실패했습니다.');
  }

  /// 5. 게시글 수정
  static Future<void> updatePost({
    required int postId,
    required String userId,
    required String title,
    required String contentJson,
    required List<int> imageIds,
  }) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/post/$postId?user_id=$userId');
    final response = await http.put(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({
        "title": title,
        "content": contentJson,
        "image_ids": imageIds,
      }),
    );

    if (response.statusCode != 200) {
      throw Exception('게시글 수정에 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }

  /// 6. 게시글 삭제
  static Future<void> deletePost({
    required int postId,
    required String userId,
  }) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/post/$postId?user_id=$userId');
    final response = await http.delete(url);

    if (response.statusCode != 200) {
      throw Exception('게시글 삭제에 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }

  /// 7. 댓글 작성
  static Future<void> addComment({
    required int postId,
    required String userId,
    required String content,
  }) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/post/$postId/comment?user_id=$userId');
    final response = await http.post(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({"content": content}),
    );

    if (response.statusCode != 200) {
      throw Exception('댓글 작성에 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }

  /// 8. 댓글 삭제
  static Future<void> deleteComment({
    required int commentId,
    required String userId,
  }) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/comment/$commentId?user_id=$userId');
    final response = await http.delete(url);

    if (response.statusCode != 200) {
      throw Exception('댓글 삭제에 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }
}

// ===============================================================
// 경로: lib/services/chart/chart_api_service.dart
// 명세: 차트 시계열 과거 데이터 조회 서비스
// ===============================================================
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:idcs/main.dart'; // AppConfig

class ChartApiService {
  static String get _baseUrl => '${AppConfig.baseUrl}/chart';

  static String get _mappingUrl => '${AppConfig.baseUrl}/chart-mapping';


  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> getHistoricalData({
    required int facilityId,
    required int roomId,
    required String scale,
    required String timeRange,
    required List<int> roleIds, // 필수로 변경
  }) async {
    final urlStr = '$_baseUrl/$facilityId/$roomId?scale=$scale&time_range=$timeRange&role_ids=${roleIds.join(',')}';
    final response = await http.get(Uri.parse(urlStr));

    final String responseBody = utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes);

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      return jsonDecode(responseBody);
    } else {
      // 💡 JSON 디코딩 실패 시 (Internal Server Error 등) 처리
      try {
        final error = jsonDecode(responseBody);
        throw Exception(error['detail'] ?? '차트 데이터를 불러오는데 실패했습니다.');
      } catch (_) {
        throw Exception('서버 오류 ($responseBody)');
      }
    }
  }
  /// 호실에 등록된 포인트 롤(측정 항목) 목록. 그룹 API는 role_id만 주고 이름을 주지 않아
  /// 그룹에 어떤 항목이 묶여 있는지 보여주려면 이 목록과 맞춰봐야 한다.
  static Future<List<dynamic>> getPointRoles(int facilityId, int roomId) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_mappingUrl/roles/$facilityId/$roomId');
    final response = await http.get(url);
    final String responseBody = utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes);

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      return jsonDecode(responseBody)['roles'] ?? [];
    } else {
      throw Exception('포인트 롤 목록을 불러오는데 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }

  static Future<List<dynamic>> getChartGroups(int facilityId, int roomId) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_mappingUrl/groups/$facilityId/$roomId');
    final response = await http.get(url);
    final String responseBody = utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes);

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final decoded = jsonDecode(responseBody);
      return decoded['groups'] ?? [];
    } else {
      throw Exception('차트 그룹 데이터를 불러오는데 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }
}

// 경로: lib/services/chat/chat_api_service.dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:idcs/main.dart';

class ChatApiService {
  static String get _baseUrl => '${AppConfig.baseUrl}/chat';

  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> getChatRooms(int facilityId, String userId) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/rooms/$facilityId?user_id=$userId');
    final response = await http.get(url);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    throw Exception('채팅방 목록을 불러오는데 실패했습니다.');
  }

  static Future<void> createGroupChat(String userId, String roomName, List<int> userIds) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/group?user_id=$userId');
    final response = await http.post(url, headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({"room_name": roomName, "user_ids": userIds}),
    );
    if (response.statusCode != 201) throw Exception('단체 채팅방 생성 실패');
  }

  static Future<void> leaveOrDeleteRoom(int roomId, String userId, {String action = "delete"}) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/room/$roomId?user_id=$userId&action=$action');
    final response = await http.delete(url);
    if (response.statusCode != 200) throw Exception(jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes))['detail'] ?? '실패했습니다.');
  }

  static Future<void> togglePinMessage(int roomId, int messageId, String userId) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/room/$roomId/pin/$messageId?user_id=$userId');
    final response = await http.post(url);
    if (response.statusCode != 200) throw Exception('메시지 고정에 실패했습니다.');
  }

  static Future<List<dynamic>> getParticipants(int roomId) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/room/$roomId/participants');
    final response = await http.get(url);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes))['participants'];
    throw Exception('참여자 조회 실패');
  }

  static Future<void> markAsRead(int roomId, String userId) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/room/$roomId/read?user_id=$userId');
    await http.put(url);
  }

  static Future<List<dynamic>> getRoomMessages(int roomId, String userId, {String keyword = ""}) async {
    final encoded = Uri.encodeComponent(keyword);
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/room/$roomId/messages?user_id=$userId&keyword=$encoded');
    final response = await http.get(url);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes))['messages'];
    throw Exception('메시지 로드 실패');
  }
}

// ===============================================================
// 경로: lib/services/common/alarm/alarm_settings.dart
// 명세: 알람 표시 방식(소리/진동/잠금화면/다시 알림) 설정 보관
// ===============================================================
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

/// 알람 설정. 기기마다 다른 취향이라 서버가 아닌 기기 로컬에 저장한다.
class AlarmSettings {
  final bool sound;
  final bool vibration;

  /// 잠금화면에서도 내용까지 보여줄지 여부.
  final bool showOnLockScreen;

  /// 확인하지 않았을 때 다시 알려줄 간격(분). 0이면 다시 알리지 않는다.
  final int repeatMinutes;

  const AlarmSettings({
    this.sound = true,
    this.vibration = true,
    this.showOnLockScreen = true,
    this.repeatMinutes = 0,
  });

  /// 다시 알림에서 고를 수 있는 간격. 0은 '없음'.
  static const List<int> repeatOptions = [0, 5, 10, 30];

  /// 다시 알림을 최대 몇 번까지 더 보낼지. 무한 반복은 알림 폭탄이 되므로 제한한다.
  static const int maxRepeatCount = 3;

  static const _kSound = 'alarm_sound';
  static const _kVibration = 'alarm_vibration';
  static const _kLockScreen = 'alarm_lock_screen';
  static const _kRepeatMinutes = 'alarm_repeat_minutes';

  AlarmSettings copyWith({
    bool? sound,
    bool? vibration,
    bool? showOnLockScreen,
    int? repeatMinutes,
  }) {
    return AlarmSettings(
      sound: sound ?? this.sound,
      vibration: vibration ?? this.vibration,
      showOnLockScreen: showOnLockScreen ?? this.showOnLockScreen,
      repeatMinutes: repeatMinutes ?? this.repeatMinutes,
    );
  }

  static Future<AlarmSettings> load() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    return AlarmSettings(
      sound: prefs.getBool(_kSound) ?? true,
      vibration: prefs.getBool(_kVibration) ?? true,
      showOnLockScreen: prefs.getBool(_kLockScreen) ?? true,
      repeatMinutes: prefs.getInt(_kRepeatMinutes) ?? 0,
    );
  }

  Future<void> save() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setBool(_kSound, sound);
    await prefs.setBool(_kVibration, vibration);
    await prefs.setBool(_kLockScreen, showOnLockScreen);
    await prefs.setInt(_kRepeatMinutes, repeatMinutes);
  }
}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import '../../main.dart';

class FacilityApiService {
  static String get _baseUrl => '${AppConfig.baseUrl}/facilities';

  // 시설 목록 조회 (권한에 따라 백엔드에서 알아서 필터링됨)
  static Future<List<dynamic>> getFacilities(String userId) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/$userId');
    final response = await http.get(url);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      return data['facilities'] ?? [];
    } else {
      throw Exception('시설 목록을 불러오는데 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }

  // 시설 등록
  static Future<String> registerFacility(String userId, String accessCode) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/$userId/register');
    final response = await http.post(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({'access_code': accessCode}),
    );
    final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return data['message'];
    throw Exception(data['detail'] ?? '시설 등록에 실패했습니다.');
  }

  // 시설 연동 해제
  static Future<String> unlinkFacility(String userId, int facilityId) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/$userId/unlink/$facilityId');
    final response = await http.delete(url);
    final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return data['message'];
    throw Exception(data['detail'] ?? '시설 해제에 실패했습니다.');
  }

  //시설 호실 목록 조회
  static Future<List<dynamic>> getFacilityRooms(String facilityId) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/rooms/$facilityId');
    final response = await http.get(url);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      return data['rooms'] ?? [];
    } else {
      throw Exception('시설 목록을 불러오는데 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }
}

■ File : fcm_service.dart
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_app\IDCS\lib\services\common\fcm_service.dart

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:firebase_core/firebase_core.dart';
import 'package:firebase_messaging/firebase_messaging.dart';
import 'package:idcs/firebase_options.dart';
import 'package:idcs/main.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:flutter_local_notifications/flutter_local_notifications.dart';
import 'package:timezone/data/latest.dart' as tz_data;
import 'package:timezone/timezone.dart' as tz;
import 'package:idcs/services/common/alarm/alarm_settings.dart';

// 안드로이드는 채널을 한 번 만들면 소리/진동 설정을 나중에 바꿀 수 없다.
// 그래서 조합별로 채널을 미리 만들어 두고, 알림을 띄울 때 설정에 맞는 채널을 고른다.
const List<AndroidNotificationChannel> alarmChannels = [
  AndroidNotificationChannel('alarm_sound_vibe', '알람 (소리+진동)',
      description: '소리와 진동을 모두 사용하는 알람', importance: Importance.max, playSound: true, enableVibration: true),
  AndroidNotificationChannel('alarm_sound', '알람 (소리만)',
      description: '소리만 사용하는 알람', importance: Importance.max, playSound: true, enableVibration: false),
  AndroidNotificationChannel('alarm_vibe', '알람 (진동만)',
      description: '진동만 사용하는 알람', importance: Importance.max, playSound: false, enableVibration: true),
  AndroidNotificationChannel('alarm_silent', '알람 (무음)',
      description: '소리와 진동 없이 표시만 하는 알람', importance: Importance.max, playSound: false, enableVibration: false),
];

AndroidNotificationChannel _channelFor(AlarmSettings s) {
  if (s.sound && s.vibration) return alarmChannels[0];
  if (s.sound) return alarmChannels[1];
  if (s.vibration) return alarmChannels[2];
  return alarmChannels[3];
}

NotificationDetails _detailsFor(AlarmSettings s) {
  final channel = _channelFor(s);
  return NotificationDetails(
    android: AndroidNotificationDetails(
      channel.id,
      channel.name,
      channelDescription: channel.description,
      icon: '@mipmap/ic_launcher',
      importance: Importance.max,
      priority: Priority.high,
      playSound: s.sound,
      enableVibration: s.vibration,
      // 잠금화면에서 내용을 숨길지 여부
      visibility: s.showOnLockScreen ? NotificationVisibility.public : NotificationVisibility.private,
    ),
  );
}

/// 알람을 띄우고, '다시 알림'이 켜져 있으면 뒤이어 재알림을 예약한다.
Future<void> showAlarmNotification(
  FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin plugin,
  String title,
  String body,
) async {
  final settings = await AlarmSettings.load();
  final details = _detailsFor(settings);
  final baseId = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch ~/ 1000;

  await plugin.show(baseId, title, body, details);

  if (settings.repeatMinutes <= 0) return;

  // 확인하지 못했을 때를 대비한 재알림. 무한 반복은 알림 폭탄이 되므로 횟수를 제한한다.
  for (int i = 1; i <= AlarmSettings.maxRepeatCount; i++) {
    await plugin.zonedSchedule(
      baseId + i,
      title,
      '$body (다시 알림 $i/${AlarmSettings.maxRepeatCount})',
      tz.TZDateTime.now(tz.local).add(Duration(minutes: settings.repeatMinutes * i)),
      details,
      androidScheduleMode: AndroidScheduleMode.exactAllowWhileIdle,
      uiLocalNotificationDateInterpretation: UILocalNotificationDateInterpretation.absoluteTime,
    );
  }
}

// 🔥 최상단 백그라운드 핸들러 (앱이 백그라운드/종료 상태일 때 처리)
@pragma('vm:entry-point')
Future<void> firebaseMessagingBackgroundHandler(RemoteMessage message) async {
  await Firebase.initializeApp(options: DefaultFirebaseOptions.currentPlatform);
  print("🌙 [백그라운드 수신] ID: ${message.messageId}");

  final FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin localNotifications = FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin();

  // 백그라운드 isolate 알림 초기화 추가
  const AndroidInitializationSettings initSettingsAndroid = AndroidInitializationSettings('@mipmap/ic_launcher');
  await localNotifications.initialize(const InitializationSettings(android: initSettingsAndroid));

  // 백그라운드는 별도 isolate라 앱에서 초기화한 타임존 정보가 없다.
  tz_data.initializeTimeZones();
  tz.setLocalLocation(tz.getLocation('Asia/Seoul'));

  String title = message.notification?.title ?? message.data['title'] ?? '';
  String body = message.notification?.body ?? message.data['body'] ?? '';

  if (title.isNotEmpty || body.isNotEmpty) {
    await showAlarmNotification(localNotifications, title, body);
  }
}

class FcmService {
  static final FcmService _instance = FcmService._internal();
  factory FcmService() => _instance;
  FcmService._internal();

  static FirebaseMessaging get _messaging => FirebaseMessaging.instance;

  static final FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin _localNotifications =
  FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin();

  static Future<void> initialize() async {
    // 다시 알림 예약에 필요. 앱이 한국 기준으로 동작하므로 서울로 고정한다.
    tz_data.initializeTimeZones();
    tz.setLocalLocation(tz.getLocation('Asia/Seoul'));

    // 💡 1. 백그라운드 핸들러 선제 등록 (경고 로그 방지)
    FirebaseMessaging.onBackgroundMessage(firebaseMessagingBackgroundHandler);

    // 2. FCM 권한 요청
    NotificationSettings settings = await _messaging.requestPermission(
      alert: true,
      badge: true,
      sound: true,
    );

    print("Notification permission status: ${settings.authorizationStatus}");

    if (settings.authorizationStatus == AuthorizationStatus.authorized ||
        settings.authorizationStatus == AuthorizationStatus.provisional) {

      // 3. 로컬 알림 초기화
      const AndroidInitializationSettings initializationSettingsAndroid =
      AndroidInitializationSettings('@mipmap/ic_launcher');
      const InitializationSettings initializationSettings =
      InitializationSettings(android: initializationSettingsAndroid);

      await _localNotifications.initialize(initializationSettings);

      // 4. Android 구현체 획득 및 채널 재등록 (채널 꼬임 방지)
      final androidPlugin = _localNotifications
          .resolvePlatformSpecificImplementation<
          AndroidFlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin>();

      if (androidPlugin != null) {
        await androidPlugin.requestNotificationsPermission();
        // 소리/진동 조합별 채널을 모두 등록해 두고, 알림을 띄울 때 설정에 맞는 것을 고른다.
        for (final channel in alarmChannels) {
          await androidPlugin.createNotificationChannel(channel);
        }
      }

      // 💡 5. 포그라운드 메시지 수신 리스너
      FirebaseMessaging.onMessage.listen((RemoteMessage message) async {
        print("🔥 [포그라운드 수신] ID: ${message.messageId}");
        print("🔥 [Notification]: ${message.notification?.title} / ${message.notification?.body}");
        print("🔥 [Data]: ${message.data}");

        String title = message.notification?.title ?? message.data['title'] ?? '알림';
        String body = message.notification?.body ?? message.data['body'] ?? '';

        try {
          await showAlarmNotification(_localNotifications, title, body);
          print("✅ 로컬 알림 show() 정상 실행 완료");
        } catch (e) {
          print("❌ 로컬 알림 show() 실행 중 에러 발생: $e");
        }
      });

      // 6. 알림 클릭 이벤트
      FirebaseMessaging.onMessageOpenedApp.listen((RemoteMessage message) {
        print('알림 클릭으로 앱 실행: ${message.data}');
      });

      // 7. 토큰 갱신 리스너
      _messaging.onTokenRefresh.listen((newToken) {
        print("FCM 토큰 변경됨: $newToken");
        if (AuthService.getCurrentUserId() != null) {
          registToken();
        }
      });
    }
  }

  static Future<String?> getToken() async {
    try {
      String? token = await _messaging.getToken();
      return token;
    } catch (e) {
      print("FCM 토큰 가져오기 실패: $e");
      return null;
    }
  }

  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>?> registToken() async {
    try {
      String? token = await getToken();
      if (token == null) {
        print("FCM 토큰이 없어 서버 등록을 건너뜁니다.");
        return null;
      }

      print("발급된 토큰: $token");
      final url = Uri.parse('${AppConfig.baseUrl}/alarms/register');
      final response = await http.post(
        url,
        headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
        body: jsonEncode({
          'fcm_token': token,
          'user_id': AuthService.getCurrentUserId()
        }),
      );

      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        return jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      } else {
        final error = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
        throw Exception(error['detail'] ?? '토큰 등록에 실패했습니다.');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      print("registToken 에러: $e");
      rethrow;
    }
  }
}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
// AppConfig가 정의된 main.dart를 임포트 (경로에 맞게 수정하세요)
import '../../../main.dart';

class AuthApiService {
  // 백엔드 auth 라우터 기본 URL
  static String get _authUrl => '${AppConfig.baseUrl}/auth';
  /// 아이디 찾기 페이지: 이메일 계정 존재 및 연동 여부 확인
  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> checkAccountExistence(String email) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_authUrl/check-account');
    final response = await http.post(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({'email': email}),
    );

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      return jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    } else {
      final error = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      throw Exception(error['detail'] ?? '계정 정보를 확인하는데 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }
}

■ File : auth_api_service.dart

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
// AppConfig가 정의된 main.dart를 임포트 (경로에 맞게 수정하세요)
import '../../../main.dart';

class AuthApiService {
  // 백엔드 auth 라우터 기본 URL
  static String get _authUrl => '${AppConfig.baseUrl}/auth';
  /// 아이디 찾기 페이지: 이메일 계정 존재 및 연동 여부 확인


  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> requestPasswordCode(String email) async {
    final response = await http.post(
      Uri.parse('$_authUrl/password/request-code'),
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({'email': email}),
    );
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    throw Exception(jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes))['detail']);
  }

  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> verifyPasswordCode(String token, String code) async {
    final response = await http.post(
      Uri.parse('$_authUrl/password/verify-code'),
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({'token': token, 'code': code}),
    );
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    throw Exception(jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes))['detail']);
  }

  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> resetPassword(String token, String newPassword) async {
    final response = await http.post(
      Uri.parse('$_authUrl/password/reset'),
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({'token': token, 'new_password': newPassword}),
    );
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    throw Exception(jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes))['detail']);
  }

}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:idcs/services/common/fcm_service.dart';
// AppConfig가 정의된 main.dart를 임포트 (경로에 맞게 수정하세요)
import '../../../main.dart';

class AuthApiService {
  // 백엔드 auth 라우터 기본 URL
  static String get _authUrl => '${AppConfig.baseUrl}/auth';

  /// Box #1: 일반 로그인 API 호출
  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> loginGeneral({
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required bool keepLogin,
  }) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_authUrl/login/general');
    final response = await http.post(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({
        'email': email,
        'password': password,
        'keep_login': keepLogin,
      }),
    );

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      //토큰 전송 추가
      dynamic decoded = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      if(decoded['user_id'] != null)
        FcmService.registToken();
      return decoded;
    } else {
      final error = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      throw Exception(error['detail'] ?? '로그인에 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }

  /// Box #2: 소셜 로그인 API 호출
  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> loginSocial({
    required String provider,
    required String providerId,
    required bool keepLogin,
  }) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_authUrl/login/social');
    final response = await http.post(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({
        'provider': provider,
        'provider_id': providerId,
        'keep_login': keepLogin,
      }),
    );

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      //토큰 전송 추가
      dynamic decoded = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      if(decoded['user_id'] != null)
        FcmService.registToken();
      return decoded;
    } else {
      final error = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      throw Exception(error['detail'] ?? '소셜 로그인에 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }
}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
// AppConfig가 정의된 main.dart를 임포트 (경로에 맞게 수정하세요)
import '../../../main.dart';

class AuthApiService {
  // 백엔드 auth 라우터 기본 URL
  static String get _authUrl => '${AppConfig.baseUrl}/auth';

  /// Box #1: 일반 회원가입 API 호출
  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> signupGeneral({
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required String name,
    String? phoneNumber,
  }) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_authUrl/signup/general');
    final response = await http.post(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({
        'email': email,
        'password': password,
        'name': name,
        if (phoneNumber != null &&
            phoneNumber.isNotEmpty) 'phone_number': phoneNumber,
      }),
    );

    if (response.statusCode == 201) {
      return jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    } else {
      final error = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      throw Exception(error['detail'] ?? '회원가입에 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }

  /// Box #2: 소셜 회원가입 API 호출
  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> signupSocial({
    required String provider,
    required String providerId,
    required String email,
    required String name,
    String? phoneNumber,
  }) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_authUrl/signup/social');
    final response = await http.post(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({
        'provider': provider,
        'provider_id': providerId,
        'email': email,
        'name': name,
        if (phoneNumber != null &&
            phoneNumber.isNotEmpty) 'phone_number': phoneNumber,
      }),
    );

    if (response.statusCode == 201) {
      return jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    } else {
      final error = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      throw Exception(error['detail'] ?? '소셜 회원가입에 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }
}

import 'package:google_sign_in/google_sign_in.dart';
import 'package:sign_in_with_apple/sign_in_with_apple.dart';



class SocialAuthService {
  static bool _isGoogleInitialized = false;

  /// 구글 SDK를 통해 프로필 정보를 가져옵니다.
  static Future<Map<String, String>?> signInWithGoogle() async {
    try {
      final googleSignIn = GoogleSignIn.instance;
      try {
        if (!_isGoogleInitialized) {
          await googleSignIn.initialize(
            // TODO: GCP에서 발급받은 유형의 클라이언트 ID
            // 근데 IDCool 용으로 아니면 설민님 계정으로
            serverClientId: '415636031627-asr8rncpbq4c5jkn3r44d0u65cf64lvb.apps.googleusercontent.com',
          );
          _isGoogleInitialized = true;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        // 이미 초기화된 상태에서 발생하는 StateError는 안전하게 무시합니다.
        if (e is StateError && e.message.contains('already been called')) {
          _isGoogleInitialized = true;
        } else {
          rethrow; // 다른 종류의 에러는 그대로 던집니다.
        }
      }
      final googleUser = await googleSignIn.authenticate();
      // 사용자가 로그인 팝업을 그냥 닫은 경우 null 반환
      if (googleUser == null) {
        return null;
      }

      return {
        'provider': 'google',
        'provider_id': googleUser.id,
        'email': googleUser.email,
        'name': googleUser.displayName ?? '구글유저',
      };
    } catch (error) {
      throw Exception('구글 로그인 진행 중 오류가 발생/취소되었습니다: $error');
    }
  }

  /// 애플 SDK를 통해 프로필 정보를 가져옵니다.
  static Future<Map<String, String>?> signInWithApple() async {
    try {
      final AuthorizationCredentialAppleID credential =
      await SignInWithApple.getAppleIDCredential(
        scopes: [
          AppleIDAuthorizationScopes.email,
          AppleIDAuthorizationScopes.fullName,
        ],
      );

      String name = '애플유저';
      if (credential.givenName != null || credential.familyName != null) {
        name = '${credential.familyName ?? ''}${credential.givenName ?? ''}';
      }

      return {
        'provider': 'apple',
        'provider_id': credential.userIdentifier ?? '',
        'email': credential.email ?? '',
        'name': name,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      throw Exception('애플 로그인 진행 중 오류가 발생/취소되었습니다: $error');
    }
  }



}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import '../../main.dart';

class UserApiService {
  static String get _baseUrl => '${AppConfig.baseUrl}/auth'; // 라우터 경로에 맞게 수정

  /// 유저 권한(Role) 조회 API
  static Future<String> getUserRole(String userId) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/$userId/role');
    final response = await http.get(url);

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      return data['role']; // 'USER', 'ADMIN', 'DEVELOPER' 중 하나 반환
    } else {
      throw Exception('권한 정보를 불러오는데 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------
  // 계정 수정 로직

  /// 유저 프로필 조회
  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> getUserProfile(String userId) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/$userId/profile');
    final response = await http.get(url);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      return jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    } else {
      throw Exception('프로필 정보를 불러오는데 실패했습니다.');
    }
  }

  /// Box #2: 이름 수정
  static Future<String> updateName(String userId, String name) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/$userId/name');
    final response = await http.put(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({'name': name}),
    );
    final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return data['message'];
    throw Exception(data['detail'] ?? '이름 수정에 실패했습니다.');
  }

  /// Box #3: 전화번호 수정
  static Future<String> updatePhone(String userId, String phone) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/$userId/phone');
    final response = await http.put(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({'phone_number': phone}),
    );
    final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return data['message'];
    throw Exception(data['detail'] ?? '전화번호 수정에 실패했습니다.');
  }

  /// Box #4: 비밀번호 수정
  static Future<String> updatePassword(String userId, String currentPw, String newPw) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/$userId/password');
    final response = await http.put(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({
        'current_password': currentPw,
        'new_password': newPw,
      }),
    );
    final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return data['message'];
    throw Exception(data['detail'] ?? '비밀번호 수정에 실패했습니다.');
  }

  /// 계정 탈퇴. 서버에서 CASCADE로 연동 계정/시설 소속 정보까지 함께 삭제된다.
  static Future<String> withdraw(String userId) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/withdraw');
    final response = await http.delete(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({'user_id': int.parse(userId)}),
    );
    final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return data['message'];
    throw Exception(data['detail'] ?? '계정 탈퇴에 실패했습니다.');
  }
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:web_socket_channel/web_socket_channel.dart';

class WebSocketService {
  WebSocketChannel? _channel;

  // 1. 단순 연결 상태 확인용 getter
  bool _isConnected = false;
  bool get isConnected => _isConnected;

  final StreamController<String> _messageStreamController = StreamController<String>.broadcast();
  Stream<String> get messageStream => _messageStreamController.stream;

  void connect(String url) {
    try {
      _channel = WebSocketChannel.connect(Uri.parse(url));

      // 연결 성공 시점에 true
      _isConnected = true;

      _channel!.stream.listen(
            (message) {
          if (!_messageStreamController.isClosed) {
            _messageStreamController.add(message.toString());
          }
        },
        onError: (error) {
          print('웹소켓 에러 발생: $error');
          _isConnected = false; // 에러 발생 시 false
          _reconnect(url);
        },
        onDone: () {
          print('웹소켓 연결 종료');
          _isConnected = false; // 연결 종료 시 false
        },
      );
    } catch (e) {
      print('웹소켓 연결 실패: $e');
      _isConnected = false; // 예외 발생 시 false
    }
  }

  void _reconnect(String url) {
    _isConnected = false;
    Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 3), () {
      print('웹소켓 재연결 시도 중...');
      connect(url);
    });
  }

  void disconnect() {
    _isConnected = false;
    _channel?.sink.close();
    _messageStreamController.close();
  }

  void send(String message) {
    // 2. 조건식 활용 예시
    if (isConnected && _channel != null) {
      _channel!.sink.add(message);
    } else {
      print('웹소켓이 연결되어 있지 않아 메시지를 전송할 수 없습니다.');
    }
  }
}
