// 경로: lib/theme/app_logo.dart
// 명세: IDCool 브랜드 마크 — 범용 머티리얼 아이콘이 아닌 워드마크/모노그램 기반
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'app_theme.dart';

/// 정사각형 그라디언트 위에 소문자 이니셜을 얹은 모노그램.
/// 스플래시/앱바 등에서 로고로 사용.
class AppMonogram extends StatelessWidget {
  final double size;

  const AppMonogram({super.key, this.size = 88});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: size,
      height: size,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(size * 0.28),
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
          colors: [context.colors.primaryLight, context.colors.primaryDark],
        ),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: context.colors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.28),
            blurRadius: size * 0.32,
            offset: Offset(0, size * 0.14),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      alignment: Alignment.center,
      child: Text(
        'ic',
        style: TextStyle(
          fontSize: size * 0.42,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
          color: Colors.white,
          height: 1,
          letterSpacing: -1,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// "ID" + "Cool" 2색 워드마크. 앱바/헤더처럼 작은 영역에 사용.
class AppWordmark extends StatelessWidget {
  final double fontSize;
  final Color? baseColor;

  const AppWordmark({super.key, this.fontSize = 18, this.baseColor});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return RichText(
      text: TextSpan(
        style: TextStyle(
          fontSize: fontSize,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
          letterSpacing: -0.3,
          color: baseColor ?? context.colors.textPrimary,
        ),
        children: [
          TextSpan(text: 'ID'),
          TextSpan(text: 'Cool', style: TextStyle(color: context.colors.primary)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// 경로: lib/theme/app_theme.dart
// 명세: 앱 전역 디자인 토큰(색상/타이포/컴포넌트 테마) — 연한 테마와 어두운 테마
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// 화면에서 쓰는 색 묶음. 테마에 따라 통째로 바뀐다.
///
/// 색을 `AppColors.surface`처럼 정적으로 읽으면 테마가 바뀌어도 값이 그대로여서
/// 어두운 모드를 만들 수 없다. ThemeExtension으로 올려두고 `context.colors.surface`
/// 처럼 읽으면 현재 테마에 맞는 값이 나온다.
///
/// 모든 값은 WCAG 대비 기준을 만족하도록 계산해서 골랐다.
/// 본문 텍스트 4.5:1, 컨트롤 경계선 3:1, 장식 테두리 1.5:1 이상.
@immutable
class AppPalette extends ThemeExtension<AppPalette> {
  final Color primary;
  final Color primaryLight;
  final Color primaryDark;

  /// 기본 버튼처럼 primary를 배경으로 깔았을 때 그 위에 올라가는 글자색.
  /// 어두운 테마의 primary는 밝은 파랑이라 흰 글자를 올리면 대비가 2.58밖에 안 된다.
  final Color onPrimary;

  final Color background;
  final Color surface;

  /// 카드/구분선용 장식 테두리.
  final Color outline;

  /// 입력창·컨트롤의 경계선. 장식이 아니므로 대비 3:1을 지킨다.
  final Color outlineStrong;

  final Color textPrimary;
  final Color textSecondary;

  final Color error;
  final Color warning;
  final Color success;

  const AppPalette({
    required this.primary,
    required this.primaryLight,
    required this.primaryDark,
    required this.onPrimary,
    required this.background,
    required this.surface,
    required this.outline,
    required this.outlineStrong,
    required this.textPrimary,
    required this.textSecondary,
    required this.error,
    required this.warning,
    required this.success,
  });

  static const AppPalette light = AppPalette(
    primary: Color(0xFF2E6BEF),
    primaryLight: Color(0xFF5C8DF5),
    primaryDark: Color(0xFF1E4FBD),
    onPrimary: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
    background: Color(0xFFF5F8FA),
    surface: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
    outline: Color(0xFFC0CED6),
    outlineStrong: Color(0xFF7B99A8),
    textPrimary: Color(0xFF16232A),
    textSecondary: Color(0xFF5B6B73),
    error: Color(0xFFD54141),
    warning: Color(0xFFA26B1C),
    success: Color(0xFF1D875A),
  );

  static const AppPalette dark = AppPalette(
    primary: Color(0xFF6FA0FF),
    primaryLight: Color(0xFF9BBEFF),
    primaryDark: Color(0xFF3F79E0),
    // 밝은 파랑 위에는 어두운 글자라야 읽힌다 (7.12:1)
    onPrimary: Color(0xFF0F151C),
    background: Color(0xFF0F151C),
    surface: Color(0xFF1B2531),
    outline: Color(0xFF36454F),
    outlineStrong: Color(0xFF6B7D8A),
    textPrimary: Color(0xFFE9EEF3),
    textSecondary: Color(0xFF9FB0BC),
    error: Color(0xFFFF6B6B),
    warning: Color(0xFFE0A94A),
    success: Color(0xFF4ECB8D),
  );

  bool get isDark => background.computeLuminance() < 0.5;

  /// 강조가 필요한 표면에만 제한적으로 사용 (리스트 행에는 사용하지 않음).
  /// 어두운 배경에서는 그림자가 거의 보이지 않으므로 더 진하게 깐다.
  List<BoxShadow> get softShadow => [
        BoxShadow(
          color: isDark ? Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.45) : primary.withValues(alpha: 0.10),
          blurRadius: 28,
          offset: const Offset(0, 10),
        ),
        BoxShadow(
          color: isDark ? Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.30) : const Color(0xFF0B1420).withValues(alpha: 0.05),
          blurRadius: 6,
          offset: const Offset(0, 2),
        ),
      ];

  /// 차트 시리즈 색. 데이터 색이라 테마와 무관하게 쓰되, 어두운 배경에서도
  /// 보이도록 테마별로 밝기를 다르게 둔다.
  List<Color> get chartSeries => isDark
      ? const [
          Color(0xFF6FA0FF),
          Color(0xFFFF6B6B),
          Color(0xFF4ECB8D),
          Color(0xFFE0A94A),
          Color(0xFFB08BFF),
          Color(0xFF4DD0C7),
        ]
      : const [
          Color(0xFF2E6BEF),
          Color(0xFFD54141),
          Color(0xFF1D875A),
          Color(0xFFA26B1C),
          Color(0xFF1E4FBD),
          Color(0xFF00897B),
        ];

  @override
  AppPalette copyWith({
    Color? primary,
    Color? primaryLight,
    Color? primaryDark,
    Color? onPrimary,
    Color? background,
    Color? surface,
    Color? outline,
    Color? outlineStrong,
    Color? textPrimary,
    Color? textSecondary,
    Color? error,
    Color? warning,
    Color? success,
  }) {
    return AppPalette(
      primary: primary ?? this.primary,
      primaryLight: primaryLight ?? this.primaryLight,
      primaryDark: primaryDark ?? this.primaryDark,
      onPrimary: onPrimary ?? this.onPrimary,
      background: background ?? this.background,
      surface: surface ?? this.surface,
      outline: outline ?? this.outline,
      outlineStrong: outlineStrong ?? this.outlineStrong,
      textPrimary: textPrimary ?? this.textPrimary,
      textSecondary: textSecondary ?? this.textSecondary,
      error: error ?? this.error,
      warning: warning ?? this.warning,
      success: success ?? this.success,
    );
  }

  @override
  AppPalette lerp(ThemeExtension<AppPalette>? other, double t) {
    if (other is! AppPalette) return this;
    return AppPalette(
      primary: Color.lerp(primary, other.primary, t)!,
      primaryLight: Color.lerp(primaryLight, other.primaryLight, t)!,
      primaryDark: Color.lerp(primaryDark, other.primaryDark, t)!,
      onPrimary: Color.lerp(onPrimary, other.onPrimary, t)!,
      background: Color.lerp(background, other.background, t)!,
      surface: Color.lerp(surface, other.surface, t)!,
      outline: Color.lerp(outline, other.outline, t)!,
      outlineStrong: Color.lerp(outlineStrong, other.outlineStrong, t)!,
      textPrimary: Color.lerp(textPrimary, other.textPrimary, t)!,
      textSecondary: Color.lerp(textSecondary, other.textSecondary, t)!,
      error: Color.lerp(error, other.error, t)!,
      warning: Color.lerp(warning, other.warning, t)!,
      success: Color.lerp(success, other.success, t)!,
    );
  }
}

/// `context.colors.surface` 처럼 현재 테마의 색을 읽는다.
extension AppPaletteContext on BuildContext {
  AppPalette get colors => Theme.of(this).extension<AppPalette>() ?? AppPalette.light;
}

class AppTheme {
  AppTheme._();

  static ThemeData get light => _build(AppPalette.light, Brightness.light);
  static ThemeData get dark => _build(AppPalette.dark, Brightness.dark);

  static ThemeData _build(AppPalette p, Brightness brightness) {
    final colorScheme = ColorScheme.fromSeed(
      seedColor: p.primary,
      brightness: brightness,
      primary: p.primary,
      onPrimary: p.onPrimary,
      surface: p.surface,
      error: p.error,
    );

    return ThemeData(
      useMaterial3: true,
      brightness: brightness,
      colorScheme: colorScheme,
      scaffoldBackgroundColor: p.background,
      fontFamily: 'Roboto',
      extensions: [p],
      textTheme: TextTheme(
        headlineMedium: TextStyle(
          fontSize: 26,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
          color: p.textPrimary,
          height: 1.25,
        ),
        titleLarge: TextStyle(
          fontSize: 20,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
          color: p.textPrimary,
        ),
        bodyMedium: TextStyle(
          fontSize: 14,
          color: p.textSecondary,
          height: 1.5,
        ),
        labelLarge: const TextStyle(
          fontSize: 15,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
        ),
      ),
      appBarTheme: AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: p.background,
        foregroundColor: p.textPrimary,
        elevation: 0,
        centerTitle: false,
        titleTextStyle: TextStyle(
          color: p.textPrimary,
          fontSize: 18,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
        ),
      ),
      elevatedButtonTheme: ElevatedButtonThemeData(
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: p.primary,
          foregroundColor: p.onPrimary,
          minimumSize: const Size(64, 52),
          elevation: 0,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          ),
          textStyle: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
        ),
      ),
      outlinedButtonTheme: OutlinedButtonThemeData(
        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
          foregroundColor: p.primary,
          minimumSize: const Size(64, 52),
          side: BorderSide(color: p.primary, width: 1.4),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          ),
          textStyle: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
        ),
      ),
      textButtonTheme: TextButtonThemeData(
        style: TextButton.styleFrom(
          foregroundColor: p.primary,
          textStyle: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
        ),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: InputDecorationTheme(
        filled: true,
        fillColor: p.surface,
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 16),
        hintStyle: TextStyle(color: p.textSecondary),
        labelStyle: TextStyle(color: p.textSecondary),
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: p.outlineStrong),
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: p.outlineStrong),
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: p.primary, width: 1.6),
        ),
        errorBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: p.error),
        ),
      ),
      checkboxTheme: CheckboxThemeData(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4)),
        fillColor: WidgetStateProperty.resolveWith(
          (states) => states.contains(WidgetState.selected) ? p.primary : Colors.transparent,
        ),
        side: BorderSide(color: p.outlineStrong, width: 1.5),
      ),
      dividerTheme: DividerThemeData(
        color: p.outline,
        thickness: 1,
      ),
      cardTheme: CardThemeData(
        color: p.surface,
        elevation: 0,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          side: BorderSide(color: p.outline),
        ),
      ),
      dialogTheme: DialogThemeData(
        backgroundColor: p.surface,
        titleTextStyle: TextStyle(color: p.textPrimary, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
        contentTextStyle: TextStyle(color: p.textSecondary, fontSize: 14, height: 1.5),
      ),
      snackBarTheme: SnackBarThemeData(
        backgroundColor: p.isDark ? p.surface : const Color(0xFF2B3843),
        contentTextStyle: TextStyle(color: p.isDark ? p.textPrimary : Colors.white, fontSize: 14),
      ),
      navigationBarTheme: NavigationBarThemeData(
        backgroundColor: p.surface,
        indicatorColor: p.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.16),
        elevation: 0,
        height: 64,
        labelTextStyle: WidgetStateProperty.resolveWith(
          (states) => TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: states.contains(WidgetState.selected) ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w500,
            color: states.contains(WidgetState.selected) ? p.primary : p.textSecondary,
          ),
        ),
        iconTheme: WidgetStateProperty.resolveWith(
          (states) => IconThemeData(
            color: states.contains(WidgetState.selected) ? p.primary : p.textSecondary,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// 경로: lib/theme/theme_controller.dart
// 명세: 연한/어두운 테마 선택값 보관 및 기기 저장
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

/// 테마 선택값. 기본은 기기 설정을 따라가고, 사용자가 직접 고르면 그 값을 기억한다.
///
/// 상태관리 패키지를 새로 들이지 않고 ValueNotifier 하나로 처리한다.
/// 앱 전체에서 바뀌는 값이 이것 하나뿐이라 그 이상은 과하다.
class ThemeController {
  ThemeController._();

  static const _key = 'theme_mode';

  static final ValueNotifier<ThemeMode> mode = ValueNotifier(ThemeMode.system);

  static Future<void> load() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    mode.value = _fromName(prefs.getString(_key));
  }

  static Future<void> set(ThemeMode next) async {
    mode.value = next;
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setString(_key, next.name);
  }

  static ThemeMode _fromName(String? name) {
    switch (name) {
      case 'light':
        return ThemeMode.light;
      case 'dark':
        return ThemeMode.dark;
      default:
        return ThemeMode.system;
    }
  }

  static String labelOf(ThemeMode m) {
    switch (m) {
      case ThemeMode.light:
        return '밝게';
      case ThemeMode.dark:
        return '어둡게';
      case ThemeMode.system:
        return '시스템 설정';
    }
  }
}

# 명세 : 데이터베이스 연결 및 세션 관리 설정
# ===============================================================
import os
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import create_async_engine, AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker, declarative_base
# ===============================================================

# [Database configuration]
DB_CONFIG = {
    "dbname": "idcool_database_v01",
    "user": "dev",
    "password": "devkey1",
    "host": "211.55.127.239",
    "port": "5432"
}

# ===============================================================

SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URL = f"postgresql+asyncpg://{DB_CONFIG['user']}:{DB_CONFIG['password']}@{DB_CONFIG['host']}:{DB_CONFIG['port']}/{DB_CONFIG['dbname']}"

DATABASE_URL = os.getenv(
    "DATABASE_URL", 
    SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URL
)

# 비동기 엔진 생성
engine = create_async_engine(DATABASE_URL, echo=True)

# 세션 팩토리 생성
AsyncSessionLocal = sessionmaker(
    bind=engine, 
    class_=AsyncSession, 
    expire_on_commit=False
)

Base = declarative_base()

# FastAPI 의존성 주입을 위한 DB 세션 함수
async def get_db():
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
        try:
            yield session
        finally:
            await session.close()

@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    # Startup: 시작 시 실행 로직
    print("LOG[STARTUP] : IDCool Server is starting up...")
    yield
    # Shutdown: 종료 시 실행 로직
    print("LOG[SHUTDOWN] : Shutting down IDCool Server...")
    await engine.dispose()

    title="IDCool Systems API",
    description="IDCool Systems 서버 백엔드 API",
    version="1.0.0",
    lifespan=lifespan
)

@router.get("/db-test")
async def test_db_connection(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    try:
        # PostgreSQL 서버의 현재 시간을 구하는 쿼리 실행
        result = await db.execute(text("SELECT NOW();"))
        db_time = result.scalar()
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "message": "211.55.127.239 DB에 성공적으로 연결되었습니다.",
            "db_current_time": db_time
        }
    except Exception as e:
        return {
            "status": "error",
            "message": "DB 연결 실패",
            "details": str(e)
        }

# ===============================================================
# 경로 : src/controllers/account/auth_controller.py
# 명세 : 유저 계정 생성 및 소셜 연동, 로그인 API
# ===============================================================
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from pydantic import BaseModel, EmailStr
from typing import Optional
from src.models.User import User, ExternalProvider, UserRole
from config.db import get_db
from pydantic import BaseModel, EmailStr
from typing import Optional
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy.future import select
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from src.models.User import User, ExternalProvider

from pydantic import BaseModel
import jwt
import hashlib
import random
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone


router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/auth",
    tags=["Authentication"]
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------

# TODO: 실제 환경에서는 환경변수(.env)로 분리해야 합니다.

ALGORITHM = "HS256"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------

class LoginGeneralRequest(BaseModel):
    email: EmailStr
    password: str
    keep_login: bool = False  # Box #3 (로그인 상태 유지) 연동

class LoginSocialRequest(BaseModel):
    provider: str
    provider_id: str
    keep_login: bool = False  # Box #3 (로그인 상태 유지) 연동

    phone_number: str

    email: EmailStr
    password: str
    name: str
    phone_number: Optional[str] = None

    email: EmailStr
    name: str

    provider_id: str
    phone_number: Optional[str] = None

class LinkSocialProvider(BaseModel):
    email: EmailStr # 실제 환경에서는 JWT 토큰에서 추출한 user_id를 사용하는 것이 안전합니다.
    provider: str

@router.post("/signup/general", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
async def create_general_user(user_in: UserCreate, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Box #1: 일반 계정 생성 (비동기 처리)"""
    
    # 1. 이메일 중복 체크
    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.email == user_in.email))
    existing_user = result.scalars().first()
    if existing_user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="이미 존재하는 이메일입니다.")

    # TODO: 실제 구현 시 password는 반드시 해싱 처리해야 합니다.
    hashed_password = user_in.password 

    # 2. 유저 생성 (권한은 USER 고정)
    new_user = User(
        email=user_in.email,
        password=hashed_password,
        name=user_in.name, # 프런트의 '아이디(필수)'에 해당
        phone_number=user_in.phone_number,
        role=UserRole.USER 
    )
    db.add(new_user)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(new_user)
    
    return {"message": "일반 계정이 성공적으로 생성되었습니다.", "user_id": new_user.id}


@router.post("/signup/social", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
async def create_social_user(user_in: SocialUserCreate, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Box #2: 구글/애플 연동 계정 생성 (비동기 처리)"""
    
    if user_in.provider not in ["google", "apple"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="지원하지 않는 소셜 제공자입니다.")

    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.email == user_in.email))
    existing_user = result.scalars().first()
    if existing_user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="이미 이메일로 가입된 내역이 있습니다. 일반 로그인 후 연동해주세요.")

    # 1. 유저 생성 (비밀번호 NULL)
    new_user = User(
        email=user_in.email,
        password=None, 
        name=user_in.name,
        phone_number=user_in.phone_number,
        role=UserRole.USER
    )
    db.add(new_user)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(new_user)

    # 2. 소셜 연동 정보 추가
    new_provider = ExternalProvider(
        account_id=new_user.id,
        provider=user_in.provider,
        provider_id=user_in.provider_id
    )
    db.add(new_provider)
    await db.commit()

    return {"message": f"{user_in.provider} 연동 계정이 생성되었습니다.", "user_id": new_user.id}


@router.post("/link-social", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def link_social_to_general(link_in: LinkSocialProvider, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """기존 계정에 소셜 연동 추가 (비동기 처리)"""
    if link_in.provider not in ["google", "apple"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="지원하지 않는 소셜 제공자입니다.")

    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.email == link_in.email))
    user = result.scalars().first()
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="사용자를 찾을 수 없습니다.")

    existing_provider_result = await db.execute(
        select(ExternalProvider).where(
            ExternalProvider.account_id == user.id,
            ExternalProvider.provider == link_in.provider
        )
    )
    if existing_provider_result.scalars().first():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="이미 해당 소셜 계정이 연동되어 있습니다.")

    new_provider = ExternalProvider(
        account_id=user.id,
        provider=link_in.provider,
        provider_id=link_in.provider_id
    )
    db.add(new_provider)
    await db.commit()

    return {"message": f"계정에 {link_in.provider} 연동이 완료되었습니다."}




@router.post("/login/general", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def login_general(req: LoginGeneralRequest, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Box #1: 일반 로그인 처리 (Box #3 로그인 유지 여부 포함)"""
    
    # 1. 유저 조회 (비동기 처리)
    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.email == req.email))
    user = result.scalars().first()

    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="가입되지 않은 이메일입니다.")
    
    # 2. 비밀번호 검증 (실제로는 pwd_context.verify 등을 사용해야 함)
    if user.password != req.password: # TODO: 해시 비교 로직으로 변경
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.")

    # 3. 로그인 상태 유지 여부에 따른 토큰 만료 시간 설정
    token_expiry = "30days" if req.keep_login else "1day"

    # TODO: JWT 토큰 생성 및 반환
    return {
        "message": "로그인 성공",
        "access_token": "dummy_token_here",
        "user_id": user.id,
        "role": user.role,
        "expiry_policy": token_expiry
    }


@router.post("/login/social", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def login_social(req: LoginSocialRequest, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Box #2: 소셜 로그인 처리"""
    
    # 1. 외부 프로바이더 연동 정보 조회
    result = await db.execute(
        select(ExternalProvider).where(
            ExternalProvider.provider == req.provider,
            ExternalProvider.provider_id == req.provider_id
        )
    )
    provider_info = result.scalars().first()

    if not provider_info:
        # 연동 정보가 없으면 프런트에서 회원가입(Box #4) 페이지로 리다이렉트 하도록 유도
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="연동된 소셜 계정이 없습니다. 회원가입을 진행해주세요.")

    # 2. 유저 정보 획득
    user_result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.id == provider_info.account_id))
    user = user_result.scalars().first()

    token_expiry = "30days" if req.keep_login else "1day"

    return {
        "message": f"{req.provider} 소셜 로그인 성공",
        "access_token": "dummy_social_token_here",
        "user_id": user.id,
        "expiry_policy": token_expiry
    }


@router.post("/find-id", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def find_id(req: FindIdRequest, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Box #5: 아이디(이메일) 찾기"""
    
    result = await db.execute(
        select(User).where(
            User.name == req.name,
            User.phone_number == req.phone_number
        )
    )
    user = result.scalars().first()

    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="일치하는 계정 정보가 없습니다.")

    # 보안을 위해 이메일 일부 마스킹 처리 (예: test***@gmail.com)
    email_parts = user.email.split("@")
    masked_id = email_parts[0][:3] + "***" if len(email_parts[0]) > 3 else email_parts[0][:1] + "***"
    masked_email = f"{masked_id}@{email_parts[1]}"

    return {"message": "아이디 찾기 성공", "email": masked_email}

@router.delete("/withdraw", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def withdraw_account(req: WithdrawRequest, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """
    [통합 계정 탈퇴 API] 일반, 구글, 애플 공통
    
    TODO (추후 세션 적용 시):
    1. 보안: 현재는 req body로 user_id를 받지만, 실제 배포 시에는 Depends(get_current_user)를 통해 검증된 JWT에서 추출해야 합니다.
    2. 애플 완전 탈퇴(Revoke): 애플 정책상 앱 내 탈퇴 시 백엔드에서 Apple Auth Server의 'Revoke Token API'를 호출하여 토큰을 만료시켜야 합니다.
       (참고: httpx 등으로 https://appleid.apple.com/auth/revoke 호출 로직이 이곳에 추가되어야 함)
    """
    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.id == req.user_id))
    user = result.scalars().first()

    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="사용자를 찾을 수 없습니다.")

    # DB에 CASCADE가 설정되어 있으므로, user_table에서 삭제 시 
    # 연동된 external_providers 및 facility_users의 정보도 자동(연쇄) 삭제됩니다.
    await db.delete(user)
    await db.commit()

    return {"message": "계정이 성공적으로 탈퇴 처리되었습니다."}


# Pydantic Schemas 상단에 추가
class CheckAccountRequest(BaseModel):
    email: EmailStr

# 라우터 영역에 추가
@router.post("/check-account", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def check_account_existence(req: CheckAccountRequest, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Box #1 (아이디 찾기 페이지): 이메일로 가입 여부 및 소셜 연동 상태 확인"""
    
    # 1. 유저 조회
    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.email == req.email))
    user = result.scalars().first()

    if not user:
        return {"exists": False, "message": "해당 이메일로 가입된 계정이 없습니다."}

    # 2. 소셜 연동 여부 확인
    providers_result = await db.execute(
        select(ExternalProvider).where(ExternalProvider.account_id == user.id)
    )
    providers = providers_result.scalars().all()

    if providers:
        provider_names = ", ".join([p.provider for p in providers])
        return {
            "exists": True, 
            "message": f"해당 이메일은 {provider_names} 소셜 계정으로 연동되어 있습니다."
        }
    else:
        return {
            "exists": True, 
            "message": "해당 이메일은 일반 계정으로 가입되어 있습니다."
        }



# --- 헬퍼 함수 ---
def hash_code(code: str) -> str:
    """인증 코드를 해싱합니다."""
    return hashlib.sha256(code.encode()).hexdigest()



@router.post("/password/request-code", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def request_password_code(req: PasswordRequestCode, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Box #1: 이메일 확인 및 인증 코드 발송 (JWT 발급)"""
    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.email == req.email))
    user = result.scalars().first()
    
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="가입된 이메일이 없습니다.")

    # 1. 6자리 난수 코드 생성
    code = str(random.randint(100000, 999999))
    hashed_code = hash_code(code)

    # 2. JWT 생성 (만료시간 5분, 해싱된 코드 포함)
    expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=5)
    payload = {
        "sub": user.email, 
        "code_hash": hashed_code, 
        "exp": expire
    }
    token = jwt.encode(payload, SECRET_KEY, algorithm=ALGORITHM)

    # TODO: 실제 서비스에서는 이메일/SMS 전송 API를 호출하여 `code`를 발송해야 합니다.
    print(f"LOG[DEV] - {user.email} 복구 코드: {code}")

    # 프런트엔드 테스트를 위해 dev_code를 반환합니다. (실 배포 시 제거)
    return {
        "message": "이메일로 복구 코드가 발송되었습니다.", 
        "token": token,
        "dev_code": code 
    }

@router.post("/password/verify-code", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def verify_password_code(req: PasswordVerifyCode):
    """Box #2: 입력 코드 확인 및 재설정 토큰 발급"""
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(req.token, SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[ALGORITHM])
        email = payload.get("sub")
        code_hash = payload.get("code_hash")

        # 클라이언트가 보낸 코드를 해싱하여 토큰의 해시값과 비교
        if hash_code(req.code) != code_hash:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="코드가 일치하지 않습니다.")

        # 인증 성공 시, 비밀번호 변경 전용 새 JWT 발급 (만료시간 10분)
        expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=10)
        reset_token = jwt.encode(
            {"sub": email, "action": "reset_pw", "exp": expire}, 
            SECRET_KEY, 
            algorithm=ALGORITHM
        )

        return {"message": "코드 인증이 완료되었습니다.", "reset_token": reset_token}
        
    except jwt.ExpiredSignatureError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="인증 시간이 초과되었습니다. 코드를 다시 요청해주세요.")
    except jwt.InvalidTokenError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="유효하지 않은 토큰입니다.")

@router.post("/password/reset", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def reset_password(req: PasswordReset, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Box #3: 새 비밀번호 적용"""
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(req.token, SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[ALGORITHM])
        email = payload.get("sub")
        action = payload.get("action")

        if action != "reset_pw":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="잘못된 요청입니다.")

        result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.email == email))
        user = result.scalars().first()
        if not user:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="사용자를 찾을 수 없습니다.")

        # TODO: 실제 구현 시 반드시 해싱 함수(pwd_context.hash)를 적용해야 합니다.
        user.password = req.new_password
        await db.commit()

        return {"message": "비밀번호가 성공적으로 변경되었습니다."}

    except jwt.ExpiredSignatureError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="재설정 시간이 초과되었습니다. 처음부터 다시 진행해주세요.")
    except jwt.InvalidTokenError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="유효하지 않은 토큰입니다.")

@router.get("/{user_id}/role", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    user_role = result.scalars().first()

    if not user_role:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="사용자를 찾을 수 없습니다.")

    # user_role은 Enum 타입이므로 .value 또는 .name으로 문자열 변환하여 반환
    return {"role": user_role.value}


@router.get("/{user_id}/profile", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def get_user_profile(user_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """계정 수정 페이지 초기 로드용 정보 조회"""
    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.id == user_id))
    user = result.scalars().first()
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="사용자를 찾을 수 없습니다.")

    providers_result = await db.execute(
        select(ExternalProvider.provider).where(ExternalProvider.account_id == user_id)
    )
    linked_providers = providers_result.scalars().all()

    return {
        "name": user.name,
        "phone_number": user.phone_number or "",
        "linked_providers": linked_providers, # 예: ['google', 'apple']
        # 소셜로만 가입한 계정은 비밀번호가 없다. 앱에서 비밀번호 변경 UI를
        # 숨길지 판단하는 데 쓴다. (연동 여부만으로는 판단할 수 없다 —
        # 일반 가입 후 소셜을 추가로 연동한 계정은 비밀번호가 그대로 있다)
        "has_password": user.password is not None
    }

@router.put("/{user_id}/password", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def update_user_password(user_id: int, req: UpdatePasswordRequest, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Box #4: 비밀번호 수정"""
    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.id == user_id))
    user = result.scalars().first()
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="사용자를 찾을 수 없습니다.")
    
    # TODO: 실제로는 해시된 비밀번호를 비교(verify)해야 합니다.
    if user.password != req.current_password:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="현재 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.")
    
    # TODO: req.new_password를 해싱하여 저장해야 합니다.
    user.password = req.new_password
    await db.commit()
    return {"message": "비밀번호가 성공적으로 변경되었습니다."}

# ===============================================================
# 경로 : src/controllers/alarm/alarm.py
# 명세 : 알람 관련 API 엔드포인트 정의
# ===============================================================

from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy.future import select
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from config.db import get_db
from src.models.User import UserFcmToken
from src.models.Facility import  FacilityUser, Facility, FacilityRoom
from src.models.Monitoring import PointRole, Alarm

router = APIRouter(prefix="/alarms", tags=["Alarms"])

class RegistTokenRequest(BaseModel):
    fcm_token: str
    user_id: int

@router.post('/register')
async def registToken(user_in: RegistTokenRequest, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Box #1: FCM 토큰 등록 (비동기 처리)"""
    
    # 1. 중복 등록 확인
    mapping_result = await db.execute(
        select(UserFcmToken).where(UserFcmToken.fcm_token == user_in.fcm_token)
    )
    existing_token = mapping_result.scalars().first()

    # 이미 동일한 토큰이 등록된 경우
    if existing_token:
        # 소유 유저가 바뀌었을 가능성이 있으므로 user_id 업데이트 후 저장
        if existing_token.user_id != user_in.user_id:
            existing_token.user_id = user_in.user_id
            await db.commit()
            return {"message": "기존 토큰의 사용자 정보가 갱신되었습니다.", "user_id": user_in.user_id}
        
        return {"message": "이미 등록된 토큰입니다.", "user_id": user_in.user_id}

    new_token = UserFcmToken(
        fcm_token=user_in.fcm_token, 
        user_id=user_in.user_id
    )
    
    db.add(new_token)
    await db.commit()

    return {"message": "토큰이 성공적으로 등록되었습니다.", "user_id": new_token.user_id}



@router.get("/{user_id}")
async def get_alarms_for_user(user_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    stmt = (
        select(
            Alarm, 
            PointRole.facility_id, 
            Facility.name.label("facility_name"), 
            FacilityRoom.id.label("room_id"), 
            FacilityRoom.name.label("room_name")
        )
        .join(PointRole, Alarm.role_id == PointRole.id)
        .join(FacilityUser, FacilityUser.facility_id == PointRole.facility_id)
        .join(Facility, Facility.id == FacilityUser.facility_id)
        .join(FacilityRoom, PointRole.room_id == FacilityRoom.id)
        .where(
            FacilityUser.user_id == user_id,
            PointRole.is_current == True
        )
        .order_by(Alarm.timestamp.desc())
    )

    result = await db.execute(stmt)
    rows = result.all()

    alarms_with_facility = []
    for alarm, facility_id, facility_name, room_id, room_name in rows:
        alarm_dict = {
            column.name: getattr(alarm, column.name)
            for column in alarm.__table__.columns
        }
        alarm_dict.update({
            'facility_id': facility_id,
            'facility_name': facility_name,
            'room_id': room_id,
            'room_name': room_name
        })
        alarms_with_facility.append(alarm_dict)

    return {'alarms': alarms_with_facility}


@router.get("/facility/{facility_id}")
async def get_alarms_for_facility(facility_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    stmt = (
        select(
            Alarm, 
            PointRole.facility_id, 
            Facility.name.label("facility_name"), 
            FacilityRoom.id.label("room_id"), 
            FacilityRoom.name.label("room_name")
        )
        .join(PointRole, Alarm.role_id == PointRole.id)
        .join(Facility, Facility.id == PointRole.facility_id)
        .join(FacilityRoom, PointRole.room_id == FacilityRoom.id)
        .where(
            PointRole.facility_id == facility_id,
            PointRole.is_current == True
        )
        .order_by(Alarm.timestamp.desc())
    )

    result = await db.execute(stmt)
    rows = result.all()

    alarms_with_facility = []
    for alarm, fac_id, facility_name, room_id, room_name in rows:
        alarm_dict = {
            column.name: getattr(alarm, column.name)
            for column in alarm.__table__.columns
        }
        alarm_dict.update({
            'facility_id': fac_id,
            'facility_name': facility_name,
            'room_id': room_id,
            'room_name': room_name
        })
        alarms_with_facility.append(alarm_dict)

    return {'alarms': alarms_with_facility}

# --- Pydantic Schemas ---
class PostCreateReq(BaseModel):
    # board_id가 아니라 facility_id를 받는다. 앱은 시설 화면에서 글을 쓰는데
    # board_id와 facility_id가 항상 같지는 않아(예: board 6 = facility 8)
    # 앱이 시설 ID를 board_id로 보내면 엉뚱한 게시판에 쓰이거나 FK 오류가 난다.

    content: str

    content: Optional[str] = None
    image_ids: Optional[List[int]] = None

    content: str



# [S/bp-02] 통합 조회 보드 포스트 리스트 (검색 조건 추가)
@router.get("/integrated")
async def get_integrated_posts(
    user_id: int, 
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1), 
    limit: int = Query(30, le=100),
    search_type: str = Query("all"), # all, title, creator, content
    keyword: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)
):
    user_query = text("SELECT role FROM user_table WHERE id = :user_id")
    user_res = await db.execute(user_query, {"user_id": user_id})
    user = user_res.fetchone()

    if not user or user.role not in ['ADMIN', 'DEVELOPER']:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="통합 조회 보드에 접근할 권한이 없습니다.")

    offset = (page - 1) * limit

    # 검색 쿼리 조건 생성
    where_clause = "WHERE 1=1"
    params = {"limit": limit, "offset": offset, "user_id": user_id}

    if keyword and keyword.strip():
        params["keyword"] = f"%{keyword.strip()}%"
        if search_type == "title":
            where_clause += " AND p.title ILIKE :keyword"
        elif search_type == "creator":
            where_clause += " AND u.name ILIKE :keyword"
        elif search_type == "content":
            where_clause += " AND p.content ILIKE :keyword"
        else: # "all"
            where_clause += " AND (p.title ILIKE :keyword OR u.name ILIKE :keyword OR p.content ILIKE :keyword)"

    # COUNT 쿼리
    count_query = text(f"""
        SELECT COUNT(*) 
        FROM posts p
        JOIN boards b ON p.board_id = b.board_id
        JOIN user_table u ON p.creator_id = u.id
        {where_clause}
    """)
    total_count = (await db.execute(count_query, params)).scalar()

    # Data 쿼리
    query = text(f"""
        SELECT p.post_id, p.title, p.content, p.creator_id, p.created_at,
               b.facility_id, b.facility_name, u.name as creator_name
        FROM posts p
        JOIN boards b ON p.board_id = b.board_id
        JOIN user_table u ON p.creator_id = u.id
        {where_clause}
        ORDER BY p.created_at DESC
        LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset
    """)
    result = await db.execute(query, params)
    posts = result.fetchall()

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "data": [dict(row._mapping) for row in posts],
        "meta": {
            "page": page,
            "limit": limit,
            "total_count": total_count,
            "total_pages": (total_count + limit - 1) // limit
        }
    }


# [S/bp-03] 시설 조회 보드 포스트 리스트 (검색 조건 추가)
@router.get("/facility/{facility_id}")
async def get_facility_posts(
    facility_id: int, 
    user_id: int, 
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1), 
    limit: int = Query(30, le=100),
    search_type: str = Query("all"),
    keyword: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)
):
    auth_query = text("""
        SELECT 1 FROM user_table u
        LEFT JOIN facility_users fu ON u.id = fu.user_id AND fu.facility_id = :facility_id
        WHERE u.id = :user_id 
          AND (u.role IN ('ADMIN', 'DEVELOPER') OR fu.id IS NOT NULL)
    """)
    auth_res = await db.execute(auth_query, {"user_id": user_id, "facility_id": facility_id})
    if not auth_res.fetchone():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="해당 시설 보드에 접근할 권한이 없습니다.")

    offset = (page - 1) * limit
    where_clause = "WHERE b.facility_id = :facility_id"
    params = {"facility_id": facility_id, "limit": limit, "offset": offset, "user_id": user_id}

    if keyword and keyword.strip():
        params["keyword"] = f"%{keyword.strip()}%"
        if search_type == "title":
            where_clause += " AND p.title ILIKE :keyword"
        elif search_type == "creator":
            where_clause += " AND u.name ILIKE :keyword"
        elif search_type == "content":
            where_clause += " AND p.content ILIKE :keyword"
        else: # "all"
            where_clause += " AND (p.title ILIKE :keyword OR u.name ILIKE :keyword OR p.content ILIKE :keyword)"

    count_query = text(f"""
        SELECT COUNT(*) 
        FROM posts p 
        JOIN boards b ON p.board_id = b.board_id 
        JOIN user_table u ON p.creator_id = u.id
        {where_clause}
    """)
    total_count = (await db.execute(count_query, params)).scalar()

    query = text(f"""
        SELECT p.post_id, p.title, p.content, p.creator_id, p.created_at,
               b.facility_id, b.facility_name, u.name as creator_name
        FROM posts p
        JOIN boards b ON p.board_id = b.board_id
        JOIN user_table u ON p.creator_id = u.id
        {where_clause}
        ORDER BY p.created_at DESC
        LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset
    """)
    result = await db.execute(query, params)
    posts = result.fetchall()

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "data": [dict(row._mapping) for row in posts],
        "meta": {
            "page": page,
            "limit": limit,
            "total_count": total_count,
            "total_pages": (total_count + limit - 1) // limit
        }
    }


# ===============================================================
# [S/bp-04] 단일 포스트 상세 조회 (댓글 포함)
# ===============================================================
@router.get("/post/{post_id}")
async def get_post_detail(post_id: int, user_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    # 1. 포스트 기본 정보 조회 (p.images 제거)
    post_query = text("""
        SELECT p.post_id, p.title, p.content, p.creator_id, p.created_at, p.updated_at,
               b.facility_id, b.facility_name, u.name as creator_name
        FROM posts p
        JOIN boards b ON p.board_id = b.board_id
        JOIN user_table u ON p.creator_id = u.id
        WHERE p.post_id = :post_id
    """)
    post = (await db.execute(post_query, {"post_id": post_id})).fetchone()

    if not post:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="포스트를 찾을 수 없습니다.")

    # 2. 권한 검증
    auth_query = text("""
        SELECT 1 FROM user_table u
        LEFT JOIN facility_users fu ON u.id = fu.user_id AND fu.facility_id = :facility_id
        WHERE u.id = :user_id AND (u.role IN ('ADMIN', 'DEVELOPER') OR fu.id IS NOT NULL)
    """)
    if not (await db.execute(auth_query, {"user_id": user_id, "facility_id": post.facility_id})).fetchone():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="조회 권한이 없습니다.")

    # 3. 댓글 목록 같이 조회
    comment_query = text("""
        SELECT c.comment_id, c.content, c.creator_id, c.created_at, u.name as creator_name
        FROM comments c
        JOIN user_table u ON c.creator_id = u.id
        WHERE c.post_id = :post_id
        ORDER BY c.created_at ASC
    """)
    comments = (await db.execute(comment_query, {"post_id": post_id})).fetchall()

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "data": dict(post._mapping),
        "comments": [dict(c._mapping) for c in comments]
    }


# ===============================================================
# [S/bp-09] 에디터용 이미지 사전 업로드 (Pre-upload) API 명세
# ===============================================================
MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024  # 10MB
MAX_IMAGE_WIDTH = 1920
# Pillow 포맷명 -> 저장 확장자
ALLOWED_IMAGE_FORMATS = {"JPEG": "jpg", "PNG": "png", "GIF": "gif", "WEBP": "webp"}


@router.post("/image/upload")
async def upload_post_image(
    request: Request,
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)
):
    """에디터에서 쓸 이미지를 먼저 올려두고(post_id=NULL) image_id와 URL을 돌려준다.

    글을 저장할 때 create_post/update_post가 image_ids를 받아 post_id를 채운다.
    """
    raw = await file.read()

    if not raw:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="빈 파일입니다.")
    if len(raw) > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=413, detail="이미지는 10MB 이하만 올릴 수 있습니다.")

    # 확장자가 아니라 실제 내용으로 이미지인지 판단한다.
    try:
        image = Image.open(io.BytesIO(raw))
        image.verify()
        image = Image.open(io.BytesIO(raw))
    except Exception:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="이미지 파일이 아닙니다.")

    if image.format not in ALLOWED_IMAGE_FORMATS:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"지원하지 않는 이미지 형식입니다. ({image.format})")

    extension = ALLOWED_IMAGE_FORMATS[image.format]

    # 휴대폰 사진은 폭이 4000px를 넘기도 해서, 긴 쪽을 기준으로 줄여 저장한다.
    if image.width > MAX_IMAGE_WIDTH:
        ratio = MAX_IMAGE_WIDTH / image.width
        image = image.convert("RGB") if image.format == "JPEG" else image
        image = image.resize((MAX_IMAGE_WIDTH, round(image.height * ratio)))

    # [YYYY/MM/DD]/[UUID].[ext] 구조로 저장해 한 폴더에 파일이 몰리지 않게 한다.
    today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y/%m/%d")
    directory = os.path.join("uploads", today)
    os.makedirs(directory, exist_ok=True)

    filename = f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}.{extension}"
    save_path = os.path.join(directory, filename)
    image.save(save_path)

    file_size = os.path.getsize(save_path)
    # 앱/웹이 어느 호스트로 접속했든 그대로 쓸 수 있도록 절대 URL로 돌려준다.
    image_url = f"{str(request.base_url).rstrip('/')}/uploads/{today}/{filename}"

    insert_query = text("""
        INSERT INTO post_images (post_id, image_url, original_name, file_size, width, height, aspect_ratio)
        VALUES (NULL, :image_url, :original_name, :file_size, :width, :height, :aspect_ratio)
        RETURNING image_id
    """)
    result = await db.execute(insert_query, {
        "image_url": image_url,
        "original_name": file.filename or filename,
        "file_size": file_size,
        "width": image.width,
        "height": image.height,
        "aspect_ratio": round(image.width / image.height, 2) if image.height else 1,
    })
    image_id = result.scalar()
    await db.commit()

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "image_id": image_id,
        "image_url": image_url,
    }


# ===============================================================
# [S/bp-05] 포스트 생성
# ===============================================================
@router.post("/post")
async def create_post(user_id: int, req: PostCreateReq, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    # 0. 시설에 연결된 게시판 찾기
    board_res = await db.execute(
        text("SELECT board_id FROM boards WHERE facility_id = :facility_id"),
        {"facility_id": req.facility_id}
    )
    board_id = board_res.scalar()
    if board_id is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="이 시설에는 아직 게시판이 없습니다.")

    # 1. 포스트 삽입
    query = text("""
        INSERT INTO posts (board_id, creator_id, updater_id, title, content)
        VALUES (:board_id, :user_id, :user_id, :title, :content)
        RETURNING post_id
    """)
    result = await db.execute(query, {
        "board_id": board_id,
        "user_id": user_id,
        "title": req.title,
        "content": req.content
    })
    new_post_id = result.scalar()

    # 2. 선(Pre) 업로드된 이미지 ID 맵핑 (임시로 post_id=NULL 이었던 것을 업데이트)
    if req.image_ids:
        mapping_query = text("""
            UPDATE post_images 
            SET post_id = :post_id 
            WHERE image_id = ANY(:image_ids)
        """)
        await db.execute(mapping_query, {"post_id": new_post_id, "image_ids": req.image_ids})
        
    await db.commit()
    return {"status": "success", "post_id": new_post_id}

# ===============================================================
# [S/bp-06] 포스트 수정
# ===============================================================
@router.put("/post/{post_id}")
async def update_post(post_id: int, user_id: int, req: PostUpdateReq, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    # 1. 작성자 본인 확인
    check_query = text("SELECT creator_id FROM posts WHERE post_id = :post_id")
    post = (await db.execute(check_query, {"post_id": post_id})).fetchone()
    if not post or post.creator_id != user_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="수정 권한이 없습니다.")

    # 2. 포스트 업데이트
    update_query = text("""
        UPDATE posts 
        SET title = COALESCE(:title, title),
            content = COALESCE(:content, content),
            updater_id = :user_id
        WHERE post_id = :post_id
    """)
    await db.execute(update_query, {
        "title": req.title, 
        "content": req.content, 
        "user_id": user_id, 
        "post_id": post_id
    })

    # 3. 추가된 이미지가 있다면 맵핑
    if req.image_ids:
        mapping_query = text("""
            UPDATE post_images 
            SET post_id = :post_id 
            WHERE image_id = ANY(:image_ids)
        """)
        await db.execute(mapping_query, {"post_id": post_id, "image_ids": req.image_ids})

    await db.commit()
    return {"status": "success", "message": "포스트 수정 완료"}

# ===============================================================
# [S/bp-07] 포스트 삭제
# ===============================================================
@router.delete("/post/{post_id}")
async def delete_post(post_id: int, user_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    check_query = text("""
        SELECT p.creator_id, u.role 
        FROM posts p JOIN user_table u ON u.id = :user_id 
        WHERE p.post_id = :post_id
    """)
    post = (await db.execute(check_query, {"post_id": post_id, "user_id": user_id})).fetchone()
    
    # 작성자 본인이거나 관리자(ADMIN, DEVELOPER)인 경우 삭제 허용
    if not post or (post.creator_id != user_id and post.role not in ['ADMIN', 'DEVELOPER']):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="삭제 권한이 없습니다.")

    # ON DELETE CASCADE로 인해 comments, post_images 등도 자동 삭제됨
    await db.execute(text("DELETE FROM posts WHERE post_id = :post_id"), {"post_id": post_id})
    await db.commit()
    return {"status": "success", "message": "포스트 삭제 완료"}

# ===============================================================
# [S/bp-08] 댓글 생성 및 삭제
# ===============================================================
@router.post("/post/{post_id}/comment")
async def create_comment(post_id: int, user_id: int, req: CommentCreateReq, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    query = text("""
        INSERT INTO comments (post_id, creator_id, content)
        VALUES (:post_id, :user_id, :content)
        RETURNING comment_id
    """)
    result = await db.execute(query, {"post_id": post_id, "user_id": user_id, "content": req.content})
    await db.commit()
    return {"status": "success", "comment_id": result.scalar()}

@router.delete("/comment/{comment_id}")
async def delete_comment(comment_id: int, user_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    check_query = text("""
        SELECT c.creator_id, u.role 
        FROM comments c JOIN user_table u ON u.id = :user_id 
        WHERE c.comment_id = :comment_id
    """)
    comment = (await db.execute(check_query, {"comment_id": comment_id, "user_id": user_id})).fetchone()
    
    if not comment or (comment.creator_id != user_id and comment.role not in ['ADMIN', 'DEVELOPER']):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="삭제 권한이 없습니다.")

    await db.execute(text("DELETE FROM comments WHERE comment_id = :comment_id"), {"comment_id": comment_id})
    await db.commit()
    return {"status": "success", "message": "댓글 삭제 완료"}

# ===============================================================
# 경로 : src/controllers/chart/chart_controller.py
# 명세 : 차트 시각화를 위한 시계열 데이터 조회 API (TimescaleDB 연동)
# ===============================================================
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query, HTTPException
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import text
from config.db import get_db

router = APIRouter(prefix="/chart", tags=["Chart"])

# 프론트엔드 파라미터를 PostgreSQL Interval 문자열로 변환하는 매핑 딕셔너리
SCALE_MAPPING = {
    "10s": "10 seconds",
    "1m": "1 minute",
    "5m": "5 minutes",
    "10m": "10 minutes",
    "1h": "1 hour"
}

TIME_RANGE_MAPPING = {
    "1h": "1 hour",
    "12h": "12 hours",
    "1d": "1 day",
    "7d": "7 days",
    "15d": "15 days",
    "30d": "30 days"
}

@router.get("/compare/{facility_id}/available-roles")
async def get_comparable_roles(facility_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """
    같은 시설 안에서 2개 이상의 호실에 공통으로 존재하는 role_name 목록을 반환한다.
    (예: 모든 냉동실에 있는 "고내온도" 센서를 한 번에 비교하기 위한 선택지)
    """
    # bool_ 타입은 꺾은선 그래프로 겹쳐 비교하기 적합하지 않아 목록에서 제외한다
    # (프론트의 MainChartViewer는 수치형 mean 값을 기준으로 그리기 때문).
    query = text("""
        SELECT pr.role_name, COUNT(DISTINCT pr.room_id) AS room_count,
               MAX(prm.display_name) AS display_name
        FROM point_role_table pr
        LEFT JOIN point_role_metadata prm ON pr.id = prm.role_id
        WHERE pr.facility_id = :facility_id AND pr.is_current = TRUE
          AND pr.role_name NOT ILIKE 'bool\\_%' ESCAPE '\\'
        GROUP BY pr.role_name
        HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT pr.room_id) > 1
        ORDER BY pr.role_name
    """)
    rows = await db.execute(query, {"facility_id": facility_id})
    roles = [
        {
            "role_name": row.role_name,
            "display_name": row.display_name or row.role_name,
            "room_count": row.room_count,
        }
        for row in rows.fetchall()
    ]
    return {"status": "success", "data": {"roles": roles}}


@router.get("/compare/{facility_id}")
async def get_compare_data(
    facility_id: int,
    role_name: str = Query(..., description="호실 간 비교할 role_name"),
    scale: str = Query("1m", description="데이터 해상도"),
    time_range: str = Query("1d", description="조회 기간"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)
):
    """
    같은 시설의 여러 호실에서 동일한 role_name(예: 고내온도)을 가진 값들을
    호실별로 한 그래프에 겹쳐볼 수 있도록 반환한다.
    """
    if scale not in SCALE_MAPPING or time_range not in TIME_RANGE_MAPPING:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="유효하지 않은 파라미터입니다.")

    bucket_interval = SCALE_MAPPING[scale]
    range_interval = TIME_RANGE_MAPPING[time_range]

    roles_query = text("""
        SELECT pr.id AS role_id, pr.room_id, fr.name AS room_name
        FROM point_role_table pr
        JOIN facilities_room_table fr ON fr.id = pr.room_id
        WHERE pr.facility_id = :facility_id AND pr.role_name = :role_name AND pr.is_current = TRUE
        ORDER BY fr.name
    """)
    role_rows = (await db.execute(roles_query, {"facility_id": facility_id, "role_name": role_name})).fetchall()

    if not role_rows:
        return {"status": "success", "data": {"datasets": []}, "message": "비교할 호실 데이터가 없습니다."}

    target_roles = [row.role_id for row in role_rows]
    room_by_role = {row.role_id: {"room_id": row.room_id, "room_name": row.room_name} for row in role_rows}

    r_name_lower = role_name.lower()
    if "temp_" in r_name_lower:
        table_name, agg_columns, data_key_mapping = "temperature_numerical_logs", "AVG(temp_mean) AS mean, MIN(temp_min) AS min, MAX(temp_max) AS max", {"mean": "mean", "min": "min", "max": "max"}
    elif "gear_" in r_name_lower:
        table_name, agg_columns, data_key_mapping = "gear_numerical_logs", "AVG(gear_mean) AS mean, MIN(gear_min) AS min, MAX(gear_max) AS max", {"mean": "mean", "min": "min", "max": "max"}
    elif "static_" in r_name_lower:
        table_name, agg_columns, data_key_mapping = "equipment_static_logs", "AVG(setnum) AS mean", {"mean": "mean"}
    elif "bool_" in r_name_lower:
        table_name, agg_columns, data_key_mapping = "bool_logs", "BOOL_OR(status_value) AS status", {"status": "status"}
    else:
        return {"status": "success", "data": {"datasets": []}, "message": "지원되지 않는 데이터 타입입니다."}

    max_time_query = text(f"SELECT MAX(timestamp) FROM {table_name} WHERE role_id = ANY(:role_ids)")
    latest_time = (await db.execute(max_time_query, {"role_ids": target_roles})).scalar()

    if not latest_time:
        return {"status": "success", "data": {"datasets": []}, "message": "기록된 로그가 없습니다."}

    datasets_map: Dict[int, Any] = {
        r_id: {
            "role_id": r_id,
            "room_id": room_by_role[r_id]["room_id"],
            "room_name": room_by_role[r_id]["room_name"],
            "role_name": role_name,
            "data": [],
        } for r_id in target_roles
    }

    query = text(f"""
        SELECT role_id, time_bucket('{bucket_interval}', timestamp) AS bucket, {agg_columns}
        FROM {table_name}
        WHERE role_id = ANY(:role_ids)
          AND timestamp >= (CAST(:latest_time AS timestamptz) - INTERVAL '{range_interval}')
          AND timestamp <= CAST(:latest_time AS timestamptz)
        GROUP BY role_id, bucket ORDER BY bucket ASC
    """)
    rows = await db.execute(query, {"role_ids": target_roles, "latest_time": latest_time})
    for r in rows.fetchall():
        r_dict = dict(r._mapping)
        point = {"timestamp": r_dict["bucket"].isoformat()}
        for db_key, out_key in data_key_mapping.items(): point[out_key] = r_dict[db_key]
        datasets_map[r_dict["role_id"]]["data"].append(point)

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "meta": {"facility_id": facility_id, "role_name": role_name, "scale": scale, "time_range": time_range, "reference_time": latest_time.isoformat()},
        "data": {"datasets": list(datasets_map.values())}
    }


@router.get("/{facility_id}/{room_id}")
async def get_chart_data(
    facility_id: int,
    room_id: int,
    scale: str = Query("1m", description="데이터 해상도"),
    time_range: str = Query("1d", description="조회 기간"),
    role_ids: str = Query(..., description="조회할 PointRole ID 목록 (콤마로 구분)"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)
):
    if scale not in SCALE_MAPPING or time_range not in TIME_RANGE_MAPPING:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="유효하지 않은 파라미터입니다.")
    
    bucket_interval = SCALE_MAPPING[scale]
    range_interval = TIME_RANGE_MAPPING[time_range]
    target_roles = [int(r.strip()) for r in role_ids.split(",")]

    # 1. PointRole 메타데이터 조회 (타입 구분을 위해 role_name 활용)
    # [주석] chart_group_table 의 구조와 그룹핑은 타입을 가리지 않습니다.
    # 포인트롤 자체가 곧 타입 정보를 가지므로(role_name), 백엔드에서 이를 분석해 각각의 하이퍼테이블을 조회합니다.
    role_query = text("""
        SELECT id, role_name, target_value, target_low_limit, target_high_limit
        FROM point_role_table
        WHERE id = ANY(:role_ids) AND is_current = TRUE
    """)
    role_result = await db.execute(role_query, {"role_ids": target_roles})
    roles_info = {row.id: dict(row._mapping) for row in role_result.fetchall()}

    if not roles_info:
        return {"status": "success", "data": {"datasets": []}, "message": "유효한 Role 데이터가 없습니다."}

    # 역할별 쿼리 대상 분류
    temp_roles, gear_roles, static_roles, bool_roles = [], [], [], []
    for r_id, r_info in roles_info.items():
        r_name = r_info["role_name"].lower()
        if "temp_" in r_name: temp_roles.append(r_id)
        elif "gear_" in r_name: gear_roles.append(r_id)
        elif "static_" in r_name: static_roles.append(r_id)
        elif "bool_" in r_name: bool_roles.append(r_id)

    # 2. 통합 타임라인을 위한 기준 시간(가장 최근 데이터 시간) 확보
    # 전체 쿼리를 단순화하기 위해 temperature 테이블 기준으로 최근 시간을 잡음 (실 환경에선 전체 테이블 MAX 비교 권장)
    max_time_query = text("SELECT MAX(timestamp) FROM temperature_numerical_logs WHERE role_id = ANY(:role_ids)")
    latest_time = (await db.execute(max_time_query, {"role_ids": temp_roles if temp_roles else target_roles})).scalar()

    if not latest_time:
        return {"status": "success", "data": {"datasets": []}, "message": "기록된 로그가 없습니다."}

    # 3. 타입별 데이터 집계 및 병합 헬퍼 함수
    datasets_map: Dict[int, Any] = {
        r_id: {
            "role_id": r_id, "role_name": roles_info[r_id]["role_name"],
            "target_value": roles_info[r_id]["target_value"],
            "target_low_limit": roles_info[r_id]["target_low_limit"],
            "target_high_limit": roles_info[r_id]["target_high_limit"],
            "data": []
        } for r_id in roles_info.keys()
    }

    async def fetch_and_map(table_name: str, agg_columns: str, type_roles: List[int], data_key_mapping: dict):
        if not type_roles: return
        query = text(f"""
            SELECT role_id, time_bucket('{bucket_interval}', timestamp) AS bucket, {agg_columns}
            FROM {table_name}
            WHERE role_id = ANY(:role_ids)
              AND timestamp >= (CAST(:latest_time AS timestamptz) - INTERVAL '{range_interval}')
              AND timestamp <= CAST(:latest_time AS timestamptz)
            GROUP BY role_id, bucket ORDER BY bucket ASC
        """)
        rows = await db.execute(query, {"role_ids": type_roles, "latest_time": latest_time})
        for r in rows.fetchall():
            r_dict = dict(r._mapping)
            point = {"timestamp": r_dict["bucket"].isoformat()}
            for db_key, out_key in data_key_mapping.items(): point[out_key] = r_dict[db_key]
            datasets_map[r_dict["role_id"]]["data"].append(point)

    # 4. 각 하이퍼테이블 병렬/순차 조회
    await fetch_and_map("temperature_numerical_logs", "AVG(temp_mean) AS mean, MIN(temp_min) AS min, MAX(temp_max) AS max", temp_roles, {"mean": "mean", "min": "min", "max": "max"})
    await fetch_and_map("gear_numerical_logs", "AVG(gear_mean) AS mean, MIN(gear_min) AS min, MAX(gear_max) AS max", gear_roles, {"mean": "mean", "min": "min", "max": "max"})
    await fetch_and_map("equipment_static_logs", "AVG(setnum) AS mean", static_roles, {"mean": "mean"})
    await fetch_and_map("bool_logs", "BOOL_OR(status_value) AS status", bool_roles, {"status": "status"})

    # 5. 조회 중인 포인트롤에 걸린 알람을 같은 기간으로 함께 조회 (그래프 위에 발생 시점 표시용)
    alarm_query = text("""
        SELECT alarm_id, role_id, alarm_name, severity, timestamp
        FROM alarms
        WHERE role_id = ANY(:role_ids)
          AND timestamp >= (CAST(:latest_time AS timestamptz) - INTERVAL '{range_interval}')
          AND timestamp <= CAST(:latest_time AS timestamptz)
        ORDER BY timestamp ASC
    """.format(range_interval=range_interval))
    alarm_rows = await db.execute(alarm_query, {"role_ids": target_roles, "latest_time": latest_time})
    alarms = [
        {
            "alarm_id": row.alarm_id,
            "role_id": row.role_id,
            "alarm_name": row.alarm_name,
            "severity": row.severity,
            "timestamp": row.timestamp.isoformat(),
        }
        for row in alarm_rows.fetchall()
    ]

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "meta": {"facility_id": facility_id, "room_id": room_id, "scale": scale, "time_range": time_range, "reference_time": latest_time.isoformat()},
        "data": {"datasets": list(datasets_map.values()), "alarms": alarms}
    }

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from typing import List
from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import text
from config.db import get_db

router = APIRouter(prefix="/chart-mapping", tags=["Chart Mapping"])

# data_type 제거
class GroupCreateReq(BaseModel):
    facility_id: int
    room_id: int
    group_name: str
    role_ids: List[int]

@router.get("/roles/{facility_id}/{room_id}")
async def get_point_roles(facility_id: int, room_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    query = text("""
        SELECT id as role_id, role_name, target_value
        FROM point_role_table
        WHERE facility_id = :facility_id AND room_id = :room_id AND is_current = TRUE
    """)
    result = await db.execute(query, {"facility_id": facility_id, "room_id": room_id})
    roles = [dict(row._mapping) for row in result.fetchall()]
    return {"status": "success", "roles": roles}

@router.post("/group")
async def create_chart_group(req: GroupCreateReq, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    try:
        # data_type 관련 로직 제거
        group_query = text("""
            INSERT INTO chart_group_table (facility_id, room_id, group_name)
            VALUES (:facility_id, :room_id, :group_name)
            RETURNING id
        """)
        result = await db.execute(group_query, {
            "facility_id": req.facility_id, 
            "room_id": req.room_id,
            "group_name": req.group_name
        })
        new_group_id = result.scalar()

        if req.role_ids:
            mapping_query = text("""
                INSERT INTO chart_group_mapping (group_id, role_id)
                VALUES (:group_id, :role_id)
            """)
            for r_id in req.role_ids:
                await db.execute(mapping_query, {"group_id": new_group_id, "role_id": r_id})
        
        await db.commit()
        return {"status": "success", "message": "그룹 생성 완료", "group_id": new_group_id}
    except Exception as e:
        await db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@router.get("/groups/{facility_id}/{room_id}")
async def get_chart_groups(facility_id: int, room_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    # cg.data_type 제거
    query = text("""
        SELECT cg.id as group_id, cg.group_name,
               array_agg(cgm.role_id) as role_ids
        FROM chart_group_table cg
        LEFT JOIN chart_group_mapping cgm ON cg.id = cgm.group_id
        WHERE cg.facility_id = :facility_id AND cg.room_id = :room_id
        GROUP BY cg.id
    """)
    result = await db.execute(query, {"facility_id": facility_id, "room_id": room_id})
    groups = [dict(row._mapping) for row in result.fetchall()]
    return {"status": "success", "groups": groups}

# ===============================================================
# 경로 : src/controllers/chat/chat_controller.py
# 명세 : 메신저 채팅방 관리 및 웹소켓 통신 컨트롤러
# ===============================================================
import json
from typing import List, Dict, Optional
from pydantic import BaseModel
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import text
from config.db import get_db
from src.models.User import UserRole, User
from src.models.Chat import ChatParticipant, Message, MessageType, ChatRoomType, ChatRoom, MessagePin
from sqlalchemy.future import select

router = APIRouter(prefix="/chat", tags=["Chat"])

# --- [Pydantic Schemas] ---
class GroupChatCreateReq(BaseModel):
    room_name: str
    user_ids: List[int]

# --- [웹소켓 매니저] ---
class ConnectionManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.active_connections: Dict[int, List[WebSocket]] = {}

    async def connect(self, websocket: WebSocket, room_id: int):
        await websocket.accept()
        if room_id not in self.active_connections:
            self.active_connections[room_id] = []
        self.active_connections[room_id].append(websocket)

    def disconnect(self, websocket: WebSocket, room_id: int):
        if room_id in self.active_connections:
            self.active_connections[room_id].remove(websocket)
            if not self.active_connections[room_id]:
                del self.active_connections[room_id]

    async def broadcast_to_room(self, room_id: int, message: dict):
        if room_id in self.active_connections:
            for connection in self.active_connections[room_id]:
                await connection.send_text(json.dumps(message))

manager = ConnectionManager()

# --- [API Endpoints] ---

@router.get("/rooms/{facility_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def get_chat_rooms(facility_id: int, user_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """[Page 1: Box #2] 참여 중인 채팅방 목록 조회 (정렬 및 타 시설 필터링 적용)"""
    user_query = text("SELECT role FROM user_table WHERE id = :user_id")
    user = (await db.execute(user_query, {"user_id": user_id})).fetchone()
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="사용자를 찾을 수 없습니다.")
    
    is_admin = user.role in [UserRole.ADMIN.value, UserRole.DEVELOPER.value]

    # 다른 시설의 공식 채팅방(FACILITY) 제외 & 상대방 권한/시설 소속 여부 파악
    query = text("""
        SELECT cr.room_id, cr.room_name, cr.type, cr.facility_id, cp.unread_count,
               (SELECT content FROM messages m WHERE m.room_id = cr.room_id ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_message,
               (SELECT created_at FROM messages m WHERE m.room_id = cr.room_id ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_message_time,
               (
                   SELECT u.role 
                   FROM chatparticipants cp2 
                   JOIN user_table u ON cp2.user_id = u.id 
                   WHERE cp2.room_id = cr.room_id AND cp2.user_id != :user_id
                   LIMIT 1
               ) as other_user_role,
               (
                   SELECT COUNT(*)
                   FROM chatparticipants cp2
                   JOIN facility_users fu ON cp2.user_id = fu.user_id
                   WHERE cp2.room_id = cr.room_id AND cp2.user_id != :user_id AND fu.facility_id = :facility_id
               ) as other_user_in_facility
        FROM chatrooms cr
        JOIN chatparticipants cp ON cr.room_id = cp.room_id
        WHERE cp.user_id = :user_id
          AND (cr.type != 'FACILITY' OR cr.facility_id = :facility_id)
    """)
    result = await db.execute(query, {"user_id": user_id, "facility_id": facility_id})
    rooms = result.fetchall()

    processed_rooms = []
    for r in rooms:
        priority = 99
        if r.type == 'FACILITY':
            priority = 1
        elif r.type == 'DIRECT':
            if is_admin:
                # 관리자 정렬: 2. 관리자vs관리자, 3. 현재 시설 유저, 6. 기타 유저
                if r.other_user_role in ['ADMIN', 'DEVELOPER']: priority = 2
                elif r.other_user_in_facility > 0: priority = 3
                else: priority = 6
            else:
                priority = 2 # 유저에게 관리자 1:1은 시설방 다음으로 중요
        elif r.type == 'GROUP':
            priority = 5 # (4는 즐겨찾기 용도로 비워둠)

        room_name_display = r.room_name
        if r.type == 'DIRECT' and not room_name_display:
            room_name_display = "1:1 채팅방"

        processed_rooms.append({
            "room_id": r.room_id,
            "room_name": room_name_display,
            "type": r.type,
            "last_message": r.last_message or "메시지가 없습니다.",
            "unread_count": r.unread_count,
            "sort_priority": priority,
            "last_message_time": str(r.last_message_time) if r.last_message_time else ""
        })

    # 정렬: 1순위(우선순위 오름차순), 2순위(최신 메시지 내림차순)
    processed_rooms.sort(key=lambda x: x["last_message_time"], reverse=True)
    processed_rooms.sort(key=lambda x: x["sort_priority"])

    return {"status": "success", "rooms": processed_rooms, "is_admin": is_admin}

@router.post("/group", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
async def create_group_chat(req: GroupChatCreateReq, user_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """[관리자 전용] 단체 채팅방 생성"""
    new_room = ChatRoom(room_name=req.room_name, type=ChatRoomType.GROUP)
    db.add(new_room)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(new_room)

    participants = [ChatParticipant(room_id=new_room.room_id, user_id=user_id)]
    for uid in req.user_ids:
        if uid != user_id:
            participants.append(ChatParticipant(room_id=new_room.room_id, user_id=uid))
    
    db.add_all(participants)
    await db.commit()
    return {"status": "success", "message": "단체 채팅방이 생성되었습니다.", "room_id": new_room.room_id}

@router.delete("/room/{room_id}")
async def leave_or_delete_room(room_id: int, user_id: int, action: str = "delete", db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """채팅방 삭제 보호 적용 (관리자라도 FACILITY, DIRECT 삭제 불가)"""
    user_res = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.id == user_id))
    user = user_res.scalars().first()
    is_admin = user.role in [UserRole.ADMIN, UserRole.DEVELOPER]

    room_res = await db.execute(select(ChatRoom).where(ChatRoom.room_id == room_id))
    room = room_res.scalars().first()
    if not room: raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="방이 없습니다.")

    if action == "delete":
        if not is_admin: raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="삭제 권한이 없습니다.")
        if room.type in [ChatRoomType.FACILITY, ChatRoomType.DIRECT]:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"[{room.type.value}] 방은 시스템 정책상 삭제할 수 없습니다.")
        
        await db.delete(room)
        await db.commit()
        return {"status": "success", "message": "단체 채팅방이 삭제되었습니다."}
    
    elif action == "hide":
        part = (await db.execute(select(ChatParticipant).where(ChatParticipant.room_id == room_id, ChatParticipant.user_id == user_id))).scalars().first()
        if part: await db.delete(part)
        await db.commit()
        return {"status": "success", "message": "채팅방에서 나갔습니다."}

@router.post("/room/{room_id}/pin/{message_id}")
async def toggle_pin_message(room_id: int, message_id: int, user_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """메시지 고정 토글 (관리자는 타인 메시지 고정 해제 가능)"""
    # 1. 고정 상태 확인
    pin_res = await db.execute(select(MessagePin).where(MessagePin.message_id == message_id))
    pin = pin_res.scalars().first()

    # 2. 유저 권한 확인
    user_res = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.id == user_id))
    user = user_res.scalars().first()
    is_admin = user.role in [UserRole.ADMIN, UserRole.DEVELOPER]

    if pin:
        # 고정 해제 (관리자 무조건 가능)
        await db.delete(pin)
        await db.commit()
        return {"status": "success", "message": "메시지 고정이 해제되었습니다."}
    else:
        # 새 고정
        new_pin = MessagePin(message_id=message_id)
        db.add(new_pin)
        await db.commit()
        return {"status": "success", "message": "메시지가 고정되었습니다."}

@router.put("/room/{room_id}/read")
async def update_last_read(room_id: int, user_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    query = text("""
        UPDATE chatparticipants 
        SET unread_count = 0, last_read_message_id = (SELECT COALESCE(MAX(message_id), 0) FROM messages WHERE room_id = :room_id)
        WHERE room_id = :room_id AND user_id = :user_id
    """)
    await db.execute(query, {"room_id": room_id, "user_id": user_id})
    await db.commit()
    return {"status": "success"}

@router.get("/room/{room_id}/messages")
async def get_chat_messages(room_id: int, user_id: int, limit: int = 50, offset: int = 0, keyword: Optional[str] = None, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """과거 메시지 로드 (답장 원문, 고정 여부 포함)"""
    where_clause = "WHERE m.room_id = :room_id"
    params = {"room_id": room_id, "limit": limit, "offset": offset}
    
    if keyword and keyword.strip():
        where_clause += " AND m.content ILIKE :keyword"
        params["keyword"] = f"%{keyword.strip()}%"

    query = text(f"""
        SELECT m.message_id, m.user_id, m.message_type, m.content, m.created_at,
               pm.content as parent_content,
               (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM messages_pin p WHERE p.message_id = m.message_id) as is_pinned
        FROM messages m
        LEFT JOIN messages pm ON m.parent_message_id = pm.message_id
        {where_clause}
        ORDER BY m.created_at DESC
        LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset
    """)
    result = await db.execute(query, params)
    messages = result.fetchall()

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "messages": [{
            "message_id": r.message_id, "user_id": r.user_id,
            "message_type": r.message_type.value if hasattr(r.message_type, 'value') else r.message_type,
            "content": r.content,
            "created_at": r.created_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M") if r.created_at else "",
            "parent_content": r.parent_content,
            "is_pinned": r.is_pinned > 0
        } for r in messages]
    }

@router.websocket("/ws/{room_id}/{user_id}")
async def chat_websocket(websocket: WebSocket, room_id: int, user_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    await manager.connect(websocket, room_id)
    try:
        while True:
            data = await websocket.receive_text()
            payload = json.loads(data)
            
            msg_type = MessageType.IMAGE if payload.get("type") == "IMAGE" else MessageType.TEXT
            parent_id = payload.get("parent_message_id") # 답장 시 활용

            new_msg = Message(
                room_id=room_id, user_id=user_id, message_type=msg_type, 
                content=payload.get("content", ""), parent_message_id=parent_id
            )
            db.add(new_msg)
            await db.commit()
            await db.refresh(new_msg)
            
            update_unread = text("UPDATE chatparticipants SET unread_count = unread_count + 1 WHERE room_id = :room_id AND user_id != :user_id")
            await db.execute(update_unread, {"room_id": room_id, "user_id": user_id})
            await db.commit()

            parent_content = None
            if parent_id:
                parent_res = await db.execute(select(Message.content).where(Message.message_id == parent_id))
                parent_content = parent_res.scalar()

            broadcast_msg = {
                "type": "chat", "message_id": new_msg.message_id, "user_id": user_id,
                "message_type": msg_type.value, "content": new_msg.content,
                "created_at": new_msg.created_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M") if new_msg.created_at else "Just now",
                "parent_content": parent_content,
                "is_pinned": False
            }
            await manager.broadcast_to_room(room_id, broadcast_msg)
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        manager.disconnect(websocket, room_id)
