# ===============================================================
# 경로 : src/controllers/facility/facility_controller.py
# 명세 : 시설 관련 API 엔드포인트 정의
# ===============================================================
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import List, Optional, Dict
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy.future import select
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from config.db import get_db
from src.models.User import User, UserRole
from src.models.Facility import Facility, FacilityUser, FacilityRoom
from src.models.Monitoring import PointRole, RemoteControlLog, BoolLog, TemperatureNumericalLog, EquipmentStaticLog
from src.models.Chat import ChatRoom, ChatParticipant
import src.services.alarm as alarm_services
import json
import re
from datetime import datetime, timezone

router = APIRouter(prefix="/facilities", tags=["Facilities"])

class RegisterFacilityRequest(BaseModel):
    access_code: str

@router.get("/{user_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def get_user_facilities(user_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """메인 색인 페이지 Box #2 및 시설 등록 페이지 Box #3용 시설 목록 조회"""
    # 1. 유저 권한 확인
    user_result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.id == user_id))
    user = user_result.scalars().first()
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="사용자를 찾을 수 없습니다.")

    # 2. 권한별 시설 목록 반환
    if user.role in [UserRole.ADMIN, UserRole.DEVELOPER]:
        # 관리자/개발자: 모든 활성 시설 조회
        facilities_result = await db.execute(select(Facility).where(Facility.is_active == True))
        facilities = facilities_result.scalars().all()
    else:
        # 일반 유저: 매핑 테이블(facility_users)에 등록된 시설만 조회 (JOIN 대체 로직)
        mapping_result = await db.execute(select(FacilityUser.facility_id).where(FacilityUser.user_id == user_id))
        facility_ids = mapping_result.scalars().all()
        
        if not facility_ids:
            return {"facilities": []}
            
        facilities_result = await db.execute(select(Facility).where(Facility.id.in_(facility_ids), Facility.is_active == True))
        facilities = facilities_result.scalars().all()

    return {
        "facilities": [
            {"id": f.id, "name": f.name, "access_code": f.access_code} 
            for f in facilities
        ]
    }

@router.post("/{user_id}/register", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def register_facility(user_id: int, req: RegisterFacilityRequest, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Box #2: 시설 접근 코드로 시설 등록"""
    # 1. 접근 코드로 시설 찾기
    fac_result = await db.execute(select(Facility).where(Facility.access_code == req.access_code, Facility.is_active == True))
    facility = fac_result.scalars().first()
    
    if not facility:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="유효하지 않거나 비활성화된 접근 코드입니다.")

    # 2. 중복 등록 확인
    mapping_result = await db.execute(
        select(FacilityUser).where(FacilityUser.facility_id == facility.id, FacilityUser.user_id == user_id)
    )
    if mapping_result.scalars().first():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="이미 등록된 시설입니다.")

    # 3. 매핑 추가
    new_mapping = FacilityUser(facility_id=facility.id, user_id=user_id)
    db.add(new_mapping)
    await db.commit()

    # 4. 해당하는 채팅방 가져오기
    #chatroom_result = await db.excute(select(ChatRoom).where(ChatRoom.facility_id == facility.id))

    # 5. 유저 채팅방 입장
    

    new_mapping = ChatRoom(facility_id=facility.id, user_id=user_id)
    db.add(new_mapping)
    await db.commit()


    return {"message": "시설이 성공적으로 등록되었습니다.", "facility_name": facility.name}

@router.delete("/{user_id}/unlink/{facility_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
async def unlink_facility(user_id: int, facility_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Box #3: 시설 연동 해제"""
    mapping_result = await db.execute(
        select(FacilityUser).where(FacilityUser.facility_id == facility_id, FacilityUser.user_id == user_id)
    )
    mapping = mapping_result.scalars().first()
    
    if not mapping:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="해당 시설에 등록되어 있지 않습니다.")

    await db.delete(mapping)
    await db.commit()
    return {"message": "시설 연동이 해제되었습니다."}

@router.get("/rooms/{facility_id}")
async def get_facility_rooms(facility_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    room_result = await db.execute(
        select(FacilityRoom).where(FacilityRoom.facility_id == facility_id)
    )
    rooms = room_result.scalars().all()

    return {
        "rooms": [
            {"id": r.id, "name": r.name}
            for r in rooms
        ]
    }

#서버-시설 간 연결 관리 및 브로드캐스팅
set_sockets: Dict[int, WebSocket] = {}
get_sockets: Dict[int, List[WebSocket]] = {}

#서버-시설 간 연결 관리 및 브로드캐스팅
@router.websocket("/data/set/{facility_id}")
async def set_facility_data(websocket: WebSocket, facility_id: int):
    if facility_id in set_sockets:
        await websocket.accept()
        await websocket.close(
            code=status.WS_1008_POLICY_VIOLATION, 
            reason="This facility is already sending data from another device."
        )
        return

    await websocket.accept()
    set_sockets[facility_id] = websocket

    print(f"[장비 연결 성공] Facility {facility_id} 데이터 수신 시작")

    try:
        while True:
            data = await websocket.receive_text()
            #print(data)

            data_dict = json.loads(data)

            if "time" in data_dict:
                bool_logs = []
                real_logs = []
                static_logs = []
                roles = await alarm_services.scan_roles_asc_name(facility_id)
                #print(roles)
                #print(f"현재시간: {datetime.strptime(data_dict["time"], "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")}")

                dt_object = datetime.strptime(data_dict["time"], "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")

                for datakey, labled_dict in data_dict.items():
                    try:
                        json_dict = json.loads(labled_dict) if isinstance(labled_dict, str) else labled_dict
                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
                        continue

                    for label, values in json_dict.items():
                        if label in roles:
                            role_obj = roles[label]

                            #print(f"✅ 매칭 성공! 라벨: {label} | 롤 정보: {values}")

                            if datakey == "bool":
                                bool_logs.append(BoolLog(
                                    role_id=role_obj.id,
                                    timestamp=dt_object,
                                    status_value=values.get("last_value")
                                ))
                            elif datakey == "real":
                                real_logs.append(TemperatureNumericalLog(
                                    role_id = role_obj.id,
                                    timestamp = dt_object,
                                    temp_min = values.get("val_min"),
                                    temp_max = values.get("val_max"),
                                    temp_mean = values.get("val_mean"),
                                    temp_stdev = values.get("val_std"),
                                    sample_count = values.get("count")
                                ))
                            elif datakey == "real_static":
                                static_logs.append(EquipmentStaticLog(
                                    role_id=role_obj.id,
                                    timestamp=dt_object,
                                    setnum=values.get("last_value")
                                ))
                        else:
                            pass
                
                async for db in get_db():
                    iscommit = False
                    if bool_logs:
                        db.add_all(bool_logs)
                        iscommit = True
                    if real_logs:
                        db.add_all(real_logs)
                        iscommit = True
                    if static_logs:
                        db.add_all(static_logs)
                        iscommit = True
                    if iscommit:
                        await db.commit()
                    
                    break

            if "datas" in data_dict:
                #역할 스캔
                roles = await alarm_services.scan_roles(facility_id)

                #알람 출력
                await alarm_services.check_roles(facility_id, data_dict["datas"], roles)

                # 모니터링 출력 
                if facility_id in get_sockets:
                    for client in get_sockets[facility_id][:]:
                        try:
                            await client.send_text(data)
                        except Exception:
                            if client in get_sockets[facility_id]:
                                get_sockets[facility_id].remove(client)

            if "status" in data_dict.keys():
                if "SUCCESS" in data_dict["status"]:
                    datas = re.split(r"[.]", data_dict["request_id"])
                    print(datas)

                    async for db in get_db():
                        stmt = select(PointRole.id).where(PointRole.role_name == datas[1]).where(PointRole.room_id == int(datas[0]))

                        result = await db.execute(stmt)

                        role_id = result.scalars().first()

                        if not role_id:
                            print(f"⚠️ [로그 저장 실패] 매칭되는 PointRole을 찾을 수 없습니다. (room_id: {datas[0]}, role_name: {datas[1]})")
                            # 필요하다면 여기서 에러 응답을 리턴하거나 로직을 중단합니다.
                            break

                        now_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

                        new_log = RemoteControlLog(
                            role_id = role_id,
                            control_value=data_dict["request_val"],
                            timestamp=now_time
                        )

                        db.add(new_log)
                        await db.commit()

                        break
                        

    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        print(f"[장비 연결 종료] Facility {facility_id} 수신 중지")
        if facility_id in set_sockets and set_sockets[facility_id] == websocket:
            del set_sockets[facility_id]

#서버-앱 간 연결 관리
@router.websocket("/data/get/{facility_id}")
async def get_facility_data(websocket: WebSocket, facility_id: int):
    print("접속 시도", facility_id)
    await websocket.accept()
    
    if facility_id not in get_sockets:
        get_sockets[facility_id] = []
    get_sockets[facility_id].append(websocket)
    print(f"[앱 연결 성공] Facility {facility_id} 모니터링 시작 (현재 접속자: {len(get_sockets[facility_id])}명)")

    try:
        while True:
            recv = await websocket.receive_text()

            if recv:
                await set_sockets[facility_id].send_text(recv)
            
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        print(f"[앱 연결 종료] Facility {facility_id} 모니터링 중지")
        if facility_id in get_sockets and websocket in get_sockets[facility_id]:
            get_sockets[facility_id].remove(websocket)
            if not get_sockets[facility_id]:
                del get_sockets[facility_id]

# 웹용도 
# 경로 : src/controllers/facility/facility_management_controller.py
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional, List
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import text
from config.db import get_db
from datetime import datetime, date, timezone

router = APIRouter(prefix="/facility-manager", tags=["Facility Manager"])

# ─── 날짜/시간 파싱 헬퍼 함수 ───
def parse_dt(dt_str: Optional[str]) -> Optional[datetime]:
    if not dt_str or not dt_str.strip():
        return None
    try:
        # ISO 포맷 ('2026-08-28T08:23', '2026-08-28T08:23:00' 등) 파싱
        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(dt_str.strip())
        if dt.tzinfo is None:
            # 타임존이 없을 경우 UTC로 지정
            dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        return dt
    except Exception:
        return None

def parse_date(d_str: Optional[str]) -> Optional[date]:
    if not d_str or not d_str.strip():
        return None
    try:
        return date.fromisoformat(d_str.strip())
    except Exception:
        return None

class FacilityCreateReq(BaseModel):
    name: str
    access_code: str
    start_date: Optional[str] = None
    end_date: Optional[str] = None

class FacilityUpdateReq(BaseModel):
    name: Optional[str] = None
    access_code: Optional[str] = None
    start_date: Optional[str] = None
    end_date: Optional[str] = None

class RoomCreateReq(BaseModel):
    name: str
    status: Optional[str] = "OPERATING"
    operation_start_date: Optional[str] = None
    operation_end_date: Optional[str] = None

class RoomUpdateReq(BaseModel):
    name: Optional[str] = None
    status: Optional[str] = None
    operation_start_date: Optional[str] = None
    operation_end_date: Optional[str] = None

class PointRoleCreateReq(BaseModel):
    role_name: str
    display_name: Optional[str] = None
    short_name: Optional[str] = None
    data_type: Optional[str] = "TEMP" # TEMP, GEAR, BOOL, STATIC
    target_value: Optional[str] = None
    target_low_limit: Optional[str] = None
    target_high_limit: Optional[str] = None
    valid_from: Optional[str] = None
    valid_to: Optional[str] = None

class PointRoleUpdateReq(BaseModel):
    display_name: Optional[str] = None
    short_name: Optional[str] = None
    data_type: Optional[str] = None
    target_value: Optional[str] = None
    target_low_limit: Optional[str] = None
    target_high_limit: Optional[str] = None
    valid_from: Optional[str] = None
    valid_to: Optional[str] = None

# ─── 시설 관리 ───
@router.get("/facilities")
async def get_all_facilities(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    sync_query = text("UPDATE facilities_table SET is_active = (end_date IS NULL OR end_date >= CURRENT_DATE)")
    await db.execute(sync_query)
    await db.commit()

    query = text("""
        SELECT id, name, access_code, is_active, start_date, end_date
        FROM facilities_table ORDER BY id ASC
    """)
    result = await db.execute(query)
    return {"status": "success", "facilities": [dict(r._mapping) for r in result.fetchall()]}

@router.post("/facility", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
async def create_facility(req: FacilityCreateReq, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    try:
        s_date = parse_date(req.start_date)
        e_date = parse_date(req.end_date)
        is_act = (e_date is None or e_date >= date.today())

        fac_query = text("""
            INSERT INTO facilities_table (name, access_code, is_active, start_date, end_date)
            VALUES (:name, :access_code, :is_active, :start_date, :end_date) 
            RETURNING id, name
        """)
        fac_res = await db.execute(fac_query, {
            "name": req.name, "access_code": req.access_code,
            "is_active": is_act, "start_date": s_date, "end_date": e_date
        })
        fac_id = fac_res.fetchone().id

        # 000호(공용) 자동 생성
        await db.execute(text("""
            INSERT INTO facilities_room_table (facility_id, name, status)
            VALUES (:facility_id, :name, 'OPERATING')
        """), {"facility_id": fac_id, "name": f"{req.name}-000호(공용)"})

        # 게시판 자동 생성
        await db.execute(text("""
            INSERT INTO boards (facility_id, facility_name)
            VALUES (:facility_id, :name)
        """), {"facility_id": fac_id, "name": f"{req.name} 메인 게시판"})

        # 공식 채팅방 생성
        chat_res = await db.execute(text("""
            INSERT INTO chatrooms (facility_id, room_name, type)
            VALUES (:facility_id, :room_name, 'FACILITY') RETURNING room_id
        """), {"facility_id": fac_id, "room_name": f"{req.name} 공식 채팅방"})
        room_id = chat_res.scalar()

        # 관리자 계정 자동 참여
        await db.execute(text("""
            INSERT INTO chatparticipants (room_id, user_id)
            SELECT :room_id, id FROM user_table WHERE role IN ('ADMIN', 'DEVELOPER')
        """), {"room_id": room_id})

        await db.commit()
        return {"status": "success", "facility_id": fac_id, "message": "시설 및 연동 리소스가 자동 생성되었습니다."}
    except Exception as e:
        await db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e))

@router.put("/facility/{facility_id}")
async def update_facility(facility_id: int, req: FacilityUpdateReq, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    s_date = parse_date(req.start_date)
    e_date = parse_date(req.end_date)
    is_act = (e_date is None or e_date >= date.today())

    query = text("""
        UPDATE facilities_table
        SET name = COALESCE(:name, name),
            access_code = COALESCE(:access_code, access_code),
            start_date = :start_date,
            end_date = :end_date,
            is_active = :is_active
        WHERE id = :facility_id
    """)
    await db.execute(query, {
        "facility_id": facility_id, "name": req.name, "access_code": req.access_code,
        "start_date": s_date, "end_date": e_date, "is_active": is_act
    })
    await db.commit()
    return {"status": "success", "message": "시설 정보가 수정되었습니다."}

@router.get("/facility/{facility_id}/users")
async def get_facility_users(facility_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    query = text("""
        SELECT u.id, u.email, u.name, u.role, u.phone_number, fu.created_at as joined_at
        FROM facility_users fu
        JOIN user_table u ON fu.user_id = u.id
        WHERE fu.facility_id = :facility_id AND u.role = 'USER'
        ORDER BY u.id ASC
    """)
    res = await db.execute(query, {"facility_id": facility_id})
    return {"status": "success", "users": [dict(r._mapping) for r in res.fetchall()]}

# ─── 호실 관리 ───
@router.post("/facility/{facility_id}/room", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
async def create_room(facility_id: int, req: RoomCreateReq, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    s_date = parse_date(req.operation_start_date)
    e_date = parse_date(req.operation_end_date)

    query = text("""
        INSERT INTO facilities_room_table (facility_id, name, status, operation_start_date, operation_end_date)
        VALUES (:facility_id, :name, CAST(:status AS room_status_type), :start_date, :end_date)
        RETURNING id
    """)
    res = await db.execute(query, {
        "facility_id": facility_id, "name": req.name, "status": req.status,
        "start_date": s_date, "end_date": e_date
    })
    new_room_id = res.scalar()
    await db.commit()
    return {"status": "success", "room_id": new_room_id, "message": "호실이 추가되었습니다."}

@router.put("/room/{room_id}")
async def update_room(room_id: int, req: RoomUpdateReq, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    s_date = parse_date(req.operation_start_date)
    e_date = parse_date(req.operation_end_date)

    query = text("""
        UPDATE facilities_room_table
        SET name = COALESCE(:name, name),
            status = COALESCE(CAST(:status AS room_status_type), status),
            operation_start_date = :start_date,
            operation_end_date = :end_date
        WHERE id = :room_id
    """)
    await db.execute(query, {
        "room_id": room_id, "name": req.name, "status": req.status,
        "start_date": s_date, "end_date": e_date
    })
    await db.commit()
    return {"status": "success", "message": "호실 정보가 수정되었습니다."}

@router.delete("/room/{room_id}")
async def delete_room(room_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    check_query = text("SELECT name FROM facilities_room_table WHERE id = :room_id")
    res = await db.execute(check_query, {"room_id": room_id})
    room = res.fetchone()
    if not room:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="호실을 찾을 수 없습니다.")
    if "000호" in room.name or "공용" in room.name:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="공용 호실(000호)은 단독으로 삭제할 수 없습니다. (시설 삭제 시에만 자동 삭제)")

    await db.execute(text("DELETE FROM facilities_room_table WHERE id = :room_id"), {"room_id": room_id})
    await db.commit()
    return {"status": "success", "message": "호실이 삭제되었습니다."}

# ─── 포인트 롤 관리 (DataError 해결) ───
@router.get("/room/{room_id}/roles")
async def get_roles_by_room(room_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    sync_roles_query = text("""
        UPDATE point_role_table 
        SET is_current = (valid_to IS NULL OR valid_to >= CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
        WHERE room_id = :room_id
    """)
    await db.execute(sync_roles_query, {"room_id": room_id})
    await db.commit()

    query = text("""
        SELECT 
            pr.id AS role_id, pr.facility_id, pr.room_id, pr.role_name,
            pr.target_value, pr.target_low_limit, pr.target_high_limit,
            pr.valid_from, pr.valid_to, pr.is_current,
            prm.display_name, prm.short_name, prm.data_type
        FROM point_role_table pr
        LEFT JOIN point_role_metadata prm ON pr.id = prm.role_id
        WHERE pr.room_id = :room_id
        ORDER BY pr.id ASC
    """)
    res = await db.execute(query, {"room_id": room_id})
    return {"status": "success", "roles": [dict(r._mapping) for r in res.fetchall()]}

@router.post("/facility/{facility_id}/room/{room_id}/role", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
async def create_point_role(facility_id: int, room_id: int, req: PointRoleCreateReq, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    try:
        # 1. 날짜/시간 파싱 (Python datetime 객체 생성)
        v_from = parse_dt(req.valid_from) or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        v_to = parse_dt(req.valid_to)
        now_utc = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        is_cur = (v_to is None or v_to >= now_utc)

        # 2. 포인트롤 생성
        role_query = text("""
            INSERT INTO point_role_table (
                facility_id, room_id, role_name, target_value, target_low_limit, target_high_limit,
                valid_from, valid_to, is_current
            ) VALUES (
                :facility_id, :room_id, :role_name, :target_value, :target_low_limit, :target_high_limit,
                :valid_from, :valid_to, :is_current
            ) RETURNING id
        """)
        res = await db.execute(role_query, {
            "facility_id": facility_id, "room_id": room_id, "role_name": req.role_name.strip(),
            "target_value": req.target_value, "target_low_limit": req.target_low_limit, "target_high_limit": req.target_high_limit,
            "valid_from": v_from, "valid_to": v_to, "is_current": is_cur
        })
        new_role_id = res.scalar()

        # 3. 메타데이터 생성
        meta_query = text("""
            INSERT INTO point_role_metadata (role_id, display_name, short_name, data_type)
            VALUES (:role_id, NULLIF(:display_name, ''), NULLIF(:short_name, ''), CAST(:data_type AS point_data_type))
        """)
        await db.execute(meta_query, {
            "role_id": new_role_id,
            "display_name": req.display_name.strip() if req.display_name else None,
            "short_name": req.short_name.strip() if req.short_name else None,
            "data_type": req.data_type
        })

        await db.commit()
        return {"status": "success", "role_id": new_role_id, "message": "포인트 롤이 추가되었습니다."}
    except Exception as e:
        await db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e))

@router.put("/role/{role_id}")
async def update_point_role(role_id: int, req: PointRoleUpdateReq, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    try:
        v_from = parse_dt(req.valid_from)
        v_to = parse_dt(req.valid_to)
        now_utc = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        is_cur = (v_to is None or v_to >= now_utc)

        # 1. 포인트롤 테이블 갱신
        role_update_query = text("""
            UPDATE point_role_table
            SET target_value = COALESCE(:target_value, target_value),
                target_low_limit = COALESCE(:target_low_limit, target_low_limit),
                target_high_limit = COALESCE(:target_high_limit, target_high_limit),
                valid_from = COALESCE(:valid_from, valid_from),
                valid_to = :valid_to,
                is_current = :is_current
            WHERE id = :role_id
        """)
        await db.execute(role_update_query, {
            "role_id": role_id, "target_value": req.target_value,
            "target_low_limit": req.target_low_limit, "target_high_limit": req.target_high_limit,
            "valid_from": v_from, "valid_to": v_to, "is_current": is_cur
        })

        # 2. 메타데이터 테이블 Upsert
        meta_upsert_query = text("""
            INSERT INTO point_role_metadata (role_id, display_name, short_name, data_type, updated_at)
            VALUES (:role_id, NULLIF(:display_name, ''), NULLIF(:short_name, ''), CAST(:data_type AS point_data_type), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
            ON CONFLICT (role_id) DO UPDATE SET
                display_name = EXCLUDED.display_name,
                short_name = EXCLUDED.short_name,
                data_type = EXCLUDED.data_type,
                updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        """)
        await db.execute(meta_upsert_query, {
            "role_id": role_id,
            "display_name": req.display_name.strip() if req.display_name else None,
            "short_name": req.short_name.strip() if req.short_name else None,
            "data_type": req.data_type
        })

        await db.commit()
        return {"status": "success", "message": "포인트 롤 정보가 수정되었습니다."}
    except Exception as e:
        await db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e))

@router.delete("/role/{role_id}")
async def delete_point_role(role_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    await db.execute(text("DELETE FROM point_role_table WHERE id = :role_id"), {"role_id": role_id})
    await db.commit()
    return {"status": "success", "message": "포인트 롤이 삭제되었습니다."}

# ===============================================================
# 경로 : src/controllers/free_later_controller.py
# 명세 : 클라이언트 테스트를 위한 임시 권한 및 시설 정보 주입 컨트롤러 (향후 삭제 및 기능 이전 요구)
# ===============================================================
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import text
from config.db import get_db

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/test-auth", tags=["Test Auth"])



@router.get("/my-context/{user_id}")
async def get_my_context(user_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    # 1. 유저 정보 조회
    user_query = text("SELECT id, email, name, role FROM user_table WHERE id = :user_id")
    user_result = await db.execute(user_query, {"user_id": user_id})
    user = user_result.fetchone()

    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")

    # 2. 시설 정보 조회 로직
    # 관리자(ADMIN, DEVELOPER)는 모든 시설에 접근 가능
    if user.role in ['ADMIN', 'DEVELOPER']:
        facility_query = text("SELECT id, name FROM facilities_table WHERE is_active = TRUE")
        facility_result = await db.execute(facility_query)
    else:
        # 일반 유저(USER)는 facility_users 매핑 테이블에 존재하는 시설만 접근 가능
        facility_query = text("""
            SELECT f.id, f.name 
            FROM facilities_table f
            JOIN facility_users fu ON f.id = fu.facility_id
            WHERE fu.user_id = :user_id AND f.is_active = TRUE
        """)
        facility_result = await db.execute(facility_query, {"user_id": user_id})
        
    facilities = [{"facility_id": row.id, "facility_name": row.name} for row in facility_result.fetchall()]

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "user": {
            "id": user.id,
            "email": user.email,
            "name": user.name,
            "role": user.role
        },
        "accessible_facilities": facilities,
        # 임시로 클라이언트가 기본으로 선택할 첫 번째 시설 ID 반환 (없으면 None)
        "default_facility_id": facilities[0]["facility_id"] if facilities else None
    }

# controllers/test_controller.py
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy import text
# 수정: 앞에 src. 을 붙입니다.
from idcool_server.config.db import get_db

router = APIRouter()

@router.get("/db-test")
def test_db_connection(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    try:
        # 간단한 SQL 실행으로 연결 확인
        result = db.execute(text("SELECT 1")).scalar()
        
        # 실제 user_table 데이터 개수 확인 (테이블 존재 여부 확인용)
        user_count = db.execute(text("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM user_table")).scalar()
        
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "db_connection": "connected",
            "test_query_result": result,
            "user_table_count": user_count
        }
    except Exception as e:
        return {
            "status": "error",
            "message": str(e)
        }

■ File : firebase.py
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_server\idcool_server\src\firebase.py

import asyncio
from firebase_admin import messaging

async def send_multicast_fcm(tokens: list[str], title: str, body: str):
    if not tokens:
        return

    message = messaging.MulticastMessage(
        data={
            "title": str(title),
            "body": str(body),
        },
        tokens=tokens,
        android=messaging.AndroidConfig(
            priority='high',
        ),
    )
    
    response = await asyncio.to_thread(messaging.send_each_for_multicast, message)
    print(f"[FCM 다중 전송] 성공: {response.success_count}건, 실패: {response.failure_count}건")

# ===============================================================
# 경로 : src/models/Board.py
# 명세 : 게시판, 게시글, 댓글 및 이미지 첨부파일 모델 정의
# ===============================================================
from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, String, Text, Date, DateTime, Numeric, ForeignKey, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from config.db import Base

class Board(Base):
    __tablename__ = "boards"

    board_id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    facility_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("facilities_table.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    facility_name = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    # 관계 정의
    facility = relationship("Facility")
    posts = relationship("Post", back_populates="board", cascade="all, delete-orphan")


class Post(Base):
    __tablename__ = "posts"

    post_id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    board_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("boards.board_id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    creator_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("user_table.id"), nullable=False)
    updater_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("user_table.id"), nullable=False)
    title = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    content = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    start_date = Column(Date, nullable=True)
    end_date = Column(Date, nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now())

    # 관계 정의
    board = relationship("Board", back_populates="posts")
    creator = relationship("User", foreign_keys=[creator_id])
    updater = relationship("User", foreign_keys=[updater_id])
    
    images = relationship("PostImage", back_populates="post", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    comments = relationship("Comment", back_populates="post", cascade="all, delete-orphan")


class PostImage(Base):
    __tablename__ = "post_images"

    image_id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    post_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("posts.post_id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=True) # 임시 저장 허용을 위해 nullable=True
    image_url = Column(String(512), nullable=False)
    original_name = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    file_size = Column(Integer, nullable=False)
    width = Column(Integer, nullable=False)
    height = Column(Integer, nullable=False)
    aspect_ratio = Column(Numeric(4, 2), nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    # 관계 정의
    post = relationship("Post", back_populates="images")


class Comment(Base):
    __tablename__ = "comments"

    comment_id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    post_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("posts.post_id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    creator_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("user_table.id"), nullable=False)
    content = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now())

    # 관계 정의
    post = relationship("Post", back_populates="comments")
    creator = relationship("User")

# ===============================================================
# 경로 : src/models/Chat.py
# 명세 : 채팅방, 메시지, 즐겨찾기, 핀 및 해시태그 모델 정의
# ===============================================================
import enum
from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, String, Text, Enum, DateTime, ForeignKey, UniqueConstraint, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from config.db import Base


class ChatRoomType(str, enum.Enum):
    DIRECT = "DIRECT"
    GROUP = "GROUP"
    FACILITY = "FACILITY"


class MessageType(str, enum.Enum):
    TEXT = "TEXT"
    IMAGE = "IMAGE"
    FILE = "FILE"


class ChatRoom(Base):
    __tablename__ = "chatrooms"

    room_id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    facility_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("facilities_table.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=True) 
    room_name = Column(String(150), nullable=False)
    type = Column(Enum(ChatRoomType, name="chatroom_type"), nullable=False, default=ChatRoomType.GROUP)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    # 관계 정의
    facility = relationship("Facility")
    participants = relationship("ChatParticipant", back_populates="room", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    messages = relationship("Message", back_populates="room", cascade="all, delete-orphan")


class ChatParticipant(Base):
    __tablename__ = "chatparticipants"

    participant_id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    room_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("chatrooms.room_id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("user_table.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    joined_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    last_read_message_id = Column(Integer, default=0)
    unread_count = Column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)

    # 관계 정의
    room = relationship("ChatRoom", back_populates="participants")
    user = relationship("User")

    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("room_id", "user_id", name="uq_room_user"),
    )


class Message(Base):
    __tablename__ = "messages"

    message_id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    room_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("chatrooms.room_id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("user_table.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    message_type = Column(Enum(MessageType, name="message_type"), nullable=False, default=MessageType.TEXT)
    content = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    parent_message_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("messages.message_id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True)

    # 관계 정의
    room = relationship("ChatRoom", back_populates="messages")
    user = relationship("User")
    
    # 자가 참조 (Self-referential) 관계 구성 (답글 기능)
    parent = relationship("Message", remote_side=[message_id], back_populates="replies")
    replies = relationship("Message", back_populates="parent", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

    # 다대다 해시태그 매핑
    hashtags = relationship(
        "Hashtag",
        secondary="message_hashtags",
        back_populates="messages"
    )


class MessageFav(Base):
    __tablename__ = "messages_fav"

    fav_id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    room_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("chatrooms.room_id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("user_table.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False) # 👈 유저 식별자 추가
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    # 관계 정의
    room = relationship("ChatRoom")
    user = relationship("User")

    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("room_id", "user_id", name="uq_user_room_fav"),
    )


class MessagePin(Base):
    __tablename__ = "messages_pin"

    pin_id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    room_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("chatrooms.room_id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False) # 👈 빠른 조회용 room_id 역정규화 추가
    message_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("messages.message_id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("user_table.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False) # 👈 고정한 주체(관리자) 식별자 추가
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    # 관계 정의
    room = relationship("ChatRoom")
    message = relationship("Message")
    user = relationship("User")


class Hashtag(Base):
    __tablename__ = "hashtags"

    hashtag_id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    tag_name = Column(String(100), nullable=False, unique=True)

    messages = relationship(
        "Message",
        secondary="message_hashtags",
        back_populates="hashtags"
    )


class MessageHashtag(Base):
    __tablename__ = "message_hashtags"

    message_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("messages.message_id", ondelete="CASCADE"), primary_key=True)
    hashtag_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("hashtags.hashtag_id", ondelete="CASCADE"), primary_key=True)

# ===============================================================
# 경로 : src/models/Facility.py
# 명세 : Facility 관련 데이터 모델 정의
# ===============================================================
import enum
from sqlalchemy import (
    Column, Integer, String, Boolean, Date, Enum, DateTime, func, ForeignKey, UniqueConstraint
)
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from config.db import Base
# ===============================================================

class RoomStatusType(str, enum.Enum):
    OPERATING = "OPERATING"
    MAINTENANCE = "MAINTENANCE"
    NOT_OPERATING = "NOT_OPERATING"


class NodeStatusType(str, enum.Enum):
    ONLINE = "ONLINE"
    OFFLINE = "OFFLINE"
    ERROR = "ERROR"
    MAINTENANCE = "MAINTENANCE"

class FacilityUser(Base):
    __tablename__ = "facility_users"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    facility_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("facilities_table.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("user_table.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    # 유니크 제약 조건 (동일한 유저가 같은 시설에 중복 등록되는 것 방지)
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("facility_id", "user_id", name="uq_facility_user"),
    )


class Facility(Base):
    __tablename__ = "facilities_table"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    name = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    access_code = Column(String(50), unique=True, nullable=False, index=True)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=True)
    start_date = Column(Date, nullable=True)
    end_date = Column(Date, nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now())

    # 관계 정의 (메모리에 실제 로드되는 FacilityRoom과의 관계만 유지)
    rooms = relationship("FacilityRoom", back_populates="facility", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    nodes = relationship("FacilityNode", back_populates="facility", cascade="all, delete-orphan")


class FacilityRoom(Base):
    __tablename__ = "facilities_room_table"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    facility_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("facilities_table.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    name = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
    status = Column(Enum(RoomStatusType, name="room_status_type"), nullable=False, default=RoomStatusType.OPERATING)
    operation_start_date = Column(Date, nullable=True)
    operation_end_date = Column(Date, nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now())

    # 관계 정의
    facility = relationship("Facility", back_populates="rooms")

class FacilityNode(Base):

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    facility_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("facilities_table.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False, index=True)
    node_code = Column(String(100), unique=True, nullable=False)
    node_name = Column(String(150), nullable=False)
    api_key_hash = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    allowed_ip_cidr = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    status = Column(Enum(NodeStatusType, name="node_status_type"), nullable=False, default=NodeStatusType.OFFLINE, index=True)
    firmware_version = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    last_heartbeat_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now())

    facility = relationship("Facility", back_populates="nodes")

# ===============================================================
# 경로 : src/models/Monitoring.py
# 명세 : 포인트 역할, 각종 계측 로그 및 알람 관련 데이터 모델 정의
# ===============================================================
from sqlalchemy import (
    Column, Integer, String, Boolean, DateTime, REAL, Interval, 
    ForeignKey, UniqueConstraint, Index, CHAR, func
)

from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from config.db import Base

class PointRole(Base):
    __tablename__ = "point_role_table"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    facility_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("facilities_table.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    room_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("facilities_room_table.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    role_name = Column(String(150), nullable=False)
    target_value = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    target_low_limit = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    target_high_limit = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    valid_from = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    valid_to = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    is_current = Column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=True)

    # Relationship
    facility = relationship("Facility")
    room = relationship("FacilityRoom")

    # is_current가 True인 대상에 대해서만 유일성을 보장하는 부분 인덱스(Partial Index) 모사
    # (SQLAlchemy 내에서 PostgreSQL partial index 선언)
    __table_args__ = (
        Index(
            "uq_point_role_current", 
            "facility_id", "room_id", "role_name", 
            unique=True, 
            postgresql_where=(is_current == True)
        ),
    )

# ---------------------------------------------------------------

class TemperatureNumericalLog(Base):
    __tablename__ = "temperature_numerical_logs"

    role_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("point_role_table.id"), primary_key=True, nullable=False)
    timestamp = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), primary_key=True, nullable=False)
    temp_min = Column(REAL, nullable=False)
    temp_max = Column(REAL, nullable=False)
    temp_mean = Column(REAL, nullable=False)
    temp_stdev = Column(REAL, nullable=True)
    sample_count = Column(Integer, nullable=False)

    role = relationship("PointRole")

    gear_min = Column(REAL, nullable=False)
    gear_max = Column(REAL, nullable=False)
    gear_mean = Column(REAL, nullable=False)
    gear_stdev = Column(REAL, nullable=True)
    sample_count = Column(Integer, nullable=False)

    role = relationship("PointRole")

    is_triggered = Column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

# ===============================================================
# 경로 : src/models/User.py
# 명세 : User 계정 및 권한(Role) 관련 데이터 모델 정의
# ===============================================================
import enum
from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, String, Enum, DateTime, func, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from config.db import Base
# ===============================================================

class UserRole(str, enum.Enum):
    USER = "USER"
    ADMIN = "ADMIN"
    DEVELOPER = "DEVELOPER"


class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "user_table"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    email = Column(String(255), unique=True, nullable=False, index=True)
    password = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    name = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
    role = Column(Enum(UserRole, name="user_role"), nullable=False, default=UserRole.USER)
    phone_number = Column(String(20), nullable=True)
    
    # 타임존을 포함하는 생성일, 수정일 (자동 갱신)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now())

    external_providers = relationship("ExternalProvider", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan")


    # 역참조 맵핑 

    fcm_tokens = relationship("UserFcmToken", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

    facilities = relationship(
        "Facility",
        secondary="facility_users",
        primaryjoin="User.id == facility_users.c.user_id",
        secondaryjoin="Facility.id == facility_users.c.facility_id",
        viewonly=True # 다대다 조회 목적
    )


class ExternalProvider(Base):
    __tablename__ = "external_providers"

    external_provider_id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    account_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("user_table.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    provider = Column(String(50), nullable=False) # 'google' or 'apple' 제한
    provider_id = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    user = relationship("User", back_populates="external_providers")


class UserFcmToken(Base):
    __tablename__ = "user_fcm_token"

    fcm_token = Column(String(500), primary_key=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("user_table.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False, index=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    user = relationship("User", back_populates="fcm_tokens")

from typing import Dict, List
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy.future import select
from sqlalchemy import or_
from fastapi import Depends
from config.db import get_db
from src.models.User import UserFcmToken, User, UserRole
from src.models.Facility import FacilityUser, Facility
from src.models.Monitoring import PointRole, Alarm
import time
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import src.firebase as msg
import src.services.alarmnamemap as AMM

#캐시 시간
CACHE_TTL = 300

role_cache: dict[int, list] = {}

def invalidate_role_cache(facility_id: int):
    role_cache.pop(f"{facility_id}_list", None)
    role_cache.pop(f"{facility_id}_dict", None)


# 2단계: 기존 리스트 형태의 함수 (캐시 키에 _list 추가)
async def scan_roles(facility_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    now = time.time()
    cache_entry = role_cache.get(facility_id)
    if not cache_entry or (now - cache_entry["updated_at"] > CACHE_TTL):
        async for db in get_db():
            role_result = await db.execute(
                select(PointRole).where(PointRole.facility_id == facility_id)
            )
            role_cache[facility_id] = {
                "roles": role_result.scalars().all(),
                "updated_at": now
            }
            #print(f"[DB 갱신] Facility {facility_id} PointRole 캐시 업데이트 완료")
            break
        
    roles = role_cache[facility_id]["roles"]
    return roles


# 3단계: 딕셔너리 형태의 함수 (캐시 키에 _dict 추가)
async def scan_roles_asc_name(facility_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    now = time.time()
    cache_key = f"{facility_id}_dict" # 🟢 키 분리
    cache_entry = role_cache.get(cache_key)
    
    if (not cache_entry or 
        (now - cache_entry["updated_at"] > CACHE_TTL)):
        
        async for db in get_db():
            role_result = await db.execute(
                select(PointRole).where(PointRole.facility_id == facility_id)
            )
            
        raw_roles = role_result.scalars().all()
        
        sorted_roles = sorted(
            raw_roles, 
            key=lambda x: f"{x.room_id}.{x.role_name}"
        )
        
        roles_dict = {
            f"{role.room_id}.{role.role_name}": role 
            for role in sorted_roles
        }
        
        role_cache[cache_key] = {
            "roles": roles_dict,
            "updated_at": now
        }
        
    roles = role_cache[cache_key]["roles"]
    return roles

async def check_roles(facility_id: int, data_dict: Dict[str, str], roles):
    tokens: List[str] = []
    # 단순 센서키 대신 (센서키, matching_role) 튜플을 저장
    triggered_alarms: List[tuple] = []
    facility_name: str = f"시설 #{facility_id}" # 기본값 설정

    async for db in get_db():
        candidate_alarms = []
        # 1. 알람 대상 조건 검사 및 발생 대상 선별
        for role in roles:
            for k, v in data_dict.items():
                if role.role_name in k and str(role.room_id) in k:
                    val = float(v)
                    low_limit = float(role.target_low_limit) if role.target_low_limit not in (None, "", "null") else float('-inf')
                    high_limit = float(role.target_high_limit) if role.target_high_limit not in (None, "", "null") else float('inf')

                    is_out_of_bounds = val <= low_limit or val >= high_limit
                    
                    if is_out_of_bounds:
                        candidate_alarms.append((k, role))

        # 2. 이상이 발생한 항목이 있을 때만 단 1번의 DB 쿼리로 기존 알람들 조회 (In-Memory 매핑)
        if candidate_alarms:
            # 검사할 알람명 목록 추출
            candidate_names = [AMM.Maped.get(k, k) for k, _ in candidate_alarms]

            # IN 쿼리로 한 번에 조회
            stmt = select(Alarm).where(Alarm.alarm_name.in_(candidate_names))
            result = await db.execute(stmt)
    
            # {alarm_name: Alarm 객체} 형태의 딕셔너리로 변환
            existing_alarms = {a.alarm_name: a for a in result.scalars().all()}

            now_utc = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

            # 3. end_time이 지나지 않은(진행 중인) 알람 필터링
            for k, role in candidate_alarms:
                am_name = AMM.Maped.get(k, k)
                mapping = existing_alarms.get(am_name)

                # 이미 활성화되어 진행 중인 알람이면 제외
                if mapping and mapping.end_time and (mapping.end_time >= now_utc):
                    continue

                # 신규 발생 대상에 추가
                triggered_alarms.append((k, role))

        # 2. 발생 대상이 있을 경우: DB에 Alarm 기록 저장 & FCM 토큰 조회
        # 관리자는 조건과 관계 없이 가능하게 해야 함
        if triggered_alarms:
            facility_stmt = select(Facility.name).where(Facility.id == facility_id)
            facility_res = await db.execute(facility_stmt)
            fetched_name = facility_res.scalar_one_or_none()
            if fetched_name:
                facility_name = fetched_name

            now_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            
            # 2-1. DB에 신규 Alarm 인스턴스 기록
            for k, role in triggered_alarms:
                am_name = AMM.Maped.get(k, k)
                
                new_alarm = Alarm(
                    role_id=role.id,                         # matching PointRole의 PK ID
                    alarm_name=am_name,                      # 알람명
                    severity=getattr(role, 'severity', 'a'), # role에 설정된 severity 사용 (기본값 'a')
                    timestamp=now_time,                      # 발생 시간
                    is_triggered=True,                       # 알람 발동 상태
                    reset_timeout_interval=timedelta(hours=24), # 기본 리셋 타임아웃
                    end_time=now_time + timedelta(hours=24)  # end_time 기본 24시간 지정 예시
                )
                db.add(new_alarm)
            
            # DB 변경사항 적용
            await db.commit()

            # 2-2. FCM 토큰 1회 조회 (JOIN)
            stmt_token = (
                select(UserFcmToken.fcm_token)
                .join(User, User.id == UserFcmToken.user_id)
                .outerjoin(FacilityUser, FacilityUser.user_id == UserFcmToken.user_id)
                .where(
                    or_(
                        FacilityUser.facility_id == facility_id,
                        User.role == UserRole.ADMIN,
                    )           
                )
                .distinct()
            )
            res_token = await db.execute(stmt_token)
            tokens = list(res_token.scalars().all())

        break # get_db() 생성기 1회 수행 후 탈출

    # 3. FCM 푸시 알림 전송
    if tokens and triggered_alarms:
        print("알람 발생 : ", [k for k, _ in triggered_alarms])
        for k, _ in triggered_alarms:
            mapped_name = AMM.Maped.get(k)
            if mapped_name:
                await msg.send_multicast_fcm(tokens, facility_name + "이상 발생", mapped_name)
            else:
                await msg.send_multicast_fcm(tokens, facility_name + "이상 발생", f"{k}")

■ File : App.tsx
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_web\setuptmp\src\App.tsx

import { useState } from 'react';
import { BrowserRouter as Router, Routes, Route, Navigate, NavLink } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ChartMappingPage } from './pages/ChartMappingPage';
import { ChartViewerPage }  from './pages/ChartViewerPage';
import { PRIndexingPage }    from './pages/PRIndexingPage';
import { FacilityManagerPage } from './pages/FacilityManagerPage';
import { RoomComparePage } from './pages/RoomComparePage';
import { LoginPage } from './pages/LoginPage';
import { MainPage } from './pages/MainPage';
import { Monogram, Wordmark } from './components/Brand';

function App() {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<any>(null); // 로그인한 유저 정보 상태

  const handleLogout = () => {
    setUser(null);
  };

  // 관리자 권한 체크용 상수
  const isAdmin = user && (user.role === 'ADMIN' || user.role === 'DEVELOPER');

  const navLinkStyle = ({ isActive }: { isActive: boolean }) =>
    `nav-link${isActive ? ' active' : ''}`;

  return (
    <Router>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100vh', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}>

        {/* 헤더 */}
        <header
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            justifyContent: 'space-between',
            alignItems: 'center',
            padding: '14px 24px',
            borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)',
            background: 'var(--surface)',
          }}
        >
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
            <Monogram size={32} />
            <Wordmark size={17} />
          </div>
          {user && (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 14 }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>
                {user.name} <span className="chip" style={{ marginLeft: 6 }}>{user.role}</span>
              </span>
              <button onClick={handleLogout} className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">로그아웃</button>
            </div>
          )}
        </header>

        {/* 글로벌 네비게이션 바 (로그인한 유저에게만 노출) */}
        {user && (
          <nav
            style={{
              display: 'flex', gap: 6, alignItems: 'center',
              padding: '10px 24px',
              borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)',
              background: 'var(--surface)',
            }}
          >
            {isAdmin && (
              <>
                <NavLink to="/main" className={navLinkStyle}>메인 대시보드</NavLink>
                <NavLink to="/mapping" className={navLinkStyle}>맵핑 관리자</NavLink>
              </>
            )}

            {/* 유저와 관리자 공통 메뉴 */}
            <NavLink to="/viewer" className={navLinkStyle}>차트 뷰어</NavLink>

            {isAdmin && (
              <>
                <NavLink to="/indexing" className={navLinkStyle}>명칭 인덱싱</NavLink>
                <NavLink to="/facility-manager" className={navLinkStyle}>시설/호실 관리</NavLink>
                <NavLink to="/compare" className={navLinkStyle}>호실 비교</NavLink>
              </>
            )}
          </nav>
        )}

        {/* 본문 영역 */}
        <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', overflow: 'auto', background: 'var(--bg)' }}>
          <Routes>
            {/* 비로그인 상태일 때 접근 가능한 Route */}
            <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage onLogin={setUser} />} />

            {/* 로그인 상태 체크 */}
            {!user ? (
              <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/login" />} />
            ) : (
              <>
                {/* Admin/Developer 전용 라우트 */}
                {isAdmin && (
                  <>
                    <Route path="/main" element={<MainPage />} />
                    <Route path="/mapping" element={<ChartMappingPage />} />
                    <Route path="/indexing" element={<PRIndexingPage />} />
                    <Route path="/facility-manager" element={<FacilityManagerPage />} />
                    <Route path="/compare" element={<RoomComparePage />} />
                  </>
                )}

                {/* User/Admin 공통 라우트 */}
                <Route path="/viewer" element={<ChartViewerPage />} />

                {/* 기본 리다이렉트 (관리자는 메인, 유저는 뷰어로) */}
                <Route path="*" element={
                  <Navigate to={isAdmin ? "/main" : "/viewer"} />
                } />
              </>
            )}
          </Routes>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Router>
  );
}

export default App;

import React from 'react';

/** 그라디언트 모노그램 — 앱 쪽 AppMonogram과 동일한 브랜드 마크 */
export const Monogram: React.FC<{ size?: number }> = ({ size = 32 }) => (
  <div
    style={{
      width: size, height: size, borderRadius: size * 0.28,
      background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, var(--primary-light, #5c8def), var(--primary-dark, #1e4fbd))',
      color: '#fff',
      display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
      fontWeight: 800, fontSize: size * 0.42, letterSpacing: -1,
      boxShadow: `0 ${size * 0.14}px ${size * 0.32}px -${size * 0.1}px rgba(46,107,239,0.45)`,
      flexShrink: 0,
    }}
  >
    ic
  </div>
);

/** 2색 워드마크 — "ID"(기본 텍스트색) + "Cool"(브랜드 블루) */
export const Wordmark: React.FC<{ size?: number }> = ({ size = 17 }) => (
  <span style={{ fontSize: size, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: -0.3 }}>
    ID<span style={{ color: 'var(--primary)' }}>Cool</span>
  </span>
);


======================================================================
■ File : LeftPanel.tsx
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_web\setuptmp\src\components\chart\mapper\LeftPanel.tsx
======================================================================

import React from 'react';
import type { Role } from '../../../types';

interface Props {
  facilities: any[];
  rooms: any[];
  selectedFacility: number | null;
  selectedRoom: number | null;
  room0Roles: Role[];
  targetRoomRoles: Role[];
  droppedRoleIds: number[];
  onFacilityChange: (id: number) => void;
  onRoomChange: (id: number) => void;
  onDragStart: (e: React.DragEvent, role: Role) => void;
}

const getRoleLabels = (roleInfo?: Role, fallbackId?: number) => {
  const roleName = roleInfo?.role_name || (fallbackId ? `Role ${fallbackId}` : '');
  const roleLabel = roleInfo ? (roleInfo.display_name || roleInfo.role_name) : `Role ${fallbackId}`;
  const badgeLabel = roleInfo ? (roleInfo.short_name || roleInfo.role_name) : `${fallbackId}`;

  return { roleName, roleLabel, badgeLabel };
};

export const LeftPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({
  facilities,
  rooms,
  selectedFacility,
  selectedRoom,
  room0Roles,
  targetRoomRoles,
  droppedRoleIds,
  onFacilityChange,
  onRoomChange,
  onDragStart,
}) => {
  const visibleRoom0Roles = room0Roles.filter(
    (role) => !droppedRoleIds.includes(role.role_id)
  );
  const visibleTargetRoles = targetRoomRoles.filter(
    (role) => !droppedRoleIds.includes(role.role_id)
  );

  const renderRoleItem = (role: Role) => {
    const { roleLabel, badgeLabel } = getRoleLabels(role, role.role_id);
    return (
      <div
        key={role.role_id}
        draggable
        onDragStart={(e) => onDragStart(e, role)}
        className="card"
        style={{
          padding: '8px 10px',
          marginBottom: 6,
          cursor: 'grab',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: 8,
        }}
      >
        <span className="chip">{badgeLabel}</span>
        <span style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>{roleLabel}</span>
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="card"
      style={{
        flex: 1,
        minHeight: 0,
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        gap: 12,
      }}
    >
      <select
        value={selectedFacility || ''}
        onChange={(e) => onFacilityChange(Number(e.target.value))}
        className="input"
      >
        <option value="">시설 선택</option>
        {facilities.map((f) => (
          <option key={f.id} value={f.id}>
            {f.name}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>

      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flex: 1, minHeight: 0, gap: 12 }}>
        {/* 0호실 (공용) 영역 */}
        <div
          style={{
            border: '1px solid var(--border)',
            borderRadius: 12,
            flex: 1,
            minHeight: 0,
            padding: 12,
            overflowY: 'auto',
          }}
        >
          <p className="label" style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}>0호실 (공용)</p>
          {visibleRoom0Roles.map((role) => renderRoleItem(role))}
        </div>

        {/* 개별 호실 영역 */}
        <div
          style={{
            border: '1px solid var(--border)',
            borderRadius: 12,
            flex: 1,
            minHeight: 0,
            padding: 12,
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            gap: 10,
          }}
        >
          <select
            value={selectedRoom || ''}
            onChange={(e) => onRoomChange(Number(e.target.value))}
            className="input"
          >
            <option value="">호실 선택</option>
            {rooms.map((r) => (
              <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>
                {r.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <div style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0, overflowY: 'auto' }}>
            {visibleTargetRoles.map((role) => renderRoleItem(role))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

■ File : RightPanel.tsx
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_web\setuptmp\src\components\chart\mapper\RightPanel.tsx
======================================================================

import React from 'react';
import type { Role, ChartGroup } from '../../../types';

interface Props {
  existingGroups: ChartGroup[];
  selectedGroupId: number | 'new';
  groupName: string;
  droppedRoles: Role[];
  onGroupSelect: (val: string) => void;
  onGroupNameChange: (val: string) => void;
  onDrop: (e: React.DragEvent) => void;
  onDragOver: (e: React.DragEvent) => void;
  onRemoveRole: (roleId: number) => void;
  onReorderRoles: (sourceIndex: number, targetIndex: number) => void;
  onSaveGroup: () => void;
}

// 명칭 및 약어 Fallback 헬퍼 함수
const getRoleLabels = (roleInfo?: Role, fallbackId?: number) => {
  const roleName = roleInfo?.role_name || (fallbackId ? `Role ${fallbackId}` : '');
  const roleLabel = roleInfo ? (roleInfo.display_name || roleInfo.role_name) : `Role ${fallbackId}`;
  const badgeLabel = roleInfo ? (roleInfo.short_name || roleInfo.role_name) : `${fallbackId}`;

  return { roleName, roleLabel, badgeLabel };
};

export const RightPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({
  existingGroups,
  selectedGroupId,
  groupName,
  droppedRoles,
  onGroupSelect,
  onGroupNameChange,
  onDrop,
  onDragOver,
  onRemoveRole,
  onReorderRoles,
  onSaveGroup,
}) => {
  // 내부 항목 간 순서 변경 드래그 시작
  const handleItemDragStart = (e: React.DragEvent, index: number) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    e.dataTransfer.setData('text/reorder-index', String(index));
  };

  // 내부 항목 드롭 시 위치 교환 또는 외부 드롭 처리
  const handleItemDrop = (e: React.DragEvent, targetIndex: number) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    e.preventDefault();
    const sourceIndexStr = e.dataTransfer.getData('text/reorder-index');

    if (sourceIndexStr !== '') {
      onReorderRoles(Number(sourceIndexStr), targetIndex);
    } else {
      onDrop(e);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="card"
      style={{
        flex: 1,
        minHeight: 0,
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        gap: 12,
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          gap: 10,
        }}
      >
        <select
          value={selectedGroupId}
          onChange={(e) => onGroupSelect(e.target.value)}
          className="input"
        >
          <option value="new">+ 새로운 그룹 추가하기</option>
          {existingGroups.map((g) => (
            <option key={g.group_id} value={g.group_id}>
              [불러오기] {g.group_name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="새 그룹 명칭 입력"
          value={groupName}
          onChange={(e) => onGroupNameChange(e.target.value)}
          className="input"
        />
      </div>

      <p className="label">드래그 앤 드롭 영역</p>
      <div
        onDrop={onDrop}
        onDragOver={onDragOver}
        style={{
          border: '2px dashed var(--border)',
          borderRadius: 12,
          flex: 1,
          minHeight: 0,
          padding: 12,
          overflowY: 'auto',
          background: 'var(--bg)',
        }}
      >
        {droppedRoles.length === 0 && (
          <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: 13 }}>여기로 포인트 롤을 드래그하세요.</p>
        )}
        {droppedRoles.map((role, idx) => {
          const { roleLabel, badgeLabel } = getRoleLabels(role, role.role_id);

          return (
            <div
              key={role.role_id}
              draggable
              onDragStart={(e) => handleItemDragStart(e, idx)}
              onDragOver={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
              onDrop={(e) => handleItemDrop(e, idx)}
              className="card"
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                alignItems: 'center',
                padding: '8px 10px',
                marginBottom: 6,
                cursor: 'grab',
              }}
            >
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>☰</span>
                <span className="chip">{badgeLabel}</span>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>{roleLabel}</span>
              </div>

              <span
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  onRemoveRole(role.role_id);
                }}
                style={{ cursor: 'pointer', marginLeft: 8, color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}
              >
                ✕
              </span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <button onClick={onSaveGroup} className="btn btn-primary" style={{ height: 44 }}>
        {selectedGroupId === 'new' ? '등록하기' : '수정하기 (Backend 연결 요망)'}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
};

// src/components/chart/EChartsViewer.tsx
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import ReactECharts from 'echarts-for-react';
import * as echarts from 'echarts';

interface Props {
  datasets?: any[];
  roleColors: Record<number, string>;
  showInfo?: boolean;
  showGrid?: boolean;
  echartsRef?: React.RefObject<ReactECharts | null>;
  activeGuidelines?: number[]; 
  isLoading?: boolean; 
  posts?: any[];
  alarms?: any[];
  onChartClick?: (type: 'post' | 'alarm', id: number | number[]) => void;
  onDataZoomPan?: (startVal: number, endVal: number) => void;
  loadedRange?: { start: number; end: number } | null;
  // 💡 [신규] 반전된 상태를 출력할 BOOL 롤 ID 목록
  invertedBools?: number[];
}

const SYNC_GROUP_NAME = 'idcool-chart-group';
echarts.connect(SYNC_GROUP_NAME);

const formatShortTime = (ts: number) => {
  const d = new Date(ts);
  return `${(d.getMonth() + 1).toString().padStart(2, '0')}/${d.getDate().toString().padStart(2, '0')} ${d.getHours().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${d.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
};

const generatePostSegments = (validPosts: any[]) => {
  if (!validPosts || validPosts.length === 0) return [];
  const points = new Set<number>();
  validPosts.forEach(p => { points.add(p.st); points.add(p.et); });
  const sortedPoints = Array.from(points).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const segments: any[] = [];
  
  for (let i = 0; i < sortedPoints.length - 1; i++) {
    const segSt = sortedPoints[i];
    const segEt = sortedPoints[i + 1];
    if (segSt === segEt) continue;
    const activePosts = validPosts.filter(p => p.st <= segSt && p.et >= segEt);
    if (activePosts.length > 0) segments.push({ st: segSt, et: segEt, activePosts });
  }
  return segments;
};

// ==========================================
// 1. 수치형 데이터 전용 메인 차트
// ==========================================
export const MainChartViewer: React.FC<Props> = ({ datasets, roleColors, showInfo = true, showGrid = true, echartsRef, activeGuidelines = [], isLoading = false, posts = [], alarms = [], onChartClick, onDataZoomPan: _onDataZoomPan, loadedRange }) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (echartsRef && echartsRef.current) {
      const instance = echartsRef.current.getEchartsInstance();
      instance.group = SYNC_GROUP_NAME; 
      instance.off('click');
      instance.on('click', (params: any) => {
        if (params.seriesName === 'GlobalAnnotations' && params.data && params.data.postIds) {
          if (onChartClick) onChartClick('post', params.data.postIds);
        }
      });
    }
  }, [echartsRef, onChartClick]);

  const getOption = () => {
    const seriesData: any[] = (datasets || []).map(ds => {
      const color = roleColors[ds.role_id];
      const seriesItem: any = {
        name: ds.display_name || ds.role_name,
        type: 'line',
        showSymbol: false,
        itemStyle: { color: color },
        data: (ds.data || []).map((pt: any) => [new Date(pt.timestamp).getTime(), pt.mean])
      };
      if (activeGuidelines.includes(ds.role_id)) {
        const markLineData: any[] = [];
        if (ds.target_value != null) markLineData.push({ yAxis: Number(ds.target_value), name: 'Target', lineStyle: { type: 'solid', color: color, width: 2 } });
        if (ds.target_low_limit != null) markLineData.push({ yAxis: Number(ds.target_low_limit), name: 'Low', lineStyle: { type: 'dashed', color: color, width: 1.5 } });
        if (ds.target_high_limit != null) markLineData.push({ yAxis: Number(ds.target_high_limit), name: 'High', lineStyle: { type: 'dashed', color: color, width: 1.5 } });
        if (markLineData.length > 0) {
          seriesItem.markLine = { data: markLineData, symbol: 'none', label: { show: true, position: 'start', distance: [10, -10], formatter: '{b}: {c}', color: color, fontSize: 11, backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.92)', padding: [2, 4], borderRadius: 2 } };
        }
      }
      return seriesItem;
    });

    const validPosts = posts.filter(p => p.start_date && p.end_date).map(p => {
      const st = new Date(p.start_date).getTime();
      let et = new Date(p.end_date).getTime();
      if (st === et) et += 60 * 60 * 1000;
      return { ...p, st, et };
    });

    const segments = generatePostSegments(validPosts);
    const postAreas = segments.map(seg => {
      const combinedTitle = seg.activePosts.map((p: any) => p.title).join('\n');
      const postIds = seg.activePosts.map((p: any) => p.id);
      return [
        { xAxis: seg.st, postIds: postIds, itemStyle: { color: 'rgba(214, 69, 69, 0.06)' }, label: { show: true, position: 'insideTop', formatter: combinedTitle, color: '#d64545', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: 12, backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)', padding: [4, 8], borderRadius: 4, lineHeight: 18 } },
        { xAxis: seg.et }
      ];
    });

    const globalLines: any[] = [];
    validPosts.forEach(p => {
      globalLines.push({ xAxis: p.st, label: { show: true, position: 'end', formatter: formatShortTime(p.st), color: '#d64545', fontSize: 10 }, lineStyle: { type: 'solid', color: '#d64545', width: 2 } });
      globalLines.push({ xAxis: p.et, label: { show: true, position: 'end', formatter: formatShortTime(p.et), color: '#d64545', fontSize: 10 }, lineStyle: { type: 'solid', color: '#d64545', width: 2 } });
    });
    alarms.forEach(a => {
      const time = new Date(a.timestamp).getTime();
      globalLines.push({ xAxis: time, label: { show: true, position: 'end', formatter: formatShortTime(time), color: '#0891b2', fontSize: 10 }, lineStyle: { type: 'dashed', color: '#0891b2', width: 2 } });
      globalLines.push({ xAxis: time, label: { show: true, position: 'start', formatter: a.alarm_name, color: '#0891b2', fontSize: 10 }, lineStyle: { type: 'dashed', color: '#0891b2', width: 2 } });
    });

    const annotationSeries: any = { name: 'GlobalAnnotations', type: 'line', xAxisIndex: 0, yAxisIndex: 0, data: [], markArea: { silent: false, data: postAreas }, markLine: { silent: false, symbol: ['none', 'none'], z: 10, data: globalLines } };
    seriesData.push(annotationSeries);

    if (loadedRange) {
      const oneYear = 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
      seriesData.push({
        name: 'InvisibleBoundary', type: 'line', itemStyle: { color: 'transparent' }, lineStyle: { width: 0 }, symbolSize: 0, showSymbol: false, tooltip: { show: false },
        data: [ [loadedRange.start - oneYear, 0], [loadedRange.end + oneYear, 0] ]
      });
    }

    return {
      tooltip: { show: showInfo, trigger: 'axis', axisPointer: { type: 'cross' } },
      axisPointer: { link: [{ xAxisIndex: 'all' }] },
      grid: { left: 60, right: 50, top: 40, bottom: 25 },
      xAxis: { 
        type: 'time', 
        min: loadedRange?.start, 
        max: loadedRange?.end,
        axisLabel: { show: false },
        axisTick: { show: false },
        splitLine: { show: showGrid, lineStyle: { color: '#e5e7eb' } }
      },
      yAxis: { type: 'value', name: '수치 (Main)', splitLine: { show: showGrid, lineStyle: { color: '#e5e7eb' } }, scale: true },
      dataZoom: [{ type: 'inside', filterMode: 'empty' }],
      series: seriesData
    }; 
  };

  return <ReactECharts ref={echartsRef as any} option={getOption()} showLoading={isLoading} style={{ height: '100%', width: '100%' }} notMerge={true} />; 
};

// ==========================================
// 2. 상태형(BOOL) 데이터 전용 서브 차트
// ==========================================
export const SubChartViewer: React.FC<Props> = ({ datasets, roleColors, showInfo = true, showGrid = true, echartsRef, isLoading = false, posts = [], alarms = [], loadedRange, invertedBools = [] }) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (echartsRef && echartsRef.current) echartsRef.current.getEchartsInstance().group = SYNC_GROUP_NAME; 
  }, [echartsRef]);

  const getOption = () => {
    const seriesData: any[] = [];
    const boolCategories = (datasets || []).map(ds => ds.display_name || ds.role_name);
     

    (datasets || []).forEach((ds, index) => {
      // 💡 [신규] 반전 설정 여부 확인
      const isInverted = invertedBools.includes(ds.role_id);

      const mappedData = (ds.data || [])
        .filter((pt: any) => {
          const isTrue = pt.status === true || pt.status === 1 || String(pt.status) === 'true' || pt.mean === 1 || String(pt.mean) === '1';
          return isInverted ? !isTrue : isTrue; // 상태 반전 적용
        })
        .map((pt: any) => [new Date(pt.timestamp).getTime(), index]); 


      seriesData.push({ name: ds.display_name || ds.role_name, type: 'scatter', symbol: 'rect', symbolSize: [6, 20], itemStyle: { color: roleColors[ds.role_id] }, data: mappedData }); 
    });

    const validPosts = posts.filter(p => p.start_date && p.end_date).map(p => {
      const st = new Date(p.start_date).getTime();
      let et = new Date(p.end_date).getTime();
      if (st === et) et += 60 * 60 * 1000;
      return { ...p, st, et };
    });
    
    const segments = generatePostSegments(validPosts);
    const globalLines: any[] = [];
    
    validPosts.forEach(p => {
      globalLines.push({ xAxis: p.st, lineStyle: { type: 'solid', color: '#d64545', width: 2 }, label: { show: false } });
      globalLines.push({ xAxis: p.et, lineStyle: { type: 'solid', color: '#d64545', width: 2 }, label: { show: false } });
    });
    alarms.forEach(a => {
      const time = new Date(a.timestamp).getTime();
      globalLines.push({ xAxis: time, lineStyle: { type: 'dashed', color: '#0891b2', width: 2 }, label: { show: false } });
    });

    const annotationSeries: any = {
      name: 'GlobalAnnotationsSub', type: 'line', xAxisIndex: 0, yAxisIndex: 0, data: [],
      markArea: { silent: true, data: segments.map(seg => [ { xAxis: seg.st, itemStyle: { color: 'rgba(214,69,69,0.06)' } }, { xAxis: seg.et } ])},
      markLine: { symbol: ['none', 'none'], z: 10, silent: true, data: globalLines }
    };
    seriesData.push(annotationSeries);

    if (loadedRange) {
      const oneYear = 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
      seriesData.push({
        name: 'InvisibleBoundary', type: 'line', itemStyle: { color: 'transparent' }, lineStyle: { width: 0 }, symbolSize: 0, showSymbol: false, tooltip: { show: false },
        data: [ [loadedRange.start - oneYear, 0], [loadedRange.end + oneYear, 0] ]
      });
    }

    return {
      tooltip: { show: showInfo, trigger: 'axis', axisPointer: { type: 'cross' } },
      grid: { left: 60, right: 50, top: 10, bottom: 25 },
      xAxis: {
        type: 'time',
        min: loadedRange?.start,
        max: loadedRange?.end,
        splitLine: { show: showGrid, lineStyle: { color: '#e5e7eb' } }
      },
      yAxis: { type: 'category', name: '상태 (Sub)', data: boolCategories.length > 0 ? boolCategories : ['데이터 없음'], splitLine: { show: showGrid, lineStyle: { color: '#e5e7eb', type: 'dashed' } }, axisLabel: { color: '#5b6b73', fontSize: 10 } },
      dataZoom: [{ type: 'inside', filterMode: 'empty' }, { type: 'slider', bottom: 0, height: 20, filterMode: 'empty' }],
      series: seriesData
    }; 
  };

  return <ReactECharts ref={echartsRef as any} option={getOption()} showLoading={isLoading} style={{ height: '100%', width: '100%' }} notMerge={true} />; 
};

import React from 'react';

interface Props {
  showInfo: boolean;
  showGrid: boolean;

  onToggleGrid: () => void;
  onSyncTop: () => void;
  onSyncSub: () => void;

export const LowerToolBar: React.FC<Props> = ({ showInfo, showGrid, onToggleInfo, onToggleGrid, onSyncTop, onSyncSub }) => (

    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>

      <button onClick={onToggleGrid} className={showGrid ? 'btn btn-primary btn-sm' : 'btn btn-ghost btn-sm'}>그리드 {showGrid ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}</button>
      <button onClick={onSyncTop} className="btn btn-outline btn-sm">시점 맞추기 (TOP 기준)</button>
      <button onClick={onSyncSub} className="btn btn-outline btn-sm">시점 맞추기 (BOOL 기준)</button>
    </div>
  </div>
);

export const BlacklistPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ hiddenPosts, onRestore }) => (
