■ File : ChartStatsPanel.tsx
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_web\setuptmp\src\components\chart\viewer\panels\ChartStatsPanel.tsx
======================================================================

import React from 'react';

interface Props {
  datasets: any[];
  roleColors: Record<number, string>;
}

interface SeriesStat {
  roleId: number;
  name: string;
  color: string;
  min: number | null;
  avg: number | null;
  max: number | null;
}

const computeStats = (datasets: any[], roleColors: Record<number, string>): SeriesStat[] => {
  const stats: SeriesStat[] = [];

  for (const ds of datasets) {
    const points: any[] = ds.data || [];
    if (points.length === 0) continue;

    let sum = 0;
    let count = 0;
    let min: number | null = null;
    let max: number | null = null;

    for (const p of points) {
      const mean = typeof p.mean === 'number' ? p.mean : null;
      const pMin = typeof p.min === 'number' ? p.min : mean;
      const pMax = typeof p.max === 'number' ? p.max : mean;

      if (mean != null) { sum += mean; count++; }
      if (pMin != null) min = min == null ? pMin : Math.min(min, pMin);
      if (pMax != null) max = max == null ? pMax : Math.max(max, pMax);
    }

    if (count === 0 && min == null) continue;

    stats.push({
      roleId: ds.role_id,
      name: ds.display_name || ds.role_name || `시리즈 ${ds.role_id}`,
      color: roleColors[ds.role_id] || '#5b6b73',
      min,
      avg: count > 0 ? sum / count : null,
      max,
    });
  }

  return stats;
};

const fmt = (v: number | null) => (v == null ? '-' : v.toFixed(1));

/** 조회된 구간의 시리즈별 최소/평균/최대값 요약. 그래프를 눈으로 훑지 않아도 수치를 바로 확인할 수 있게 한다. */
export const ChartStatsPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ datasets, roleColors }) => {
  const stats = computeStats(datasets, roleColors);
  if (stats.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div className="card" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 8 }}>
      <h4 style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', margin: 0 }}>구간 요약</h4>
      {stats.map(s => (
        <div key={s.roleId} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, fontSize: 12 }}>
          <span style={{ width: 8, height: 8, borderRadius: '50%', background: s.color, flexShrink: 0 }} />
          <span style={{ flex: 1, fontWeight: 600, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>{s.name}</span>
          <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>최소 {fmt(s.min)}</span>
          <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>평균 {fmt(s.avg)}</span>
          <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>최대 {fmt(s.max)}</span>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
};

■ File : MappingActivePanel.tsx
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_web\setuptmp\src\components\chart\viewer\panels\MappingActivePanel.tsx
======================================================================

import React from 'react';
import type { ChartGroup, Role } from '../../../../types';

interface Props {
  groups: ChartGroup[];
  allRoles: Role[];
  roleColors: Record<number, string>;
  selectedGroupId: number | '';
  selectedRoom: number | '';
  isChartLoading: boolean;
  checkedRoles: number[];
  activeGuidelines: number[];
  invertedBools: number[];
  openMenuRoleId: number | null;
  onGroupChange: (val: number | '') => void;
  onRoleToggle: (id: number) => void;
  onMenuToggle: (id: number | null) => void;
  onColorChange: (id: number, color: string) => void;
  onGuidelineToggle: (id: number) => void;
  onInvertToggle: (id: number) => void;
  isBoolRole: (id: number) => boolean;
}

export const MappingActivePanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ groups, allRoles, roleColors, selectedGroupId, selectedRoom, isChartLoading, checkedRoles, activeGuidelines, invertedBools, openMenuRoleId, onGroupChange, onRoleToggle, onMenuToggle, onColorChange, onGuidelineToggle, onInvertToggle, isBoolRole }) => {
  const currentGroup = groups.find(g => g.group_id === Number(selectedGroupId));
  const groupRoleIds = currentGroup && currentGroup.role_ids ? currentGroup.role_ids.filter(id => id !== null) : [];


  return (
    <div className="card" style={{ flex: 3, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10 }}>
      <h4 style={{ fontSize: 14 }}>맵핑 그룹 활성화</h4>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 15, alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <select value={selectedGroupId} onChange={e => onGroupChange(e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : '')} disabled={!selectedRoom || groups.length === 0 || isChartLoading} className="input" style={{ width: 200 }}>
          <option value="">-- 맵핑 그룹 선택 --</option>
          {groups.map(g => <option key={g.group_id} value={g.group_id}>{g.group_name}</option>)}
        </select>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 15 }}>
          {selectedGroupId && groupRoleIds.map(roleId => {
            const roleInfo = allRoles.find(r => r.role_id === roleId);
            const roleLabel = roleInfo ? (roleInfo.display_name || roleInfo.role_name) : `Role ${roleId}`;  // 표시용 명칭
            const badgeLabel = roleInfo ? (roleInfo.short_name || roleInfo.role_name) : `${roleId}`;        // 약어용 명칭
            const color = roleColors[roleId] || 'var(--text)';
            const isBool = isBoolRole(roleId);

            return (
              <div key={roleId} style={{ position: 'relative', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <label style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', cursor: isChartLoading ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer', color: color, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 13, opacity: isChartLoading ? 0.5 : 1 }}>
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={checkedRoles.includes(roleId)} onChange={() => onRoleToggle(roleId)} disabled={isChartLoading} style={{ marginRight: 5, accentColor: color }} />
                  <span style={{ display: 'inline-block', width: 12, height: 12, backgroundColor: color, marginRight: 5, borderRadius: 2 }}></span>
                  {/* 약어 배지 표시 */}
                  <span className="chip" style={{ marginRight: 5 }}>
                    {badgeLabel}
                  </span>
                  {roleLabel}
                </label>
                <span onClick={() => onMenuToggle(openMenuRoleId === roleId ? null : roleId)} style={{ marginLeft: 5, cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: 16 }}>⚙️</span>
                {openMenuRoleId === roleId && (
                  <div className="card" style={{ position: 'absolute', bottom: 'calc(100% + 8px)', left: 0, zIndex: 1000, padding: 10, minWidth: 170, boxShadow: 'var(--shadow)' }}>
                    <div style={{ marginBottom: 10, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>색상 변경:</span>
                      <input type="color" value={color} onChange={(e) => onColorChange(roleId, e.target.value)} style={{ cursor: 'pointer', border: 'none', background: 'transparent', width: 30, height: 25 }} />
                    </div>
                    {!isBool && (
                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                        <label style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', cursor: 'pointer' }}>
                          <input type="checkbox" checked={activeGuidelines.includes(roleId)} onChange={() => onGuidelineToggle(roleId)} style={{ marginRight: 5 }} /> 가이드라인 표시
                        </label>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {isBool && (
                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                        <label style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', cursor: 'pointer' }}>
                          <input type="checkbox" checked={invertedBools.includes(roleId)} onChange={() => onInvertToggle(roleId)} style={{ marginRight: 5 }} /> 상태 반전 (Invert)
                        </label>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

■ File : PostViewerPanel.tsx

import React from 'react';

interface Props {
  selectedPostIds: number[];
  posts: any[];
  selectedGroupId: number | '';
  onHidePost: (id: number) => void;
}

export const PostViewerPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ selectedPostIds, posts, selectedGroupId, onHidePost }) => (
  <div className="card" style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
    <h4 style={{ margin: '0 0 10px 0', textAlign: 'center', fontSize: 14 }}>소요기록 Viewer</h4>
    {selectedPostIds.length > 0 ? (
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 15 }}>
        {selectedPostIds.map(postId => {
          const post = posts.find(p => p.id === postId);
          if (!post) return null;
          return (
            <div key={postId} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)', paddingBottom: 10 }}>
              <h5 style={{ color: 'var(--error)', margin: '0 0 5px 0', fontSize: 13 }}>{post.title}</h5>
              <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', margin: '0 0 10px 0' }}>{post.content}</p>
              <button
                onClick={() => onHidePost(postId)} disabled={!selectedGroupId}
                className="btn btn-danger btn-sm"
                style={{ alignSelf: 'flex-end' }}
              >
                이 그룹에서 숨기기
              </button>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    ) : (
      <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: 13, textAlign: 'center' }}>
        차트에서 붉은 영역(소요기록)을 클릭하세요.
      </div>
    )}
  </div>
);

import React from 'react';
import type { ChartMode, ChartScale } from '../../../../constants/chartConfig';

interface Props {
  mode: ChartMode;
  scale: ChartScale;
  facilities: any[];
  rooms: any[];
  selectedFacility: number | '';
  selectedRoom: number | '';
  isChartLoading: boolean;
  onModeToggle: () => void;
  onScaleChange: (val: ChartScale) => void;
  onFacilityChange: (val: number | '') => void;
  onRoomChange: (val: number | '') => void;
  onQuickTime: (hours: number) => void;
  onQuickDay: (dayIdx: number) => void;
}

export const ScopingPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ mode, scale, facilities, rooms, selectedFacility, selectedRoom, isChartLoading, onModeToggle, onScaleChange, onFacilityChange, onRoomChange, onQuickTime, onQuickDay }) => (
  <div className="card" style={{ padding: 15, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10 }}>
    <h4 style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: 14 }}>조회 범위 설정</h4>
    <button onClick={onModeToggle} disabled={isChartLoading} className="btn btn-outline">현재 모드: {mode} (토글)</button>
    <select value={scale} onChange={e => onScaleChange(e.target.value as ChartScale)} disabled={isChartLoading} className="input">
      <option value="1m">1분 단위</option><option value="5m">5분 단위</option><option value="10m">10분 단위</option><option value="15m">15분 단위</option><option value="1h">1시간 단위</option>
    </select>
    <select value={selectedFacility} onChange={e => onFacilityChange(e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : '')} disabled={isChartLoading} className="input">
      <option value="">-- 시설 선택 --</option>
      {facilities.map(fac => <option key={fac.id} value={fac.id}>{fac.name}</option>)}
    </select>
    <select value={selectedRoom} onChange={e => onRoomChange(e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : '')} disabled={!selectedFacility || isChartLoading} className="input">
      <option value="">-- 호실 선택 --</option>
      {rooms.map(room => <option key={room.id} value={room.id}>{room.name}</option>)}
    </select>

    <div style={{ border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRadius: 12, padding: 10, marginTop: 5 }}>
      <div className="label" style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>최근 시간 이동 (현재 기준)</div>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 5, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        {[{ label: '1H', h: 1 }, { label: '3H', h: 3 }, { label: '6H', h: 6 }, { label: '12H', h: 12 }, { label: '1D', h: 24 }, { label: '1W', h: 24 * 7 }].map(btn => (
          <button key={btn.label} onClick={() => onQuickTime(btn.h)} disabled={isChartLoading} className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style={{ flex: 1 }}>{btn.label}</button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style={{ border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRadius: 12, padding: 10 }}>
      <div className="label" style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>최근 요일 이동</div>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 5 }}>
        {['일', '월', '화', '수', '목', '금', '토'].map((dayStr, idx) => (
          <button key={dayStr} onClick={() => onQuickDay(idx)} disabled={isChartLoading} className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" style={{ flex: 1 }}>{dayStr}</button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
);

import React from 'react';

interface Props {
  input: string;
  suggestions: any[];
  isChartLoading: boolean;
  onInputChange: (val: string) => void;
  onSubmit: () => void;
  onSelect: (sug: any) => void;
}

export const SmartOmniboxPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ input, suggestions, isChartLoading, onInputChange, onSubmit, onSelect }) => (
  <div className="card" style={{ padding: 15, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10 }}>
    <h4 style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: 14 }}>스마트 검색</h4>
    <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
      <input
        type="text" value={input} onChange={e => onInputChange(e.target.value)}
        onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') onSubmit(); }}
        disabled={isChartLoading} placeholder="예: 제1시설 101호 8월17일 1234"
        className="input"
      />
      {suggestions.length > 0 && (
        <div className="card" style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '110%', left: 0, right: 0, zIndex: 100, padding: 6 }}>
          {suggestions.slice(0, 12).map((sug, i) => (
            <div
              key={i} onClick={() => onSelect(sug)}
              style={{ padding: '8px 10px', cursor: 'pointer', borderRadius: 8, color: 'var(--primary)', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, textAlign: 'left' }}
              onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--primary-bg)')}
              onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent')}
            >
              {sug.label}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
    <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', textAlign: 'center' }}>엔터키 입력 시 자동 파싱 적용</div>
  </div>
);

■ File : IndexingTable.tsx
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_web\setuptmp\src\components\indexing\IndexingTable.tsx

import React from 'react';
import type { Role } from '../../types';

interface IndexEditMap {
  [roleId: number]: {
    display_name: string;
    short_name: string;
  };
}

interface Props {
  roles: Role[];
  editMap: IndexEditMap;
  onFieldChange: (roleId: number, field: 'display_name' | 'short_name', value: string) => void;
}

export const IndexingTable: React.FC<Props> = ({ roles, editMap, onFieldChange }) => {
  return (
    <div className="card" style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0, overflowY: 'auto', padding: 0 }}>
      <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', textAlign: 'left', fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
        <thead style={{ position: 'sticky', top: 0, backgroundColor: 'var(--surface)', zIndex: 1, borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
          <tr>
            <th style={{ padding: '12px 16px', width: '80px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 12 }}>ID</th>
            <th style={{ padding: '12px 16px', width: '220px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 12 }}>기능형 명칭 (수정 불가)</th>
            <th style={{ padding: '12px 16px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 12 }}>디스플레이용 명칭</th>
            <th style={{ padding: '12px 16px', width: '150px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 12 }}>약어 명칭 (3자)</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {roles.map((r) => {
            const currentEdit = editMap[r.role_id] || {
              display_name: r.display_name !== r.role_name ? (r.display_name || '') : '',
              short_name: r.short_name !== r.role_name ? (r.short_name || '') : '',
            };

            return (
              <tr key={r.role_id} style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
                <td style={{ padding: '8px 16px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>{r.role_id}</td>
                <td style={{ padding: '8px 16px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontFamily: 'ui-monospace, monospace', fontSize: 12 }}>{r.role_name}</td>
                <td style={{ padding: '6px 16px' }}>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={currentEdit.display_name}
                    placeholder={r.role_name}
                    onChange={(e) => onFieldChange(r.role_id, 'display_name', e.target.value)}
                    className="input"
                    style={{ height: 36 }}
                  />
                </td>
                <td style={{ padding: '6px 16px' }}>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    maxLength={3}
                    value={currentEdit.short_name}
                    placeholder={r.role_name.slice(0, 3)}
                    onChange={(e) => onFieldChange(r.role_id, 'short_name', e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
                    className="input"
                    style={{ height: 36, textAlign: 'center', fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--primary)' }}
                  />
                </td>
              </tr>
            );
          })}
          {roles.length === 0 && (
            <tr>
              <td colSpan={4} style={{ padding: 48, textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: 13 }}>
                선택된 호실에 포인트 롤이 없습니다.
              </td>
            </tr>
          )}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
};

■ File : ChartMappingPage.tsx
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_web\setuptmp\src\pages\ChartMappingPage.tsx
======================================================================

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { apiService } from '../services/apiService';
import { LeftPanel } from '../components/chart/mapper/LeftPanel';
import { RightPanel } from '../components/chart/mapper/RightPanel';
import type { Role, ChartGroup } from '../types';

export const ChartMappingPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [facilities, setFacilities] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [rooms, setRooms] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedFacility, setSelectedFacility] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [selectedRoom, setSelectedRoom] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const [room0Roles, setRoom0Roles] = useState<Role[]>([]);
  const [targetRoomRoles, setTargetRoomRoles] = useState<Role[]>([]);

  const [existingGroups, setExistingGroups] = useState<ChartGroup[]>([]);
  const [selectedGroupId, setSelectedGroupId] = useState<number | 'new'>('new');
  const [groupName, setGroupName] = useState('');
  const [droppedRoles, setDroppedRoles] = useState<Role[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    apiService.getFacilities().then((data) => setFacilities(data.facilities || []));
  }, []);

  const handleFacilityChange = async (facId: number) => {
    setSelectedFacility(facId);
    setSelectedRoom(null);
    setTargetRoomRoles([]);
    setExistingGroups([]);
    resetGroupForm();

    const roomData = await apiService.getRooms(facId);
    setRooms(roomData.rooms || []);
    const roleData = await apiService.getPointRoles(facId, 0);
    setRoom0Roles(roleData.roles || []);
  };

  const handleRoomChange = async (roomId: number) => {
    setSelectedRoom(roomId);
    resetGroupForm();
    if (selectedFacility) {
      const roleData = await apiService.getPointRoles(selectedFacility, roomId);
      setTargetRoomRoles(roleData.roles || []);
      const groupData = await apiService.getChartGroups(selectedFacility, roomId);
      setExistingGroups(groupData.groups || []);
    }
  };

  const resetGroupForm = () => {
    setSelectedGroupId('new');
    setGroupName('');
    setDroppedRoles([]);
  };

  const handleGroupSelect = (val: string) => {
    if (val === 'new') {
      resetGroupForm();
      return;
    }
    const gId = Number(val);
    setSelectedGroupId(gId);
    const targetGroup = existingGroups.find((g) => g.group_id === gId);
    if (targetGroup) {
      setGroupName(targetGroup.group_name);
      const allAvailableRoles = [...room0Roles, ...targetRoomRoles];
      const validRoleIds = (targetGroup.role_ids || []).filter((id) => id !== null);
      const loadedRoles = validRoleIds
        .map((id) => allAvailableRoles.find((r) => r.role_id === id))
        .filter(Boolean) as Role[];
      setDroppedRoles(loadedRoles);
    }
  };

  const onDragStart = (e: React.DragEvent, role: Role) => {
    e.dataTransfer.setData('application/json', JSON.stringify(role));
  };

  const onDragOver = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
  };

  const onDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const roleData = e.dataTransfer.getData('application/json');
    if (roleData) {
      const role: Role = JSON.parse(roleData);
      if (!droppedRoles.find((r) => r.role_id === role.role_id)) {
        setDroppedRoles([...droppedRoles, role]);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleReorderRoles = (sourceIndex: number, targetIndex: number) => {
    if (sourceIndex === targetIndex) return;
    const updated = [...droppedRoles];
    const [movedItem] = updated.splice(sourceIndex, 1);
    updated.splice(targetIndex, 0, movedItem);
    setDroppedRoles(updated);
  };

  const handleSaveGroup = async () => {
    if (!selectedFacility || !selectedRoom) return alert('시설과 호실을 선택하세요.');
    if (!groupName) return alert('그룹 명칭을 입력하세요.');
    if (droppedRoles.length === 0) return alert('포인트 롤을 추가하세요.');
    if (selectedGroupId !== 'new') return alert('수정 기능은 백엔드 구현 후 지원됩니다.');

    try {
      await apiService.createChartGroup({
        facility_id: selectedFacility,
        room_id: selectedRoom,
        group_name: groupName,
        role_ids: droppedRoles.map((r) => r.role_id),
      });
      alert('생성 완료!');
      handleRoomChange(selectedRoom);
    } catch (e) {
      alert('저장 실패');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flex: 1, minHeight: 0, height: '100%', gap: 12, padding: 24, boxSizing: 'border-box' }}>
      <LeftPanel
        facilities={facilities}
        rooms={rooms}
        selectedFacility={selectedFacility}
        selectedRoom={selectedRoom}
        room0Roles={room0Roles}
        targetRoomRoles={targetRoomRoles}
        droppedRoleIds={droppedRoles.map((r) => r.role_id)}
        onFacilityChange={handleFacilityChange}
        onRoomChange={handleRoomChange}
        onDragStart={onDragStart}
      />
      <RightPanel
        existingGroups={existingGroups}
        selectedGroupId={selectedGroupId}
        groupName={groupName}
        droppedRoles={droppedRoles}
        onGroupSelect={handleGroupSelect}
        onGroupNameChange={setGroupName}
        onDrop={onDrop}
        onDragOver={onDragOver}
        onRemoveRole={(id) => setDroppedRoles(droppedRoles.filter((r) => r.role_id !== id))}
        onReorderRoles={handleReorderRoles}
        onSaveGroup={handleSaveGroup}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import ReactECharts from 'echarts-for-react';
import { MainChartViewer, SubChartViewer } from '../components/chart/viewer/EChartsViewer';

import { SmartOmniboxPanel } from '../components/chart/viewer/panels/SmartOmniboxPanel';
import { ScopingPanel } from '../components/chart/viewer/panels/ScopingPanel';
import { PostViewerPanel } from '../components/chart/viewer/panels/PostViewerPanel';
import { MappingActivePanel } from '../components/chart/viewer/panels/MappingActivePanel';
import { BlacklistPanel } from '../components/chart/viewer/panels/BlacklistPanel';
import { ChartStatsPanel } from '../components/chart/viewer/panels/ChartStatsPanel';
import { CHART_SCOPING_CONFIG} from '../constants/chartConfig';

const getLocalDatetimeString = (date: Date) => {
  const offset = date.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000;
  return (new Date(date.getTime() - offset)).toISOString().slice(0, 16);
};


const COLOR_PALETTE = ['#5470c6', '#91cc75', '#fac858', '#ee6666', '#73c0de', '#3ba272', '#fc8452', '#9a60b4', '#ea7ccc', '#ff9f7f'];

function useDebouncedCallback<T extends (...args: any[]) => void>(callback: T, delay: number) {
  const timeoutRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  return useCallback((...args: Parameters<T>) => {
    if (timeoutRef.current) window.clearTimeout(timeoutRef.current);
    timeoutRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => callback(...args), delay);
  }, [callback, delay]);
}

export const ChartViewerPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<ChartMode>('DEFAULT');
  const [scale, setScale] = useState<ChartScale>('1m');
  const [targetDate, setTargetDate] = useState<string>(getLocalDatetimeString(new Date()));
  const [refreshTrigger, setRefreshTrigger] = useState<number>(0); 
  
  const [facilities, setFacilities] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [rooms, setRooms] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [groups, setGroups] = useState<ChartGroup[]>([]);
  const [allRoles, setAllRoles] = useState<Role[]>([]);

  const [selectedFacility, setSelectedFacility] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [selectedRoom, setSelectedRoom] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [selectedGroupId, setSelectedGroupId] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [checkedRoles, setCheckedRoles] = useState<number[]>([]);
  
  const [chartDataSets, setChartDataSets] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [roleColors, setRoleColors] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});

  const [showMainInfo, setShowMainInfo] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [showMainGrid, setShowMainGrid] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [showSubInfo, setShowSubInfo] = useState<boolean>(false); 
  const [showSubGrid, setShowSubGrid] = useState<boolean>(true);

  const [openMenuRoleId, setOpenMenuRoleId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [activeGuidelines, setActiveGuidelines] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [invertedBools, setInvertedBools] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [isChartLoading, setIsChartLoading] = useState<boolean>(false); 

  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [alarms, setAlarms] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [hiddenPosts, setHiddenPosts] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedPostViewerIds, setSelectedPostViewerIds] = useState<number[]>([]);

  const [omniboxInput, setOmniboxInput] = useState('');
  const [omniboxSuggestions, setOmniboxSuggestions] = useState<any[]>([]);

  const mainChartRef = useRef<ReactECharts | null>(null);
  const subChartRef = useRef<ReactECharts | null>(null); 
  const loadedRangeRef = useRef<{ start: number, end: number } | null>(null);
  const [loadedRangeState, setLoadedRangeState] = useState<{ start: number, end: number } | null>(null);
  const [currentViewStart, setCurrentViewStart] = useState<number | null>(null);

  // useEffect(() => { 
  //   //apiService.getFacilities().then(res => setFacilities(res.facilities || [])); 
  //   apiService.getUserFacilities(user.id).then(res => setFacilities(res.facilities || [])); 
  // }, [user.id]);
  useEffect(() => { 
    apiService.getFacilities().then(res => setFacilities(res.facilities || [])); 
  }, []);

  // useEffect(() => {
  //   if (selectedFacility) {
  //     apiService.getRooms(Number(selectedFacility)).then(res => {
  //       const fetchedRooms = res.rooms || [];
  //       setRooms(fetchedRooms);
  //       if (fetchedRooms.length > 0) setSelectedRoom(fetchedRooms[0].id); 
  //     });
  //     setSelectedRoom(''); setGroups([]); setAllRoles([]); setSelectedGroupId(''); setCheckedRoles([]); 
  //     setChartDataSets([]); setPosts([]); setHiddenPosts([]); setAlarms([]); setSelectedPostViewerIds([]); setInvertedBools([]);
  //     loadedRangeRef.current = null; setLoadedRangeState(null); setCurrentViewStart(null);
  //   }
  // }, [selectedFacility]); 
  useEffect(() => {
  if (!selectedFacility) {
    setRooms([]);
    setSelectedRoom('');
    return;
  }

  // 1. 시설이 변경되었을 때 하위 데이터 먼저 초기화
  setSelectedRoom('');
  setRooms([]);
  setGroups([]);
  setAllRoles([]);
  setSelectedGroupId('');
  setCheckedRoles([]);
  setChartDataSets([]);
  setPosts([]);
  setHiddenPosts([]);
  setAlarms([]);
  setSelectedPostViewerIds([]);
  setInvertedBools([]);
  loadedRangeRef.current = null;
  setLoadedRangeState(null);
  setCurrentViewStart(null);

  // 2. 초기화 완료 후 방 목록 요청
  apiService.getRooms(Number(selectedFacility)).then(res => {
    const fetchedRooms = res.rooms || [];
    setRooms(fetchedRooms);
    if (fetchedRooms.length > 0) {
      setSelectedRoom(fetchedRooms[0].id);
    }
  }).catch(err => {
    console.error("호실 불러오기 실패:", err);
  });
}, [selectedFacility]);


  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedFacility && selectedRoom) {
      apiService.getChartGroups(Number(selectedFacility), Number(selectedRoom)).then(res => {
        const fetchedGroups = res.groups || [];
        setGroups(fetchedGroups);
        if (fetchedGroups.length > 0) setSelectedGroupId(fetchedGroups[0].group_id); 
      }); 
      apiService.getPointRoles(Number(selectedFacility), Number(selectedRoom)).then(res => {
        const roles = res.roles || [];
        setAllRoles(roles);
        const newColorMap: Record<number, string> = {};
        roles.forEach((r: Role, idx: number) => { newColorMap[r.role_id] = COLOR_PALETTE[idx % COLOR_PALETTE.length]; });
        setRoleColors(newColorMap);
      });
      setSelectedGroupId(''); setCheckedRoles([]); setChartDataSets([]); setPosts([]); setHiddenPosts([]); setAlarms([]); setSelectedPostViewerIds([]); setInvertedBools([]);
      loadedRangeRef.current = null; setLoadedRangeState(null); setCurrentViewStart(null);
    }
  }, [selectedRoom, selectedFacility]); 

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedGroupId) {
      const group = groups.find(g => g.group_id === Number(selectedGroupId));
      if (group && group.role_ids) setCheckedRoles(group.role_ids.filter(id => id !== null));
    } else {
      setCheckedRoles([]); setChartDataSets([]); setPosts([]); setHiddenPosts([]); setAlarms([]); setSelectedPostViewerIds([]); setInvertedBools([]);
      loadedRangeRef.current = null; setLoadedRangeState(null); setCurrentViewStart(null);
    }
  }, [selectedGroupId, groups]); 

  useEffect(() => {
    const q = omniboxInput.replace(/\s+/g, '').toLowerCase();
    if (!q) { setOmniboxSuggestions([]); return; }
    const sugs: any[] = [];
    facilities.forEach(f => { if (f.name.replace(/\s+/g, '').toLowerCase().includes(q)) sugs.push({ type: 'facility', id: f.id, name: f.name, label: `${f.name} (시설)` }); });
    if (selectedFacility) {
      rooms.forEach(r => { if (r.name.replace(/\s+/g, '').toLowerCase().includes(q)) sugs.push({ type: 'room', id: r.id, name: r.name, label: `${r.name} (호실)` }); });
    }
    if (selectedRoom) {
      groups.forEach(g => { if (g.group_name.replace(/\s+/g, '').toLowerCase().includes(q)) sugs.push({ type: 'group', id: g.group_id, name: g.group_name, label: `${g.group_name} (맵핑)` }); });
    }
    sugs.sort((a, b) => a.name.length - b.name.length);
    setOmniboxSuggestions(sugs);
  }, [omniboxInput, facilities, rooms, groups, selectedFacility, selectedRoom]);

  const handleOmniboxSelect = (sug: any) => {
    if (sug.type === 'facility') setSelectedFacility(sug.id);
    else if (sug.type === 'room') setSelectedRoom(sug.id);
    else if (sug.type === 'group') setSelectedGroupId(sug.id);
    setOmniboxInput('');
  };

  const handleOmniboxSubmit = () => {
    let remain = omniboxInput;
    let updatedDate = new Date(targetDate);
    let dateChanged = false; let timeChanged = false;

    const dateRegex1 = /(?:^|\s)(\d{4})[./-](\d{1,2})[./-](\d{1,2})(?:\s|$)/; 
    const dateRegex2 = /(?:^|\s)(\d{1,2})[./-](\d{1,2})(?:\s|$)/;             
    const dateRegex3 = /(?:^|\s)(\d{1,2})\s*월\s*(\d{1,2})\s*일(?:\s|$)/;     

    let m;
    if ((m = remain.match(dateRegex1))) { updatedDate.setFullYear(parseInt(m[1]), parseInt(m[2]) - 1, parseInt(m[3])); remain = remain.replace(m[0], ' '); dateChanged = true; } 
    else if ((m = remain.match(dateRegex3))) { updatedDate.setMonth(parseInt(m[1]) - 1, parseInt(m[2])); remain = remain.replace(m[0], ' '); dateChanged = true; } 
    else if ((m = remain.match(dateRegex2))) { updatedDate.setMonth(parseInt(m[1]) - 1, parseInt(m[2])); remain = remain.replace(m[0], ' '); dateChanged = true; }

    const timeRegex1 = /(?:^|\s)([01]\d|2[0-3]):([0-5]\d)(?:\s|$)/; 
    const timeRegex2 = /(?:^|\s)([01]\d|2[0-3])([0-5]\d)(?:\s|$)/;  
    
    if ((m = remain.match(timeRegex1))) { updatedDate.setHours(parseInt(m[1]), parseInt(m[2]), 0, 0); remain = remain.replace(m[0], ' '); timeChanged = true; } 
    else if ((m = remain.match(timeRegex2))) { updatedDate.setHours(parseInt(m[1]), parseInt(m[2]), 0, 0); remain = remain.replace(m[0], ' '); timeChanged = true; }

    if (dateChanged && !timeChanged) updatedDate.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    if (dateChanged || timeChanged) setTargetDate(getLocalDatetimeString(updatedDate));

    const q = remain.replace(/\s+/g, '');
    if (q && omniboxSuggestions.length > 0) handleOmniboxSelect(omniboxSuggestions[0]);
    else setOmniboxInput('');
  };

  const fetchAndMergeChunk = async (fetchStartMs: number, fetchEndMs: number, direction: 'replace' | 'append' | 'prepend') => {
    if (!selectedFacility || !selectedRoom || checkedRoles.length === 0) return;
    setIsChartLoading(true);
    try {
      const startIso = new Date(fetchStartMs).toISOString();
      const endIso = new Date(fetchEndMs).toISOString();
      const res = await apiService.getHistoricalData(Number(selectedFacility), Number(selectedRoom), selectedGroupId, scale, startIso, endIso, checkedRoles);
      
      const newDatasets = res.data?.datasets || [];
      const newPosts = res.data?.posts || [];
      const newAlarms = res.data?.alarms || [];
      const newHiddenPosts = res.data?.hidden_posts || [];

      if (direction === 'replace') {
        setChartDataSets(newDatasets); setPosts(newPosts); setAlarms(newAlarms); setHiddenPosts(newHiddenPosts);
        loadedRangeRef.current = { start: fetchStartMs, end: fetchEndMs };
        setCurrentViewStart(fetchStartMs);
      } else {
        setChartDataSets(prev => prev.map(prevDs => {
          const newDs = newDatasets.find((n: any) => n.role_id === prevDs.role_id);
          if (!newDs) return prevDs;
          let mergedData = direction === 'append' ? [...prevDs.data, ...newDs.data] : [...newDs.data, ...prevDs.data];
          mergedData.sort((a, b) => new Date(a.timestamp).getTime() - new Date(b.timestamp).getTime());
          if (mergedData.length > 5000) mergedData = direction === 'append' ? mergedData.slice(-5000) : mergedData.slice(0, 5000);
          return { ...prevDs, data: mergedData };
        }));

        setPosts(prev => { const combined = direction === 'append' ? [...prev, ...newPosts] : [...newPosts, ...prev]; return Array.from(new Map(combined.map(p => [p.id, p])).values()); });
        setAlarms(prev => { const combined = direction === 'append' ? [...prev, ...newAlarms] : [...newAlarms, ...prev]; return Array.from(new Map(combined.map(a => [a.alarm_id, a])).values()); });

        if (loadedRangeRef.current) {
          const config = CHART_SCOPING_CONFIG[mode][scale];
          const maxMsRange = config.chunkSize * config.maxChunks;
          let newStart = Math.min(loadedRangeRef.current.start, fetchStartMs);
          let newEnd = Math.max(loadedRangeRef.current.end, fetchEndMs);
          if (newEnd - newStart > maxMsRange) {
            if (direction === 'append') newStart = newEnd - maxMsRange;
            if (direction === 'prepend') newEnd = newStart + maxMsRange;
          }
          loadedRangeRef.current = { start: newStart, end: newEnd };
        }
      }
      setLoadedRangeState(loadedRangeRef.current);
    } catch (err) { console.error(err); } 
    finally { setIsChartLoading(false); }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedFacility && selectedRoom && checkedRoles.length > 0 && targetDate) {
      const endMs = new Date(targetDate).getTime();
      const chunkMs = CHART_SCOPING_CONFIG[mode][scale].chunkSize;
      fetchAndMergeChunk(endMs - chunkMs, endMs, 'replace');
    } else {
      setChartDataSets([]); setPosts([]); setAlarms([]); setHiddenPosts([]);
      loadedRangeRef.current = null; setLoadedRangeState(null); setCurrentViewStart(null);
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [selectedFacility, selectedRoom, scale, targetDate, checkedRoles, refreshTrigger]); 

  const handleDataZoomFetch = useDebouncedCallback((viewStartMs: number, viewEndMs: number) => {
    if (isChartLoading || !loadedRangeRef.current || checkedRoles.length === 0) return;
    const config = CHART_SCOPING_CONFIG[mode][scale];
    const buffer = config.chunkSize * 0.2; 
    if (viewStartMs < loadedRangeRef.current.start + buffer) fetchAndMergeChunk(loadedRangeRef.current.start - config.chunkSize, loadedRangeRef.current.start, 'prepend');
    else if (viewEndMs > loadedRangeRef.current.end - buffer) fetchAndMergeChunk(loadedRangeRef.current.end, loadedRangeRef.current.end + config.chunkSize, 'append');
  }, 500);

  const handleZoomCapture = useCallback((start: number, end: number) => {
    setCurrentViewStart(start); 
    handleDataZoomFetch(start, end);
  }, [handleDataZoomFetch]);

  const isBoolRole = (roleId: number) => {
    const role = allRoles.find(r => String(r.role_id) === String(roleId));
    if (!role) return false;
    return role.data_type ? role.data_type === 'BOOL' : role.role_name.toLowerCase().includes('bool');
  }; 

  const handleModeToggle = () => {
    if (isChartLoading) return; 
    setMode(prev => {
      const nextMode = prev === 'DEFAULT' ? 'EXTENT' : 'DEFAULT';
      if (nextMode === 'EXTENT') {
        const boolRoles = checkedRoles.filter(id => isBoolRole(id));
        const mainRoles = checkedRoles.filter(id => !isBoolRole(id));
        if (mainRoles.length > 1) setCheckedRoles([...boolRoles, mainRoles[0]]);
      }
      return nextMode;
    });
  }; 

  const handleRoleToggle = (roleId: number) => {
    if (isChartLoading) return; 
    const isBool = isBoolRole(roleId);
    setCheckedRoles(prev => prev.includes(roleId) ? prev.filter(id => id !== roleId) : (mode === 'EXTENT' && !isBool ? [...prev.filter(id => isBoolRole(id)), roleId] : [...prev, roleId]));
  }; 

  const handleInvertToggle = (roleId: number) => setInvertedBools(prev => prev.includes(roleId) ? prev.filter(id => id !== roleId) : [...prev, roleId]);
  const handleColorChange = (roleId: number, color: string) => setRoleColors(prev => ({ ...prev, [roleId]: color }));
  const handleGuidelineToggle = (roleId: number) => setActiveGuidelines(prev => prev.includes(roleId) ? prev.filter(id => id !== roleId) : [...prev, roleId]);

  const syncChartDataZoom = (sourceRef: React.RefObject<ReactECharts | null>, targetRef: React.RefObject<ReactECharts | null>) => {
    const option = sourceRef.current?.getEchartsInstance().getOption() as any;
    if (option?.dataZoom?.length > 0) targetRef.current?.getEchartsInstance().dispatchAction({ type: 'dataZoom', startValue: option.dataZoom[0].startValue, endValue: option.dataZoom[0].endValue });
  }; 

  const applyOneHourView = () => {
    const option = mainChartRef.current?.getEchartsInstance().getOption() as any;
    let centerTime = new Date(targetDate).getTime();
    if (option?.dataZoom?.length > 0 && option.dataZoom[0].startValue) centerTime = (option.dataZoom[0].startValue + option.dataZoom[0].endValue) / 2;
    const zoomPayload = { type: 'dataZoom', startValue: centerTime - 1800000, endValue: centerTime + 1800000 };
    mainChartRef.current?.getEchartsInstance().dispatchAction(zoomPayload);
    subChartRef.current?.getEchartsInstance().dispatchAction(zoomPayload);
  }; 

  const handleQuickTimeSelect = (hours: number) => {
    if (isChartLoading) return; 
    const now = new Date(); setTargetDate(getLocalDatetimeString(now)); 
    setTimeout(() => {
      const zoomPayload = { type: 'dataZoom', startValue: now.getTime() - (hours * 3600000), endValue: now.getTime() };
      mainChartRef.current?.getEchartsInstance().dispatchAction(zoomPayload);
      subChartRef.current?.getEchartsInstance().dispatchAction(zoomPayload);
    }, 300); 
  }; 

  const handleQuickDaySelect = (targetDayOfWeek: number) => {
    if (isChartLoading) return; 
    const now = new Date();
    let diff = now.getDay() - targetDayOfWeek;
    if (diff < 0) diff += 7;
    setTargetDate(getLocalDatetimeString(new Date(now.getTime() - (diff * 86400000))));
  }; 

  const handleChartClick = (type: 'post' | 'alarm', ids: number | number[]) => {
    if (type === 'post') setSelectedPostViewerIds(Array.isArray(ids) ? ids : [ids]);
  };

  const handleHidePost = async (postId: number) => {
    if (!selectedGroupId) return;
    try { await apiService.hidePostFromGroup(Number(selectedGroupId), postId); setSelectedPostViewerIds(prev => prev.filter(id => id !== postId)); setRefreshTrigger(prev => prev + 1); } catch (e) {}
  };

  const handleRestorePost = async (postId: number) => {
    if (!selectedGroupId) return;
    try { await apiService.showPostInGroup(Number(selectedGroupId), postId); setRefreshTrigger(prev => prev + 1); } catch (e) {}
  };

  const handleCopySearchQuery = () => {
    if (!selectedFacility || !selectedRoom || !currentViewStart) return;
    const facName = facilities.find(f => f.id === selectedFacility)?.name || '';
    const roomName = rooms.find(r => r.id === selectedRoom)?.name || '';
    const d = new Date(currentViewStart);
    const query = `${facName} ${roomName} ${d.getMonth() + 1}/${d.getDate()} ${d.getHours().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${d.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(query).then(() => alert(`클립보드에 복사되었습니다:\n${query}`));
  };

  const mainDataSets = chartDataSets.filter(ds => ds.role_name && !isBoolRole(ds.role_id));
  const subDataSets = chartDataSets.filter(ds => ds.role_name && isBoolRole(ds.role_id)); 

  // 💡 [UI 핵심 수정] 최상위 컨테이너에 overflowY: 'auto' 적용
  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%', overflowY: 'auto', gap: 12, boxSizing: 'border-box', padding: 24 }}>

      {/* 상단: 차트 + 검색/스코핑 패널 (최소 높이 보장) */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, minHeight: 650, flexShrink: 0 }}>

        {/* 왼쪽 차트 영역 */}
        <div style={{ flex: 3, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10 }}>
          <TopToolBar showInfo={showMainInfo} showGrid={showMainGrid} targetDate={targetDate} isChartLoading={isChartLoading} onCopy={handleCopySearchQuery} onToggleInfo={() => setShowMainInfo(!showMainInfo)} onToggleGrid={() => setShowMainGrid(!showMainGrid)} onOneHourView={applyOneHourView} onDateChange={setTargetDate} />
          <div className="card" style={{ flex: 2, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', minHeight: 0, padding: 12 }}>
            <MainChartViewer echartsRef={mainChartRef} datasets={mainDataSets} roleColors={roleColors} showInfo={showMainInfo} showGrid={showMainGrid} activeGuidelines={activeGuidelines} isLoading={isChartLoading} posts={posts} alarms={alarms} onChartClick={handleChartClick} loadedRange={loadedRangeState} onDataZoomPan={handleZoomCapture} />
          </div>
          <LowerToolBar showInfo={showSubInfo} showGrid={showSubGrid} onToggleInfo={() => setShowSubInfo(!showSubInfo)} onToggleGrid={() => setShowSubGrid(!showSubGrid)} onSyncTop={() => syncChartDataZoom(mainChartRef, subChartRef)} onSyncSub={() => syncChartDataZoom(subChartRef, mainChartRef)} />
          <div className="card" style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', minHeight: 0, padding: 12 }}>
            <SubChartViewer echartsRef={subChartRef} datasets={subDataSets} roleColors={roleColors} showInfo={showSubInfo} showGrid={showSubGrid} isLoading={isChartLoading} posts={posts} alarms={alarms} invertedBools={invertedBools} loadedRange={loadedRangeState} />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 오른쪽 패널 영역 */}
        <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10 }}>
          <SmartOmniboxPanel input={omniboxInput} suggestions={omniboxSuggestions} isChartLoading={isChartLoading} onInputChange={setOmniboxInput} onSubmit={handleOmniboxSubmit} onSelect={handleOmniboxSelect} />
          <ScopingPanel mode={mode} scale={scale} facilities={facilities} rooms={rooms} selectedFacility={selectedFacility} selectedRoom={selectedRoom} isChartLoading={isChartLoading} onModeToggle={handleModeToggle} onScaleChange={setScale} onFacilityChange={setSelectedFacility} onRoomChange={setSelectedRoom} onQuickTime={handleQuickTimeSelect} onQuickDay={handleQuickDaySelect} />
          <PostViewerPanel selectedPostIds={selectedPostViewerIds} posts={posts} selectedGroupId={selectedGroupId} onHidePost={handleHidePost} />
          <ChartStatsPanel datasets={mainDataSets} roleColors={roleColors} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 하단: 맵핑 활성화 + 블랙리스트 (내용에 따라 자동 확장) */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, flexShrink: 0 }}>
        <MappingActivePanel groups={groups} allRoles={allRoles} roleColors={roleColors} selectedGroupId={selectedGroupId} selectedRoom={selectedRoom} isChartLoading={isChartLoading} checkedRoles={checkedRoles} activeGuidelines={activeGuidelines} invertedBools={invertedBools} openMenuRoleId={openMenuRoleId} onGroupChange={setSelectedGroupId} onRoleToggle={handleRoleToggle} onMenuToggle={setOpenMenuRoleId} onColorChange={handleColorChange} onGuidelineToggle={handleGuidelineToggle} onInvertToggle={handleInvertToggle} isBoolRole={isBoolRole} />
        <BlacklistPanel hiddenPosts={hiddenPosts} onRestore={handleRestorePost} />
      </div>

    </div>
  );
};

======================================================================
■ File : FacilityManagerPage.tsx
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_web\setuptmp\src\pages\FacilityManagerPage.tsx
======================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { apiService } from '../services/apiService';

export const FacilityManagerPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [facilities, setFacilities] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [rooms, setRooms] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [users, setUsers] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedFacility, setSelectedFacility] = useState<number | ''>('');

  // 호실별 접기/펼치기 상태 및 역할 캐시
  const [expandedRoomIds, setExpandedRoomIds] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [roomRolesMap, setRoomRolesMap] = useState<Record<number, any[]>>({});

  // 선택 및 수정 상태
  const [selectedRoom, setSelectedRoom] = useState<any | null>(null);
  const [selectedRole, setSelectedRole] = useState<any | null>(null);
  const [editFac, setEditFac] = useState<any>(null);

  // 생성 폼 상태
  const [newFac, setNewFac] = useState({ name: '', access_code: '', start_date: '', end_date: '' });
  const [newRoomName, setNewRoomName] = useState('');
  const [newRole, setNewRole] = useState({
    role_name: '', display_name: '', short_name: '', data_type: 'TEMP',
    target_value: '', target_low_limit: '', target_high_limit: '', valid_from: '', valid_to: ''
  });

  // 검색창
  const [searchInput, setSearchInput] = useState('');
  const [suggestions, setSuggestions] = useState<any[]>([]);

  const loadFacilities = async () => {
    const res = await apiService.getAllFacilitiesAdmin();
    setFacilities(res.facilities || []);
  };

  const loadRoomsAndUsers = async (facId: number) => {
    const rRes = await apiService.getRooms(facId);
    setRooms(rRes.rooms || []);
    const uRes = await apiService.getFacilityUsersAdmin(facId);
    setUsers(uRes.users || []);
  };

  const loadRolesForRoom = async (roomId: number) => {
    const res = await apiService.getRolesByRoomAdmin(roomId);
    setRoomRolesMap(prev => ({ ...prev, [roomId]: res.roles || [] }));
  };

  useEffect(() => { loadFacilities(); }, []);

  const handleFacilityChange = (facId: number) => {
    setSelectedFacility(facId);
    setSelectedRoom(null);
    setSelectedRole(null);
    setExpandedRoomIds([]);
    setRoomRolesMap({});
    const target = facilities.find(f => f.id === facId);
    setEditFac(target ? { ...target, start_date: target.start_date || '', end_date: target.end_date || '' } : null);
    if (facId) loadRoomsAndUsers(facId);
    else { setRooms([]); setUsers([]); }
  };

  const toggleRoomExpand = (roomId: number, e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (expandedRoomIds.includes(roomId)) {
      setExpandedRoomIds(prev => prev.filter(id => id !== roomId));
    } else {
      setExpandedRoomIds(prev => [...prev, roomId]);
      if (!roomRolesMap[roomId]) loadRolesForRoom(roomId);
    }
  };

  // 하향식 검색
  useEffect(() => {
    const q = searchInput.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) { setSuggestions([]); return; }
    const res: any[] = [];
    facilities.forEach(f => {
      if (f.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) res.push({ type: 'facility', id: f.id, label: `[시설] ${f.name}` });
    });
    if (selectedFacility) {
      rooms.forEach(r => {
        if (r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) res.push({ type: 'room', id: r.id, raw: r, label: `[호실] ${r.name}` });
      });
    }
    setSuggestions(res.slice(0, 10));
  }, [searchInput, facilities, rooms, selectedFacility]);

  const handleApplySuggestion = (sug: any) => {
    if (sug.type === 'facility') handleFacilityChange(sug.id);
    else if (sug.type === 'room') {
      setSelectedRoom(sug.raw);
      setSelectedRole(null);
      if (!expandedRoomIds.includes(sug.id)) {
        setExpandedRoomIds(prev => [...prev, sug.id]);
        loadRolesForRoom(sug.id);
      }
    }
    setSearchInput('');
    setSuggestions([]);
  };

  // 핸들러들
  const handleCreateFacility = async () => {
    if (!newFac.name || !newFac.access_code) return alert('시설명과 접근 코드는 필수입니다.');
    await apiService.createFacilityAdmin(newFac);
    alert('시설이 추가되었습니다.');
    setNewFac({ name: '', access_code: '', start_date: '', end_date: '' });
    loadFacilities();
  };

  const handleUpdateFacility = async () => {
    if (!editFac) return;
    await apiService.updateFacilityAdmin(editFac.id, editFac);
    alert('시설 정보가 수정되었습니다.');
    loadFacilities();
  };

  const handleCreateRoom = async () => {
    if (!selectedFacility || !newRoomName) return alert('호실명을 입력하세요.');
    await apiService.createRoomAdmin(Number(selectedFacility), { name: newRoomName });
    setNewRoomName('');
    loadRoomsAndUsers(Number(selectedFacility));
  };

  const handleUpdateRoom = async () => {
    if (!selectedRoom) return;
    await apiService.updateRoomAdmin(selectedRoom.id, selectedRoom);
    alert('호실 정보 수정 완료');
    loadRoomsAndUsers(Number(selectedFacility));
  };

  const handleDeleteRoom = async (roomId: number) => {
    try {
      if (!window.confirm('정말 삭제하시겠습니까?')) return;
      await apiService.deleteRoomAdmin(roomId);
      if (selectedRoom?.id === roomId) setSelectedRoom(null);
      loadRoomsAndUsers(Number(selectedFacility));
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e.message || '삭제 실패');
    }
  };

  const handleCreateRole = async () => {
    if (!selectedFacility || !selectedRoom || !newRole.role_name) return alert('기능 명칭을 입력하세요.');
    try {
      await apiService.createPointRoleAdmin(Number(selectedFacility), selectedRoom.id, newRole);
      alert('포인트 롤이 생성되었습니다.');
      setNewRole({
        role_name: '', display_name: '', short_name: '', data_type: 'TEMP',
        target_value: '', target_low_limit: '', target_high_limit: '', valid_from: '', valid_to: ''
      });
      loadRolesForRoom(selectedRoom.id);
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e.message || '생성 실패');
    }
  };

  const handleUpdateRole = async () => {
    if (!selectedRole) return;
    try {
      await apiService.updatePointRoleAdmin(selectedRole.role_id, selectedRole);
      alert('포인트 롤이 수정되었습니다.');
      loadRolesForRoom(selectedRole.room_id);
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e.message || '수정 실패');
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteRole = async (roleId: number, roomId: number) => {
    if (!window.confirm('포인트 롤을 삭제하시겠습니까?')) return;
    await apiService.deletePointRoleAdmin(roleId);
    if (selectedRole?.role_id === roleId) setSelectedRole(null);
    loadRolesForRoom(roomId);
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%', minHeight: 0, gap: 12, padding: 24, boxSizing: 'border-box' }}>

      <h1 style={{ fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: -0.3, flexShrink: 0 }}>시설 / 호실 관리</h1>

      {/* 1. 상단 하향식 검색바 */}
      <div className="card" style={{ position: 'relative', padding: 8, flexShrink: 0 }}>
        <input
          type="text" placeholder="시설 → 호실 검색 후 Enter"
          value={searchInput} onChange={e => setSearchInput(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && suggestions.length > 0) handleApplySuggestion(suggestions[0]); }}
          className="input"
        />
        {suggestions.length > 0 && (
          <div className="card" style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 'calc(100% + 4px)', left: 8, right: 8, zIndex: 10, padding: 6 }}>
            {suggestions.map((sug, idx) => (
              <div
                key={idx}
                onClick={() => handleApplySuggestion(sug)}
                style={{ padding: '8px 10px', cursor: 'pointer', borderRadius: 8, color: 'var(--primary)', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600 }}
                onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--primary-bg)')}
                onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent')}
              >
                {sug.label}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* 2. 시설/호실/포인트롤 매니저 3단 레이아웃 */}
      {/* 세 패널의 최소 너비 합이 창 너비보다 커지면 전체 페이지 대신 이 영역만 가로 스크롤되도록 함 */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, flex: 1, minHeight: 0, overflowX: 'auto' }}>

        {/* [1열] 시설 관리 패널 */}
        <div className="card" style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 260, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10, minHeight: 0 }}>
          <h4 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 800 }}>시설 관리</h4>

          <select value={selectedFacility} onChange={e => handleFacilityChange(Number(e.target.value))} className="input">
            <option value="">-- [시설 선택] --</option>
            {facilities.map(f => (
              <option key={f.id} value={f.id}>
                {f.name} ({f.access_code}) [{f.is_active ? '운영중' : '숨김/만료'}]
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          {editFac ? (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6, borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)', paddingTop: 10 }}>
              <span className="label">선택 시설 수정 ({editFac.is_active ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'})</span>
              <input type="text" placeholder="시설 이름" value={editFac.name} onChange={e => setEditFac({ ...editFac, name: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <input type="text" placeholder="접근 코드" value={editFac.access_code} onChange={e => setEditFac({ ...editFac, access_code: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>(계약-숨김) 시작일</label>
              <input type="date" value={editFac.start_date || ''} onChange={e => setEditFac({ ...editFac, start_date: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>(계약-숨김) 종료일</label>
              <input type="date" value={editFac.end_date || ''} onChange={e => setEditFac({ ...editFac, end_date: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <button onClick={handleUpdateFacility} className="btn btn-primary" style={{ marginTop: 2 }}>시설 갱신</button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6, borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)', paddingTop: 10 }}>
              <span className="label">신규 시설 등록</span>
              <input type="text" placeholder="시설 이름" value={newFac.name} onChange={e => setNewFac({ ...newFac, name: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <input type="text" placeholder="접근 코드" value={newFac.access_code} onChange={e => setNewFac({ ...newFac, access_code: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <input type="date" value={newFac.start_date} onChange={e => setNewFac({ ...newFac, start_date: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <input type="date" value={newFac.end_date} onChange={e => setNewFac({ ...newFac, end_date: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <button onClick={handleCreateFacility} className="btn btn-primary" style={{ marginTop: 2 }}>시설 생성</button>
            </div>
          )}

          <h5 style={{ margin: '6px 0 0 0', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>소속 유저 ({users.length}명)</h5>
          <div style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0, overflowY: 'auto', background: 'var(--bg)', borderRadius: 10, padding: 8, fontSize: 12, border: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
            {users.map(u => (
              <div key={u.id} style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)', padding: '4px 0' }}>
                <strong style={{ color: 'var(--primary)' }}>{u.name}</strong> ({u.email})
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* [2열] 호실 트리 및 관리 */}
        <div className="card" style={{ flex: 1.8, minWidth: 340, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10, minHeight: 0 }}>
          <h4 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 800 }}>호실 및 포인트 롤 트리</h4>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, flexShrink: 0 }}>
            <input type="text" placeholder="새 호실 명칭" value={newRoomName} onChange={e => setNewRoomName(e.target.value)} disabled={!selectedFacility} className="input" style={{ flex: 1, height: 36 }} />
            <button onClick={handleCreateRoom} disabled={!selectedFacility} className="btn btn-primary btn-sm" style={{ whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>호실 추가</button>
          </div>

          <div style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0, overflowY: 'auto', border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRadius: 10, background: 'var(--bg)', padding: 8 }}>
            {rooms.map(r => {
              const isExpanded = expandedRoomIds.includes(r.id);
              const isSelected = selectedRoom?.id === r.id;
              const isCommonRoom = r.name.includes('000호') || r.name.includes('공용');
              const roles = roomRolesMap[r.id] || [];

              return (
                <div key={r.id} className="card" style={{ marginBottom: 6, padding: 0, borderColor: isSelected ? 'var(--primary)' : 'var(--border)', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
                  <div
                    onClick={() => { setSelectedRoom({ ...r }); setSelectedRole(null); }}
                    style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', padding: '8px 12px', cursor: 'pointer', background: isSelected ? 'var(--primary-bg)' : 'transparent' }}
                  >
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                      <span onClick={(e) => toggleRoomExpand(r.id, e)} style={{ cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: 13, userSelect: 'none', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>
                        {isExpanded ? '▼' : '▶'}
                      </span>
                      <strong style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>{r.name}</strong>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>[{r.status}]</span>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      {!isCommonRoom && (
                        <button onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); handleDeleteRoom(r.id); }} className="btn btn-danger btn-sm" style={{ height: 26, padding: '0 8px' }}>삭제</button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* 호실 하위 포인트롤 리스트 (접기/펼치기) */}
                  {isExpanded && (
                    <div style={{ padding: '8px 12px 8px 30px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
                      {roles.map(role => (
                        <div
                          key={role.role_id}
                          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setSelectedRole({ ...role }); }}
                          style={{
                            display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center',
                            padding: '5px 8px', marginBottom: 3, fontSize: 12, borderRadius: 8, cursor: 'pointer',
                            backgroundColor: selectedRole?.role_id === role.role_id ? 'var(--primary-bg)' : 'transparent',
                            color: role.is_current ? 'var(--text)' : 'var(--text-secondary)'
                          }}
                        >
                          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                            <span className="chip" style={{ fontSize: 10, padding: '1px 6px', background: 'var(--surface)' }}>{role.short_name || 'N/A'}</span>
                            <span style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{role.display_name || role.role_name}</span>
                            <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>({role.role_name})</span>
                          </div>
                          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, alignItems: 'center', flexShrink: 0 }}>
                            <span style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700, color: role.is_current ? '#2fae66' : 'var(--error)' }}>
                              {role.is_current ? 'Active' : 'Expired'}
                            </span>
                            <button onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); handleDeleteRole(role.role_id, r.id); }} style={{ background: 'transparent', border: 'none', color: 'var(--error)', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: 12 }}>✕</button>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                      {roles.length === 0 && <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>등록된 포인트 롤이 없습니다.</span>}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          {selectedRoom && (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, alignItems: 'center', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)', paddingTop: 10, flexShrink: 0 }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>호실 수정:</span>
              <input type="text" value={selectedRoom.name} onChange={e => setSelectedRoom({ ...selectedRoom, name: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ flex: 1, height: 36 }} />
              <select value={selectedRoom.status} onChange={e => setSelectedRoom({ ...selectedRoom, status: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ height: 36, width: 160 }}>
                <option value="OPERATING">OPERATING</option>
                <option value="MAINTENANCE">MAINTENANCE</option>
                <option value="NOT_OPERATING">NOT_OPERATING</option>
              </select>
              <button onClick={handleUpdateRoom} className="btn btn-primary btn-sm" style={{ whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>저장</button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* [3열] 포인트 롤 등록 및 수정 패널 */}
        <div className="card" style={{ flex: 1.2, minWidth: 280, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 10, minHeight: 0 }}>
          <h4 style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 800, flexShrink: 0 }}>
            포인트 롤 관리 {selectedRoom ? `(${selectedRoom.name})` : ''}
          </h4>

          {selectedRole ? (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6, overflowY: 'auto', flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', flexShrink: 0 }}>
                <span className="label">선택 포인트롤 수정</span>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: selectedRole.is_current ? '#2fae66' : 'var(--error)' }}>
                  {selectedRole.is_current ? '운영 중 (Active)' : '만료/숨김 (Inactive)'}
                </span>
              </div>
              <input type="text" value={selectedRole.role_name} disabled className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} title="기능명칭은 수정 불가" />
              <input type="text" placeholder="디스플레이 명칭" value={selectedRole.display_name || ''} onChange={e => setSelectedRole({ ...selectedRole, display_name: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <input type="text" maxLength={3} placeholder="약어 명칭 (3자)" value={selectedRole.short_name || ''} onChange={e => setSelectedRole({ ...selectedRole, short_name: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="input" style={{ height: 36, color: 'var(--primary)', fontWeight: 700 }} />
              <select value={selectedRole.data_type || 'TEMP'} onChange={e => setSelectedRole({ ...selectedRole, data_type: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }}>
                <option value="TEMP">TEMP</option><option value="GEAR">GEAR</option><option value="BOOL">BOOL</option><option value="STATIC">STATIC</option>
              </select>
              <input type="text" placeholder="목표 수치 (target_value)" value={selectedRole.target_value || ''} onChange={e => setSelectedRole({ ...selectedRole, target_value: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="하한 (low_limit)"
                    value={selectedRole.target_low_limit || ''}
                    onChange={e => setSelectedRole({ ...selectedRole, target_low_limit: e.target.value })}
                    className="input"
                    style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, height: 36 }}
                />
                <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="상한 (high_limit)"
                    value={selectedRole.target_high_limit || ''}
                    onChange={e => setSelectedRole({ ...selectedRole, target_high_limit: e.target.value })}
                    className="input"
                    style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, height: 36 }}
                />
              </div>
              <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>적용 시작 일시 (valid_from)</label>
              <input type="datetime-local" value={selectedRole.valid_from ? selectedRole.valid_from.slice(0, 16) : ''} onChange={e => setSelectedRole({ ...selectedRole, valid_from: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>적용 종료 일시 (valid_to - 미입력 시 계속 활성화)</label>
              <input type="datetime-local" value={selectedRole.valid_to ? selectedRole.valid_to.slice(0, 16) : ''} onChange={e => setSelectedRole({ ...selectedRole, valid_to: e.target.value })} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, marginTop: 4, flexShrink: 0 }}>
                <button onClick={handleUpdateRole} className="btn btn-primary" style={{ flex: 1 }}>롤 정보 수정</button>
                <button onClick={() => setSelectedRole(null)} className="btn btn-ghost">취소</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6, overflowY: 'auto', flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
              <span className="label">신규 포인트 롤 추가</span>
              <input type="text" placeholder="기능 명칭 (role_name: 고유 식별)" value={newRole.role_name} onChange={e => setNewRole({ ...newRole, role_name: e.target.value })} disabled={!selectedRoom} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <input type="text" placeholder="디스플레이 명칭" value={newRole.display_name} onChange={e => setNewRole({ ...newRole, display_name: e.target.value })} disabled={!selectedRoom} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <input type="text" maxLength={3} placeholder="약어 명칭 (3자)" value={newRole.short_name} onChange={e => setNewRole({ ...newRole, short_name: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} disabled={!selectedRoom} className="input" style={{ height: 36, color: 'var(--primary)', fontWeight: 700 }} />
              <select value={newRole.data_type} onChange={e => setNewRole({ ...newRole, data_type: e.target.value })} disabled={!selectedRoom} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }}>
                <option value="TEMP">TEMP</option><option value="GEAR">GEAR</option><option value="BOOL">BOOL</option><option value="STATIC">STATIC</option>
              </select>
              <input type="text" placeholder="목표 수치 (target_value)" value={newRole.target_value} onChange={e => setNewRole({ ...newRole, target_value: e.target.value })} disabled={!selectedRoom} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="하한 수치"
                    value={newRole.target_low_limit}
                    onChange={e => setNewRole({ ...newRole, target_low_limit: e.target.value })}
                    disabled={!selectedRoom}
                    className="input"
                    style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, height: 36 }}
                />
                <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="상한 수치"
                    value={newRole.target_high_limit}
                    onChange={e => setNewRole({ ...newRole, target_high_limit: e.target.value })}
                    disabled={!selectedRoom}
                    className="input"
                    style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, height: 36 }}
                />
              </div>
              <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>적용 시작 일시 (valid_from)</label>
              <input type="datetime-local" value={newRole.valid_from} onChange={e => setNewRole({ ...newRole, valid_from: e.target.value })} disabled={!selectedRoom} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <label style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>적용 종료 일시 (valid_to - 미입력 시 계속 활성화)</label>
              <input type="datetime-local" value={newRole.valid_to} onChange={e => setNewRole({ ...newRole, valid_to: e.target.value })} disabled={!selectedRoom} className="input" style={{ height: 36 }} />
              <button onClick={handleCreateRole} disabled={!selectedRoom} className="btn btn-primary" style={{ marginTop: 4, flexShrink: 0 }}>신규 롤 생성</button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

■ File : LoginPage.tsx
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_web\setuptmp\src\pages\LoginPage.tsx
======================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { apiService } from '../services/apiService';
import { Monogram, Wordmark } from '../components/Brand';

export const LoginPage: React.FC<{ onLogin: (userData: any) => void }> = ({ onLogin }) => {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showDevTools, setShowDevTools] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  // [개발용] 관리자 계정 주입
  const injectAdmin = () => {
    setEmail('admin1@system.com');
    setPassword('hashed_password');
  };

  // [개발용] 사용자 계정 주입
  const injectUser = () => {
    setEmail('user_a@test.com');
    setPassword('hashed_password');
  };

  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      const loginRes = await apiService.login(email, password);
      const profile = await apiService.getUserProfile(loginRes.user_id);

      const user = {
        id: loginRes.user_id,
        email,
        name: profile.name,
        role: loginRes.role,
      };

      onLogin(user);

      // 권한별 리다이렉트
      if (user.role === 'ADMIN' || user.role === 'DEVELOPER') {
        navigate('/main');
      } else {
        navigate('/viewer');
      }
    } catch (error: any) {
      alert(error.message || '로그인 실패');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', flex: 1, gap: 24, padding: 24 }}>

      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, marginBottom: 4 }}>
        <Monogram size={56} />
        <div style={{ marginTop: 4 }}><Wordmark size={22} /></div>
        <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: 13 }}>관리자 계정으로 로그인해주세요.</p>
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="card" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 14, width: 320 }}>
        <input
          type="email"
          placeholder="이메일"
          value={email}
          onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          required
          className="input"
        />
        <input
          type="password"
          placeholder="비밀번호"
          value={password}
          onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          required
          className="input"
        />
        <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary" style={{ height: 44 }}>
          로그인
        </button>
      </form>

      {/* 개발용 계정 주입 도구 (기본 접힘) */}
      <div style={{ width: 320 }}>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setShowDevTools(v => !v)}
          className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm"
          style={{ width: '100%' }}
        >
          {showDevTools ? '▲' : '▼'} 개발자 도구 (DEBUG 전용)
        </button>
        {showDevTools && (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, marginTop: 8 }}>
            <button type="button" onClick={injectAdmin} className="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              관리자 계정 주입
            </button>
            <button type="button" onClick={injectUser} className="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              사용자 계정 주입
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
