■ File : PRIndexingPage.tsx
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_web\setuptmp\src\pages\PRIndexingPage.tsx
======================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { apiService } from '../services/apiService';
import { IndexingTable } from '../components/indexing/IndexingTable';
import type { Role } from '../types';

export const PRIndexingPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [facilities, setFacilities] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [rooms, setRooms] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [roles, setRoles] = useState<Role[]>([]);

  const [selectedFacility, setSelectedFacility] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [selectedRoom, setSelectedRoom] = useState<number | ''>('');

  // 편집 버퍼 상태
  const [editMap, setEditMap] = useState<{ [roleId: number]: { display_name: string; short_name: string } }>({});
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);

  // 검색창 상태
  const [searchInput, setSearchInput] = useState('');
  const [suggestions, setSuggestions] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    apiService.getFacilities().then(res => setFacilities(res.facilities || []));
  }, []);

  const handleFacilitySelect = async (facId: number) => {
    setSelectedFacility(facId);
    setSelectedRoom('');
    setRoles([]);
    setEditMap({});
    const res = await apiService.getRooms(facId);
    setRooms(res.rooms || []);
  };

  const handleRoomSelect = async (roomId: number, facId: number = Number(selectedFacility)) => {
    setSelectedRoom(roomId);
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await apiService.getPointRoles(facId, roomId);
      const fetchedRoles: Role[] = res.roles || [];
      setRoles(fetchedRoles);

      const initialEdits: any = {};
      fetchedRoles.forEach(r => {
        initialEdits[r.role_id] = {
          display_name: r.display_name !== r.role_name ? (r.display_name || '') : '',
          short_name: r.short_name !== r.role_name ? (r.short_name || '') : '',
        };
      });
      setEditMap(initialEdits);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // 하향식(시설 -> 호실 -> 포인트롤) 검색어 필터링
  useEffect(() => {
    const q = searchInput.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) { setSuggestions([]); return; }

    const results: any[] = [];
    // 1. 시설 검색
    facilities.forEach(f => {
      if (f.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) {
        results.push({ type: 'facility', id: f.id, label: `[시설] ${f.name}` });
      }
    });
    // 2. 호실 검색 (시설이 선택되어 있을 때)
    if (selectedFacility) {
      rooms.forEach(r => {
        if (r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) {
          results.push({ type: 'room', id: r.id, label: `[호실] ${r.name}` });
        }
      });
    }
    // 3. 포인트롤 검색 (호실이 선택되어 있을 때)
    if (selectedRoom) {
      roles.forEach(r => {
        if (r.role_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.display_name?.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.short_name?.toLowerCase().includes(q)) {
          results.push({ type: 'role', id: r.role_id, label: `[롤] ${r.display_name || r.role_name}` });
        }
      });
    }
    setSuggestions(results.slice(0, 10));
  }, [searchInput, facilities, rooms, roles, selectedFacility, selectedRoom]);

  const handleApplySuggestion = (sug: any) => {
    if (sug.type === 'facility') handleFacilitySelect(sug.id);
    else if (sug.type === 'room') handleRoomSelect(sug.id);
    setSearchInput('');
    setSuggestions([]);
  };

  const handleSearchSubmit = () => {
    if (suggestions.length > 0) {
      handleApplySuggestion(suggestions[0]);
    }
  };

  const handleFieldChange = (roleId: number, field: 'display_name' | 'short_name', value: string) => {
    setEditMap(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [roleId]: {
        ...prev[roleId],
        [field]: value
      }
    }));
  };

  // 전체 수정 저장
  const handleSaveAll = async () => {
    if (!selectedFacility || !selectedRoom) return alert('시설과 호실을 먼저 선택하세요.');

    const payload = roles.map(r => ({
      role_id: r.role_id,
      display_name: editMap[r.role_id]?.display_name || '',
      short_name: editMap[r.role_id]?.short_name || ''
    }));

    try {
      await apiService.updatePointRoleIndexing(payload);
      alert('인덱싱 명칭이 성공적으로 저장되었습니다.');
      handleRoomSelect(Number(selectedRoom)); // 새로고침
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(`저장 실패: ${e.message}`);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', flex: 1, minHeight: 0, gap: 12, padding: 24, boxSizing: 'border-box' }}>

      {/* 상단 컨트롤 바: 선택 + 검색 + 저장 */}
      <div className="card" style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, alignItems: 'center', padding: 14 }}>

        {/* 시설 / 호실 선택 드롭다운 */}
        <select
          value={selectedFacility}
          onChange={(e) => handleFacilitySelect(Number(e.target.value))}
          className="input"
          style={{ width: 180 }}
        >
          <option value="">-- 시설 선택 --</option>
          {facilities.map(f => <option key={f.id} value={f.id}>{f.name}</option>)}
        </select>

        <select
          value={selectedRoom}
          onChange={(e) => handleRoomSelect(Number(e.target.value))}
          disabled={!selectedFacility}
          className="input"
          style={{ width: 180 }}
        >
          <option value="">-- 호실 선택 --</option>
          {rooms.map(r => <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>{r.name}</option>)}
        </select>

        {/* 하향식 인덱싱 검색창 */}
        <div style={{ position: 'relative', flex: 1 }}>
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="시설 → 호실 → 포인트롤 검색 후 Enter"
            value={searchInput}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchInput(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleSearchSubmit(); }}
            className="input"
          />
          {suggestions.length > 0 && (
            <div
              className="card"
              style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '110%', left: 0, right: 0, zIndex: 10, padding: 6 }}
            >
              {suggestions.map((sug, i) => (
                <div
                  key={i}
                  onClick={() => handleApplySuggestion(sug)}
                  style={{ padding: '8px 10px', cursor: 'pointer', borderRadius: 8, color: 'var(--primary)', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600 }}
                  onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--primary-bg)')}
                  onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent')}
                >
                  {sug.label}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* 일괄 수정 확인 버튼 */}
        <button
          onClick={handleSaveAll}
          disabled={roles.length === 0 || isLoading}
          className="btn btn-primary"
        >
          수정 확인
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* 메인 인덱싱 테이블 */}
      <IndexingTable roles={roles} editMap={editMap} onFieldChange={handleFieldChange} />
    </div>
  );
};


======================================================================
■ File : RoomComparePage.tsx
■ Path : C:\Users\ynham\devspace\IDCool_Systems\idc_web\setuptmp\src\pages\RoomComparePage.tsx
======================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { MainChartViewer } from '../components/chart/viewer/EChartsViewer';
import { apiService } from '../services/apiService';

const COLOR_PALETTE = ['#2e6bef', '#d64545', '#1f8f5f', '#b6791f', '#1e4fbd', '#0c8599', '#9a60b4', '#ea7ccc'];

const SCALE_OPTIONS = [
  { value: '1m', label: '1분 단위' },
  { value: '5m', label: '5분 단위' },
  { value: '10m', label: '10분 단위' },
  { value: '1h', label: '1시간 단위' },
];

const RANGE_OPTIONS = [
  { value: '1h', label: '최근 1시간' },
  { value: '12h', label: '최근 12시간' },
  { value: '1d', label: '최근 1일' },
  { value: '7d', label: '최근 7일' },
  { value: '15d', label: '최근 15일' },
];

export const RoomComparePage: React.FC = () => {
  const [facilities, setFacilities] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedFacility, setSelectedFacility] = useState<number | ''>('');

  const [roles, setRoles] = useState<{ role_name: string; display_name: string; room_count: number }[]>([]);
  const [selectedRole, setSelectedRole] = useState<string>('');

  const [scale, setScale] = useState('1m');
  const [timeRange, setTimeRange] = useState('1d');

  const [datasets, setDatasets] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [roleColors, setRoleColors] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    apiService.getAllFacilitiesAdmin().then(res => setFacilities(res.facilities || []));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedRole('');
    setRoles([]);
    setDatasets([]);
    if (selectedFacility === '') return;
    apiService.getComparableRoles(Number(selectedFacility))
      .then(res => setRoles(res.data?.roles || []))
      .catch(err => setErrorMsg(err.message));
  }, [selectedFacility]);

  const handleCompare = async () => {
    if (selectedFacility === '' || !selectedRole) return;
    setIsLoading(true);
    setErrorMsg('');
    try {
      const res = await apiService.getCompareData(Number(selectedFacility), selectedRole, scale, timeRange);
      const rawDatasets = res.data?.datasets || [];
      // MainChartViewer는 role_id를 시리즈 색상 키로 쓰므로, 색상표를 여기서 구성해서 넘긴다.
      const colors: Record<number, string> = {};
      rawDatasets.forEach((ds: any, idx: number) => { colors[ds.role_id] = COLOR_PALETTE[idx % COLOR_PALETTE.length]; });
      // 같은 role_name이 반복되므로, 시리즈 이름에는 role_name 대신 호실 이름이 나오도록 display_name을 덮어씌운다.
      const labeled = rawDatasets.map((ds: any) => ({ ...ds, display_name: ds.room_name }));
      setDatasets(labeled);
      setRoleColors(colors);
      if (rawDatasets.length === 0) setErrorMsg(res.message || '조회된 데이터가 없습니다.');
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMsg(err.message || '조회에 실패했습니다.');
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', flex: 1, gap: 12, padding: 24, boxSizing: 'border-box', minHeight: 0 }}>
      <h1 style={{ fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: -0.3, flexShrink: 0 }}>호실 비교</h1>
      <p style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', margin: 0, flexShrink: 0 }}>
        같은 시설 안의 여러 호실에서 같은 종류의 값을 한 그래프에 겹쳐서, 어느 호실이 이상하게 동작하는지 한눈에 비교합니다.
      </p>

      <div className="card" style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, alignItems: 'flex-end', flexWrap: 'wrap', flexShrink: 0 }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4, minWidth: 180 }}>
          <span className="label">시설</span>
          <select className="input" value={selectedFacility} onChange={e => setSelectedFacility(e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}>
            <option value="">-- 시설 선택 --</option>
            {facilities.map(f => <option key={f.id} value={f.id}>{f.name}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4, minWidth: 220 }}>
          <span className="label">비교할 항목</span>
          <select className="input" value={selectedRole} onChange={e => setSelectedRole(e.target.value)} disabled={roles.length === 0}>
            <option value="">-- 항목 선택 --</option>
            {roles.map(r => <option key={r.role_name} value={r.role_name}>{r.display_name} ({r.room_count}개 호실)</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4, minWidth: 130 }}>
          <span className="label">해상도</span>
          <select className="input" value={scale} onChange={e => setScale(e.target.value)}>
            {SCALE_OPTIONS.map(o => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4, minWidth: 130 }}>
          <span className="label">조회 기간</span>
          <select className="input" value={timeRange} onChange={e => setTimeRange(e.target.value)}>
            {RANGE_OPTIONS.map(o => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>

        <button className="btn btn-primary" style={{ height: 42 }} onClick={handleCompare} disabled={!selectedFacility || !selectedRole || isLoading}>
          비교하기
        </button>
      </div>

      {errorMsg && (
        <div className="card" style={{ borderColor: 'var(--error)', color: 'var(--error)', fontSize: 13, flexShrink: 0 }}>{errorMsg}</div>
      )}

      <div className="card" style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 400, padding: 12 }}>
        {datasets.length === 0 && !isLoading ? (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', height: '100%', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: 13 }}>
            시설과 비교할 항목을 선택하고 "비교하기"를 눌러주세요.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <MainChartViewer datasets={datasets} roleColors={roleColors} isLoading={isLoading} showInfo showGrid />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

  group_name: string;
  role_ids: number[];
}
